17085
-- name Simcade Engine Module
-- shared

SimcadeEngine = SimcadeEngine or {}

local function normalizeTorqueCurve(curve, idleRPM, limiterRPM)
    if curve == nil then return nil end
    if type(curve) ~= "table" or #curve < 2 or #curve > 32 then
        error("ENGINE_CFG.TorqueCurve must contain 2 to 32 { rpm = number, torqueNm = number } points")
    end

    local points = {}
    local previousRPM = -math.huge
    for i = 1, #curve do
        local point = curve[i]
        local rpm = type(point) == "table" and point.rpm or nil
        local torqueNm = type(point) == "table" and point.torqueNm or nil
        if type(rpm) ~= "number" or rpm ~= rpm or math.abs(rpm) == math.huge
            or type(torqueNm) ~= "number" or torqueNm ~= torqueNm or math.abs(torqueNm) == math.huge
            or rpm < idleRPM or rpm > limiterRPM or rpm <= previousRPM or torqueNm < 0 then
            error("ENGINE_CFG.TorqueCurve RPMs must increase within the idleRPM-to-limiterRPM range; torqueNm must be finite and non-negative")
        end
        points[i] = { rpm = rpm, torqueNm = torqueNm }
        previousRPM = rpm
    end
    return points
end

local function sampleTorqueCurve(points, rpm)
    if rpm <= points[1].rpm then return points[1].torqueNm end
    for i = 2, #points do
        local previous, current = points[i - 1], points[i]
        if rpm <= current.rpm then
            local fraction = (rpm - previous.rpm) / (current.rpm - previous.rpm)
            return previous.torqueNm + (current.torqueNm - previous.torqueNm) * fraction
        end
    end
    return points[#points].torqueNm
end

local function normalizeSharpness(value, name)
    if value == nil then return 1.0 end
    local sharpness = tonumber(value)
    if not sharpness or sharpness ~= sharpness or math.abs(sharpness) == math.huge then
        error("ENGINE_CFG." .. name .. " must be a finite number")
    end
    return math.max(0.25, sharpness)
end

-- Synthesize a 9-point torque curve (c[128..136]) and 3-point engine-brake curve (c[146..148])
-- from DisplacementLiters, PeakHP, PeakTorqueNm, Cylinders, peakTorqueRPM, peakPowerRPM,
-- redlineRPM, limiterRPM, idleRPM, and Powerband. TorqueCurve, when provided, overrides the
-- generated shape with exact piecewise-linear RPM/torque points.
function SimcadeEngine.synthesizeSpec(cfg)
    cfg = cfg or {}
    local liters      = math.max(0.3, tonumber(cfg.DisplacementLiters) or 2.0)
    local cylinders   = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(cfg.Cylinders) or 4))
    local idleRPM     = math.max(400, tonumber(cfg.idleRPM) or 1000)
    local redlineRPM  = math.max(idleRPM + 1500, tonumber(cfg.redlineRPM) or 8000)
    local limiterRPM  = math.max(redlineRPM, tonumber(cfg.limiterRPM) or (redlineRPM + 250))
    local customCurve = normalizeTorqueCurve(cfg.TorqueCurve, idleRPM, limiterRPM)

    -- Peak torque & peak HP (with backward-compatible fallback to baseTorque * TorqueMultiplier)
    local peakTorqueNm = tonumber(cfg.PeakTorqueNm)
    if not peakTorqueNm then
        peakTorqueNm = (tonumber(cfg.baseTorque) or 160) * (tonumber(cfg.TorqueMultiplier) or 1.25)
    end
    local peakTorqueRPM = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.peakTorqueRPM) or (idleRPM + (redlineRPM - idleRPM) * 0.48), idleRPM + 400, redlineRPM - 400)
    local peakPowerRPM  = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.peakPowerRPM)  or (idleRPM + (redlineRPM - idleRPM) * 0.80), peakTorqueRPM + 300, redlineRPM)

    -- Compute PeakHP from PeakTorqueNm if omitted, or refine high-RPM torque to hit PeakHP at peakPowerRPM
    -- Power(HP) = Torque(Nm) * RPM / 7127
    local peakHP = tonumber(cfg.PeakHP)
    if not peakHP or peakHP <= 0 then
        peakHP = (peakTorqueNm * 0.88 * peakPowerRPM) / 7127.0
    end
    if customCurve then
        peakTorqueNm = customCurve[1].torqueNm
        peakTorqueRPM = customCurve[1].rpm
        peakHP = 0
        peakPowerRPM = customCurve[1].rpm
        for i = 1, #customCurve do
            local point = customCurve[i]
            if point.torqueNm > peakTorqueNm then
                peakTorqueNm = point.torqueNm
                peakTorqueRPM = point.rpm
            end
            local pointHP = point.torqueNm * point.rpm / 7127.0
            if pointHP > peakHP then
                peakHP = pointHP
                peakPowerRPM = point.rpm
            end
            if i < #customCurve then
                local nextPoint = customCurve[i + 1]
                local slope = (nextPoint.torqueNm - point.torqueNm) / (nextPoint.rpm - point.rpm)
                if slope < 0 then
                    local intercept = point.torqueNm - slope * point.rpm
                    local peakRPM = -intercept / (2 * slope)
                    if peakRPM > point.rpm and peakRPM < nextPoint.rpm then
                        local peakAtRPM = intercept + slope * peakRPM
                        local segmentHP = peakAtRPM * peakRPM / 7127.0
                        if segmentHP > peakHP then
                            peakHP = segmentHP
                            peakPowerRPM = peakRPM
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local torqueAtPeakPowerNm = math.clamp((peakHP * 7127.0) / math.max(500, peakPowerRPM), peakTorqueNm * 0.35, peakTorqueNm * 1.05)

    -- Powerband shaping:
    --   PowerbandStartRPM .. PowerbandEndRPM define the effective RPM range where the engine is "on cam".
    --   Also supports Powerband = { startRPM = ..., endRPM = ... } or scalar Powerband (0..1 fullness).
    local pbStartRPM, pbEndRPM, pbLowFloor, pbTopFloor, pbRiseSharpness, pbDropSharpness
    if not customCurve then
        local pb = type(cfg.Powerband) == "table" and cfg.Powerband or {}
        local pbScalar = (type(cfg.Powerband) == "number") and math.clamp(cfg.Powerband, 0.0, 1.0) or nil
        pbStartRPM = math.clamp(tonumber(pb.startRPM or cfg.PowerbandStartRPM) or (idleRPM + (peakTorqueRPM - idleRPM) * 0.45), idleRPM + 200, peakTorqueRPM)
        pbEndRPM   = math.clamp(tonumber(pb.endRPM   or cfg.PowerbandEndRPM)   or math.min(redlineRPM, peakPowerRPM + (redlineRPM - peakPowerRPM) * 0.65), peakPowerRPM, limiterRPM)
        pbLowFloor = math.clamp(tonumber(pb.lowEndScale) or (pbScalar and (0.52 + 0.30 * pbScalar) or 0.60), 0.35, 0.92)
        pbTopFloor = math.clamp(tonumber(pb.topEndScale) or (pbScalar and (0.62 + 0.28 * pbScalar) or 0.78), 0.40, 1.00)
        pbRiseSharpness = normalizeSharpness(cfg.PowerbandRiseSharpness, "PowerbandRiseSharpness")
        pbDropSharpness = normalizeSharpness(cfg.PowerbandDropSharpness, "PowerbandDropSharpness")
    end

    -- Keep nine compatibility samples in c and use a denser runtime curve for the shaped taper.
    local span = math.max(500, limiterRPM - idleRPM)
    local torqueCurveKNm = {}
    local function generatedTorqueNm(rpm)
        local tqNm
        if rpm < pbStartRPM then
            -- Below powerband: smooth build from idle floor up to ~86% of peak torque at PowerbandStartRPM
            local u = math.clamp((rpm - idleRPM) / math.max(200, pbStartRPM - idleRPM), 0, 1)
            local riseProgress = u <= 0 and 0 or u ^ (1.0 / pbRiseSharpness)
            local s = riseProgress * riseProgress * (3 - 2 * riseProgress)
            local startScale = math.max(pbLowFloor + 0.12, 0.84)
            tqNm = peakTorqueNm * (pbLowFloor + (startScale - pbLowFloor) * s)
        elseif rpm <= peakTorqueRPM then
            -- Entering powerband: climbs from startScale to 100% PeakTorqueNm
            local u = math.clamp((rpm - pbStartRPM) / math.max(200, peakTorqueRPM - pbStartRPM), 0, 1)
            local riseProgress = u <= 0 and 0 or u ^ (1.0 / pbRiseSharpness)
            local s = riseProgress * riseProgress * (3 - 2 * riseProgress)
            local startScale = math.max(pbLowFloor + 0.12, 0.84)
            tqNm = peakTorqueNm * (startScale + (1.0 - startScale) * s)
        elseif rpm <= peakPowerRPM then
            -- Mid-to-upper powerband: transitions from PeakTorqueNm to torqueAtPeakPowerNm (= PeakHP * 7127 / peakPowerRPM)
            local u = math.clamp((rpm - peakTorqueRPM) / math.max(200, peakPowerRPM - peakTorqueRPM), 0, 1)
            local s = u * u * (3 - 2 * u)
            tqNm = peakTorqueNm + (torqueAtPeakPowerNm - peakTorqueNm) * s
        elseif rpm <= pbEndRPM then
            -- Upper powerband: sustains power cleanly up to PowerbandEndRPM
            local u = math.clamp((rpm - peakPowerRPM) / math.max(150, pbEndRPM - peakPowerRPM), 0, 1)
            local endTarget = torqueAtPeakPowerNm * math.max(0.88, pbTopFloor)
            tqNm = torqueAtPeakPowerNm + (endTarget - torqueAtPeakPowerNm) * (u * 0.65)
        else
            -- Above powerband (towards redline/limiter): shape how quickly torque falls.
            local u = math.clamp((rpm - pbEndRPM) / math.max(150, limiterRPM - pbEndRPM), 0, 1)
            local endTarget = torqueAtPeakPowerNm * math.max(0.88, pbTopFloor)
            local limTarget = torqueAtPeakPowerNm * pbTopFloor * 0.85
            local dropStart = torqueAtPeakPowerNm
            if pbEndRPM > peakPowerRPM then
                dropStart = torqueAtPeakPowerNm + (endTarget - torqueAtPeakPowerNm) * 0.65
            end
            local dropProgress = u <= 0 and 0 or u ^ (1.0 / pbDropSharpness)
            tqNm = dropStart + (limTarget - dropStart) * dropProgress
        end
        return math.max(20, tqNm)
    end

    for i = 0, 8 do
        local rpm = idleRPM + (span * (i / 8))
        local tqNm = customCurve and sampleTorqueCurve(customCurve, rpm) or generatedTorqueNm(rpm)
        torqueCurveKNm[i] = tqNm * 0.001
    end

    local runtimeTorqueCurve
    if customCurve then
        runtimeTorqueCurve = { n = #customCurve, points = {} }
        for i = 1, #customCurve do
            runtimeTorqueCurve.points[i] = {
                rpm = customCurve[i].rpm,
                value = customCurve[i].torqueNm * 0.001,
            }
        end
    else
        local runtimePointCount = 33
        runtimeTorqueCurve = {
            n = runtimePointCount,
            lo = idleRPM,
            k = (runtimePointCount - 1) / span,
            v = {},
        }
        for i = 0, runtimePointCount - 1 do
            local rpm = idleRPM + span * (i / (runtimePointCount - 1))
            runtimeTorqueCurve.v[i] = generatedTorqueNm(rpm) * 0.001
        end
    end

    -- Auto-derive flywheel inertia and engine braking from displacement & cylinders unless overridden
    local autoInertia = math.clamp(0.06 + liters * 0.035 + (cylinders - 4) * 0.008, 0.07, 0.45)
    local flywheelInertia = tonumber(cfg.flywheelInertia) or autoInertia
    local autoEngBrake = math.clamp(35 + liters * 24 + cylinders * 4, 40, 260)
    local engineBrakeTorque = tonumber(cfg.engineBrakeTorque) or autoEngBrake

    return {
        idleRPM           = idleRPM,
        peakTorqueRPM     = peakTorqueRPM,
        peakPowerRPM      = peakPowerRPM,
        redlineRPM        = redlineRPM,
        limiterRPM        = limiterRPM,
        peakTorqueNm      = peakTorqueNm,
        peakHP            = peakHP,
        flywheelInertia   = flywheelInertia,
        engineBrakeTorque = engineBrakeTorque,
        torqueCurveKNm    = torqueCurveKNm,
        torqueCurve       = runtimeTorqueCurve,
        brakeCurveKNm     = {
            [0] = (engineBrakeTorque * 0.42) * 0.001,
            [1] = (engineBrakeTorque * 0.78) * 0.001,
            [2] = (engineBrakeTorque * 1.15) * 0.001,
        },
    }
end

-- Sim-Engine 4-State Ignition Machine:
--   0 = Off (electronics off, engine dead)
--   1 = Ignition On (electronics on; if running -> engine stays running)
--   2 = Cranking (starter motor active; catches after crankDuration)
--   3 = Catch / Running transition -> settles to state 1 with running = true
function SimcadeEngine.newIgnitionState(cfg)
    cfg = cfg or {}
    local autoStart = (cfg.StartRunning ~= false) and (cfg.AutoStartOnEnter ~= false)
    return {
        state         = autoStart and 1 or 0,
        running       = autoStart,
        lastKey       = false,
        crankTimer    = 0,
        crankDuration = math.max(0.15, tonumber(cfg.StarterCrankTime or cfg.crankTime) or 0.45),
        crankRPM      = math.max(150, tonumber(cfg.StarterRPM or cfg.crankRPM) or 320),
        autoStart     = autoStart,
    }
end

function SimcadeEngine.stepIgnition(ign, keyDown, dt, hasDriver)
    if not ign then return { mode = 0, running = false, cranking = false, crankRPM = 0 } end
    if hasDriver == false then
        ign.lastKey = false
        ign.crankTimer = 0
        if ign.state == 2 then ign.state = 1 end
        return { mode = ign.state, running = ign.running, cranking = false, crankRPM = 0 }
    end

    local pressed = (keyDown == true or keyDown == 1)
    if pressed and not ign.lastKey then
        if ign.state == 0 then
            ign.state = 1
        elseif ign.state == 1 then
            if ign.running then
                ign.running = false
                ign.state = 0
            else
                ign.state = 2
                ign.crankTimer = 0
            end
        elseif ign.state == 2 then
            ign.state = 1
            ign.crankTimer = 0
        end
    end

    if ign.state == 2 then
        if pressed then
            ign.crankTimer = ign.crankTimer + (dt or 0.015)
            if ign.crankTimer >= ign.crankDuration then
                ign.running = true
                ign.state = 3
            end
        else
            ign.state = 1
            ign.crankTimer = 0
        end
    elseif ign.state == 3 then
        if ign.running then
            ign.state = 1
        end
    end

    ign.lastKey = pressed
    return {
        mode     = ign.state,
        running  = ign.running,
        cranking = (ign.state == 2),
        crankRPM = (ign.state == 2) and ign.crankRPM or 0,
    }
end

-- Sim-Engine In-Depth Turbo Spool & Boost Simulation
function SimcadeEngine.newTurboState()
    return { rpm = 0, psi = 0, boost = 1.0 }
end

function SimcadeEngine.stepTurbo(turboState, cfg, rpm, throttle, dt)
    if not (cfg and cfg.Turbo) then
        turboState.rpm, turboState.psi, turboState.boost = 0, 0, 1.0
        return turboState
    end
    local maxRPM    = math.max(1000, tonumber(cfg.Turbo_MaxRPM) or 9000)
    local psiMax    = math.max(0, tonumber(cfg.Turbo_PSIMax or cfg.BoostPSI) or 12)
    local startRPM  = math.max(0, tonumber(cfg.Turbo_StartRPM) or 2000)
    local wastegate = tonumber(cfg.Turbo_WastegatePSI) or psiMax
    local airPress  = math.max(1, tonumber(cfg.Turbo_AirPressure) or 18.4)
    local maxFlow   = math.max(100, tonumber(cfg.Turbo_MaxFlow) or 9000)
    local flowScale = math.max(0, tonumber(cfg.Turbo_FlowScale) or 100)
    local dragScale = math.max(0, tonumber(cfg.Turbo_DragScale) or 0.01)
    local inertia   = math.max(0.005, tonumber(cfg.Turbo_Inertia) or 0.035)
    local exp       = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.Turbo_Exp) or 1.0, 0.1, 3.0)
    local stepDt    = math.clamp(tonumber(dt) or 0.015, 0, 0.1)
    local targetPsi = math.clamp(wastegate, 0, psiMax)

    local tRPM   = turboState.rpm or 0
    local psi    = targetPsi * (tRPM / maxRPM)
    local boost  = (psi + airPress) / airPress
    local engineRPM = math.max(0, tonumber(rpm) or 0)
    local flow   = math.max(0, engineRPM - startRPM) * boost / maxFlow
    local thr    = math.clamp(throttle or 0, 0, 1)
    local dragThrottle = (engineRPM > startRPM) and thr or 0
    local drag   = (maxFlow * dragScale) * (tRPM / maxRPM) * (1 - dragThrottle) / inertia
    local dTurbo = (flowScale * (flow ^ exp)) / inertia - drag

    turboState.rpm   = math.clamp(tRPM + dTurbo * stepDt, 1, maxRPM)
    turboState.psi   = targetPsi * (turboState.rpm / maxRPM)
    turboState.boost = (turboState.psi + airPress) / airPress
    return turboState
end

function SimcadeEngine.newSuperchargerState()
    return { psi = 0, boost = 1.0 }
end

function SimcadeEngine.stepSupercharger(superchargerState, cfg, rpm, throttle)
    if not (cfg and cfg.Supercharger) then
        superchargerState.psi, superchargerState.boost = 0, 1.0
        return superchargerState
    end

    local superchargerType = string.lower(tostring(cfg.Supercharger_Type or "centrifugal"))
    local typeExp = (superchargerType == "roots" and 0.65)
        or ((superchargerType == "twin_screw" or superchargerType == "twin-screw") and 0.8)
        or 2.0
    local maxPSI = math.max(0, tonumber(cfg.Supercharger_MaxPSI) or 8)
    local airPressure = math.max(1, tonumber(cfg.Supercharger_AirPressure) or 18.4)
    local ratedRPM = math.max(1000, tonumber(cfg.Supercharger_RatedRPM) or 6500)
    local pressureExp = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.Supercharger_PressureExp) or typeExp, 0.1, 3.0)
    local engineRPM = math.max(0, tonumber(rpm) or 0)
    local speedRatio = math.clamp(engineRPM / ratedRPM, 0, 1)
    local throttleLoad = math.clamp(tonumber(throttle) or 0, 0, 1)

    superchargerState.psi = maxPSI * (speedRatio ^ pressureExp) * throttleLoad
    superchargerState.boost = (superchargerState.psi + airPressure) / airPressure
    return superchargerState
end
3637
-- name Simcade Aerodynamics Module
-- shared

SimcadeAero = SimcadeAero or {}

local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b)
    if v < a then return a elseif v > b then return b else return v end
end
local max = math.max
local sqrt = math.sqrt

SimcadeAero.frontForceOffset = SimcadeAero.frontForceOffset or { len = 3, [0] = 1, 0, 0 }
SimcadeAero.rearForceOffset = SimcadeAero.rearForceOffset or { len = 3, [0] = -1, 0, 0 }

local function computeWheelLoadKN(aeroCfg, totalMassKG, speedMs, isFront)
    if not aeroCfg then return 0 end
    local factor = isFront and (aeroCfg.FrontAeroFactor or 0) or (aeroCfg.RearAeroFactor or 0)
    if factor <= 0 then return 0 end
    local speedMph = math.abs(speedMs or 0) * 2.23693629
    local mphDiv100 = speedMph / 100.0
    return max(0, factor * (totalMassKG * 0.004905) * (mphDiv100 * mphDiv100))
end

function SimcadeAero.addTireLoadKN(tireLoadKN, aeroCfg, totalMassKG, speedMs, isFront)
    return (tireLoadKN or 0) + computeWheelLoadKN(aeroCfg, totalMassKG, speedMs, isFront)
end

function SimcadeAero.buildSolverCoefficients(aeroCfg, massScale)
    local dragCoeff
    if aeroCfg and (aeroCfg.DragCoeff or aeroCfg.FrontalAreaM2) then
        local cd = max(0.01, aeroCfg.DragCoeff or 0.32)
        local area = max(0.1, aeroCfg.FrontalAreaM2 or 2.15)
        dragCoeff = clamp(0.5 * 1.225 * cd * area * 0.001, 0.0, 0.0025)
    else
        dragCoeff = clamp(0.00034 * (0.85 + 0.15 * massScale), 0.00024, 0.00058)
    end

    return dragCoeff,
        clamp(0.00020 * massScale, 0.00012, 0.00055),
        clamp(0.00024 * massScale, 0.00015, 0.00065)
end

function SimcadeAero.applyForces(chassis)
    local A, c, v, R = chassis.aero, chassis.c, chassis.v, chassis.R
    local speed = sqrt(v[0] * v[0] + v[1] * v[1] + v[2] * v[2])
    local scaledSpeed = speed / chassis.perf
    local drag = -(A.grip * c[152] * scaledSpeed * A.on)
    local armz = 0
    if A.grounded >= 0.5 then armz = (1 - A.T) * -0.10000000149011612 end
    chassis:addForce3(drag * v[0], drag * v[1], drag * v[2], 0, 0, armz)

    local groundFactor = R[8]
    if groundFactor < 0 then groundFactor = 0 end
    if A.grounded >= 0.5 then groundFactor = 1 end
    local n0, n1, n2 = 1, 0, 0
    if scaledSpeed > 0.0001 then
        n0, n1, n2 = v[0] / scaledSpeed, v[1] / scaledSpeed, v[2] / scaledSpeed
    end
    local alignment = n2 * R[2] + n1 * R[1] + n0 * R[0]
    if alignment < 0 then alignment = 0 end
    local alignmentSq = alignment * alignment
    if alignmentSq < 0.4000000059604645 then alignmentSq = 0.4000000059604645 end

    local frontLoad, rearLoad
    if A.grounded == 0 then
        local totalLoad = groundFactor * ((c[154] + c[153]) * alignmentSq) * scaledSpeed
        frontLoad = 0.4000000059604645 * totalLoad
        rearLoad = totalLoad * 0.4099999964237213
    else
        frontLoad = alignmentSq * c[153] * groundFactor * scaledSpeed
        rearLoad = alignmentSq * c[154] * groundFactor * scaledSpeed
    end

    local af = chassis.T.aero_front or SimcadeAero.frontForceOffset
    local ar = chassis.T.aero_rear or SimcadeAero.rearForceOffset
    local force = -(frontLoad * A.on)
    chassis:addForce3(force * R[6], force * R[7], force * R[8],
        af[0] * R[0] + af[1] * R[3] + af[2] * R[6],
        af[0] * R[1] + af[1] * R[4] + af[2] * R[7],
        af[0] * R[2] + af[1] * R[5] + af[2] * R[8])

    force = -(rearLoad * A.on)
    chassis:addForce3(force * R[6], force * R[7], force * R[8],
        ar[0] * R[0] + ar[1] * R[3] + ar[2] * R[6],
        ar[0] * R[1] + ar[1] * R[4] + ar[2] * R[7],
        ar[0] * R[2] + ar[1] * R[5] + ar[2] * R[8])
end

return SimcadeAero

-- name Simcade Tires Module
-- shared

SimcadeTires = SimcadeTires or {}

local Aero = assert(SimcadeAero, "Load simcade_aero.txt before simcade_tires.txt")
local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b) return v < a and a or (v > b and b or v) end

SimcadeTires.COMPOUNDS = {
    Comfort = { CoF = 1.15, Lat_B =  8.0, Lat_C = 1.30, Lat_E = 0.10, Long_B = 10.0, Long_C = 1.50, Long_E = 0.40, LoadSens = 0.10, offroadRetain = 0.25 },
    Sport   = { CoF = 1.72, Lat_B = 10.0, Lat_C = 1.35, Lat_E = 0.10, Long_B = 12.0, Long_C = 1.55, Long_E = 0.30, LoadSens = 0.10, offroadRetain = 0.15 },
    Slick   = { CoF = 2.15, Lat_B = 12.0, Lat_C = 1.40, Lat_E = 0.10, Long_B = 14.0, Long_C = 1.60, Long_E = 0.60, LoadSens = 0.08, offroadRetain = 0.00 },
    Rally   = { CoF = 1.52, Lat_B =  9.0, Lat_C = 1.45, Lat_E = 0.10, Long_B = 11.0, Long_C = 1.50, Long_E = 0.50, LoadSens = 0.08, offroadRetain = 0.95 },
    Drift   = { CoF = 1.02, Lat_B =  8.5, Lat_C = 1.25, Lat_E = 0.25, Long_B = 11.0, Long_C = 1.45, Long_E = 0.50, LoadSens = 0.10, offroadRetain = 0.20 },
}

SimcadeTires.SURFACE_MAP = {
    [67] = 1,   -- MAT_CONCRETE
    [84] = 1,   -- MAT_TILE
    [76] = 1,   -- MAT_PLASTIC
    [80] = 1,   -- MAT_COMPUTER / hard brush
    [83] = 13,  -- MAT_SLOSH -> wet puddle
    [88] = 1,   -- MAT_DEFAULT
    [73] = 1,   -- MAT_CLIP
    [71] = 26,  -- MAT_GRATE
    [86] = 26,  -- MAT_VENT
    [68] = 8,   -- MAT_DIRT
    [79] = 15,  -- MAT_FOLIAGE
    [78] = 9,   -- MAT_SAND
    [85] = 15,  -- MAT_GRASS
    [74] = 13,  -- MAT_SNOW -> low grip
    [77] = 26,  -- MAT_METAL
    [87] = 17,  -- MAT_WOOD
}

SimcadeTires.SURFACE_GRIP_MULT = {
    [0]  = { lat = 1.00, lon = 1.00 },
    [1]  = { lat = 1.00, lon = 1.00 },
    [8]  = { lat = 0.62, lon = 0.65, offroadLatFloor = 0.90, offroadLonFloor = 0.92 },
    [9]  = { lat = 0.66, lon = 0.68, offroadLatFloor = 0.92, offroadLonFloor = 0.94 },
    [13] = { lat = 0.54, lon = 0.58, offroadLatFloor = 0.80, offroadLonFloor = 0.82 },
    [15] = { lat = 0.56, lon = 0.58, offroadLatFloor = 0.86, offroadLonFloor = 0.88 },
    [17] = { lat = 0.88, lon = 0.88, offroadLatFloor = 0.92, offroadLonFloor = 0.92 },
    [26] = { lat = 0.93, lon = 0.93, offroadLatFloor = 0.95, offroadLonFloor = 0.95 },
}

SimcadeTires.SURFACE_ROLLING_MULT = {
    [67] = 1.0,  -- MAT_CONCRETE
    [68] = 2.4,  -- MAT_DIRT
    [71] = 1.2,  -- MAT_GRATE
    [74] = 3.2,  -- MAT_SNOW
    [77] = 1.0,  -- MAT_METAL
    [78] = 12.0, -- MAT_SAND
    [84] = 0.95, -- MAT_TILE
    [85] = 2.8,  -- MAT_GRASS
    [87] = 1.25, -- MAT_WOOD
    [88] = 1.0,  -- MAT_DEFAULT
}

-- Compatibility for older configs that used the fixed ice key.
SimcadeTires.ICE = 250
SimcadeTires.SURFACE_MAP[SimcadeTires.ICE] = 14
local namedMaterials = { ice = SimcadeTires.ICE, gmod_ice = SimcadeTires.ICE }
local surfaceNames = {}

function SimcadeTires.applySurfaceProfiles(cfg)
    if not cfg.SURFACE_PROFILES then return end
    local tire = cfg.TIRE_CFG or {}
    cfg.TIRE_CFG = tire
    tire.SurfacePropertyOverrides = tire.SurfacePropertyOverrides or {}
    tire.SurfaceIndexOverrides = tire.SurfaceIndexOverrides or {}
    tire.SurfaceTextureOverrides = tire.SurfaceTextureOverrides or {}
    tire.SurfaceMultipliers = tire.SurfaceMultipliers or {}
    tire.SurfaceRollingResistance = tire.SurfaceRollingResistance or {}

    local function applySection(sectionName, key, base, settings)
        local section = cfg[sectionName] or {}
        cfg[sectionName] = section
        section.Surfaces = section.Surfaces or {}
        section.Surfaces[key] = settings or section.Surfaces[key] or section.Surfaces[base]
    end

    local function applyProfile(key, profile, defaultProperty)
        for _, name in ipairs(profile.Properties or { defaultProperty }) do
            tire.SurfacePropertyOverrides[name:lower()] = key
        end
        for index, matType in pairs(profile.IndexHints or {}) do
            tire.SurfaceIndexOverrides[index] = { matType = matType, profile = key }
        end
        for _, texture in ipairs(profile.Textures or {}) do
            tire.SurfaceTextureOverrides[texture:lower():gsub("\\", "/")] = key
        end
        if profile.Grip ~= nil then
            local grip = profile.Grip
            tire.SurfaceMultipliers[key] = type(grip) == "table" and grip or { lat = grip, lon = grip }
        end
        if profile.RollingResistance ~= nil then tire.SurfaceRollingResistance[key] = profile.RollingResistance end
        applySection("SOUND_CFG", key, profile.BaseMaterial, profile.Audio)
        applySection("SKID_CFG", key, profile.BaseMaterial, profile.Skidmarks)
        applySection("EFFECT_CFG", key, profile.BaseMaterial, profile.Effects)
    end

    -- Both realms assign the same byte IDs, regardless of Lua's table order.
    tire.SurfaceProfileIds = {}
    local names = {}
    local used = { [SimcadeTires.ICE] = true }
    for key, profile in pairs(cfg.SURFACE_PROFILES) do
        if type(key) == "string" then
            names[#names + 1] = key
        else
            assert(type(key) == "number" and key >= 1 and key <= 255 and key % 1 == 0,
                "Surface profiles use names or legacy integer keys from 1 to 255")
            used[key] = true
            applyProfile(key, profile)
        end
    end
    table.sort(names)
    local key = 255
    for _, name in ipairs(names) do
        while used[key] do key = key - 1 end
        assert(key >= 128, "Too many named surface profiles")
        tire.SurfaceProfileIds[name] = key
        applyProfile(key, cfg.SURFACE_PROFILES[name], name)
        key = key - 1
    end
end

function SimcadeTires.resolveMaterial(hit, cfg)
    local mat = hit.MatType or 67
    local properties = cfg and cfg.SurfacePropertyOverrides or namedMaterials
    local id = hit.SurfaceProps
    local propName
    if not hit.HitWorld and isValid(hit.Entity) and hit.Entity.getPhysMaterial then
        local ok, name = pcall(hit.Entity.getPhysMaterial, hit.Entity)
        if ok and type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" then
            propName = name:lower()
            if properties[propName] then return properties[propName] end
        end
    end
    local name = id
    if type(id) == "number" and id >= 0 and util and util.GetSurfaceData then
        name = surfaceNames[id]
        if name == nil then
            local ok, data = pcall(util.GetSurfaceData, id)
            name = ok and data and data.name or false
            surfaceNames[id] = name
        end
    end
    if type(name) == "string" and properties[name:lower()] then return properties[name:lower()] end
    -- HitTexture identifies the struck brush face. Models can report generic
    -- placeholders here, so only consider explicit brush texture mappings.
    local textures = cfg and cfg.SurfaceTextureOverrides
    if textures and type(hit.HitTexture) == "string" and hit.HitTexture:sub(1, 1) ~= "*" then
        local key = hit.HitTexture:lower():gsub("\\", "/")
        if textures[key] then return textures[key] end
    end
    -- Only consult index hints when neither lookup identified the surface.
    if not propName and (type(name) ~= "string" or name == "") then
        local indices = cfg and cfg.SurfaceIndexOverrides
        local override = indices and indices[id]
        if type(override) == "number" then return override end
        if override and override.matType == mat then return override.profile end
    end
    return mat
end

function SimcadeTires.mapSurface(matType)
    return SimcadeTires.SURFACE_MAP[matType] or 1
end

function SimcadeTires.evaluateMagicFormula(B, C, E, x)
    local Bx = B * x
    return math.sin(C * math.atan(Bx - E * (Bx - math.atan(Bx))))
end

function SimcadeTires.buildAxleSpec(axleCfg, compoundName, cornerLoadKN, tireCfg, axleName)
    local cName = (axleCfg and axleCfg.Compound) or compoundName or "Sport"
    local customComp = tireCfg and tireCfg.Compounds and tireCfg.Compounds[cName]
    local compoundAxle = customComp and customComp[axleName]
    local baseComp = SimcadeTires.COMPOUNDS[cName] or SimcadeTires.COMPOUNDS.Sport
    local axFormula = axleCfg and axleCfg.Formula
    local glFormula = tireCfg and tireCfg.Formula
    local function pick(k, def)
        if axleCfg and axleCfg[k] ~= nil then return axleCfg[k] end
        if axFormula and axFormula[k] ~= nil then return axFormula[k] end
        if compoundAxle and compoundAxle[k] ~= nil then return compoundAxle[k] end
        if customComp and customComp[k] ~= nil then return customComp[k] end
        if glFormula and glFormula[k] ~= nil then return glFormula[k] end
        if baseComp and baseComp[k] ~= nil then return baseComp[k] end
        return def
    end

    local function surfaceMap(field)
        local merged = {}
        local function overlay(settings)
            for key, value in pairs(settings and settings[field] or {}) do
                local ids = tireCfg and tireCfg.SurfaceProfileIds
                merged[(ids and ids[key]) or key] = value
            end
        end
        overlay(tireCfg)
        overlay(customComp)
        overlay(compoundAxle)
        overlay(axleCfg)
        return merged
    end

    local widthMm = (axleCfg and axleCfg.Width) or 245
    local direct  = (tireCfg and tireCfg.UseDirectMultipliers) and true or false
    local ovDef   = direct and 1.0 or 1.38
    local laDef   = direct and 1.0 or 1.25
    local loDef   = direct and 1.0 or 1.35
    local glOv    = (tireCfg and (tireCfg.Grip or tireCfg.OverallGripMultiplier)) or 1.0
    local glLa    = (tireCfg and (tireCfg.LateralGrip or tireCfg.LateralGripMultiplier)) or 1.0
    local glLo    = (tireCfg and (tireCfg.LongitudinalGrip or tireCfg.LongitudinalGripMultiplier)) or 1.0
    local ovMult  = (axleCfg and (axleCfg.Grip or axleCfg.OverallGripMultiplier)) or ovDef
    local laMult  = (axleCfg and (axleCfg.LateralGrip or axleCfg.LateralGripMultiplier)) or laDef
    local loMult  = (axleCfg and (axleCfg.LongitudinalGrip or axleCfg.LongitudinalGripMultiplier)) or loDef
    local gBias   = (axleCfg and axleCfg.GripBias) or 1.0

    local widthFactor = clamp((widthMm / 245) ^ 0.18, 0.88, 1.15)
    local baseFactor  = widthFactor * gBias

    return {
        compoundName    = cName,
        CoF             = pick("CoF", 1.72),
        Lat_B           = pick("Lat_B", 10.0),
        Lat_C           = pick("Lat_C", 1.35),
        Lat_E           = pick("Lat_E", 0.10),
        camberStiffness = math.max(0, pick("CamberStiffness", 0.65)),
        Long_B          = pick("Long_B", 12.0),
        Long_C          = pick("Long_C", 1.55),
        Long_E          = pick("Long_E", 0.60),
        LoadSens        = pick("LoadSens", (tireCfg and tireCfg.LoadSensitivity) or 0.12),
        offroadRetain   = pick("offroadRetain", 0.15),
        rollingRes      = pick("RollingResistance", pick("BaseRollingResistance", (tireCfg and tireCfg.RollingResistance) or 0.015)),
        rollingResMults = surfaceMap("SurfaceRollingResistance"),
        surfaceMults    = surfaceMap("SurfaceMultipliers"),
        nominalLoadKN   = math.max(0.5, cornerLoadKN or 3.68),
        latScale        = baseFactor * glOv * glLa * (ovMult / ovDef) * (laMult / laDef),
        longScale       = baseFactor * glOv * glLo * (ovMult / ovDef) * (loMult / loDef),
        -- TireSimV2 low-speed stability & grip damping (prevents small-steer / 10km/h overload)
        V0              = (tireCfg and tireCfg.V0) or 1.016, -- 40 in/s
        lowSpeedDeadzone = (tireCfg and tireCfg.LowSpeedLateralHoldDeadzone) or 0.08,
        lowSpeedHoldScale = (tireCfg and tireCfg.LowSpeedHoldScale) or 0.35,
        gripDamping     = (tireCfg and tireCfg.GripDampingFactor) or 0.95,
    }
end

function SimcadeTires.computeSurfaceMult(spec, surfaceId, matType)
    local configured = spec and spec.surfaceMults and spec.surfaceMults[matType]
    if type(configured) == "table" then
        return configured.lat or 1, configured.lon or 1
    end
    if matType == SimcadeTires.ICE then
        local grip = configured or 0.12
        return grip, grip
    end
    if spec and spec.surfaceMults and matType ~= nil then
        local custom = spec.surfaceMults[matType] or spec.surfaceMults.Default
        if type(custom) == "number" then
            local retain = spec.offroadRetain or 0
            if custom < 0.90 then
                if retain <= 0.02 and (surfaceId or 1) > 1 then
                    local low = custom * 0.55
                    return low, low
                end
                local effective = custom + (math.max(custom, 0.85) - custom) * retain * 0.5
                return effective, effective
            end
            return custom, custom
        end
    end
    local base = SimcadeTires.SURFACE_GRIP_MULT[surfaceId or 1] or SimcadeTires.SURFACE_GRIP_MULT[1]
    local retain = spec and spec.offroadRetain or 0
    if (surfaceId or 1) > 1 and retain <= 0.02 then
        return base.lat * 0.35, base.lon * 0.35
    end
    local sLat = base.offroadLatFloor and (base.lat + (math.max(base.lat, base.offroadLatFloor) - base.lat) * retain) or base.lat
    local sLon = base.offroadLonFloor and (base.lon + (math.max(base.lon, base.offroadLonFloor) - base.lon) * retain) or base.lon
    return sLat, sLon
end

function SimcadeTires.computeRollingResistanceMult(spec, surfaceId, matType)
    if matType == SimcadeTires.ICE then return spec and spec.rollingResMults and spec.rollingResMults[matType] or 1 end
    if spec and spec.rollingResMults and matType ~= nil then
        local custom = spec.rollingResMults[matType] or spec.rollingResMults.Default
        if custom ~= nil then return math.max(0.1, tonumber(custom) or 1.0) end
    end
    if matType ~= nil and SimcadeTires.SURFACE_ROLLING_MULT[matType] then
        return SimcadeTires.SURFACE_ROLLING_MULT[matType]
    end
    if surfaceId == 8 or surfaceId == 9 then return 2.4
    elseif surfaceId == 15 then return 2.8
    elseif surfaceId == 13 or surfaceId == 14 then return 2.5
    end
    return 1.0
end

function SimcadeTires.addAeroLoadKN(tireLoadKN, aeroCfg, totalMassKG, speedMs, isFront)
    return Aero.addTireLoadKN(tireLoadKN, aeroCfg, totalMassKG, speedMs, isFront)
end

7874
-- name Simcade Gearbox & Differential Module
-- shared

SimcadeGearbox = SimcadeGearbox or {}

local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b) return v < a and a or (v > b and b or v) end

-- Compute Sim-Engine style Master Clutch disengagement (0 = engaged, 1 = disengaged)
function SimcadeGearbox.computeMasterClutch(params)
    local gear         = params.gear or 2
    local state        = params.state or 2
    local clutchPedal  = params.clutchPedal
    local handbrake    = params.handbrake or 0
    local brkIn        = params.brake or 0
    local thrIn        = params.throttle or 0
    local speedKmh     = math.abs(params.speedKmh or 0)
    local stopSpd      = params.stoppedPhaseBrakeSpeed or 5
    local rpmNow       = params.rpm or 1000
    local redline      = math.max(1000, params.redlineRPM or 8000)
    local isManual     = params.isManual and true or false
    local useInManual  = (params.useClutchLerpInManual ~= false)
    local inReverse    = (gear == 0) or (params.reversing == true)
    local lerpLim      = inReverse and (params.clutchLerpReverse or 0.5) or (params.clutchLerp or 0.4)

    local hasThrInput = thrIn > 0.05
    local simClutch = 0
    if gear == 1 or state == 0 or state == 1 or (clutchPedal == 0) or (handbrake > 0.5) then
        simClutch = 1
    elseif brkIn > 0.05 and not hasThrInput and speedKmh <= stopSpd then
        simClutch = 1
    elseif brkIn > 0.05 and not hasThrInput and speedKmh <= (stopSpd + 15) then
        simClutch = 1.0 - clamp((speedKmh - stopSpd) / 15.0, 0, 1)
    elseif not hasThrInput and speedKmh <= stopSpd then
        simClutch = 1
    end

    if simClutch < 1 and (not isManual or useInManual) then
        local rpmClutch = math.max(0, lerpLim - (rpmNow / redline))
        simClutch = math.max(simClutch, rpmClutch)
    end
    return clamp(simClutch, 0, 1)
end

-- Sim-Engine Fixed-Gearing Upshift & Downshift with Anti-Hunt, Slip & Over-Rev Guards
function SimcadeGearbox.evaluateAutoShift(car, dt)
    local d, c = car.dt, car.c
    if d.gear == 1 or d.shifting ~= 0 then return nil end

    local function getRatio(g)
        return (car.gearRatio and car:gearRatio(g)) or c[116 + g] or 0
    end

    local thr = (car.drv and car.drv.thr) or 0
    local redline = math.max(1000, c[125] or c[126] or 8000)
    local rpmNow = car.eng.w * 9.54929676
    local rpmPct = (rpmNow / redline) * 100

    local comfortUp   = car.comfortUpshiftRPM   or 62
    local sportUp     = car.sportUpshiftRPM     or 90
    local comfortDown = car.comfortDownshiftRPM or 34
    local sportDown   = car.sportDownshiftRPM   or 55
    local thrEff      = car.alwaysSportMode and 1.0 or clamp(thr, 0, 1)
    local upPct       = comfortUp   + (sportUp   - comfortUp)   * thrEff
    local downPct     = comfortDown + (sportDown - comfortDown) * thrEff
    local downRPM     = redline * (downPct / 100)
    local sportDownRPM = redline * (sportDown / 100)

    local roadMS = math.abs(car.forward or 0)
    local speedKmh = roadMS * 3.6
    local now = car.now or 0
    local antiHuntCooldown = math.max(0.25, car.downshiftCooldown or 0.45)
    local sinceLastShift = now - (car.lastShiftTime or -10)

    if d.gear <= 0 then
        local minGear = car.minGear or 0
        if minGear >= 0 or d.state ~= 3 then return nil end
        local revIdx = 1 - d.gear -- 0 -> R1 (1), -1 -> R2 (2)
        local revSpeeds = car.reverseGearSpeeds
        local upshiftSpd = revSpeeds and revSpeeds[revIdx]
        if d.gear > minGear and thr > 0.05 and ((upshiftSpd and speedKmh >= upshiftSpd) or rpmPct >= upPct) then
            if car.lastShiftDir ~= "down" or sinceLastShift >= antiHuntCooldown then
                car.lastShiftDir      = "up"
                car.lastShiftTime     = now
                car.nextDownshiftTime = now + antiHuntCooldown
                return d.gear - 1
            end
        elseif d.gear < 0 and now >= (car.nextDownshiftTime or 0) then
            local downSpd = revSpeeds and revSpeeds[revIdx - 1]
            if (downSpd and speedKmh <= downSpd * 0.72) or rpmPct <= downPct then
                if car.lastShiftDir ~= "up" or sinceLastShift >= antiHuntCooldown then
                    car.nextDownshiftTime = now + antiHuntCooldown
                    car.lastShiftDir      = "down"
                    car.lastShiftTime     = now
                    return d.gear + 1
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local topGear = (car.top or 6) + 1
    local gearSpeeds = car.gearSpeeds
    local wRad = (car.wheels and car.wheels[2] and car.wheels[2].radius) or 0.34
    local curRatio = math.abs(getRatio(d.gear))
    if curRatio < 0.01 then curRatio = 1 end
    local finalDrive = math.abs(c[111] or 3.9)
    local roadSyncRPM = (roadMS / math.max(0.20, wRad)) * finalDrive * curRatio * 9.54929676

    local wheelSpinMS = 0
    if car.wheels then
        for wi = 0, 3 do
            local W = car.wheels[wi]
            if W then
                local ws = math.abs((W.w or 0) * (W.radius or wRad))
                if ws > wheelSpinMS then wheelSpinMS = ws end
            end
        end
    end
    local isWheelSpinning = (wheelSpinMS - roadMS) > 4.0

    local stopSpd = (car.stoppedPhaseBrakeSpeed or 5) + 3
    if d.gear > 2 and speedKmh <= stopSpd and (roadSyncRPM <= (redline * 0.40) or rpmNow <= (redline * 0.45)) then
        car.nextDownshiftTime = now + 0.15
        car.lastShiftDir      = "down"
        car.lastShiftTime     = now
        return 2
    end
    if d.state ~= 3 then return nil end

    -- Upshift guard: anti-loop (next gear must land well above downshift threshold), wheelspin & min speed guards
    if d.gear < topGear and rpmPct >= upPct and thr > 0.05 and not isWheelSpinning then
        if car.lastShiftDir ~= "down" or sinceLastShift >= antiHuntCooldown then
            local nextRatio = math.abs(getRatio(d.gear + 1))
            if nextRatio < 0.01 then nextRatio = curRatio end
            local nextProjectedRPM = math.max(rpmNow, roadSyncRPM) * (nextRatio / math.max(0.05, curRatio))
            local safeToUpshift = (nextProjectedRPM > downRPM * 1.18) and (thr <= 0.25 or nextProjectedRPM > sportDownRPM * 0.95)
            local mustUpshift   = (rpmNow >= redline * 0.97)
            local gearMaxKmh = ((redline / 9.54929676) / math.max(0.1, curRatio * finalDrive)) * wRad * 3.6
            local spdEntry = (car.fwdGearSpeeds and car.fwdGearSpeeds[d.gear - 1]) or (gearSpeeds and gearSpeeds[d.gear + 1])
            local minUpshiftKmh = spdEntry and (spdEntry * 0.65) or (gearMaxKmh * 0.50)
            if (safeToUpshift or mustUpshift) and speedKmh >= minUpshiftKmh then
                car.lastShiftDir      = "up"
                car.lastShiftTime     = now
                car.nextDownshiftTime = now + antiHuntCooldown
                return d.gear + 1
            end
        end
    end

    -- Downshift guard: strictly respect DownshiftCooldown & anti-hunt direction reversal cooldown + over-rev protection
    local effRPM = (not isWheelSpinning) and math.min(rpmNow, math.max(rpmNow * 0.65, roadSyncRPM * 1.15 + 400)) or rpmNow
    local effPct = (effRPM / redline) * 100
    if d.gear > 2 and (rpmPct <= downPct or effPct <= downPct) and now >= (car.nextDownshiftTime or 0) then
        if car.lastShiftDir ~= "up" or sinceLastShift >= antiHuntCooldown then
            local prevRatio = math.abs(getRatio(d.gear - 1))
            if prevRatio < 0.01 then prevRatio = curRatio end
            local projectedRPM = math.max(rpmNow, roadSyncRPM) * (prevRatio / math.max(0.05, curRatio))
            if projectedRPM < redline * 0.92 then
                car.nextDownshiftTime = now + antiHuntCooldown
                car.lastShiftDir      = "down"
                car.lastShiftTime     = now
                return d.gear - 1
            end
        end
    end

    return nil
end
27824
-- name Simcade Suspension Module (Cars & Heavy Trucks)
-- shared

SimcadeSuspension = SimcadeSuspension or {}

local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b) return v < a and a or (v > b and b or v) end

-- Resolve per-axle suspension config with Truck/Heavy-Vehicle mass scaling
-- Uses VirtualMassKG for virtual spring/damper feel while scaling applied GMod impulse by PhysMass/VirtualMassKG.
function SimcadeSuspension.getAxleCfg(suspCfg, isFront)
    local sub = suspCfg[isFront and "Front" or "Rear"]
    if sub then return sub end
    return suspCfg
end

function SimcadeSuspension.mountHeight(w, cfg)
    if cfg.solidAxle and cfg.axleCenterZ ~= nil then
        return cfg.axleCenterZ + math.max(8, cfg.rideHeight or 24) * (1 - (cfg.restCompression or 0.45))
    end
    return w.pos.z
end

local stations = {0, 1, -1}
local lanes = {0, 0.5, -0.5}

function SimcadeSuspension.eachTreadPoint(mount, up, axle, radius, width, visit)
    local forward = axle:cross(up):getNormalized()
    local radialUp = forward:cross(axle):getNormalized()
    for _, station in ipairs(stations) do
        for _, y in ipairs(lanes) do
            local x = station * (y == 0 and 0.45 or 0.8)
            local arc = (forward * x - radialUp * math.sqrt(1 - x*x)) * radius
            local tread = arc + axle * (y * width)
            local height = tread:dot(up)
            visit(mount + tread - up * height, radius + height, x == 0 and y == 0)
        end
    end
end

if CLIENT then return end

local function supportTrace(startPos, endPos, filter, minMass, hullSize)
    for attempt = 1, 4 do
        local tr = hullSize and trace.hull(startPos, endPos, -hullSize, hullSize, filter, MASK.SOLID)
            or trace.line(startPos, endPos, filter, MASK.SOLID)
        local ent = tr.Entity
        if not tr.Hit or tr.HitWorld or not isValid(ent) then return tr end
        if not (ent.isPlayer and ent:isPlayer()) then
            local phys = ent.getPhysicsObject and ent:getPhysicsObject()
            if not isValid(phys) or (phys.isMoveable and not phys:isMoveable()) or phys:getMass() >= minMass then return tr end
        end
        if attempt == 4 then return nil end
        filter[#filter + 1] = ent
    end
end

function SimcadeSuspension.traceTread(w, mount, up, axle, reach, filter, minMass, cfg, recordRays)
    local filterSize = #filter
    local rays = recordRays and {} or nil
    local best, bestDist, selected
    local function sample(startPos, correction, central, hullSize)
        local length = reach - correction
        if length <= 0 then
            if rays then rays[#rays + 1] = { skipped = true } end
            return
        end
        local endPos = startPos - up * length
        local tr = supportTrace(startPos, endPos, filter, minMass, hullSize)
        if rays then
            rays[#rays + 1] = { depth = tr and tr.Hit and (startPos - tr.HitPos):dot(up) or length, hit = tr and tr.Hit }
        end
        if not tr or not tr.Hit then return end
        if tr.StartSolid then
            -- Only the central probe can recover a buried wheel. A shoulder inside a wall cannot support it.
            if central then best, bestDist = tr, w.radius * 0.6 end
        elseif tr.HitNormal:dot(up) > 0.55 then
            local dist = (startPos - tr.HitPos):dot(up) + correction
            if not bestDist or dist < bestDist - 0.001 then best, bestDist = tr, dist end
        end
        if rays and best == tr then selected = #rays end
    end

    if cfg.useHullTrace or cfg.useTreadTrace == false then
        local hw = math.max(2, w.width * 0.25)
        sample(mount, 0, true, cfg.useHullTrace and Vector(hw, hw, 1))
    else
        SimcadeSuspension.eachTreadPoint(mount, up, axle, w.radius, w.width, sample)
    end
    for i = #filter, filterSize + 1, -1 do filter[i] = nil end
    if selected then rays[selected].selected = true end
    return best, bestDist, rays
end

-- Piecewise-linear camber curve, measured from the nominal ride position.
function SimcadeSuspension.travelCamber(cfg, compression, height, droop)
    if cfg.solidAxle then return 0 end
    local rest = height * clamp(cfg.restCompression or 0.45, 0.15, 0.85)
    local travel = compression - rest
    if travel < 0 then
        return (cfg.droopCamberDeg or 0) * clamp(-travel / (rest + droop), 0, 1)
    end
    local bump = math.min(cfg.bumpLimit or height, height - 2)
    if bump <= rest then return 0 end
    return (cfg.bumpCamberDeg or 0) * clamp(travel / (bump - rest), 0, 1)
end

-- The contact rays give the two hub heights along chassis up. Both spindles
-- follow the same beam, including when one end reaches full droop.
function SimcadeSuspension.solveBeam(left, right, cfg)
    if not left or not right then return 0 end
    local track = left.pos.y - right.pos.y
    if track <= 0 then return 0 end
    local near = (1 + clamp(cfg.springSpanRatio or 0.75, 0, 1)) * 0.5
    local limit = track * math.sin(math.rad(clamp(cfg.maxAxleRollDeg or 15, 0, 75)))
    local function hub(w)
        local zero = w.mountZ - w.rideHeight
        return zero - w.airTravel, w.isGrounded and zero + math.min(w.compIn, w.rideHeight - 2) or -math.huge
    end
    local ld, lh = hub(left)
    local rd, rh = hub(right)
    -- Full extension is limited at the inboard spring seats. A tire can
    -- reach below its level-beam droop position while the opposite end rises.
    local lz = math.max(lh, ld)
    local rz = math.max(rh, rd)
    if lz - ld >= rz - rd then
        rz = math.max(rh, rd - (lz - ld) * (1 - near) / near, lz - limit)
        lz = math.max(lz, rz - limit)
    else
        lz = math.max(lh, ld - (rz - rd) * (1 - near) / near, rz - limit)
        rz = math.max(rz, lz - limit)
    end
    local function place(w, other, z, hit)
        if z > hit + 0.01 then w.isGrounded = false end
        w.compIn = z - w.mountZ + w.rideHeight + (w.isGrounded and math.max(0, w.compIn - (w.rideHeight - 2)) or 0)
        w.beamMate, w.seatNear = other, near
    end
    place(left, right, lz, lh)
    place(right, left, rz, rh)
    local freeRate = math.abs(lz - rz) >= limit - 0.001 and 1 or -(1 - near) / near
    left.freeRate, right.freeRate = freeRate, freeRate
    local sinRoll = clamp((lz - rz) / track, -1, 1)
    local inset = 0.5 * track * (1 - math.sqrt(1 - sinRoll * sinRoll))
    left.beamInset, right.beamInset = inset, -inset
    return math.deg(math.asin(sinRoll))
end

-- Tangential contact impulses in Source units. Store anchors on the support so a
-- stationary tire can ride a moving prop. Forces at the patches also resist yaw.
function SimcadeSuspension.applyStaticGrip(phys, w, tire, canPark, brake, forward, left, com, vel, omega, mass, brakeMass, scale, gravity, dt, base)
    if not w.isGrounded or w.normalLoad <= 0 or not tire or (tire.load or 0) <= 0 then
        w.restAnchor = nil
        return
    end
    local n, patch, support = w.hitNormal, w.contactPos, w.support
    local supportValid = support and support:isValid()
    if support ~= w.restSupport then w.restAnchor = nil end
    local anchored = w.restAnchor ~= nil
    -- Track the chassis point captured on engagement. The winning tread ray
    -- can move across a stationary wheel as the suspension settles on a curb.
    local tracked = anchored and base:localToWorld(w.restChassisPoint) or patch
    local anchor = anchored and (supportValid and support:localToWorld(w.restAnchor) or w.restAnchor) or patch
    local supportVel = supportValid and support:getVelocity() or Vector()
    if anchored and supportValid then supportVel = (anchor - w.lastRestWorld) / dt end
    local velocity = vel + omega:cross(patch - com) - supportVel
    velocity = velocity - n * velocity:dot(n)
    local heading = forward * (tire.axleY or 1) - left * (tire.axleX or 0)
    local lateral = n:cross(heading):getNormalized()
    local rolling = lateral:cross(n)
    local sideSpeed = velocity:dot(lateral)
    -- A patch can catch a small slide if its remaining friction impulse can
    -- arrest the slip over roughly one tire contact length.
    local knImpulse = 1000 * scale * dt / 0.0254
    local fx = tire.Fx or 0
    local fy = tire.Fy or 0
    local peakX, peakY = tire.peakLongKN or 0, tire.peakLatKN or 0
    if peakX <= 0 or peakY <= 0 then
        w.restAnchor = nil
        return
    end
    local contactLength = math.max(1, (w.radius or 15) * 0.35)
    local rollSpeed = velocity:dot(rolling)
    local brakeAccel = peakX * brake * knImpulse / (dt * math.max(0.001, brakeMass))
    local parked = canPark and brake > 0 and rollSpeed^2 <= 2 * brakeAccel * contactLength
    -- A locked, moving tread already gets kinetic friction from the tire solver.
    -- Capture parking only when BOTH components can settle within the patch.
    if tire.locked == 1 then
        local lateralAccel = peakY * knImpulse / (mass * dt)
        if not parked or rollSpeed^2 / math.max(0.001, brakeAccel)
                + sideSpeed^2 / math.max(0.001, lateralAccel) > 2 * contactLength then
            w.restAnchor = nil
            return
        end
    end
    -- A stationary free wheel can resist lateral displacement. Advance its
    -- anchor along the rolling direction so releasing the brake frees that axis.
    local lateralHold = canPark and math.abs(rollSpeed) * dt < contactLength and math.abs(rollSpeed) < 8
    -- At road speed the dynamic solver already supplies the entire tire force.
    -- Subtracting its cornering force here also adds an unintended roll moment.
    if not parked and not lateralHold then
        w.restAnchor = nil
        return
    end
    if lateralHold and not parked then
        anchor = tracked + lateral * (anchor - tracked):dot(lateral)
    end
    local err = anchor - tracked
    local acceleration = err * (48 * dt) - velocity * (1 - math.exp(-18 * dt)) + Vector(0, 0, gravity * dt)
    local demand = acceleration * mass - w.suspImpulseVec * scale
    local targetX = fx
    local requestedX = fx
    if parked then
        requestedX = demand:dot(rolling) * brakeMass / (mass * knImpulse)
        targetX = clamp(requestedX, -peakX * brake, peakX * brake)
    end
    local sideLimit = peakY * math.sqrt(math.max(0, 1 - (targetX / peakX)^2))
    local bank = (Vector(0, 0, gravity * mass * dt) - w.suspImpulseVec * scale):dot(lateral)
    local spareAccel = math.max(0, sideLimit * knImpulse - math.abs(bank)) / (mass * dt)
    local captureSpeedSq = 2 * spareAccel * contactLength
    local adhesion = clamp(1 - sideSpeed^2 / math.max(0.001, captureSpeedSq), 0, 1)
    local requestedY = demand:dot(lateral) / knImpulse
    local targetY = clamp(requestedY, -sideLimit, sideLimit)
    local correction = rolling * (targetX - fx) + lateral * (targetY - fy)
    phys:applyForceOffset(correction * (adhesion * knImpulse), patch)

    if adhesion > 0 and math.abs(requestedY) <= sideLimit
        and (not parked or math.abs(requestedX) <= peakX * brake) then
        w.restAnchor = supportValid and support:worldToLocal(anchor) or anchor
        if not anchored then w.restChassisPoint = base:worldToLocal(patch) end
        w.lastRestWorld, w.restSupport = anchor, support
    else
        w.restAnchor = nil
    end
end

-- Sweep the leading tread and sidewall independently. A glancing scrape can
-- have substantial forward speed with only a small velocity into the curb.
function SimcadeSuspension.applyCurbBlock(phys, w, up, axle, dt, mass, wheels, gravity, cfg, filter)
    if cfg.curbTripEnabled == false then return end
    local radius, halfWidth = w.radius, w.width * 0.5
    local forward = axle:cross(up):getNormalized()
    local side = up:cross(forward)
    local hub = w.mountPos + up * math.min(w.compIn - w.rideHeight, -2)
    local velocity = phys:getVelocityAtPoint(hub)
    if w.support then velocity = velocity - w.support:getVelocity() end
    local reachScale = math.max(0, cfg.curbProbeDistMult or 1)
    if reachScale == 0 then return end
    -- Include the lower end of this tick's travel when the tread has just
    -- climbed onto a lip. The new contact patch may already be on top of it.
    local rise = w.wasGrounded and math.max(0, w.compIn - (w.prevCompIn or w.compIn)) or 0
    local bottom = hub - up * (radius + math.min(radius, rise))
    local probeHeight = clamp(radius * (cfg.curbProbeHeightRatio or 0.14), 0.1,
        math.max(0.1, cfg.curbProbeMaxHeight or 4.5))
    local wallHeight = radius * clamp(cfg.curbWallHeightRatio or 0.82, 0.1, 1.5)
    local best, bestClosing = nil, -1
    local filterSize = #filter
    local minMass = math.max(35, mass * 0.15)

    local function probe(axis, extent, rollingProbe)
        local speed = velocity:dot(axis)
        if math.abs(speed) < 0.1 then
            if not w.curbNormal or math.abs(w.curbNormal:dot(axis)) < 0.25 then return end
            speed = -w.curbNormal:dot(axis) * 0.1
        end
        local dir = speed > 0 and axis or -axis
        local sweep = math.abs(speed) * dt
        -- Once the wheel travels more than its radius in a tick a narrow lip
        -- can pass between tread samples. Sweep close to the road in that case.
        local rayHeight = rollingProbe and sweep > radius and math.min(probeHeight, 0.25) or probeHeight
        local start = bottom + up * rayHeight
        local tr = supportTrace(start, start + dir * (extent * reachScale + sweep), filter, minMass)
        if not tr or not tr.Hit or tr.StartSolid or math.abs(tr.HitNormal:dot(up)) >= 0.55 then return end
        local normal = (tr.HitNormal - up * tr.HitNormal:dot(up)):getNormalized()
        local relative = phys:getVelocityAtPoint(tr.HitPos)
        if not tr.HitWorld and isValid(tr.Entity) then
            local body = tr.Entity:getPhysicsObject()
            relative = relative - (isValid(body) and body:getVelocityAtPoint(tr.HitPos) or tr.Entity:getVelocity())
        end
        local closing = -relative:dot(normal)
        if closing < 0 or closing <= bestClosing then return end
        -- Measure the top just inside the struck face. StartSolid here means
        -- the face continues above the wheel's wall-height threshold.
        local inset = math.min(0.5, radius * 0.03)
        local inside = tr.HitPos - normal * inset
        local top = supportTrace(inside + up * (wallHeight + 0.1 - rayHeight), inside, filter, minMass)
        if not top or not top.Hit then return end
        local height = wallHeight
        if not top.StartSolid then
            if top.HitNormal:dot(up) <= 0.55 then return end
            -- Measure the rise from the road immediately outside the face.
            -- The hub-derived bottom can lie below both sides of a flat seam.
            local outside = tr.HitPos + normal * inset
            local road = supportTrace(outside + up * (wallHeight + 0.1 - rayHeight),
                outside - up * (radius + rayHeight), filter, minMass)
            if not road or not road.Hit or road.StartSolid then return end
            local roadAlign = road.HitNormal:dot(up)
            if roadAlign <= 0.55 then return end
            height = math.min(wallHeight, (top.HitPos - road.HitPos):dot(road.HitNormal) / roadAlign)
            if height <= 0.25 then return end
        end
        local wall = height >= wallHeight
        local chord = math.sqrt(math.max(0, height * (2 * radius - height)))
        local sideDot = math.abs(normal:dot(side))
        local footprint = halfWidth * sideDot + (wall and radius or chord) * math.abs(normal:dot(forward))
        local gap = (start - tr.HitPos):dot(normal) - footprint
        if gap > closing * dt then return end
        bestClosing = closing
        best = { normal = normal, pos = tr.HitPos, height = height, wall = wall,
            sideDot = sideDot, relative = relative, gap = gap, entity = tr.Entity }
    end

    probe(forward, radius, true)
    probe(side, halfWidth)
    -- The tread can step onto the top between updates, or cross a chamfer
    -- without ever seeing a vertical face. Compare actual support points
    -- against the preceding road plane to exclude travel along a uniform grade.
    local previous = w.curbRoadPoint
    local previousNormal = w.curbRoadNormal
    if previous and w.support and w.curbRoadSupport == w.support then
        previous = w.support:localToWorld(previous)
        previousNormal = w.support:localToWorldVector(previousNormal)
    elseif w.curbRoadSupport then
        previous = nil
    end
    w.curbRoadSupport = w.support
    w.curbRoadPoint = w.support and w.support:worldToLocal(w.contactPos) or w.contactPos
    w.curbRoadNormal = w.support and w.support:worldToLocalVector(w.hitNormal) or w.hitNormal
    local rolling = velocity:dot(forward)
    if not best and cfg.curbFwdEnabled ~= false and previous and w.contactPos
        and math.abs(rolling) >= (cfg.curbFwdMinSpeed or 80)
        and math.abs(rolling) > math.abs(velocity:dot(side)) then
        local travel = w.contactPos - previous
        local height = travel:dot(previousNormal) / math.max(0.55, previousNormal:dot(up))
        if height > 0.25 and height < wallHeight and travel:dot(forward) * rolling > 0 then
            -- Check for a riser between the samples. A change of road normal
            -- alone can produce an apparent rise over a long physics tick.
            local edge = supportTrace(previous + up * (height * 0.5),
                w.contactPos - up * (height * 0.5), filter, minMass)
            if edge and edge.Hit and not edge.StartSolid and edge.HitNormal:dot(up) < 0.85
                and edge.HitNormal:dot(forward) * rolling < 0 then
                bestClosing = math.abs(rolling)
                best = { normal = rolling > 0 and -forward or forward, pos = w.contactPos,
                    height = height, sideDot = 0, relative = velocity, gap = 0, treadRise = true }
            end
        end
    end
    for i = #filter, filterSize + 1, -1 do filter[i] = nil end
    if not best then w.curbNormal, w.curbJolted = nil, nil return end

    local sideBlend = clamp((best.sideDot - (cfg.curbSideAlignMin or 0.45)) /
        math.max(0.01, 1 - (cfg.curbSideAlignMin or 0.45)), 0, 1)
    -- An angled face can be crossed by pure rolling. Count only the portion
    -- of inward motion actually supplied by sideways travel as a sidewall hit.
    local sideIn = math.max(0, -best.relative:dot(side) * best.normal:dot(side))
    local rollIn = math.max(0, -best.relative:dot(forward) * best.normal:dot(forward))
    if sideIn + rollIn > 0.1 then
        sideBlend = sideBlend * sideIn / (sideIn + rollIn)
    elseif w.curbNormal and w.curbNormal:dot(best.normal) > 0.8 then
        sideBlend = w.curbSideBlend or sideBlend
    end
    local forwardBlend = cfg.curbFwdEnabled == false and 0 or 1 - sideBlend
    local resistance = best.wall and (cfg.curbWallImpulseMult or 0.22) or
        sideBlend * (cfg.curbSideImpulseMult or 0.30) + forwardBlend * (cfg.curbFwdImpulseMult or 0.04)
    local severity = best.wall and 1 or clamp(best.height / (radius * math.max(0.05, cfg.curbClimbRatio or 0.42)), 0, 1)
    local response = 1 - math.exp(-60 * math.max(0, resistance) * dt * severity)
    local now = timer.curtime()
    local newFace = not w.curbNormal or w.curbNormal:dot(best.normal) < 0.8 or w.curbEntity ~= best.entity
    if newFace then w.curbJolted = nil end
    local sideHop, forwardHop = 0, 0
    if not w.curbJolted and not best.wall and now >= (w.nextCurbJolt or 0) then
        if sideIn >= (cfg.curbMinSpeed or 90) then sideHop = math.max(0, cfg.curbVerticalHopMult or 0.12) end
        if bestClosing >= (cfg.curbFwdMinSpeed or 80) then forwardHop = math.max(0, cfg.curbFwdHopMult or 0.14) end
    end
    w.curbNormal, w.curbEntity, w.curbSideBlend = best.normal, best.entity, sideBlend
    local upwardSpeed = math.max(0, best.relative:dot(up))
    local sideLimit = math.max(0, math.sqrt(2 * gravity * best.height) - upwardSpeed)
    local lift = sideBlend * math.min(sideIn * sideHop, sideLimit)
    if forwardHop > 0 and forwardBlend > 0 then
        -- Above unit gain, scale the reference hop height as well as demand.
        -- Velocity grows with the square root of height. The speed limit still
        -- scales with the obstacle so a seam cannot get a full curb's kick.
        local heightFraction = clamp(best.height / wallHeight, 0, 1)
        local peak = math.min(math.sqrt(2 * gravity * math.max(wallHeight, w.rideHeight))
            * math.sqrt(math.max(1, forwardHop)), bestClosing) * heightFraction
        local requested = bestClosing * forwardHop * math.sqrt(heightFraction)
        if peak > 0 and requested > 0 then
            local target = peak / (1 + peak / requested)
            lift = lift + forwardBlend * math.max(0, target - upwardSpeed)
        end
    end
    if lift > 0 then
        w.curbJolted = true
        w.nextCurbJolt = now + (cfg.curbCooldown or 0.16)
    end
    local into = math.max(0, bestClosing - math.max(0, best.gap) / dt)
    local separation = math.min(8, math.max(0, -best.gap - 0.1) * 8) * response
    local push = best.treadRise and 0 or math.min(into, response * bestClosing) + separation
    if push + lift > 0 then
        phys:applyForceOffset((best.normal * push + up * lift) * (mass / wheels), best.pos)
    end
end

-- Compute vertical suspension + anti-roll + bumpstop + pitch/roll impulses
-- Scales virtual suspension force by (physMass / virtualMass) so heavy trucks (e.g. 6000 kg)
-- and light karts (e.g. 600 kg) both ride stably on any GMod prop mass.
function SimcadeSuspension.computeWheelForce(params)
    local compIn       = params.compIn or 0
    local prevCompIn   = params.prevCompIn or compIn
    local rideHeight   = math.max(8, params.rideHeight or 24)
    local virtualMass  = math.max(50, params.virtualMassKG or 1450)
    local physMass     = math.max(15, params.physMassKG or virtualMass)
    local numWheels    = math.max(1, params.numWheels or 4)
    local dt           = math.max(0.001, params.dt or 0.015)
    local axleSusp     = params.axleSusp or {}
    local isTruck      = params.isTruck or (virtualMass >= 2600)

    local cornerMassV  = virtualMass / numWheels
    local gravity      = 600 -- Source Engine gravity (in/s^2)
    local restComp     = math.clamp(axleSusp.restCompression or 0.45, 0.15, 0.85)
    local targetDefl   = rideHeight * restComp

    -- Truck/Heavy vehicle auto-stabilization: higher bumpstop headroom & asymmetric rebound damping
    local stiffnessMult = axleSusp.springStiffnessMult or axleSusp.springStiffness or 1.0
    local bumpDampMult  = axleSusp.bumpDamping or (isTruck and 0.85 or 1.0)
    local rebDampMult   = axleSusp.reboundDamping or (isTruck and 1.35 or 1.25)
    local bumpRatio     = axleSusp.bumpDampingRatio or axleSusp.dampingRatio or 0.72
    local rebRatio      = axleSusp.reboundDampingRatio or (bumpRatio * 1.25)

    local kSpring = (cornerMassV * gravity / math.max(1, targetDefl)) * stiffnessMult
    local cCrit   = 2.0 * math.sqrt(math.max(1, kSpring * cornerMassV))
    local velComp
    if params.compressionRate ~= nil then
        velComp = clamp(params.compressionRate, -450, 450)
    elseif params.wasGrounded == false then
        -- First frame of landing from air: use actual downward corner velocity instead of (compIn - (-airTravel))/dt spike
        velComp = clamp(-(params.chassisVelZ or 0), -450, 450)
    else
        velComp = clamp((compIn - prevCompIn) / dt, -450, 450)
    end
    local dampK   = cCrit * ((velComp >= 0) and (bumpRatio * bumpDampMult) or (rebRatio * rebDampMult))

    local springComp = math.min(compIn, axleSusp.bumpLimit or rideHeight)
    -- Digressive compression damping above 60 in/s so sharp curb/step spikes don't violently kick the chassis
    local effVelComp = velComp
    if velComp > 60 then
        effVelComp = math.min(140, 60 + (velComp - 60) * 0.20)
    end
    local springForceV = springComp * kSpring + effVelComp * dampK

    -- Progressive rubber bumpstop in top 15% of travel (no catapult spring)
    local bumpLimit = math.max(4, axleSusp.bumpLimit or rideHeight)
    local bumpStart = bumpLimit * 0.85
    if compIn > bumpStart and velComp >= 0 then
        local over = clamp((compIn - bumpStart) / math.max(1, bumpLimit - bumpStart), 0, 1.5)
        springForceV = springForceV + (cornerMassV * gravity * 0.65) * (over * over)
    end

    local maxForceMult = (velComp <= 0) and 2.5 or 4.2
    springForceV = clamp(springForceV, 0, cornerMassV * gravity * maxForceMult)

    -- Inelastic bottom-out arrest in top 15% of travel: absorbs downward slam without rebounding upward
    local cornerVelZ = params.chassisVelZ or 0
    if compIn > bumpStart and cornerVelZ < -5 then
        local arrestDt = math.max(0.001, params.arrestDt or dt)
        local arrestFrac = clamp((compIn - bumpStart) / math.max(1, rideHeight - bumpStart), 0.35, 1.0)
        local arrestForce = clamp((-cornerVelZ) * cornerMassV / arrestDt, 0, cornerMassV * gravity * 10.0) * arrestFrac
        springForceV = springForceV + arrestForce
    end

    -- Scale virtual force -> physical GMod prop force
    local physScale = physMass / virtualMass
    return springForceV * physScale, springForceV, kSpring
end

-- Evaluate travel and velocity at each inboard spring seat. With a single
-- supporting tire, that patch carries both springs, including a roll-stop load.
function SimcadeSuspension.beamSeatForce(w, params, rate, dvz, dwr, dwp)
    local mate, near = w.beamMate, w.seatNear
    local far = 1 - near
    local mateComp, mateRate
    if mate.isGrounded then
        mateComp = mate._subComp
        mateRate = mate._rate0 - dvz - dwr * mate._ly - dwp * mate._lx
    else
        mateComp = mate.compIn + (w._subComp - w.compIn) * w.freeRate
        mateRate = rate * w.freeRate
    end
    local function seat(a, b)
        params.compIn = a * w._subComp + b * mateComp
        params.compressionRate = a * rate + b * mateRate
        local _, force, stiffness = SimcadeSuspension.computeWheelForce(params)
        return force, stiffness
    end
    local force, stiffness = seat(near, far)
    if not mate.isGrounded then force = force + seat(far, near) end
    return force, stiffness
end

-- In each substep the bar transfers support from the extending to the
-- compressed wheel. It cannot pull either contact patch into the road.
function SimcadeSuspension.applyAntiRoll(left, right)
    if not left or not right then return end
    local stiffness = clamp(left._axleSusp.antiRollStiffness or 0, 0, 1.2)
    local springRate = math.min(left._springRate, right._springRate)
    local barRate = 0.70 * stiffness * springRate
    local travel = left._subComp - right._subComp
    local transfer = travel * barRate

    if left.beamMate == right then
        -- Resolve the two seat forces back to reactions at the tire patches.
        transfer = transfer - (left._springForce - right._springForce) * (1 - left.seatNear)
    end

    transfer = clamp(transfer, -left._springForce, right._springForce)
    left._springForce = left._springForce + transfer
    right._springForce = right._springForce - transfer
end

-- The extra recovery response belongs to the cornering load. The passive
-- suspension alone sets the settled pose on a bank or an uneven surface.
function SimcadeSuspension.filterRollLoad(previous, demand, blend, recovery)
    if blend <= 0 then return previous end
    if blend >= 1 then return demand end
    local releasing = previous * demand < 0 or math.abs(demand) < math.abs(previous)
    if recovery == 1 or not releasing then return previous + (demand - previous) * blend end

    local retained = (1 - blend) ^ math.max(0.1, recovery)
    if previous * demand < 0 then
        -- Shed the old turn and build the opposite turn at its usual rate.
        return previous * retained + demand * blend
    end
    return demand + (previous - demand) * retained
end
3769
-- name Simcade Controls & Input Module
-- shared

SimcadeControls = SimcadeControls or {}

local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b) return v < a and a or (v > b and b or v) end

-- Poll driver/wire controls: W = half throttle (0.5), W+Shift = full throttle (1.0)
function SimcadeControls.pollInputs(Driver, hasDriver, wireInputs, physicsCar, prevState, controlsCfg)
    controlsCfg = controlsCfg or {}
    local kFullThr  = controlsCfg.FullThrottle or IN_KEY.SPEED
    local kHbk      = controlsCfg.Handbrake    or IN_KEY.JUMP
    local kClutch   = controlsCfg.Clutch       or IN_KEY.WALK
    local kGearUp   = controlsCfg.GearUp       or IN_KEY.ATTACK
    local kGearDown = controlsCfg.GearDown     or IN_KEY.ATTACK2

    local wKey, sKey, aKey, dKey, spaceKey, shiftKey, altKey, rKey, fKey = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
    local m1Key, m2Key = 0, 0

    if hasDriver and isValid(Driver) then
        wKey     = Driver:keyDown(IN_KEY.FORWARD)   and 1 or 0
        sKey     = Driver:keyDown(IN_KEY.BACK)      and 1 or 0
        aKey     = Driver:keyDown(IN_KEY.MOVELEFT)  and 1 or 0
        dKey     = Driver:keyDown(IN_KEY.MOVERIGHT) and 1 or 0
        spaceKey = Driver:keyDown(kHbk)             and 1 or 0
        shiftKey = Driver:keyDown(kFullThr)         and 1 or 0
        altKey   = Driver:keyDown(kClutch)          and 1 or 0
        rKey     = Driver:keyDown(IN_KEY.RELOAD)    and 1 or 0
        fKey     = (IN_KEY and IN_KEY.FLASHLIGHT and Driver:keyDown(IN_KEY.FLASHLIGHT)) and 1 or 0
        m1Key    = Driver:keyDown(kGearUp)          and 1 or 0
        m2Key    = Driver:keyDown(kGearDown)        and 1 or 0
    end

    local isActive = (wireInputs and wireInputs.Active and wireInputs.Active > 0) or hasDriver
    local driverThrottle = (wKey == 1) and ((shiftKey == 1) and 1.0 or 0.5) or 0
    local driverReverseThrottle = (sKey == 1) and ((shiftKey == 1) and 1.0 or 0.5) or 0
    local rawThrottle = (wireInputs and wireInputs.EngineThrottle and wireInputs.EngineThrottle > 0) and wireInputs.EngineThrottle or driverThrottle
    local rawReverseThrottle = (wireInputs and wireInputs.EngineBrake and wireInputs.EngineBrake > 0) and wireInputs.EngineBrake or driverReverseThrottle
    local halfThrottleActive = (wKey == 1 and shiftKey ~= 1)
    local reverseHalfThrottleActive = (sKey == 1 and shiftKey ~= 1)

    local rawBrake     = (wireInputs and wireInputs.EngineBrake and wireInputs.EngineBrake > 0) and wireInputs.EngineBrake or sKey
    local rawHandbrake = (wireInputs and wireInputs.Handbrake and wireInputs.Handbrake > 0) and wireInputs.Handbrake or spaceKey
    local clutchDown   = ((wireInputs and wireInputs.Clutch and wireInputs.Clutch > 0.5) or (altKey == 1))
    local gearUpIn     = ((wireInputs and wireInputs.GearUp and wireInputs.GearUp > 0) or (m1Key == 1))
    local gearDownIn   = ((wireInputs and wireInputs.GearDown and wireInputs.GearDown > 0) or (m2Key == 1))

    if physicsCar then
        if gearUpIn and not prevState.gearUp then physicsCar:shiftKey(true) end
        if gearDownIn and not prevState.gearDown then physicsCar:shiftKey(false) end
    end
    prevState.gearUp   = gearUpIn
    prevState.gearDown = gearDownIn

    return {
        wKey = wKey, sKey = sKey, aKey = aKey, dKey = dKey,
        spaceKey = spaceKey, shiftKey = shiftKey, altKey = altKey, rKey = rKey, fKey = fKey,
        isActive = isActive,
        rawThrottle = clamp(rawThrottle, 0, 1),
        rawReverseThrottle = clamp(rawReverseThrottle, 0, 1),
        halfThrottleActive = halfThrottleActive,
        reverseHalfThrottleActive = reverseHalfThrottleActive,
        rawBrake = clamp(rawBrake, 0, 1),
        rawHandbrake = clamp(rawHandbrake, 0, 1),
        clutchDown = clutchDown,
        steerTarget = aKey - dKey,
    }
end

15610
-- name Simcade Camera Module (Chase & FPV, ported from Advanced Car Camera V1.8)
-- shared

SimcadeCamera = SimcadeCamera or {}

local function modeEnabled(cam, mode)
    if mode == 0 then return true end
    if cam.enabled == false then return false end
    return (mode == 1 and cam.chaseEnabled ~= false) or (mode == 2 and cam.fpvEnabled ~= false)
end

local function resolveMode(cam, mode)
    if modeEnabled(cam, mode) then return mode end
    if modeEnabled(cam, 1) then return 1 end
    if modeEnabled(cam, 2) then return 2 end
    return 0
end

if SERVER then
    local _srvState = { camMode = 1, trailerMode = false, cameraConfig = {} }
    function SimcadeCamera.initServer(state, cfg)
        state = state or _srvState
        local cam = cfg and cfg.CAMERA_CFG or {}
        state.cameraConfig = cam
        state.camMode = resolveMode(cam, cam.defaultMode or 1)
        state.trailerMode = false
        return state
    end

    function SimcadeCamera.toggleMode(state, mode)
        state = state or _srvState
        local cam = state.cameraConfig or {}
        if mode == nil then
            mode = state.camMode or 0
            repeat
                mode = (mode + 1) % 3
            until modeEnabled(cam, mode)
        end
        state.camMode = resolveMode(cam, mode)
        return state.camMode
    end
    return
end

local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b) return v < a and a or (v > b and b or v) end

local camState = {
    camMode       = 1,
    trailerActive = false,
    currentDist   = 185,
    targetDist    = 185,
    distVel       = 0,
    currentAng    = nil,
    targetAng     = nil,
    lastMouseTime = 0,
    lastSpeed     = 0,
    currentFOV    = 90,
    trackAng      = nil,
    smoothSeatAng = nil,
    initialized   = false,
}

function SimcadeCamera.initClient(cfg)
    local CAM = cfg.CAMERA_CFG or {}
    if not (modeEnabled(CAM, 1) or modeEnabled(CAM, 2)) then return end

    pcall(function() if enableHud then enableHud(player(), true) end end)

    camState.camMode     = resolveMode(CAM, CAM.defaultMode or 1)
    camState.targetDist  = CAM.maxDist or CAM.chaseDist or 190
    camState.currentDist = camState.targetDist

    local localFwd = (cfg.VEHICLE_CFG and cfg.VEHICLE_CFG.chassisForward or Vector(1, 0, 0)):getNormalized()
    local localRight = localFwd:cross(Vector(0, 0, 1))
    if localRight:getLengthSqr() < 0.01 then localRight = Vector(0, -1, 0) end
    localRight = localRight:getNormalized()
    local localUp = localRight:cross(localFwd):getNormalized()

    local function isInThisVehicle()
        local lp = player()
        if not (lp and lp:isValid() and lp:inVehicle()) then return false end
        local hudSt = SimcadeHUD and SimcadeHUD.getState and SimcadeHUD.getState() or nil
        local targetPod = hudSt and hudSt.pod or nil
        if not (targetPod and targetPod:isValid()) then return false end
        if lp.getVehicle then
            return lp:getVehicle() == targetPod
        end
        return false
    end

    net.receive("Simcade_CameraMode", function()
        local mode = net.readUInt(2)
        camState.camMode = resolveMode(CAM, mode)
        camState.initialized = false
        camState.currentAng = nil
        camState.targetAng = nil
    end)

    hook.add("mouseMoved", "SimcadeCam_MouseLook", function(dx, dy)
        if camState.camMode == 0 then return end
        if not isInThisVehicle() then return end
        local thresh = CAM.mouseThreshold or 2
        if math.abs(dx) < thresh and math.abs(dy) < thresh then return end
        local sens = CAM.mouseSens or 0.05
        local pSign = CAM.invertPitch and -1 or 1
        if camState.targetAng then
            camState.targetAng.y = camState.targetAng.y - dx * sens
            camState.targetAng.p = clamp(camState.targetAng.p + dy * sens * pSign, -65, 75)
        end
        camState.lastMouseTime = timer.curtime()
    end)

    hook.add("mouseWheeled", "SimcadeCam_Zoom", function(delta)
        if not isInThisVehicle() then return end
        if camState.camMode == 1 then
            local maxD = clamp((CAM.maxDist or CAM.chaseDist or 190) - delta * 14, 90, 450)
            CAM.maxDist = maxD
            CAM.minDist = math.max(70, maxD - 30)
            camState.targetDist = maxD
        end
    end)

    hook.add("CalcView", "SimcadeCam_CalcView", function(origin, angles, fov)
        if not isInThisVehicle() then
            camState.initialized = false
            camState.currentAng = nil
            camState.targetAng = nil
            return
        end
        if camState.camMode == 0 then return end

        local hudSt = SimcadeHUD and SimcadeHUD.getState and SimcadeHUD.getState() or nil
        local base = hudSt and hudSt.base or nil
        if not (base and base:isValid()) then return end

        local lp = player()
        local dt = clamp(timer.frametime() or 0.015, 0.001, 0.05)
        local vel = base:getVelocity()
        local fwd   = base:localToWorldVector(localFwd):getNormalized()
        local right = base:localToWorldVector(localRight):getNormalized()
        local up    = base:localToWorldVector(localUp):getNormalized()
        local vehAng = fwd:getAngle()

        if camState.camMode == 2 then
            -- First-Person View (Advanced Car Camera V1.8) - anchored to seat when available
            local curTime = timer.curtime()
            local fpv = CAM.fpv or {}
            local fpvOff = fpv.offset or CAM.fpvOffset or Vector(2, 0, 28)
            local rawSeatAng = base.localToWorldAngles and base:localToWorldAngles(localFwd:getAngle()) or vehAng
