            local seatBump = fpv.seatBumpSmoothing or 5
            camState.smoothSeatAng = camState.smoothSeatAng and math.lerpAngle(clamp(seatBump * dt, 0, 1), camState.smoothSeatAng, rawSeatAng) or rawSeatAng
            local sAng = camState.smoothSeatAng
            camState.trackAng = camState.trackAng and math.lerpAngle(clamp(1.5 * dt, 0, 1), camState.trackAng, sAng) or sAng

            local seatEnt = (hudSt and hudSt.pod and hudSt.pod:isValid() and hudSt.pod) or (lp and lp:isValid() and lp.getVehicle and lp:getVehicle()) or nil
            local anchorPos = (seatEnt and seatEnt:isValid()) and seatEnt:getPos() or base:getPos()
            local camPos = anchorPos + fwd * fpvOff.x - right * fpvOff.y + up * fpvOff.z
            local isLocked = (curTime - camState.lastMouseTime) > (CAM.idleTimeout or 2.0)
            local mph = vel:getLength() * 0.0568

            local camAng
            if isLocked or not camState.targetAng then
                local hLockP = fpv.horizonLockPitch or 0.5
                local hLockR = fpv.horizonLockRoll or 0.7
                local idealP = math.lerp(hLockP, sAng.p, camState.trackAng.p)
                local idealR = math.lerp(hLockR, sAng.r, 0)
                local idealY = sAng.y

                camState.angHistory = camState.angHistory or {}
                camState.velHistory = camState.velHistory or {}
                table.insert(camState.angHistory, { a = sAng, t = curTime })
                table.insert(camState.velHistory, { v = vel, t = curTime })
                while #camState.angHistory > 0 and (curTime - camState.angHistory[1].t) > 0.1 do table.remove(camState.angHistory, 1) end
                while #camState.velHistory > 0 and (curTime - camState.velHistory[1].t) > 0.1 do table.remove(camState.velHistory, 1) end

                local rawYawRate = 0
                local rawAccel = Vector(0, 0, 0)
                if #camState.angHistory > 1 and #camState.velHistory > 1 then
                    local oa, na = camState.angHistory[1], camState.angHistory[#camState.angHistory]
                    local tda = na.t - oa.t
                    if tda > 0.01 then rawYawRate = math.normalizeAngle(na.a.y - oa.a.y) / tda end
                    local ov, nv = camState.velHistory[1], camState.velHistory[#camState.velHistory]
                    local tdv = nv.t - ov.t
                    if tdv > 0.01 then rawAccel = (nv.v - ov.v) / tdv end
                end

                local yawOffset = 0
                local maxApex = fpv.apexMaxYaw or 65
                if fpv.apexLookSteer == true and hudSt then
                    local tgtSteer = (hudSt.steer or 0) * (fpv.apexSteerFactor or 12)
                    local recov = math.abs(camState.smoothSteerOff or 0) > math.abs(tgtSteer) + 0.1
                    local damp = clamp((recov and 10 or (fpv.headSpringSpeed or 8)) * dt, 0, 1)
                    camState.smoothSteerOff = math.lerp(damp, camState.smoothSteerOff or 0, tgtSteer)
                    yawOffset = clamp(camState.smoothSteerOff, -maxApex, maxApex)
                elseif fpv.apexLookYaw ~= false then
                    local recov = math.abs(camState.smoothYawRate or 0) > math.abs(rawYawRate) + 0.5
                    local damp = clamp((recov and 6 or (fpv.physicsDamping or 1.8)) * dt, 0, 1)
                    camState.smoothYawRate = math.lerp(damp, camState.smoothYawRate or 0, rawYawRate)
                    yawOffset = clamp(camState.smoothYawRate * (fpv.apexYawFactor or 0.45), -maxApex, maxApex)
                end
                idealY = idealY + yawOffset

                if fpv.enableGForces ~= false then
                    local fAcc = clamp(rawAccel:dot(fwd), -1500, 1500)
                    local rAcc = clamp(rawAccel:dot(right), -1500, 1500)
                    local gDamp = clamp((fpv.physicsDamping or 1.8) * dt, 0, 1)
                    camState.smoothFA = math.lerp(gDamp, camState.smoothFA or 0, fAcc)
                    camState.smoothRA = math.lerp(gDamp, camState.smoothRA or 0, rAcc)
                    idealP = idealP - camState.smoothFA * (fpv.gForcePitch or 0.009)
                    idealR = idealR - camState.smoothRA * (fpv.gForceRoll or 0.007)
                    camPos = camPos + fwd * clamp(-camState.smoothFA * 0.005, -1.5, 2.5) + right * clamp(-camState.smoothRA * 0.0025, -1.0, 1.0)
                end

                local idealAng = Angle(idealP + (fpv.pitchOffset or 0), idealY, idealR)
                local headLerp = clamp((fpv.headSpringSpeed or 8) * dt, 0, 1)
                camState.currentAng = math.lerpAngle(headLerp, camState.currentAng or sAng, idealAng)
                camState.targetAng = Angle(camState.currentAng.p, camState.currentAng.y, camState.currentAng.r)
                camAng = camState.currentAng
            else
                local seatYawDelta = math.normalizeAngle(sAng.y - (camState.lastFrameSeatYaw or sAng.y))
                camState.targetAng.y = camState.targetAng.y + seatYawDelta
                local idealRoll = math.lerp(fpv.horizonLockRoll or 0.7, sAng.r, 0)
                camState.targetAng.r = math.lerp(clamp(5 * dt, 0, 1), camState.targetAng.r, idealRoll)
                camAng = camState.targetAng
                camState.currentAng = camAng
            end
            camState.lastFrameSeatYaw = sAng.y

            local baseFOV = fpv.baseFOV or 90
            local targetFOV = baseFOV
            if fpv.enableFOV ~= false and mph > (fpv.fovSpeedThreshold or 20) then
                targetFOV = math.min(baseFOV + (mph - (fpv.fovSpeedThreshold or 20)) * (fpv.fovScale or 0.18), fpv.maxFOV or 120)
            end
            camState.currentFOV = math.lerp(clamp((fpv.fovSmoothing or 1.5) * dt, 0, 1), camState.currentFOV or baseFOV, targetFOV)

            local viewOut = { origin = camPos, angles = camAng, fov = camState.currentFOV, drawviewer = false }
            if SimcadeAddons and SimcadeAddons._clientHost then
                SimcadeAddons._clientHost.runHook("OnCalcView", viewOut, camState)
            end
            return viewOut
        end

        -- 3rd-Person Chase Camera (Advanced Car Camera V1.8)
        local maxDist = camState.trailerActive and (CAM.trailerDist or 350) or (CAM.maxDist or CAM.chaseDist or 190)
        local minDist = camState.trailerActive and (CAM.trailerMinDist or 300) or (CAM.minDist or math.max(70, maxDist - 30))
        local hOff    = camState.trailerActive and (CAM.trailerHeight or 80) or (CAM.heightOffset or CAM.chaseHeight or 55)
        local pOff    = camState.trailerActive and (CAM.trailerPitch or 10) or (CAM.pitchOffset or 5)

        local target = base:getPos() + up * hOff
        local isLocked = (timer.curtime() - camState.lastMouseTime) > (CAM.idleTimeout or 2.0)

        if not camState.initialized then
            local initDist = (minDist + maxDist) * 0.5
            camState.currentDist = initDist
            camState.targetDist  = initDist
            camState.distVel     = 0
            camState.lastSpeed   = vel:dot(fwd)
            camState.smoothAccel = 0
            camState.currentAng  = Angle(vehAng.p + pOff, vehAng.y, 0)
            camState.targetAng   = Angle(vehAng.p + pOff, vehAng.y, 0)
            camState.initialized = true
        end

        local finalCamAng
        if isLocked then
            local lookTarget = target
            if CAM.lookAhead ~= false and vel:getLengthSqr() > 2500 then
                lookTarget = target + vel:getNormalized() * (CAM.lookAheadDist or 200)
            end
            local idealAng = (lookTarget - (target - fwd)):getAngle()
            idealAng.p = idealAng.p + pOff
            idealAng.r = 0
            local resp = clamp((CAM.angleResponsiveness or 3.5) * dt, 0, 1)
            camState.currentAng = math.lerpAngle(resp, camState.currentAng or idealAng, idealAng)
            camState.targetAng  = Angle(camState.currentAng.p, camState.currentAng.y, 0)
            finalCamAng = camState.currentAng
        else
            finalCamAng = camState.targetAng or vehAng
            camState.currentAng = finalCamAng
        end

        local fwdSpd = vel:dot(fwd)
        local speedDelta = fwdSpd - (camState.lastSpeed or fwdSpd)
        camState.lastSpeed = fwdSpd
        local instAccel = speedDelta / dt
        camState.smoothAccel = math.lerp(clamp(8 * dt, 0, 1), camState.smoothAccel or 0, instAccel)

        local restDist = (minDist + maxDist) * 0.5
        local halfSpan = math.max(12, (maxDist - minDist) * 0.5)
        local dynamicTarget = restDist + clamp(camState.smoothAccel * (CAM.accelOffsetScale or 0.045), -halfSpan, halfSpan)
        local impulse = clamp(speedDelta * (CAM.accelImpulseScale or 0.18), -12, 12)

        local springForce = (dynamicTarget - camState.currentDist) * (CAM.centerForce or 15)
        local dampingForce = -camState.distVel * (CAM.posDamping or 10)
        local boundaryImpulse = 0
        if camState.currentDist > maxDist then
            boundaryImpulse = (maxDist - camState.currentDist) * (CAM.boundaryForce or 75)
        elseif camState.currentDist < minDist then
            boundaryImpulse = (minDist - camState.currentDist) * (CAM.boundaryForce or 75)
        end
        camState.distVel = camState.distVel + (springForce + dampingForce + boundaryImpulse) * dt + impulse
        camState.currentDist = clamp(camState.currentDist + camState.distVel * dt, 45, 600)

        local desiredOrigin = target - (finalCamAng:getForward() * camState.currentDist)
        if CAM.doCollision ~= false then
            local tr = trace.line(target, desiredOrigin, { base, lp }, CAM.colMask or 131083)
            if tr.Hit then
                desiredOrigin = tr.HitPos + tr.HitNormal * (CAM.colExtra or 8)
                camState.distVel = 0
                camState.currentDist = (tr.HitPos - target):getLength()
            end
        end

        return { origin = desiredOrigin, angles = finalCamAng, fov = CAM.chaseFOV or 90, drawviewer = true }
    end)
end
42336
-- name Simcade Sounds Module (Sim-Engine & Tire Audio)
-- shared

SimcadeSounds = SimcadeSounds or {}

if SERVER then return end

local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b) return v < a and a or (v > b and b or v) end

local DEFAULT_SOUNDS = {
    on          = "acf_extra/vehiclefx/engines/l4/mini_onhigh.WAV",
    off         = "acf_extra/vehiclefx/engines/l4/mini_onlow.WAV",
    fallbackOn  = "vehicles/v8/v8_firstgear_rev_loop1.wav",
    fallbackOff = "vehicles/v8/v8_idle_loop1.wav",
    turbo       = "acf_engines/turbine_small.wav",
    bov         = "acf_extra/vehiclefx/boost/turbo_hiss1.wav",
    shift       = "physics/plaster/ceiling_tile_impact_hard3.wav",
    starter     = "acf_extra/vehiclefx/starters/starter2.wav",
    fallbackStarter = "vehicles/v8/v8_start_loop1.wav",
    skid        = "vehicles/v8/skid_highfriction.wav",
    roll        = "vehicles/v8/tires_roll1.wav",
    suspBump    = "physics/metal/metal_solid_impact_soft1.wav",
    suspDroop   = "physics/metal/metal_box_strain1.wav",
}

local downloadedSoundCache = {}
local preloadedOneShots    = {}

local function normalizeWebURL(url)
    if type(url) ~= "string" then return url end
    if string.find(url, "github.com/", 1, true) then
        local u = string.gsub(url, "https://github.com/", "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/", 1)
        u = string.gsub(u, "http://github.com/", "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/", 1)
        u = string.gsub(u, "/blob/", "/", 1)
        u = string.gsub(u, "/raw/", "/", 1)
        return u
    end
    return url
end

local function canUseTempDownload()
    if not (http and http.get and file and file.writeTemp and bass and bass.loadFile) then return false end
    if hasPermission then
        local ok, res = pcall(hasPermission, "file.writeTemp")
        if ok and not res then return false end
    end
    return true
end

local function loadBassAudio(sndPath, flags, forceDownload, onLoaded)
    if not sndPath or sndPath == "" or not bass then return false end
    sndPath = normalizeWebURL(sndPath)
    if forceDownload and canUseTempDownload() then
        local cached = downloadedSoundCache[sndPath]
        if cached and cached ~= "loading" then
            pcall(bass.loadFile, cached, flags, onLoaded)
            return true
        elseif cached == nil then
            downloadedSoundCache[sndPath] = "loading"
            local safeName = "simcade_snd_" .. tostring(math.abs(#sndPath * 131 + (string.byte(sndPath, 1) or 1))) .. ".dat"
            pcall(http.get, sndPath, function(data)
                if data and data ~= "" then
                    local okW, tempPath = pcall(file.writeTemp, safeName, data)
                    if okW and tempPath then
                        downloadedSoundCache[sndPath] = tempPath
                        pcall(bass.loadFile, tempPath, flags, onLoaded)
                        return
                    end
                end
                downloadedSoundCache[sndPath] = nil
                if bass.loadURL then pcall(bass.loadURL, sndPath, flags, onLoaded) end
            end, function()
                downloadedSoundCache[sndPath] = nil
                if bass.loadURL then pcall(bass.loadURL, sndPath, flags, onLoaded) end
            end)
            return true
        end
    end
    if bass.loadURL then
        pcall(bass.loadURL, sndPath, flags, onLoaded)
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local function shouldDownloadEntry(entryCfg, globalDownload)
    if type(entryCfg) == "table" then
        if entryCfg.loadMode ~= nil then return entryCfg.loadMode == "download" end
        if entryCfg.download ~= nil then return entryCfg.download == true end
    end
    return globalDownload == true
end

local function createLoopSound(ent, primaryPath, fallbackPath, forceDownload)
    if not (ent and ent:isValid()) then return nil, false end

    local function tryPath(sndPath)
        if not sndPath or sndPath == "" then return nil, false end
        if sndPath:match("^https?://") then
            if bass then
                local holder = { snd = nil, isBass = true }
                loadBassAudio(sndPath, "3d noblock noplay", forceDownload, function(s)
                    if s and isValid(s) and isValid(ent) then
                        s:setPos(ent:getPos())
                        s:setVolume(0)
                        if s.setLooping then s:setLooping(true) end
                        s:play()
                        holder.snd = s
                    end
                end)
                return holder, true
            end
            return nil, false
        end
        local createFn = (sound and sound.create) or (sounds and sounds.create)
        if not createFn then return nil, false end
        local ok, s = pcall(createFn, ent, sndPath, true)
        if ok and s then
            pcall(function()
                s:play()
                s:setVolume(0)
                s:setPitch(100)
            end)
            return s, false
        end
        return nil, false
    end

    local s, isBass = tryPath(primaryPath)
    if s then return s, isBass end
    if fallbackPath and fallbackPath ~= "" then
        return tryPath(fallbackPath)
    end
    return nil, false
end

local function applyManagedVolPitch(s, isBass, pos, vol, pitch100)
    if s.setPos and pos then s:setPos(pos) end
    if s.setVolume then
        if isBass then s:setVolume(vol) else pcall(s.setVolume, s, vol, 0.04) end
    end
    if s.setPitch and pitch100 then
        if isBass then
            s:setPitch(clamp(pitch100 / 100, 0.35, 2.2))
        else
            pcall(s.setPitch, s, clamp(pitch100, 25, 250), 0.04)
        end
    end
    if not isBass and vol > 0.01 and s.isPlaying and not s:isPlaying() then
        s:play()
    end
end

local function setManagedVolPitch(sndObj, isBass, pos, vol, pitch100)
    if not sndObj then return end
    local s = isBass and sndObj.snd or sndObj
    if not s then return end
    pcall(applyManagedVolPitch, s, isBass, pos, vol, pitch100)
end

local function stopManagedSound(sndObj, isBass)
    if not sndObj then return end
    local s = isBass and sndObj.snd or sndObj
    if s and s.stop then pcall(function() s:stop() end) end
end

function SimcadeSounds.initClient(cfg)
    local SND = cfg.SOUND_CFG or {}
    local TIRE = cfg.TIRE_CFG or {}
    if TIRE.EnableAudio == false or SND.enabled == false then return end

    local ENG = cfg.ENGINE_CFG or {}
    local turboEnabled = (ENG.Turbo == true)
    local inductionSounds = {
        turbo = { snd = nil, bass = false },
        supercharger = {
            cfg = (type(SND.SuperchargerSound) == "table") and SND.SuperchargerSound or {},
            path = "",
            enabled = false,
            snd = nil,
            bass = false,
        },
    }
    local superchargerPath = inductionSounds.supercharger.cfg.sndsource or inductionSounds.supercharger.cfg.path
    inductionSounds.supercharger.path = type(superchargerPath) == "string" and superchargerPath or ""
    inductionSounds.supercharger.enabled = ENG.Supercharger == true
        and inductionSounds.supercharger.cfg.enabled == true
        and inductionSounds.supercharger.path ~= ""
    local gearWhine = {
        cfg = (type(SND.GearWhine) == "table") and SND.GearWhine or {},
        enabled = false,
        legacy = false,
        tracks = {},
        onClank = {},
        offClank = {},
        pendingOnClank = false,
        pendingOffClank = false,
        lastNetThrottle = nil,
        nextOnClank = 0,
        nextOffClank = 0,
    }
    local legacyPath = gearWhine.cfg.sndsource or gearWhine.cfg.path
    local onTrackCfg = type(gearWhine.cfg.OnThrottle) == "table" and gearWhine.cfg.OnThrottle or {}
    local offTrackCfg = type(gearWhine.cfg.OffThrottle) == "table" and gearWhine.cfg.OffThrottle or {}
    local onTrackPath = onTrackCfg.sndsource or onTrackCfg.path
    local offTrackPath = offTrackCfg.sndsource or offTrackCfg.path
    local hasSeparateTracks = (type(onTrackPath) == "string" and onTrackPath ~= "")
        or (type(offTrackPath) == "string" and offTrackPath ~= "")
    gearWhine.legacy = not hasSeparateTracks and type(legacyPath) == "string" and legacyPath ~= ""
    if gearWhine.legacy then
        onTrackCfg, onTrackPath = gearWhine.cfg, legacyPath
    end
    gearWhine.tracks = {
        on = { cfg = onTrackCfg, path = type(onTrackPath) == "string" and onTrackPath or "", snd = nil, bass = false, volume = 0, pitch = 100 },
        off = { cfg = offTrackCfg, path = type(offTrackPath) == "string" and offTrackPath or "", snd = nil, bass = false, volume = 0, pitch = 100 },
    }
    gearWhine.tracks.on.enabled = gearWhine.cfg.enabled == true and onTrackCfg.enabled ~= false and gearWhine.tracks.on.path ~= ""
    gearWhine.tracks.off.enabled = gearWhine.cfg.enabled == true and offTrackCfg.enabled ~= false and gearWhine.tracks.off.path ~= ""
    gearWhine.onClank = type(gearWhine.cfg.OnClank) == "table" and gearWhine.cfg.OnClank or {}
    gearWhine.offClank = type(gearWhine.cfg.OffClank) == "table" and gearWhine.cfg.OffClank or {}
    gearWhine.enabled = gearWhine.tracks.on.enabled or gearWhine.tracks.off.enabled
    local baseEnt = nil
    local pendingBaseEnt = nil
    local soundsInitialized = false
    local engineOnSnd, engineOnBass   = nil, false
    local engineOffSnd, engineOffBass = nil, false
    local starterSnd, starterBass     = nil, false
    local skidSnd, skidBass           = nil, false
    local rollSnd, rollBass           = nil, false
    local surfaceSounds               = {}
    local activeSurfKey               = 67
    local globalDownload              = (SND.WebLoadMode == "download") or (SND.DownloadWebSounds == true)

    local function surfIdToKey(matType)
        if SND.Surfaces and SND.Surfaces[matType] then return matType end
        if SND[matType] then return matType end
        if SND.Surfaces then
            if matType == SimcadeTires.ICE and SND.Surfaces[74] then return 74 end
            if matType == 84 or matType == 88 or matType == 0 then
                if SND.Surfaces[67] then return 67 end
            elseif matType == 78 and SND.Surfaces[68] then
                return 68
            elseif matType == 71 and SND.Surfaces[77] then
                return 77
            end
        end
        return 67
    end

    local function getSurfCfg(key)
        return (SND.Surfaces and (SND.Surfaces[key] or SND.Surfaces[67] or SND.Surfaces[0])) or SND[key] or {}
    end

    local currentRPM = 0
    local lerpedThrottle = 0
    local currentSpeed = 0
    local currentSkid = 0
    local curSkidVol, curSkidPitch = 0, 100
    local curRollVol, curRollPitch = 0, 100
    local lastSoundUpdateTime = 0
    local lastAudio = { rpm = 0, redline = 8000, thr = 0, boost = 0, skid = 0, speed = 0, cranking = false, running = false, surfId = 67 }

    local onPath      = SND.Engine_SoundOn  or DEFAULT_SOUNDS.on
    local offPath     = SND.Engine_SoundOff or DEFAULT_SOUNDS.off
    local turboPath   = turboEnabled and (SND.TurboSound or DEFAULT_SOUNDS.turbo) or ""
    local bovPath     = turboEnabled and (SND.TurboBOV or DEFAULT_SOUNDS.bov) or ""
    local shiftPath   = SND.GearChange      or DEFAULT_SOUNDS.shift
    local starterPath = SND.Starter         or DEFAULT_SOUNDS.starter
    local defSurf     = getSurfCfg(67)
    local skidPath    = (defSurf.skid and defSurf.skid.sndsource) or DEFAULT_SOUNDS.skid
    local rollPath    = (defSurf.roll and defSurf.roll.sndsource) or DEFAULT_SOUNDS.roll

    local pendingGearSound      = false
    local pendingBovSound       = false
    local previousPrediction
    local predictedShifts = {}
    local predictedBovUntil = 0
    local confirmedBovUntil = 0
    local pendingSuspBumpInt    = 0
    local pendingSuspDroopInt   = 0
    local soundInitStage        = 0
    local soundTick = { due = 0, interval = math.min(0.02, SimcadeCore.networkInterval(cfg.VEHICLE_CFG)) }

    local function collectVariants(cfgEntry)
        if type(cfgEntry) ~= "table" then return nil end
        local list = (type(cfgEntry.variants) == "table" and #cfgEntry.variants > 0) and cfgEntry.variants or ((#cfgEntry > 0) and cfgEntry or nil)
        if not list then return nil end
        local out = {}
        for _, v in ipairs(list) do
            if type(v) == "string" then
                table.insert(out, {
                    sndsource = v,
                    volume    = cfgEntry.volume,
                    pitchMin  = cfgEntry.pitchMin,
                    pitchMax  = cfgEntry.pitchMax,
                    loadMode  = cfgEntry.loadMode,
                })
            elseif type(v) == "table" and v.enabled ~= false then
                table.insert(out, {
                    sndsource = v.sndsource or v.path,
                    volume    = v.volume ~= nil and v.volume or cfgEntry.volume,
                    pitchMin  = v.pitchMin ~= nil and v.pitchMin or cfgEntry.pitchMin,
                    pitchMax  = v.pitchMax ~= nil and v.pitchMax or cfgEntry.pitchMax,
                    loadMode  = v.loadMode ~= nil and v.loadMode or cfgEntry.loadMode,
                    minInten  = v.minInten,
                    maxInten  = v.maxInten,
                })
            end
        end
        return (#out > 0) and out or nil
    end

    local function pickOneShotVariant(cfgEntry, inten)
        local list = collectVariants(cfgEntry)
        if not list then return cfgEntry end
        if inten ~= nil then
            local matched = {}
            for _, v in ipairs(list) do
                local lo = v.minInten or 0
                local hi = v.maxInten or 1.0
                if inten >= lo and inten <= hi then
                    table.insert(matched, v)
                end
            end
            if #matched > 0 then
                return matched[math.random(1, #matched)]
            end
        end
        return list[math.random(1, #list)]
    end

    local function preloadSingleOneShot(cfgEntry, fallbackPath)
        local rawPath = (type(cfgEntry) == "table" and cfgEntry.sndsource) or (type(cfgEntry) == "string" and cfgEntry) or fallbackPath
        if not rawPath or rawPath == "" then return end
        local sndPath = normalizeWebURL(rawPath)
        if preloadedOneShots[sndPath] ~= nil then return end
        if sndPath:match("^https?://") then
            preloadedOneShots[sndPath] = "loading"
            local forceDl = shouldDownloadEntry(cfgEntry, globalDownload)
            loadBassAudio(sndPath, "3d noblock noplay", forceDl, function(s)
                if s and isValid(s) then
                    preloadedOneShots[sndPath] = s
                else
                    preloadedOneShots[sndPath] = nil
                end
            end)
        elseif sndPath:match("^data/") and bass and bass.loadFile then
            preloadedOneShots[sndPath] = "loading"
            pcall(bass.loadFile, sndPath, "3d noblock noplay", function(s)
                if s and isValid(s) then
                    preloadedOneShots[sndPath] = s
                else
                    preloadedOneShots[sndPath] = nil
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function preloadOneShot(cfgEntry, fallbackPath)
        local list = collectVariants(cfgEntry)
        if list then
            for _, v in ipairs(list) do
                preloadSingleOneShot(v, fallbackPath)
            end
        else
            preloadSingleOneShot(cfgEntry, fallbackPath)
        end
    end

    local function playOneShot(baseEntRef, basePos, cfgEntry, fallbackPath, defVol, defPitch100, volScale, inten)
        local entry = pickOneShotVariant(cfgEntry, inten)
        local rawPath = (type(entry) == "table" and entry.sndsource) or (type(entry) == "string" and entry) or fallbackPath
        if not rawPath or rawPath == "" then return end
        local sndPath = normalizeWebURL(rawPath)
        local vol = clamp(((type(entry) == "table" and entry.volume) or defVol or 0.6) * (volScale or 1.0), 0, 1)
        if vol <= 0.01 then return end
        local pMin = (type(entry) == "table" and entry.pitchMin) or ((defPitch100 or 100) / 100)
        local pMax = (type(entry) == "table" and entry.pitchMax) or pMin
        local pitch100 = math.random(math.floor(pMin * 100), math.floor(math.max(pMin, pMax) * 100))
        if sndPath:match("^https?://") or sndPath:match("^data/") then
            local cached = preloadedOneShots[sndPath]
            if cached == "loading" then return end
            if cached and isValid(cached) then
                pcall(function()
                    if cached.setPos then cached:setPos(basePos) end
                    if cached.setVolume then cached:setVolume(vol) end
                    if cached.setPitch then cached:setPitch(clamp(pitch100 / 100, 0.35, 2.2)) end
                    if cached.setLooping then cached:setLooping(false) end
                    if cached.setTime then cached:setTime(0) end
                    cached:play()
                end)
                return
            end
            local forceDl = shouldDownloadEntry(entry, globalDownload)
            local loader = function(s)
                if s and isValid(s) then
                    preloadedOneShots[sndPath] = s
                    if s.setPos then s:setPos(basePos) end
                    if s.setVolume then s:setVolume(vol) end
                    if s.setPitch then s:setPitch(clamp(pitch100 / 100, 0.35, 2.2)) end
                    if s.setLooping then s:setLooping(false) end
                    s:play()
                end
            end
            if sndPath:match("^https?://") then
                loadBassAudio(sndPath, "3d noblock noplay", forceDl, loader)
            elseif bass and bass.loadFile then
                pcall(bass.loadFile, sndPath, "3d noblock noplay", loader)
            end
        else
            pcall(function()
                if sound and sound.emitSound then
                    sound.emitSound(sndPath, basePos, nil, pitch100, vol)
                else
                    baseEntRef:emitSound(sndPath, 75, pitch100, vol)
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    if SimcadeAddons and SimcadeAddons._clientHost then
        SimcadeAddons._clientHost.runHook("OnSoundReady", preloadOneShot, playOneShot)
    end
    preloadOneShot(SND.SuspensionBump, DEFAULT_SOUNDS.suspBump)
    preloadOneShot(SND.SuspensionDroop, DEFAULT_SOUNDS.suspDroop)
    preloadOneShot(SND.GearChange, shiftPath)
    if turboEnabled then preloadOneShot(SND.TurboBOV, bovPath) end
    if gearWhine.cfg.enabled == true then
        if gearWhine.onClank.enabled == true then preloadOneShot(gearWhine.onClank, nil) end
        if gearWhine.offClank.enabled == true then preloadOneShot(gearWhine.offClank, nil) end
    end

    local function isCpuHot(limit)
        local qMax = quotaMax and quotaMax() or 0
        if qMax <= 0 then return false end
        if quotaUsed and (quotaUsed() / qMax) > limit then return true end
        if quotaAverage and (quotaAverage() / qMax) > limit then return true end
        return false
    end

    net.receive("Simcade_AudioSync", function()
        if isCpuHot(0.85) then return end
        local ent         = net.readEntity()
        local rpm         = net.readUInt(16)
        local redline     = net.readUInt(16)
        local thr         = net.readUInt(8) / 255
        local boost       = net.readInt(16) * 0.1
        local skid        = net.readUInt(8) / 170
        local speed       = net.readUInt(16)
        local cranking    = net.readBool()
        local running     = net.readBool()
        local gearChanged   = net.readBool()
        local gear          = net.readString()
        local bov           = net.readBool()
        local suspBumpQ     = net.readUInt(4)
        local suspDroopQ    = net.readUInt(4)
        local surfId        = net.readUInt(8)

        lastAudio.rpm      = rpm
        lastAudio.redline  = math.max(3000, redline or 8000)
        lastAudio.thr      = thr
        lastAudio.boost    = boost
        lastAudio.skid     = skid
        lastAudio.speed    = speed
        lastAudio.cranking = cranking
        lastAudio.running  = running
        lastAudio.surfId   = surfId or 1
        lastAudio.gear     = gear

        if ent and ent:isValid() then
            if baseEnt ~= ent then
                pendingBaseEnt = ent
                soundsInitialized = false
            end
            if gearChanged and shiftPath ~= "" then pendingGearSound = gear end
            if turboEnabled and bov and bovPath ~= "" then pendingBovSound = true end
            if (suspBumpQ or 0) > 0 then pendingSuspBumpInt = math.max(pendingSuspBumpInt, suspBumpQ / 15) end
            if (suspDroopQ or 0) > 0 then pendingSuspDroopInt = math.max(pendingSuspDroopInt, suspDroopQ / 15) end
        end
    end)

    hook.add("think", "SimcadeSounds_Update", function()
        local now = timer.curtime()
        if now < soundTick.due then return end
        soundTick.due = math.max(soundTick.due + soundTick.interval, now)
        if isCpuHot(0.75) then return end
        local soundDt = (lastSoundUpdateTime > 0) and math.clamp(now - lastSoundUpdateTime, 0.005, 0.1) or 0.02
        lastSoundUpdateTime = now

        if pendingBaseEnt and pendingBaseEnt:isValid() and not soundsInitialized then
            if soundInitStage == 0 then
                previousPrediction, predictedShifts, predictedBovUntil = nil, {}, 0
                confirmedBovUntil = 0
                gearWhine.lastThrottle, gearWhine.usingPrediction = nil, nil
                stopManagedSound(engineOnSnd, engineOnBass)
                stopManagedSound(engineOffSnd, engineOffBass)
                stopManagedSound(inductionSounds.turbo.snd, inductionSounds.turbo.bass)
                stopManagedSound(inductionSounds.supercharger.snd, inductionSounds.supercharger.bass)
                for _, track in pairs(gearWhine.tracks) do
                    stopManagedSound(track.snd, track.bass)
                end
                stopManagedSound(starterSnd, starterBass)
                stopManagedSound(skidSnd, skidBass)
                stopManagedSound(rollSnd, rollBass)
                baseEnt = pendingBaseEnt
                soundInitStage = 1
                return
            elseif soundInitStage == 1 then
                engineOnSnd, engineOnBass = createLoopSound(baseEnt, onPath, DEFAULT_SOUNDS.fallbackOn, globalDownload)
                soundInitStage = 2
                return
            elseif soundInitStage == 2 then
                engineOffSnd, engineOffBass = createLoopSound(baseEnt, offPath, DEFAULT_SOUNDS.fallbackOff, globalDownload)
                soundInitStage = 3
                return
            elseif soundInitStage == 3 then
                if turboEnabled and turboPath ~= "" then
                    inductionSounds.turbo.snd, inductionSounds.turbo.bass = createLoopSound(baseEnt, turboPath, nil, globalDownload)
                else
                    inductionSounds.turbo.snd = nil
                end
                soundInitStage = 4
                return
            elseif soundInitStage == 4 then
                local supercharger = inductionSounds.supercharger
                if supercharger.enabled then
                    supercharger.snd, supercharger.bass = createLoopSound(baseEnt, supercharger.path, nil, shouldDownloadEntry(supercharger.cfg, globalDownload))
                else
                    supercharger.snd, supercharger.bass = nil, false
                end
                soundInitStage = 5
                return
            elseif soundInitStage == 5 then
                starterSnd, starterBass = createLoopSound(baseEnt, starterPath, DEFAULT_SOUNDS.fallbackStarter, globalDownload)
                soundInitStage = 6
                return
            elseif soundInitStage == 6 then
                local track = gearWhine.tracks.on
                if track.enabled then
                    track.snd, track.bass = createLoopSound(baseEnt, track.path, nil, shouldDownloadEntry(track.cfg, globalDownload))
                else
                    track.snd, track.bass = nil, false
                end
                soundInitStage = 7
                return
            elseif soundInitStage == 7 then
                local track = gearWhine.tracks.off
                if track.enabled then
                    track.snd, track.bass = createLoopSound(baseEnt, track.path, nil, shouldDownloadEntry(track.cfg, globalDownload))
                else
                    track.snd, track.bass = nil, false
                end
                soundInitStage = 8
                return
            elseif soundInitStage == 8 then
                skidSnd, skidBass = createLoopSound(baseEnt, skidPath, DEFAULT_SOUNDS.skid, shouldDownloadEntry(defSurf.skid, globalDownload))
                soundInitStage = 9
                return
            else
                rollSnd, rollBass = createLoopSound(baseEnt, rollPath, DEFAULT_SOUNDS.roll, shouldDownloadEntry(defSurf.roll, globalDownload))
                surfaceSounds = { [67] = { skidSnd = skidSnd, skidBass = skidBass, rollSnd = rollSnd, rollBass = rollBass } }
                activeSurfKey = 67
                soundInitStage = 0
                soundsInitialized = true
            end
        end

        if not (baseEnt and baseEnt:isValid()) then return end

        local basePos = baseEnt:getPos()
        local pred = SimcadePrediction and SimcadePrediction.get and SimcadePrediction.get(baseEnt)
        local throttle = pred and pred.result.throttle or lastAudio.thr
        local speed = pred and math.abs(pred.result.forward_ms) * 3.6 or lastAudio.speed
        local skid = pred and pred.skid or lastAudio.skid
        local boost = pred and pred.turboPSI or lastAudio.boost

        local confirmationWindow = pred and pred.roundTrip + 0.25 or 0.3
        if pred and previousPrediction and lastAudio.running then
            local gear = pred.result.gearName
            if shiftPath ~= "" and gear ~= previousPrediction.result.gearName and gear ~= lastAudio.gear
                and now >= (predictedShifts[gear] or 0) then
                predictedShifts[gear] = now + confirmationWindow
                playOneShot(baseEnt, basePos, SND.GearChange, shiftPath, 0.50, 100)
            end
            local bovThreshold = math.max(0, tonumber(ENG.Turbo_BOVThresholdPSI) or 3.5)
            if turboEnabled and bovPath ~= "" and not pendingBovSound and now >= math.max(predictedBovUntil, confirmedBovUntil)
                and previousPrediction.result.throttle > 0.45 and throttle < 0.20
                and previousPrediction.turboPSI > bovThreshold then
                predictedBovUntil = now + confirmationWindow
                playOneShot(baseEnt, basePos, SND.TurboBOV, bovPath, SND.TurboBOVVolume or 0.65, 102)
            end
        end
        previousPrediction = pred
        if pendingGearSound then
            if now >= (predictedShifts[pendingGearSound] or 0) then
                playOneShot(baseEnt, basePos, SND.GearChange, shiftPath, 0.50, 100)
            end
            predictedShifts[pendingGearSound] = nil
            pendingGearSound = false
        end
        if pendingBovSound then
            pendingBovSound = false
            if now >= predictedBovUntil then
                playOneShot(baseEnt, basePos, SND.TurboBOV, bovPath, SND.TurboBOVVolume or 0.65, 102)
            end
            predictedBovUntil = 0
            confirmedBovUntil = now + confirmationWindow
        end
        if gearWhine.cfg.enabled == true and lastAudio.running and gearWhine.lastThrottle
            and gearWhine.usingPrediction == (pred ~= nil) then
            local onThreshold = clamp(tonumber(gearWhine.onClank.threshold) or 0.6, 0, 1)
            local offThreshold = clamp(tonumber(gearWhine.offClank.threshold) or 0.4, 0, 1)
            gearWhine.pendingOnClank = gearWhine.lastThrottle < onThreshold and throttle >= onThreshold
            gearWhine.pendingOffClank = gearWhine.lastThrottle > offThreshold and throttle <= offThreshold
        end
        gearWhine.lastThrottle, gearWhine.usingPrediction = throttle, pred ~= nil
        if gearWhine.pendingOnClank then
            gearWhine.pendingOnClank = false
            local clank = gearWhine.onClank
            local now = timer.curtime()
            local soundPath = clank.sndsource or clank.path
            local hasSound = (type(soundPath) == "string" and soundPath ~= "")
                or (type(clank.variants) == "table" and #clank.variants > 0)
            if clank.enabled == true and hasSound
                and speed >= (tonumber(clank.minSpeed) or 10)
                and now >= gearWhine.nextOnClank then
                gearWhine.nextOnClank = now + math.max(0, tonumber(clank.cooldown) or 0.35)
                playOneShot(baseEnt, basePos, clank, nil, 0.55, 100)
            end
        end
        if gearWhine.pendingOffClank then
            gearWhine.pendingOffClank = false
            local clank = gearWhine.offClank
            local now = timer.curtime()
            local soundPath = clank.sndsource or clank.path
            local hasSound = (type(soundPath) == "string" and soundPath ~= "")
                or (type(clank.variants) == "table" and #clank.variants > 0)
            if clank.enabled == true and hasSound
                and speed >= (tonumber(clank.minSpeed) or 10)
                and now >= gearWhine.nextOffClank then
                gearWhine.nextOffClank = now + math.max(0, tonumber(clank.cooldown) or 0.35)
                playOneShot(baseEnt, basePos, clank, nil, 0.55, 100)
            end
        end
        if pendingSuspBumpInt > 0 then
            local inten = pendingSuspBumpInt
            pendingSuspBumpInt = 0
            if not (SND.SuspensionBump and SND.SuspensionBump.enabled == false) then
                playOneShot(baseEnt, basePos, SND.SuspensionBump, DEFAULT_SOUNDS.suspBump, 0.65, 100, 0.25 + 0.75 * inten, inten)
            end
        end
        if pendingSuspDroopInt > 0 then
            local inten = pendingSuspDroopInt
            pendingSuspDroopInt = 0
            if not (SND.SuspensionDroop and SND.SuspensionDroop.enabled == false) then
                playOneShot(baseEnt, basePos, SND.SuspensionDroop, DEFAULT_SOUNDS.suspDroop, 0.50, 100, 0.25 + 0.75 * inten, inten)
            end
        end
        local ft = soundDt

        if not lastAudio.running and not lastAudio.cranking then
            setManagedVolPitch(engineOnSnd, engineOnBass, basePos, 0, 100)
            setManagedVolPitch(engineOffSnd, engineOffBass, basePos, 0, 100)
            setManagedVolPitch(inductionSounds.turbo.snd, inductionSounds.turbo.bass, basePos, 0, 100)
            setManagedVolPitch(inductionSounds.supercharger.snd, inductionSounds.supercharger.bass, basePos, 0, 100)
            setManagedVolPitch(starterSnd, starterBass, basePos, 0, 100)
        else
            local targetRPM = pred and pred.result.flywheel_rpm or lastAudio.rpm
            currentRPM = math.max(0, currentRPM + (targetRPM - currentRPM) * math.min(1, ft * 14))
            local blendRate = (throttle > lerpedThrottle) and 12 or (SND.Engine_SoundBlendRate or 4)
            lerpedThrottle = lerpedThrottle + (throttle - lerpedThrottle) * math.min(1, ft * blendRate)

            local rpm        = currentRPM
            local redline    = lastAudio.redline
            local rpmPercent = clamp(rpm / redline, 0, 1.15)
            local isRunning  = lastAudio.running and (rpm > 350)

            -- Red Fox Engine V3 Exact Crossfade & Pitch Formula (0..255 where 100 = 1.0x pitch)
            local blend       = clamp(lerpedThrottle, 0, 1) ^ 1.1
            local throttleOn  = clamp(blend + 0.12, 0, 1)
            local throttleOff = clamp((1.0 - blend) + 0.15, 0, 1)

            local onDiv     = SND.Engine_SoundOnDiv or 4000
            local offDiv    = SND.Engine_SoundOffDiv or 3500
            local onPitchM  = SND.Engine_SoundOnPitchMult or 1.0
            local offPitchM = SND.Engine_SoundOffPitchMult or 1.0
            local onVolM    = SND.Engine_SoundOnVolume or 0.80
            local offVolM   = SND.Engine_SoundOffVolume or 0.45
            local overall   = SND.Engine_SoundOverallDiv or 1.0

            if engineOnSnd then
                local pitchBase = (rpm / onDiv) * 155 + (isRunning and 50 or 0)
                local pitch100  = clamp((pitchBase / 200) * onPitchM * 100, 25, 250)
                local onRpmFactor = 0.15 + 0.85 * (rpmPercent ^ 0.25)
                local volFinal  = lastAudio.running and clamp(onRpmFactor * throttleOn * onVolM * overall, 0, 1) or 0
                setManagedVolPitch(engineOnSnd, engineOnBass, basePos, volFinal, pitch100)
            end

            if engineOffSnd then
                local pitchBase = (rpm / offDiv) * 155 + (isRunning and 50 or 0)
                local pitch100  = clamp((pitchBase / 200) * offPitchM * 100, 25, 250)
                local offRpmFactor = 0.20 + 0.80 * (rpmPercent ^ 0.30)
                local volFinal  = lastAudio.running and clamp(offRpmFactor * throttleOff * offVolM * overall, 0, 1) or 0
                setManagedVolPitch(engineOffSnd, engineOffBass, basePos, volFinal, pitch100)
            end

            local turboSound = inductionSounds.turbo
            if turboSound.snd then
                if lastAudio.running and boost > 0.2 then
                    local bFrac    = clamp(boost / 14, 0, 1.3)
                    local pitch100 = clamp((75 + 50 * bFrac) * (SND.TurboPitch or 1.0), 40, 220)
                    local vol      = clamp(bFrac * 0.75 * (SND.TurboVolume or 1.0) * overall, 0, 1)
                    setManagedVolPitch(turboSound.snd, turboSound.bass, basePos, vol, pitch100)
                else
                    setManagedVolPitch(turboSound.snd, turboSound.bass, basePos, 0, 100)
                end
            end

            local supercharger = inductionSounds.supercharger
            if supercharger.snd then
                local soundCfg = supercharger.cfg
                local minRPM = math.max(0, tonumber(soundCfg.minRPM) or 1000)
                local maxRPM = math.max(minRPM + 1, tonumber(soundCfg.maxRPM) or redline)
                local rpmFrac = clamp((rpm - minRPM) / (maxRPM - minRPM), 0, 1)
                local pitchMin = tonumber(soundCfg.pitchMin) or 0.75
                local pitchMax = math.max(pitchMin, tonumber(soundCfg.pitchMax) or 1.60)
                local pitchExp = math.max(0.1, tonumber(soundCfg.pitchExp) or 1.0)
                local volExp = math.max(0.1, tonumber(soundCfg.volExp) or 0.8)
                local coastVolume = clamp(tonumber(soundCfg.coastVolume) or 0.15, 0, 1)
                local loadFactor = coastVolume + (1 - coastVolume) * clamp(lerpedThrottle, 0, 1)
                local vol = lastAudio.running and clamp((rpmFrac ^ volExp) * (tonumber(soundCfg.volume) or 0.65) * loadFactor * overall, 0, 1) or 0
                local pitch = clamp((pitchMin + (pitchMax - pitchMin) * (rpmFrac ^ pitchExp)) * 100, 40, 220)
                setManagedVolPitch(supercharger.snd, supercharger.bass, basePos, vol, pitch)
            end

            if starterSnd then
                local vol = lastAudio.cranking and clamp(SND.StarterVolume or 0.75, 0, 1) or 0
                local pitch100 = clamp(100 * (SND.StarterPitch or 1.0), 40, 200)
                setManagedVolPitch(starterSnd, starterBass, basePos, vol, pitch100)
            end
        end

        local surfId = lastAudio.surfId or 1
        local targetKey = (surfId > 0) and surfIdToKey(surfId) or activeSurfKey
        if targetKey ~= activeSurfKey then
            local prevEntry = surfaceSounds[activeSurfKey]
            if prevEntry then
                setManagedVolPitch(prevEntry.skidSnd, prevEntry.skidBass, basePos, 0, 100)
                setManagedVolPitch(prevEntry.rollSnd, prevEntry.rollBass, basePos, 0, 100)
            end
            activeSurfKey = targetKey
        end

        local surfEntry = surfaceSounds[activeSurfKey]
        if not surfEntry then
            local sCfg = getSurfCfg(activeSurfKey)
            local sPath = (sCfg.skid and sCfg.skid.sndsource) or skidPath
            local rPath = (sCfg.roll and sCfg.roll.sndsource) or rollPath
            local sObj, sB = createLoopSound(baseEnt, sPath, skidPath, shouldDownloadEntry(sCfg.skid, globalDownload))
            local rObj, rB = createLoopSound(baseEnt, rPath, rollPath, shouldDownloadEntry(sCfg.roll, globalDownload))
            surfEntry = { skidSnd = sObj, skidBass = sB, rollSnd = rObj, rollBass = rB }
            surfaceSounds[activeSurfKey] = surfEntry
        end

        local curSkidSnd, curSkidBass = surfEntry.skidSnd, surfEntry.skidBass
        local curRollSnd, curRollBass = surfEntry.rollSnd, surfEntry.rollBass
        local activeCfg = getSurfCfg(activeSurfKey)
        local isGrounded = (surfId > 0)

        currentSpeed = currentSpeed + (speed - currentSpeed) * math.min(1, soundDt * 8)
        currentSkid  = currentSkid  + (skid - currentSkid) * math.min(1, soundDt * 10)
        local outAlpha = math.min(1, soundDt * 8)

        if gearWhine.enabled then
            local minSpeed = math.max(0, tonumber(gearWhine.cfg.minSpeed) or 5)
            local maxSpeed = math.max(minSpeed + 1, tonumber(gearWhine.cfg.maxSpeed) or 180)
            local speedFrac = clamp((currentSpeed - minSpeed) / (maxSpeed - minSpeed), 0, 1)
            local coastVolume = clamp(tonumber(gearWhine.cfg.coastVolume) or 0.35, 0, 1)
            local throttleLoad = clamp(lerpedThrottle, 0, 1)
            local overall = math.max(0, tonumber(SND.Engine_SoundOverallDiv) or 1.0)
            for name, track in pairs(gearWhine.tracks) do
                if track.snd then
                    local trackCfg = track.cfg
                    local pitchMin = tonumber(trackCfg.pitchMin) or (name == "on" and 0.70 or 0.65)
                    local pitchMax = math.max(pitchMin, tonumber(trackCfg.pitchMax) or (name == "on" and 1.60 or 1.35))
                    local pitchExp = math.max(0.1, tonumber(trackCfg.pitchExp) or 1.0)
                    local volExp = math.max(0.1, tonumber(trackCfg.volExp) or 0.8)
                    local loadFactor
                    if gearWhine.legacy then
                        loadFactor = coastVolume + (1 - coastVolume) * throttleLoad
                    elseif name == "on" then
                        loadFactor = throttleLoad
                    else
                        loadFactor = 1 - throttleLoad
                    end
                    local targetVol = lastAudio.running and clamp((speedFrac ^ volExp) * (tonumber(trackCfg.volume) or 0.65) * loadFactor * overall, 0, 1) or 0
                    local targetPitch = clamp((pitchMin + (pitchMax - pitchMin) * (speedFrac ^ pitchExp)) * 100, 40, 220)
                    track.volume = track.volume + (targetVol - track.volume) * outAlpha
                    track.pitch = track.pitch + (targetPitch - track.pitch) * outAlpha
                    setManagedVolPitch(track.snd, track.bass, basePos, track.volume, track.pitch)
                end
            end
        end

        if curSkidSnd then
            local skidCfg = activeCfg.skid or {}
            local minSk = skidCfg.minSkid or 0.05
            local maxSk = math.max(minSk + 0.05, skidCfg.maxSkid or 1.0)
            local rawSk = isGrounded and currentSkid or 0
            local sAmt = isGrounded and clamp((rawSk - minSk) / (maxSk - minSk), 0, 1) or 0
            local volFrac = (skidCfg.volExp and skidCfg.volExp ~= 1) and (sAmt ^ skidCfg.volExp) or sAmt
            local pitchFrac = (skidCfg.pitchExp and skidCfg.pitchExp ~= 1) and (sAmt ^ skidCfg.pitchExp) or sAmt
            local targetVol = volFrac * (skidCfg.volume or 0.75)
            local pMin, pMax = skidCfg.pitchMin or 0.85, skidCfg.pitchMax or 1.35
            local targetPitch = clamp((pMin + (pMax - pMin) * pitchFrac) * 100, 40, 220)
            curSkidVol   = curSkidVol   + (targetVol   - curSkidVol)   * outAlpha
            curSkidPitch = curSkidPitch + (targetPitch - curSkidPitch) * outAlpha
            setManagedVolPitch(curSkidSnd, curSkidBass, basePos, curSkidVol, curSkidPitch)
        end

        if curRollSnd then
            local rollCfg = activeCfg.roll or {}
            local minSpd = rollCfg.minSpeed or 5
            local maxSpd = math.max(minSpd + 10, rollCfg.maxSpeed or 200)
            local curSpd = currentSpeed
            local spdFrac = (isGrounded and curSpd > minSpd) and clamp((curSpd - minSpd) / (maxSpd - minSpd), 0, 1) or 0
            local volFrac = (rollCfg.volExp and rollCfg.volExp ~= 1) and (spdFrac ^ rollCfg.volExp) or spdFrac
            local pitchFrac = (rollCfg.pitchExp and rollCfg.pitchExp ~= 1) and (spdFrac ^ rollCfg.pitchExp) or spdFrac
            local targetVol = (isGrounded and curSpd > minSpd) and (volFrac * (rollCfg.volume or 0.35)) or 0
            local pMin, pMax = rollCfg.pitchMin or 0.65, rollCfg.pitchMax or 1.45
            local targetPitch = clamp((pMin + (pMax - pMin) * pitchFrac) * 100, 40, 220)
            curRollVol   = curRollVol   + (targetVol   - curRollVol)   * outAlpha
            curRollPitch = curRollPitch + (targetPitch - curRollPitch) * outAlpha
            setManagedVolPitch(curRollSnd, curRollBass, basePos, curRollVol, curRollPitch)
        end

        if SimcadeAddons and SimcadeAddons._clientHost then
            SimcadeAddons._clientHost.runHook("OnSoundTick", baseEnt, lastAudio)
        end
    end)

    hook.add("removed", "SimcadeSounds_Cleanup", function()
        stopManagedSound(engineOnSnd, engineOnBass)
        stopManagedSound(engineOffSnd, engineOffBass)
        stopManagedSound(inductionSounds.turbo.snd, inductionSounds.turbo.bass)
        stopManagedSound(inductionSounds.supercharger.snd, inductionSounds.supercharger.bass)
        for _, track in pairs(gearWhine.tracks) do
            stopManagedSound(track.snd, track.bass)
        end
        stopManagedSound(starterSnd, starterBass)
        for _, entry in pairs(surfaceSounds) do
            stopManagedSound(entry.skidSnd, entry.skidBass)
            stopManagedSound(entry.rollSnd, entry.rollBass)
        end
    end)
end
21219
-- name Simcade Skidmarks Module (Pooled 3D Quad Mesh Skidmarks)
-- shared

SimcadeSkidmarks = SimcadeSkidmarks or {}

if SERVER then return end

local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b) return v < a and a or (v > b and b or v) end

function SimcadeSkidmarks.initClient(cfg)
    local SKID = cfg.SKID_CFG or {}
    local TIRE = cfg.TIRE_CFG or {}
    if TIRE.EnableSkidmarks == false or SKID.enabled == false then return end

    local matCfg = SKID.MaterialConfig or {}
    local surfacesCfg = SKID.Surfaces or {}

    local Materials = {}
    local MaterialUsesCustomTexture = {}

    local function buildMaterialCacheKey(sCfg, axle)
        local useModTexture = sCfg and sCfg.UseModTexture
        if useModTexture == nil then
            useModTexture = matCfg.UseModTexture
        end
        local shader = (sCfg and sCfg.Shader) or matCfg.Shader or "VertexLitGeneric"
        local materialPath = (sCfg and sCfg.Material) or matCfg.Material or ""
        local baseTexture
        if axle == "front" then
            baseTexture = (sCfg and sCfg.FrontTexture) or matCfg.FrontTexture or (sCfg and sCfg.Texture) or matCfg.Texture or SKID.Texture or "vgui/white"
        elseif axle == "rear" then
            baseTexture = (sCfg and sCfg.RearTexture) or matCfg.RearTexture or (sCfg and sCfg.Texture) or matCfg.Texture or SKID.Texture or "vgui/white"
        else
            baseTexture = (sCfg and sCfg.Texture) or matCfg.Texture or SKID.Texture or "vgui/white"
        end
        local alphaTest = tostring((sCfg and sCfg.AlphaTest) or matCfg.AlphaTest or false)
        local alphaRef = tostring((sCfg and sCfg.AlphaTestReference) or matCfg.AlphaTestReference or "")
        local scaleU = tostring((sCfg and sCfg.TextureScaleU) or matCfg.TextureScaleU or 1)
        local scaleV = tostring((sCfg and sCfg.TextureScaleV) or matCfg.TextureScaleV or 1)
        local tint = tostring((sCfg and sCfg.TintTexture) or matCfg.TintTexture or false)

        return table.concat({
            tostring(useModTexture),
            axle or "generic",
            shader,
            materialPath,
            baseTexture,
            alphaTest,
            alphaRef,
            scaleU,
            scaleV,
            tint
        }, "|"), baseTexture, materialPath, shader, useModTexture
    end

    local function loadMaterial(surfaceId, sCfg, axle)
        local cacheKey, baseTexture, materialPath, shader, useModTexture = buildMaterialCacheKey(sCfg, axle)
        if Materials[cacheKey] then
            MaterialUsesCustomTexture[surfaceId] = MaterialUsesCustomTexture[cacheKey]
            return Materials[cacheKey], cacheKey
        end

        if useModTexture == false and materialPath ~= "" then
            local ok, m = pcall(function() return material.load(materialPath) end)
            Materials[cacheKey] = ok and m or nil
            MaterialUsesCustomTexture[cacheKey] = false
            MaterialUsesCustomTexture[surfaceId] = false
            return Materials[cacheKey], cacheKey
        end

        local okMat, mat = pcall(function() return material.create(shader) end)
        if not okMat or not mat then return nil, cacheKey end

        mat:setInt("$model", 1)
        mat:setInt("$ignorez", 0)
        mat:setInt("$vertexcolor", 1)
        mat:setInt("$vertexalpha", 1)
        mat:setInt("$translucent", 1)
        local useAlphaTest = (sCfg and sCfg.AlphaTest) or matCfg.AlphaTest
        mat:setInt("$alphatest", useAlphaTest and 1 or 0)
        local alphaRef = (sCfg and sCfg.AlphaTestReference) or matCfg.AlphaTestReference
        if alphaRef then
            mat:setFloat("$alphatestreference", alphaRef)
        end

        if string.find(baseTexture, "https://", 1, true) or string.find(baseTexture, "http://", 1, true) then
            if string.find(baseTexture, "github.com/", 1, true) then
                baseTexture = string.gsub(baseTexture, "https://github.com/", "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/", 1)
                baseTexture = string.gsub(baseTexture, "http://github.com/", "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/", 1)
                baseTexture = string.gsub(baseTexture, "/blob/", "/", 1)
                baseTexture = string.gsub(baseTexture, "/raw/", "/", 1)
            end
            mat:setTexture("$basetexture", "vgui/white")
            local webMode = (sCfg and sCfg.WebLoadMode) or matCfg.WebLoadMode or "stream"
            local function applyStreamURL()
                if mat.setTextureURL then
                    mat:setTextureURL("$basetexture", baseTexture, function(_, _, w, h, layout)
                        if not w or not h then
                            print("[SimcadeSkid] Failed to load texture URL: " .. baseTexture)
                            return
                        end
                        local sx, sy = 1024 / w, 1024 / h
                        layout(0, 0, w * sx, h * sy)
                    end)
                end
            end
            local canTemp = (webMode == "download") and http and http.get and file and file.writeTemp
            if canTemp and hasPermission then
                local okP, resP = pcall(hasPermission, "file.writeTemp")
                if okP and not resP then canTemp = false end
            end
            if canTemp then
                local ext = string.match(baseTexture, "%.([a-zA-Z0-9]+)%?") or string.match(baseTexture, "%.([a-zA-Z0-9]+)$") or "png"
                local fname = "simcade_skid_" .. tostring(math.abs(#baseTexture * 131 + (string.byte(baseTexture, 1) or 1))) .. "." .. ext
                pcall(http.get, baseTexture, function(data)
                    if data and data ~= "" then
                        local okW, tempPath = pcall(file.writeTemp, fname, data)
                        if okW and tempPath then
                            pcall(function() mat:setTexture("$basetexture", "../data/" .. tempPath) end)
                            return
                        end
                    end
                    applyStreamURL()
                end, applyStreamURL)
            else
                applyStreamURL()
            end
            MaterialUsesCustomTexture[cacheKey] = true
            MaterialUsesCustomTexture[surfaceId] = true
        else
            local sourceMaterial = material.load and material.load(baseTexture) or nil
            if sourceMaterial and sourceMaterial.getTexture and sourceMaterial:getTexture("$basetexture") then
                mat:setTexture("$basetexture", sourceMaterial:getTexture("$basetexture"))
                MaterialUsesCustomTexture[cacheKey] = false
                MaterialUsesCustomTexture[surfaceId] = false
            else
                mat:setTexture("$basetexture", baseTexture)
                MaterialUsesCustomTexture[cacheKey] = (baseTexture ~= "vgui/white" and baseTexture ~= "models/debug/debugwhite")
                MaterialUsesCustomTexture[surfaceId] = MaterialUsesCustomTexture[cacheKey]
            end
        end

        if Matrix and mat.setMatrix then
            local texMatrix = Matrix()
            texMatrix:setScale(Vector(
                (sCfg and sCfg.TextureScaleU) or matCfg.TextureScaleU or 1,
                (sCfg and sCfg.TextureScaleV) or matCfg.TextureScaleV or 1,
                1
            ))
            mat:setMatrix("$basetexturetransform", texMatrix)
        end

        Materials[cacheKey] = mat
        return mat, cacheKey
    end

    local function shouldTintTexture(sCfg)
        if sCfg and sCfg.TintTexture ~= nil then return sCfg.TintTexture end
        if matCfg.TintTexture ~= nil then return matCfg.TintTexture end
        return true
    end

    local function mapSegmentUV(u, v, sCfg)
        local rot = ((sCfg and sCfg.TextureRotationDeg) or matCfg.TextureRotationDeg or 0) % 360
        if rot < 0 then rot = rot + 360 end

        local outU, outV = u, v
        if rot == 90 then
            outU, outV = v, 1 - u
        elseif rot == 180 then
            outU, outV = 1 - u, 1 - v
        elseif rot == 270 then
            outU, outV = 1 - v, u
        end

        if (sCfg and sCfg.FlipTextureU) or matCfg.FlipTextureU then
            outU = 1 - outU
        end
        if (sCfg and sCfg.FlipTextureV) or matCfg.FlipTextureV then
            outV = 1 - outV
        end

        return outU, outV
    end

    local function baseSurfaceCfg(surfId)
        local sCfg = surfacesCfg[surfId] or surfacesCfg[0] or surfacesCfg[88]
        if sCfg then return sCfg end
        if surfId == 68 or surfId == 78 then
            return { CreateMarks = true, SkidColor = Color(85, 62, 42, 180) }
        elseif surfId == 85 then
            return { CreateMarks = true, SkidColor = Color(52, 74, 34, 150) }
        elseif surfId == 71 then
            return { CreateMarks = false }
        end
        return { CreateMarks = true, SkidColor = Color(18, 18, 20, 200) }
    end

    local axleSurfaces = { front = {}, rear = {} }
    local function resolveSurfaceCfg(surfId, axle)
        local overrides = axle == "rear" and SKID.Rear or nil
        if axle == "front" then overrides = SKID.Front end
        if not overrides then return baseSurfaceCfg(surfId) end
        local cached = axleSurfaces[axle][surfId]
        if cached then return cached end
        local settings = {}
        for k, v in pairs(baseSurfaceCfg(surfId)) do settings[k] = v end
        for k, v in pairs(overrides) do settings[k] = v end
        if overrides.Texture then settings[axle == "rear" and "RearTexture" or "FrontTexture"] = overrides.Texture end
        axleSurfaces[axle][surfId] = settings
        return settings
    end

    local holoPool = {}
    local activeQuads = {}
    local prevWheelState = {}
    local maxQuads     = math.min(SKID.MaxQuads or SKID.maxSegments or TIRE.MaxSkidQuads or 96, SKID.HologramLimit or 180)
    local quadLifetime = SKID.Lifetime or SKID.lifetime or TIRE.SkidLifetime or 10.0
    local skidThresh   = SKID.SkidThreshold or SKID.threshold or TIRE.SkidThreshold or 0.16
    local opacityExp = math.max(0.05, SKID.OpacityExp or 1)
    local skidRelease  = SKID.SkidReleaseThreshold or (skidThresh * 0.55)
    local minSegLen    = SKID.MinSegmentLength or 8.0
    local worldUVScale = SKID.TextureWorldScale or 64.0
    local surfaceLift  = SKID.SurfaceLift or 0.08
    local widthMult    = SKID.WidthMult or 1.0
    local frontWMult   = SKID.FrontWheelWidthMul or 1.0
    local rearWMult    = SKID.RearWheelWidthMul or 1.0

    local function acquireHolo()
        if #holoPool > 0 then
            return table.remove(holoPool)
        end
        if #activeQuads >= maxQuads then
            local old = table.remove(activeQuads, 1)
            if old then
                if old.mesh and old.mesh.destroy then pcall(function() old.mesh:destroy() end) end
                if old.holo and old.holo:isValid() then return old.holo end
            end
        end
        if hologram and hologram.create then
            local ok, h = pcall(function()
                return hologram.create(Vector(), Angle(), "models/Combine_Helicopter/helicopter_bomb01.mdl")
            end)
            return ok and h or nil
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function addSkidQuad(pStart, pEnd, normal, halfWidth, col, sCfg, axle, surfId, prevEdge, uStart, uEnd)
        if not (mesh and mesh.createFromTable) then return nil end
        if mesh.trianglesLeft and mesh.trianglesLeft() < 12 then return nil end

        local mat, cacheKey = loadMaterial(surfId, sCfg, axle)
        if not mat then return nil end

        local delta = pEnd - pStart
        local len = delta:getLength()
        if len < 1.5 or len > 220 then return nil end

        local fwd = delta / len
        local right = normal:cross(fwd):getNormalized()
        if right:getLengthSqr() < 0.01 then return nil end

        local lift = normal * surfaceLift
        local useCustomTex = MaterialUsesCustomTexture[cacheKey] == true
        local overlapMul = useCustomTex and (SKID.TexturedSegmentOverlapMul or 0.0) or (SKID.SegmentOverlapMul or 0.0)

        local p1, p2
        if prevEdge then
            -- Continuous edge-stitching: reuse previous end vertices so segments join with zero gap and zero overlap!
            p1 = prevEdge.left
            p2 = prevEdge.right
        else
            local sPos = pStart - fwd * (halfWidth * overlapMul) + lift
            p1 = sPos + right * halfWidth
            p2 = sPos - right * halfWidth
        end

        local ePos = pEnd + fwd * (halfWidth * overlapMul) + lift
        local p4 = ePos + right * halfWidth
        local p3 = ePos - right * halfWidth

        local center = (p1 + p2 + p3 + p4) * 0.25
        local lp1, lp2, lp3, lp4 = p1 - center, p2 - center, p3 - center, p4 - center

        local u1, v1 = mapSegmentUV(uStart, 0, sCfg)
        local u2, v2 = mapSegmentUV(uStart, 1, sCfg)
        local u3, v3 = mapSegmentUV(uEnd, 1, sCfg)
        local u4, v4 = mapSegmentUV(uEnd, 0, sCfg)

        local vCol = Color(255, 255, 255, 255)
        local okMesh, qMesh = pcall(function()
            return mesh.createFromTable({
                { pos = lp1, normal = normal, u = u1, v = v1, color = vCol },
                { pos = lp2, normal = normal, u = u2, v = v2, color = vCol },
                { pos = lp3, normal = normal, u = u3, v = v3, color = vCol },
                { pos = lp3, normal = normal, u = u3, v = v3, color = vCol },
                { pos = lp4, normal = normal, u = u4, v = v4, color = vCol },
                { pos = lp1, normal = normal, u = u1, v = v1, color = vCol },
                { pos = lp1, normal = -normal, u = u1, v = v1, color = vCol },
                { pos = lp4, normal = -normal, u = u4, v = v4, color = vCol },
                { pos = lp3, normal = -normal, u = u3, v = v3, color = vCol },
                { pos = lp3, normal = -normal, u = u3, v = v3, color = vCol },
                { pos = lp2, normal = -normal, u = u2, v = v2, color = vCol },
                { pos = lp1, normal = -normal, u = u1, v = v1, color = vCol },
            })
        end)
        if not okMesh or not qMesh then return nil end

        local holo = acquireHolo()
        if not (holo and holo:isValid()) then
            pcall(function() qMesh:destroy() end)
            return nil
        end

        local minB = Vector(math.min(lp1.x, lp2.x, lp3.x, lp4.x), math.min(lp1.y, lp2.y, lp3.y, lp4.y), math.min(lp1.z, lp2.z, lp3.z, lp4.z)) - Vector(1, 1, 1)
        local maxB = Vector(math.max(lp1.x, lp2.x, lp3.x, lp4.x), math.max(lp1.y, lp2.y, lp3.y, lp4.y), math.max(lp1.z, lp2.z, lp3.z, lp4.z)) + Vector(1, 1, 1)

        holo:setPos(center)
        holo:setAngles(Angle(0, 0, 0))
        holo:setScale(Vector(1, 1, 1))
        holo:setMesh(qMesh)
        holo:setMeshMaterial(mat)
        holo:setRenderBounds(minB, maxB)
        if shouldTintTexture(sCfg) then
            holo:setColor(col)
        else
            holo:setColor(Color(255, 255, 255, col.a))
        end
        holo:suppressEngineLighting(true)
        holo:setNoDraw(false)

        activeQuads[#activeQuads + 1] = {
            holo = holo,
            mesh = qMesh,
            born = timer.curtime(),
        }

        return { left = p4, right = p3 }
    end

    local pendingSkids = {}
    local nextSkidTick = 0
    local skidInterval = math.min(0.015, SimcadeCore.networkInterval(cfg.VEHICLE_CFG))

    local function isCpuHot(limit)
        local qMax = quotaMax and quotaMax() or 0
        if qMax <= 0 then return false end
        if quotaUsed and (quotaUsed() / qMax) > limit then return true end
        if quotaAverage and (quotaAverage() / qMax) > limit then return true end
        return false
    end

    local predictedSample
    local usingPrediction = false
    local function trackWheel(i, pos, norm, skid, surfId, width, isRear)
        local axle = isRear and "rear" or "front"
        local sCfg = resolveSurfaceCfg(surfId, axle)
        if surfId == 0 or sCfg.CreateMarks == false then
            prevWheelState[i] = nil
            return
        end

        local st = prevWheelState[i]
        local entry = sCfg.SkidThreshold or skidThresh
        local release = math.min(entry, sCfg.SkidReleaseThreshold or skidRelease)
        local active = skid >= (st and release or entry)
        if active then
            if not st then
                prevWheelState[i] = { pos = pos, u = 0, edge = nil, surfId = surfId }
            else
                local dist = pos:getDistance(st.pos)
                if dist >= minSegLen and dist <= 350 then
                    local uStart = st.u or 0
                    local uEnd = uStart + (dist / worldUVScale)
                    if #pendingSkids < 32 then
                        pendingSkids[#pendingSkids + 1] = {
                            wheelIdx = i,
                            prev     = st.pos,
                            pos      = pos,
                            norm     = norm,
                            skid     = skid,
                            surfId   = surfId,
                            width    = width,
                            axle     = axle,
                            uStart   = uStart,
                            uEnd     = uEnd,
                            resetEdge = (st.surfId ~= surfId),
                        }
                    end
                    st.pos = pos
                    st.u = uEnd
                    st.surfId = surfId
                elseif dist > 350 then
                    st.pos = pos
                    st.u = 0
                    st.edge = nil
                    st.surfId = surfId
                end
            end
        else
            prevWheelState[i] = nil
        end
    end

    function SimcadeSkidmarks.sampleWheel(i, pos, normal, skid, material, width, rear)
        if not usingPrediction then trackWheel(i, pos, normal, skid, material, width, rear) end
    end

    hook.add("think", "SimcadeSkidmarks_Update", function()
        local now = timer.curtime()
        if now < nextSkidTick then return end
        nextSkidTick = math.max(nextSkidTick + skidInterval, now)
        if isCpuHot(0.78) then return end

        local sample = SimcadePrediction and SimcadePrediction.getTireEffects and SimcadePrediction.getTireEffects()
        if usingPrediction ~= (sample ~= nil)
            or (sample and predictedSample and sample.base ~= predictedSample.base) then
            usingPrediction = sample ~= nil
            prevWheelState, pendingSkids = {}, {}
        end
        if sample and sample ~= predictedSample then
            for i, wheel in ipairs(sample.wheels) do
                trackWheel(i, wheel.pos, wheel.norm, wheel.skid, wheel.matType or 0, wheel.width, wheel.isRear)
            end
        end
        predictedSample = sample

        local cleaned = 0
        for i = #activeQuads, 1, -1 do
            local q = activeQuads[i]
            if (now - q.born) > quadLifetime then
                if q.mesh and q.mesh.destroy then pcall(function() q.mesh:destroy() end) end
                if q.holo and q.holo:isValid() then
                    q.holo:setNoDraw(true)
                    holoPool[#holoPool + 1] = q.holo
                end
                table.remove(activeQuads, i)
                cleaned = cleaned + 1
                if cleaned >= 4 then break end
            end
        end

        local spawned = 0
        while #pendingSkids > 0 and spawned < 8 do
            if isCpuHot(0.78) then break end
            local item = table.remove(pendingSkids, 1)
            local sCfg = resolveSurfaceCfg(item.surfId, item.axle)
            local baseCol = sCfg.SkidColor or Color(18, 18, 20, 200)
            local strength = (clamp(item.skid, 0, 1) ^ math.max(0.05, sCfg.OpacityExp or opacityExp)) * (sCfg.StrengthMul or 1.0)
            local alpha = math.floor(clamp(strength, 0, 1) * (baseCol.a or 200))
            local col = Color(baseCol.r, baseCol.g, baseCol.b, alpha)

            local axleMul = (item.axle == "rear") and rearWMult or frontWMult
            local halfW = math.max(3.0, (item.width or 9) * 0.5 * widthMult * axleMul * (sCfg.SkidWidthMul or 1.0))

            local st = prevWheelState[item.wheelIdx]
            local prevEdge = (st and not item.resetEdge) and st.edge or nil
            local newEdge = addSkidQuad(item.prev, item.pos, item.norm, halfW, col, sCfg, item.axle, item.surfId, prevEdge, item.uStart, item.uEnd)
            if st then
                st.edge = newEdge
            end
            if SimcadeAddons and SimcadeAddons._clientHost then
                SimcadeAddons._clientHost.runHook("OnSkidQuad", item, col, halfW)
            end
            spawned = spawned + 1
        end
    end)

    hook.add("removed", "SimcadeSkidmarks_Cleanup", function()
        for _, q in ipairs(activeQuads) do
            if q.mesh and q.mesh.destroy then pcall(function() q.mesh:destroy() end) end
            if q.holo and q.holo:isValid() then pcall(function() q.holo:remove() end) end
        end
        for _, h in ipairs(holoPool) do
            if h and h:isValid() then pcall(function() h:remove() end) end
        end
        activeQuads = {}
        holoPool = {}
    end)
end
12832
-- name Simcade Particle Effects Module (Smoke, Dirt, Grass, Snow - Skid & Roll)
-- shared

SimcadeEffects = SimcadeEffects or {}

if SERVER then return end

local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b) return v < a and a or (v > b and b or v) end

function SimcadeEffects.initClient(cfg)
    local FX = cfg.EFFECT_CFG or {}
    local TIRE = cfg.TIRE_CFG or {}
    if TIRE.EnableParticles == false or FX.enabled == false then return end

    local emitter = nil
    local emitterOrigin = nil
    local permWarned = false
    local nextFxTick = 0
    local latestWheels = {}
    local lastCarFwd = Vector(1, 0, 0)
    local lastCarUp  = Vector(0, 0, 1)
    local wheelEmitStamp = {}
    local surfacesCfg = FX.Surfaces or {}

    local defaultSmokePaths = FX.SmokeMaterials or { FX.SmokeMaterial or "particle/particle_smokegrenade", "particle/particle_smokegrenade1" }
    local defaultDirtPaths  = FX.DirtMaterials  or { FX.DirtMaterial  or "particle/particle_smokegrenade", "particle/particle_smokegrenade1" }
    local defaultGrassPaths = FX.GrassMaterials or { "particle/debris2/debris_2", "particle/particle_smokegrenade" }
    local matCache = {}

    local function loadFxMat(path)
        if not path or path == "" then return nil end
        if matCache[path] ~= nil then return matCache[path] end
        local ok, m = pcall(function() return material.load(path) end)
        matCache[path] = (ok and m) or false
        return matCache[path]
    end

    local function pickRandomMat(paths, fallbackPaths)
        local list = (type(paths) == "table" and #paths > 0) and paths or fallbackPaths
        if type(list) == "string" then list = { list } end
        if type(list) ~= "table" or #list == 0 then return nil end
        local tries = #list
        local startIdx = math.random(1, tries)
        for k = 0, tries - 1 do
            local idx = ((startIdx + k - 1) % tries) + 1
            local m = loadFxMat(list[idx])
            if m then return m end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local DEFAULT_SKID_SMOKE = {
        enabled = true, spawnOnSkid = true,
        path = defaultSmokePaths, color = Color(255, 255, 255), affectByLight = false,
        startAt = 0.14, maxAt = 1.0,
        sizeStart = 30, sizeEnd = 120, alphaStart = 255, alphaEnd = 0,
        multMin = 0.9, multMax = 1.1,
        velocityMult = Vector(0.1, 1.0, 0.01), gravity = Vector(0, 0, 20),
        airResistance = 50, ttl = 5.0, interval = 0.10,
    }

    local DEFAULT_SURFACES = {
        [68] = { -- MAT_DIRT (wheels_fx roaddust)
            roll = {
                enabled = true, spawnOnSkid = false, path = defaultDirtPaths,
                color = Color(189, 157, 121), affectByLight = false,
                startAt = 20, maxAt = 800, sizeStart = 20, sizeEnd = 300,
                alphaStart = 255, alphaEnd = 0, multMin = 0.9, multMax = 1.1,
                velocityMult = Vector(0.1, 1.0, 0.01), gravity = Vector(0, 0, 20),
                airResistance = 50, ttl = 2.0, interval = 0.07,
            },
        },
        [71] = { enabled = false }, -- MAT_GRATE
        [74] = { -- MAT_SNOW (wheels_fx snow)
            roll = {
                enabled = true, spawnOnSkid = false, path = defaultSmokePaths,
                color = Color(255, 255, 255), affectByLight = false,
                startAt = 60, maxAt = 1800, sizeStart = 20, sizeEnd = 200,
                alphaStart = 255, alphaEnd = 0, multMin = 0.9, multMax = 1.1,
                velocityMult = Vector(0.1, 1.0, 0.1), gravity = Vector(0, 0, 60),
                airResistance = 80, ttl = 2.0, interval = 0.07,
            },
        },
        [78] = { -- MAT_SAND (wheels_fx roaddust)
            roll = {
                enabled = true, spawnOnSkid = false, path = defaultDirtPaths,
                color = Color(189, 157, 121), affectByLight = false,
                startAt = 20, maxAt = 800, sizeStart = 20, sizeEnd = 300,
                alphaStart = 255, alphaEnd = 0, multMin = 0.9, multMax = 1.1,
                velocityMult = Vector(0.1, 1.0, 0.01), gravity = Vector(0, 0, 20),
                airResistance = 50, ttl = 2.0, interval = 0.07,
            },
        },
        [85] = { -- MAT_GRASS (wheels_fx grass)
            roll = {
                enabled = true, spawnOnSkid = false, path = { "particle/debris2/debris_2" },
                color = Color(100, 120, 70), affectByLight = true,
                startAt = 200, maxAt = 800, sizeStart = 10, sizeEnd = 10,
                alphaStart = 255, alphaEnd = 255, multMin = 0.5, multMax = 1.1,
                velocityMult = Vector(0.1, 1.0, 0.1), gravity = Vector(0, 0, -600),
                airResistance = 5, ttl = 2.0, interval = 0.05,
            },
        },
    }

    local function resolveSurfaceCfg(matType)
        local sCfg = surfacesCfg[matType] or DEFAULT_SURFACES[matType] or surfacesCfg[0] or surfacesCfg[88]
        if sCfg then return sCfg end
        return { enabled = true, skid = DEFAULT_SKID_SMOKE }
    end

    local function isCpuHot(limit)
        local qMax = quotaMax and quotaMax() or 0
        if qMax <= 0 then return false end
        if quotaUsed and (quotaUsed() / qMax) > limit then return true end
        if quotaAverage and (quotaAverage() / qMax) > limit then return true end
        return false
    end

    local skidEffectExp = math.max(0.05, FX.skidEffectExp or 1)
    local fxThresh = FX.smokeThreshold or 0.14
    local emitInterval = math.max(0.015, FX.emitInterval or 0.025)
    local maxPerBatch = FX.maxParticlesPerBatch or 12

    function SimcadeEffects.sampleWheel(i, pos, skid, matType, rimSpeed, grounded, now, fwd, up)
        lastCarFwd, lastCarUp = fwd, up
        latestWheels[i] = {pos = pos, skid = skid, matType = matType, rimSpeed = rimSpeed, grounded = grounded, time = now}
    end

    local function ensureEmitter(anchorPos)
        if hasPermission and not hasPermission("particle.create") then
            if not permWarned then
                permWarned = true
                print("[SimcadeEffects] Particle creation permission not granted (particle.create).")
            end
            return nil
        end
        if not (particle and particle.create) then return nil end
        if emitter and emitterOrigin and emitterOrigin:getDistance(anchorPos) > 2048 then
            if emitter.destroy then pcall(function() emitter:destroy() end) end
            emitter = nil
