        end
        if not emitter then
            local ok, em = pcall(function() return particle.create(anchorPos, false) end)
            if ok and em then
                emitter = em
                emitterOrigin = anchorPos
            elseif not permWarned then
                permWarned = true
                print("[SimcadeEffects] particle.create failed: " .. tostring(em))
            end
        elseif emitter.setPos then
            pcall(function() emitter:setPos(anchorPos) end)
        end
        return emitter
    end

    local function spawnLayer(em, item, layerCfg, isRollLayer, now, key, carFwd, carUp)
        if not layerCfg or layerCfg.enabled == false then return false end
        local interval = layerCfg.interval or emitInterval
        local lastT = wheelEmitStamp[key] or 0
        if (now - lastT) < interval then return false end

        local strength = 0
        if isRollLayer then
            local startAt = layerCfg.startAt or 60
            local maxAt   = math.max(startAt + 1, layerCfg.maxAt or 700)
            if item.rimSpeed < startAt then return false end
            strength = clamp((item.rimSpeed - startAt) / (maxAt - startAt), 0.1, 1.0)
        else
            local startAt = layerCfg.startAt or fxThresh
            local maxAt   = math.max(startAt + 0.05, layerCfg.maxAt or 1.0)
            if item.skid < startAt then return false end
            strength = clamp((item.skid - startAt) / (maxAt - startAt), 0, 1) ^ skidEffectExp
        end

        if em.getParticlesLeft and em:getParticlesLeft() == 0 then return false end

        local fallback = isRollLayer and defaultDirtPaths or defaultSmokePaths
        local pMat = pickRandomMat(layerCfg.path, fallback)
        if not pMat then return false end

        local rMin = layerCfg.multMin or 0.85
        local rMax = layerCfg.multMax or 1.15
        local rMult = (rMin == rMax) and rMin or (rMin + math.random() * (rMax - rMin))

        local sizeStart  = math.max(2, (layerCfg.sizeStart or 12) * (0.5 + strength * 0.7) * rMult)
        local sizeEnd    = math.max(sizeStart, (layerCfg.sizeEnd or 48) * (0.5 + strength * 0.7) * rMult)
        local startA     = math.floor(clamp((layerCfg.alphaStart or 160) * (0.4 + strength * 0.6), 20, 255))
        local endA       = layerCfg.alphaEnd or 0
        local ttl        = math.max(0.15, (layerCfg.ttl or 1.0) * (0.7 + strength * 0.4) * rMult)
        local col        = layerCfg.color or Color(235, 235, 235)
        local grav       = layerCfg.gravity or Vector(0, 0, 20)
        local airRes     = layerCfg.airResistance or 50
        local vm         = layerCfg.velocityMult or Vector(-0.35, 0.15, 0.06)

        local latSide = carFwd:cross(carUp):getNormalized()
        local baseSpd = math.max(60, item.rimSpeed)
        local backMult = (vm.x < 0) and (-vm.x) or math.max(0.28, vm.x)
        local latMult  = math.min(0.18, math.abs(vm.y) * 0.15)
        local vel = (-carFwd * (backMult * baseSpd) + latSide * (latMult * baseSpd * (math.random() * 2 - 1)) + carUp * (vm.z * baseSpd)) * rMult

        local okSpawn = pcall(function()
            local p = em:add(pMat, item.pos + carUp * 3, sizeStart, sizeEnd, 0, 0, startA, endA, ttl)
            if p then
                if p.setStartSize then p:setStartSize(sizeStart) end
                if p.setEndSize then p:setEndSize(sizeEnd) end
                if p.setStartAlpha then p:setStartAlpha(startA) end
                if p.setEndAlpha then p:setEndAlpha(endA) end
                if p.setCollide then p:setCollide(true) end
                if p.setRoll then p:setRoll(math.random(0, 360)) end
                if p.setRollDelta then p:setRollDelta((math.random() * 2 - 1) * 1.0) end
                if p.setLighting then p:setLighting(layerCfg.affectByLight == true) end
                if p.setColor then p:setColor(col) end
                if p.setVelocity then p:setVelocity(vel) end
                if p.setGravity then p:setGravity(grav) end
                if p.setAirResistance then p:setAirResistance(airRes) end
            end
        end)

        if okSpawn then
            wheelEmitStamp[key] = now
            if SimcadeAddons and SimcadeAddons._clientHost then
                SimcadeAddons._clientHost.runHook("OnEffectEmit", item, layerCfg, isRollLayer)
            end
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    hook.add("think", "SimcadeEffects_Update", function()
        local now = timer.curtime()
        if now < nextFxTick then return end
        nextFxTick = now + emitInterval
        if isCpuHot(0.78) then return end

        local sample = SimcadePrediction and SimcadePrediction.getTireEffects and SimcadePrediction.getTireEffects()
        local wheels = sample and sample.wheels or latestWheels
        local carFwd, carUp = sample and sample.fwd or lastCarFwd, sample and sample.up or lastCarUp
        local anchorPos = nil
        for i = 1, #wheels do
            local w = wheels[i]
            if w and w.grounded and (now - w.time) <= 0.25 and (w.skid >= fxThresh or w.rimSpeed >= 40) then
                anchorPos = w.pos
                break
            end
        end
        if not anchorPos then return end

        local em = ensureEmitter(anchorPos)
        if not em then return end

        local emitted = 0
        for i = 1, #wheels do
            if emitted >= maxPerBatch then break end
            local item = wheels[i]
            if item and item.grounded and (now - item.time) <= 0.25 then
                local sCfg = resolveSurfaceCfg(item.matType)
                if sCfg.enabled ~= false then
                    local skidLayer = sCfg.skid or (sCfg.path and sCfg) or ((not sCfg.roll) and DEFAULT_SKID_SMOKE) or nil
                    local rollLayer = sCfg.roll
                    if skidLayer and spawnLayer(em, item, skidLayer, false, now, "s" .. i, carFwd, carUp) then
                        emitted = emitted + 1
                    end
                    if rollLayer and emitted < maxPerBatch and spawnLayer(em, item, rollLayer, true, now, "r" .. i, carFwd, carUp) then
                        emitted = emitted + 1
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    hook.add("removed", "SimcadeEffects_Cleanup", function()
        if emitter and emitter.destroy then pcall(function() emitter:destroy() end) end
        emitter = nil
    end)
end
34465
-- name Simcade HUD Module (HUD & 3D Wheel Telemetry)
-- shared

SimcadeHUD = SimcadeHUD or {}

-- A reliable wheel page carries absolute field values only where they changed.
-- Baselines are per wheel and are committed by the server after a successful send.
function SimcadeHUD.wheelFields(previous, writing)
    local values, index = {}, 0
    local io = {}
    local function field(value, write, read, bits)
        index = index + 1
        local old
        if previous then old = previous[index] end
        if writing then
            local changed = value ~= old
            net.writeBool(changed)
            if changed then
                if bits then write(value, bits) else write(value) end
            end
        elseif net.readBool() then
            if bits then value = read(bits) else value = read() end
        else
            value = old
        end
        values[index] = value
        return value
    end
    function io.int(v, bits) return field(v, net.writeInt, net.readInt, bits) end
    function io.uint(v, bits) return field(v, net.writeUInt, net.readUInt, bits) end
    function io.bool(v) return field(v, net.writeBool, net.readBool) end
    function io.float(v) return field(v, net.writeFloat, net.readFloat) end
    function io.point(v)
        local function coordinate(x)
            return io.int(x and math.clamp(math.round(x * 8), -32768, 32767), 16) / 8
        end
        local x, y, z = coordinate(v and v.x), coordinate(v and v.y), coordinate(v and v.z)
        if not writing then return Vector(x, y, z) end
    end
    function io.direction(v)
        local function coordinate(x)
            return io.int(x and math.clamp(math.round(x * 127), -127, 127), 8)
        end
        local x, y, z = coordinate(v and v.x), coordinate(v and v.y), coordinate(v and v.z)
        if not writing then return Vector(x, y, z):getNormalized() end
    end
    return io, values
end

if SERVER then return end

local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b) return v < a and a or (v > b and b or v) end

local function readDirection()
    return Vector(net.readInt(8), net.readInt(8), net.readInt(8)):getNormalized()
end

local hudState = nil
function SimcadeHUD.getState()
    return hudState
end

function SimcadeHUD.initClient(cfg)
    local HUD  = cfg.HUD_CFG or {}
    local TIRE = cfg.TIRE_CFG or {}

    local wheelDebug = { base = nil, fwdLocal = Vector(1, 0, 0), rightLocal = Vector(0, -1, 0), wheels = {} }
    local wheelBaselines = {}
    local smoothTelemetry = {}
    local cachedPacejkaLat = nil
    local cachedPacejkaLong = nil
    local lastPacejkaKey = nil

    local specCompound, frontSpec, rearSpec

    local function evalPacejka(B, C, D, E, x)
        local bx = B * x
        return D * math.sin(C * math.atan(bx - E * (bx - math.atan(bx))))
    end

    local mathFont, smallFont
    if render and render.createFont then
        pcall(function()
            mathFont  = render.createFont("Roboto Mono", 15, 500, true)
            smallFont = render.createFont("Roboto Mono", 13, 600, true)
        end)
    end
    local function setFontSafe(f)
        if f and render.setFont then render.setFont(f) end
    end
    local function drawTextAligned(x, y, txt, ax, ay)
        if render.drawSimpleText then
            render.drawSimpleText(x, y, txt, ax or 0, ay or 0)
        else
            render.drawText(x, y, txt)
        end
    end
    local function drawOutlineSafe(x, y, w, h)
        if render.drawRectOutline then
            render.drawRectOutline(x, y, w, h)
        end
    end
    local function forceReading(kn)
        if math.abs(kn) < 0.005 then return "0.00 kN" end
        return string.format("%.2f kN %s", math.abs(kn), kn > 0 and "left" or "right")
    end

    local function placeWheelCard(points, i, sw, sh, width, height, driving)
        local p = points[i]
        local gap = 5
        if driving and #cfg.WHEELS <= 4 then
            -- Keep each corner in its own slot through suspension travel and contact changes.
            local inset = math.min(sw * 0.085, math.max(0, sw / 2 - width - 4))
            local x = sw / 2 + (p.wheel.isRightSide and inset or -inset - width)
            local top = clamp(sh * 0.69 - 155, 4, sh - 159)
            return x, top + (p.wheel.isFront and 0 or 80)
        end
        local x = clamp(p.x - width / 2, 4, sw - width - 4)
        local idealY = p.y + 18
        local y, bestScore
        local function tryY(candidate)
            candidate = clamp(candidate, 4, sh - height - 4)
            local overlap = 0
            for j = 1, i - 1 do
                local other = points[j]
                local dx = width + gap - math.abs(x - other.cardX)
                local dy = math.min(candidate + height, other.cardBottom) - math.max(candidate, other.cardY) + gap
                if dx > 0 and dy > 0 then overlap = overlap + dx * dy end
            end
            local score = overlap * sh + math.abs(candidate - idealY)
            if not bestScore or score < bestScore then y, bestScore = candidate, score end
        end
        tryY(idealY)
        for j = 1, i - 1 do
            local other = points[j]
            tryY(other.cardY - height - gap)
            tryY(other.cardBottom + gap)
        end
        return x, y
    end

    local function drawWheelCallouts(points, sw, sh, driving)
        local width = 216
        for i, p in ipairs(points) do
            local w = p.wheel
            local height = w.isGrounded and 75 or 25
            local x, y = placeWheelCard(points, i, sw, sh, width, height, driving)
            p.cardX, p.cardY, p.cardBottom = x, y, y + height
            local accent = w.isFront and Color(95, 220, 245) or Color(255, 186, 96)
            render.setColor(accent)
            render.drawLine(p.x, p.y, clamp(p.x, x, x + width), clamp(p.y, y, y + height))
            render.setColor(Color(16, 22, 29, 206))
            render.drawRect(x, y, width, height)
            render.setColor(accent)
            render.drawRect(x, y + 5, 2, 15)
            setFontSafe(mathFont)
            drawTextAligned(x + 8, y + 4, w.name, 0, 0)
            if not w.isGrounded then
                drawTextAligned(x + width - 8, y + 4, "No ground contact", 2, 0)
            else
                drawTextAligned(x + width - 8, y + 4, string.format("%.1f kN load", w.loadKN), 2, 0)
                setFontSafe(smallFont)
                render.setColor(Color(242, 245, 250))
                local lean = math.abs(w.camberDeg) < 0.05 and "upright" or string.format("%.1f° %s", math.abs(w.camberDeg), w.camberDeg < 0 and "in" or "out")
                drawTextAligned(x + 8, y + 23, "Camber " .. lean, 0, 0)
                drawTextAligned(x + width - 8, y + 23, string.format("Slip %.1f°", w.slipAngle), 2, 0)
                drawTextAligned(x + 8, y + 40, "Camber thrust", 0, 0)
                drawTextAligned(x + width - 8, y + 40, forceReading(w.camberFy), 2, 0)
                drawTextAligned(x + 8, y + 56, "Total lateral", 0, 0)
                drawTextAligned(x + width - 8, y + 56, forceReading(w.lateralFy), 2, 0)
            end
        end
    end
    local function drawCircleSafe(x, y, r)
        if render.drawFilledCircle then
            render.drawFilledCircle(x, y, r)
        else
            render.drawRect(x - r, y - r, r * 2, r * 2)
        end
    end

    net.receive("Simcade_StateSync", function()
        hudState = {
            base         = net.readEntity(),
            manual       = net.readBool(),
            gear         = net.readString(),
            rpm          = net.readFloat(),
            redline      = net.readFloat(),
            kmh          = net.readFloat(),
            compound     = net.readString(),
            halfThrottle = net.readBool(),
            clutchDown   = net.readBool(),
            ignition     = net.readUInt(2),
            running      = net.readBool(),
            steer        = net.readFloat(),
            pod          = net.readBool() and net.readEntity() or nil,
            wsEnabled    = net.readBool(),
            wsFrontPct   = net.readFloat(),
            wsLeftPct    = net.readFloat(),
            wsFwdG       = net.readFloat(),
            wsLatG       = net.readFloat(),
            wsVertMult   = net.readFloat(),
        }
    end)

    net.receive("Simcade_WheelDebug", function()
        wheelDebug.base       = net.readEntity()
        wheelDebug.fwdLocal   = readDirection()
        wheelDebug.rightLocal = readDirection()
        wheelDebug.comLocal = net.readVector()
        local hasRays = net.readBool()
        if net.readBool() then
            wheelBaselines, wheelDebug.wheels, smoothTelemetry = {}, {}, {}
        end
        local full = net.readBool()
        local first, count = net.readUInt(32), net.readUInt(4)
        if not full and not wheelBaselines[first] then return end
        local list = wheelDebug.wheels
        for i = first, first + count - 1 do
            local io, values = SimcadeHUD.wheelFields(not full and wheelBaselines[i], false)
            wheelBaselines[i] = values
            local hx = io.int(nil, 16) * 0.1
            local hy = io.int(nil, 16) * 0.1
            local hz = io.int(nil, 16) * 0.1
            list[i] = {
                hubLocal   = Vector(hx, hy, hz),
                latRatio   = io.int(nil, 16) * 0.001,
                longRatio  = io.int(nil, 16) * 0.001,
                slipAngle  = io.int(nil, 16) * 0.01,
                slipRatio  = io.int(nil, 16) * 0.001,
                isFront    = io.bool(),
                name       = (cfg.WHEELS[i] and cfg.WHEELS[i].name) or ("W" .. i),
                isGrounded = io.bool(),
                loadKN     = io.uint(nil, 16) * 0.01,
                camberDeg  = io.int(nil, 16) * 0.01,
                camberFy   = io.float(),
                lateralFy  = io.float(),
                isRightSide = io.bool(),
                axleLocal   = io.direction(),
                normalLocal = io.direction(),
            }
            if io.bool() then
                list[i].springTop = io.point()
                list[i].seatNear = io.uint(nil, 8) / 255
            end
            if hasRays then
                local debugRays = {}
                list[i].rays = debugRays
                local mount, axle = io.point(), io.direction()
                local up = wheelDebug.rightLocal:cross(wheelDebug.fwdLocal):getNormalized()
                local function readRay(start)
                    local state = io.uint(nil, 2)
                    if state == 3 then return end
                    local depth = io.uint(nil, 16) / 8
                    debugRays[#debugRays + 1] = { startLocal = start, hitLocal = start - up * depth, state = state }
                end
                if io.bool() then
                    local w = cfg.WHEELS[i]
                    SimcadeSuspension.eachTreadPoint(mount, up, axle, w.radius or 15, w.width or 9, readRay)
                else
                    readRay(mount)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    hook.add("drawhud", "SimcadeHUD_Draw", function()
        if HUD.enabled == false then return end
        local qMax = quotaMax and quotaMax() or 0
        if qMax > 0 then
            if quotaUsed and (quotaUsed() / qMax) > 0.80 then return end
            if quotaAverage and (quotaAverage() / qMax) > 0.80 then return end
        end
        local lp = player()
        if not (lp and lp:isValid()) then return end
        local st = hudState
        local inThisSeat = lp:inVehicle() and (not st or not st.pod or not st.pod:isValid() or not lp.getVehicle or lp:getVehicle() == st.pod)
        if lp ~= owner() and not inThisSeat then return end
        local sw, sh = render.getResolution()

        if HUD.showSpeedo ~= false and inThisSeat and st then
            local pred = SimcadePrediction and SimcadePrediction.get and SimcadePrediction.get(st.base)
            local kmh = pred and math.abs(pred.result.forward_ms) * 3.6 or st.kmh
            local rpm = pred and pred.result.flywheel_rpm or st.rpm
            local x = sw * 0.5 - 150
            local y = sh - 102

            render.setColor(Color(16, 18, 24, 215))
            render.drawRect(x, y, 300, 74)

            local ignCol = st.running and Color(80, 220, 120) or (st.ignition == 2 and Color(255, 195, 65) or Color(235, 75, 75))
            render.setColor(ignCol)
            render.drawRect(x, y, 4, 74)

            render.setColor(Color(235, 240, 250, 255))
            render.drawText(x + 12, y + 8, string.format("%d KM/H", math.floor(kmh or 0)))

            render.setColor(Color(255, 195, 65, 255))
            render.drawText(x + 108, y + 8, "G:" .. tostring(pred and pred.result.gearName or st.gear or "1"))

            render.setColor(Color(120, 200, 255, 255))
            render.drawText(x + 158, y + 8, string.upper(tostring(st.compound or "SPORT")) .. " [C]")

            render.setColor(st.manual and Color(255, 130, 80, 255) or Color(120, 220, 140, 255))
            render.drawText(x + 236, y + 8, st.manual and "MAN [M]" or "AUTO [M]")

            local rpmFrac = clamp((rpm or 0) / math.max(1000, st.redline or 8000), 0, 1)
            render.setColor(Color(40, 44, 54, 255))
            render.drawRect(x + 12, y + 52, 276, 12)
            render.setColor(rpmFrac > 0.88 and Color(255, 70, 70, 255) or Color(70, 185, 255, 255))
            render.drawRect(x + 12, y + 52, math.floor(276 * rpmFrac), 12)

            render.setColor(Color(200, 208, 220, 230))
            render.drawText(x + 12, y + 32, string.format("%d RPM", math.floor(rpm or 0)))
            render.setColor(st.halfThrottle and Color(120, 235, 175, 255) or Color(130, 140, 155, 190))
            render.drawText(x + 150, y + 32, st.halfThrottle and "1/2 THR" or "SHIFT:FULL")
            render.setColor(Color(170, 180, 195, 210))
            render.drawText(x + 230, y + 32, st.running and "IGN:ON" or (st.ignition == 2 and "CRANK" or "IGN:OFF"))
        end

        local canSeeDebug = (lp == owner()) or inThisSeat
        local scale = (sh and sh > 0) and (sh / 1080) or 1

        if canSeeDebug and #wheelDebug.wheels > 0 then
            for i, w in ipairs(wheelDebug.wheels) do
                smoothTelemetry[i] = smoothTelemetry[i] or { latRatio = 0, longRatio = 0, slipLat = 0, slipLong = 0 }
                local sm = smoothTelemetry[i]
                if w.isGrounded then
                    sm.latRatio  = sm.latRatio  + (math.abs(w.latRatio or 0) - sm.latRatio) * 0.4
                    sm.longRatio = sm.longRatio + (math.abs(w.longRatio or 0) - sm.longRatio) * 0.4
                    sm.slipLat   = sm.slipLat   + (math.abs(math.rad(w.slipAngle or 0)) - sm.slipLat) * 0.4
                    sm.slipLong  = sm.slipLong  + (math.abs(w.slipRatio or 0) - sm.slipLong) * 0.4
                else
                    sm.latRatio, sm.longRatio, sm.slipLat, sm.slipLong = 0, 0, 0, 0
                end
            end
        end

        local show3D = canSeeDebug and (TIRE.ShowTelemetryHUD ~= false) and ((TIRE.ShowTelemetry3D == true) or (TIRE.ShowTelemetry3D == nil and TIRE.ShowTireDebug == true))
        local baseEnt = wheelDebug.base
        if canSeeDebug and baseEnt and baseEnt:isValid() and cfg.SUSPENSION_CFG and cfg.SUSPENSION_CFG.showAxleDebug then
            local function line(a, b)
                local p, q = baseEnt:localToWorld(a):toScreen(), baseEnt:localToWorld(b):toScreen()
                if p.visible and q.visible then render.drawLine(p.x, p.y, q.x, q.y) end
            end
            local up = wheelDebug.rightLocal:cross(wheelDebug.fwdLocal):getNormalized()
            for _, left in ipairs(wheelDebug.wheels) do
                if left.springTop and not left.isRightSide then
                    for _, right in ipairs(wheelDebug.wheels) do
                        if right.springTop and right.isRightSide and right.isFront == left.isFront then
                            local l, r = left.hubLocal, right.hubLocal
                            local near = left.seatNear
                            render.setColor(Color(255, 182, 58))
                            line(l, r)
                            local mid = (l + r) * 0.5
                            line(mid - up * 2, mid + up * 2)
                            render.setColor(Color(95, 235, 155))
                            line(left.springTop, l * near + r * (1 - near))
                            line(right.springTop, r * near + l * (1 - near))
                            local label = baseEnt:localToWorld(mid + up * 3):toScreen()
                            if label.visible then
                                local roll = math.deg(math.atan2((l - r):dot(up), (r - l):dot(wheelDebug.rightLocal)))
                                setFontSafe(smallFont)
                                drawTextAligned(label.x, label.y, string.format("%s beam  Z %+.1f su  roll %+.1f°",
                                    left.isFront and "Front" or "Rear", (mid - wheelDebug.comLocal):dot(up), roll), 1, 1)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if show3D and baseEnt and baseEnt:isValid() and #wheelDebug.wheels > 0 then
            local callouts = TIRE.ShowCamberDebug and {} or nil
            local cPos = baseEnt:getPos()
            local liveFwd = baseEnt:localToWorldVector(wheelDebug.fwdLocal)
            local liveRight = baseEnt:localToWorldVector(wheelDebug.rightLocal)
            local liveUp = liveRight:cross(liveFwd):getNormalized()
            local sCenter = cPos:toScreen()
            local sFront  = (cPos + liveFwd * 65):toScreen()
            local sRight  = (cPos + liveRight * 45):toScreen()
            local sUp     = (cPos + liveUp * 45):toScreen()
            if sCenter.visible and sFront.visible then
                render.setColor(Color(60, 220, 255, 220))
                render.drawLine(sCenter.x, sCenter.y, sFront.x, sFront.y)
                setFontSafe(smallFont)
                drawTextAligned(sFront.x, sFront.y - 8 * scale, ">>> FRONT (FWD) >>>", 1, 1)
            end
            if sCenter.visible and sRight.visible then
                render.setColor(Color(255, 120, 80, 190))
                render.drawLine(sCenter.x, sCenter.y, sRight.x, sRight.y)
                setFontSafe(smallFont)
                drawTextAligned(sRight.x, sRight.y - 8 * scale, "RIGHT", 1, 1)
            end
            if sCenter.visible and sUp.visible then
                render.setColor(Color(90, 150, 255, 220))
                render.drawLine(sCenter.x, sCenter.y, sUp.x, sUp.y)
                setFontSafe(smallFont)
                drawTextAligned(sUp.x, sUp.y - 8 * scale, "UP", 1, 1)
            end

            for i, w in ipairs(wheelDebug.wheels) do
                local hub = w.hubLocal and baseEnt:localToWorld(w.hubLocal)
                local sPos = hub and hub:toScreen()
                if sPos and sPos.visible and callouts then
                    callouts[#callouts + 1] = { wheel = w, x = sPos.x, y = sPos.y }
                end
                if sPos and sPos.visible and not w.isGrounded then
                    setFontSafe(smallFont)
                    render.setColor(Color(225, 225, 225))
                    drawTextAligned(sPos.x, sPos.y, w.name .. " · air", 1, 1)
                elseif sPos and sPos.visible then
                    if callouts then
                        local axle = baseEnt:localToWorldVector(w.axleLocal)
                        local normal = baseEnt:localToWorldVector(w.normalLocal)
                        local wheelUp = normal - axle * normal:dot(axle)
                        if wheelUp:getLengthSqr() > 0.001 then
                            local roadTip = (hub + normal * 30):toScreen()
                            local tireTip = (hub + wheelUp:getNormalized() * 30):toScreen()
                            if roadTip.visible and tireTip.visible then
                                render.setColor(Color(245, 245, 245))
                                render.drawLine(sPos.x, sPos.y, roadTip.x, roadTip.y)
                                render.setColor(w.isFront and Color(95, 220, 245) or Color(255, 186, 96))
                                render.drawLine(sPos.x, sPos.y, tireTip.x, tireTip.y)
                                render.drawRect(tireTip.x - 2, tireTip.y - 2, 4, 4)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    local sm = smoothTelemetry[i] or {}
                    local barW = 10 * scale
                    local gap = 12 * scale
                    local barH = 44 * scale
                    local latX = sPos.x - barW - gap * 0.5 - 6 * scale
                    local longX = sPos.x + 6 * scale
                    local latR = clamp(sm.latRatio or w.latRatio or 0, 0, 1.5)
                    local longR = clamp(sm.longRatio or w.longRatio or 0, 0, 1.5)
                    local cLat = (latR >= 1.0 and Color(255, 40, 40)) or (latR > 0.85 and Color(255, 255, 0)) or Color(0, 255, 80)
                    local cLng = (longR >= 1.0 and Color(255, 40, 40)) or (longR > 0.85 and Color(255, 180, 0)) or Color(0, 200, 255)

                    render.setColor(Color(0, 0, 0, 200))
                    drawOutlineSafe(latX - 1, sPos.y - barH - 1, barW + 2, barH + 2)
                    drawOutlineSafe(longX - 1, sPos.y - barH - 1, barW + 2, barH + 2)
                    render.setColor(Color(0, 0, 0, 150))
                    render.drawRect(latX, sPos.y - barH, barW, barH)
                    render.drawRect(longX, sPos.y - barH, barW, barH)

                    local latFill = barH * math.min(1, latR)
                    local longFill = barH * math.min(1, longR)
                    render.setColor(cLat)
                    render.drawRect(latX, sPos.y - latFill, barW, latFill)
                    render.setColor(cLng)
                    render.drawRect(longX, sPos.y - longFill, barW, longFill)

                    setFontSafe(smallFont)
                    render.setColor(cLat)
                    drawTextAligned(latX + barW * 0.5, sPos.y - barH - 10 * scale, string.format("L:%d%%", math.floor(latR * 100)), 1, 1)
                    render.setColor(cLng)
                    drawTextAligned(longX + barW * 0.5, sPos.y - barH - 10 * scale, string.format("X:%d%%", math.floor(longR * 100)), 1, 1)

                    local axleTag = w.isFront and "[FRONT]" or "[REAR]"
                    local wName = (w.name and w.name ~= "") and w.name or ("W" .. i)
                    render.setColor(w.isFront and Color(80, 230, 255) or Color(255, 180, 80))
                    local label = TIRE.ShowCamberDebug and wName or string.format("%s %s %.0f° | %.1fkN", axleTag, wName, w.slipAngle or 0, w.loadKN or 0)
                    drawTextAligned(sPos.x, sPos.y + 10 * scale, label, 1, 1)
                end
            end
            if callouts then drawWheelCallouts(callouts, sw, sh, inThisSeat) end
        end

        local wsCfg = cfg.WEIGHT_SHIFT_CFG
        local showWS = canSeeDebug and (wsCfg == nil or wsCfg.ShowDebug ~= false) and (TIRE.ShowTelemetryHUD ~= false or (wsCfg and wsCfg.ShowDebug))
        if showWS and (st or #wheelDebug.wheels > 0) then
            local wx = 40 * scale
            local wy = sh - 454 * scale
            local ww = 268 * scale
            local wh = 94 * scale
            render.setColor(Color(12, 16, 24, 195))
            render.drawRect(wx, wy, ww, wh)
            render.setColor(Color(70, 90, 120, 220))
            drawOutlineSafe(wx, wy, ww, wh)

            setFontSafe(smallFont)
            local wsOn  = (st and st.wsEnabled ~= nil) and st.wsEnabled or (wheelDebug.wsEnabled ~= false)
            local fPct  = clamp((st and st.wsFrontPct) or wheelDebug.wsFrontPct or 50, 0, 100)
            local rPct  = 100 - fPct
            local lPct  = clamp((st and st.wsLeftPct) or wheelDebug.wsLeftPct or 50, 0, 100)
            local riPct = 100 - lPct
            local fwdG  = (st and st.wsFwdG) or wheelDebug.wsFwdG or 0
            local latG  = (st and st.wsLatG) or wheelDebug.wsLatG or 0
            local vertM = (st and st.wsVertMult) or 1.0
            render.setColor(wsOn and Color(110, 230, 160) or Color(180, 180, 180))
            drawTextAligned(wx + 8 * scale, wy + 13 * scale, wsOn and "Weight Shift [ON]" or "Weight Shift [OFF]", 0, 1)
            render.setColor(Color(230, 235, 245))
            drawTextAligned(wx + 8 * scale, wy + 33 * scale, string.format("F: %d%%   R: %d%%", math.floor(fPct + 0.5), math.floor(rPct + 0.5)), 0, 1)
            drawTextAligned(wx + 8 * scale, wy + 51 * scale, string.format("L: %d%%   R: %d%%", math.floor(lPct + 0.5), math.floor(riPct + 0.5)), 0, 1)
            render.setColor(Color(160, 195, 235))
            drawTextAligned(wx + 8 * scale, wy + 71 * scale, string.format("G: %.2fF / %.2fL", fwdG, latG), 0, 1)

            -- 2D Mass Center / Horizontal G-ball
            local bx = wx + ww - 76 * scale
            local by = wy + wh * 0.5
            local br = 26 * scale
            render.setColor(Color(35, 42, 55, 220))
            render.drawRect(bx - br, by - br, br * 2, br * 2)
            render.setColor(Color(90, 110, 140, 220))
            render.drawLine(bx - br, by, bx + br, by)
            render.drawLine(bx, by - br, bx, by + br)
            local dotX = bx + clamp((riPct - 50) / 50, -1, 1) * (br - 3 * scale)
            local dotY = by - clamp((fPct - 50) / 50, -1, 1) * (br - 3 * scale)
            render.setColor(Color(255, 190, 70))
            render.drawRect(dotX - 3 * scale, dotY - 3 * scale, 6 * scale, 6 * scale)

            -- Separate Vertical G / Slope Load Gauge (1.0g center = normal weight)
            local vx = wx + ww - 34 * scale
            local vy = by - br
            local vw = 12 * scale
            local vh = br * 2
            render.setColor(Color(35, 42, 55, 220))
            render.drawRect(vx, vy, vw, vh)
            render.setColor(Color(120, 140, 175, 220))
            render.drawLine(vx - 2 * scale, by, vx + vw + 2 * scale, by)
            local vFrac = clamp(vertM - 1.0, -1.0, 1.0)
            local vCol = (vertM >= 1.05 and Color(80, 225, 255)) or (vertM <= 0.92 and Color(255, 110, 80)) or Color(120, 230, 150)
            render.setColor(vCol)
            local barH = math.abs(vFrac) * (br - 1 * scale)
            if vFrac >= 0 then
                render.drawRect(vx + 2 * scale, by - barH, vw - 4 * scale, math.max(2 * scale, barH))
            else
                render.drawRect(vx + 2 * scale, by, vw - 4 * scale, math.max(2 * scale, barH))
            end
            render.setColor(vCol)
            drawTextAligned(vx + vw * 0.5, vy + vh + 10 * scale, string.format("%.2fg", vertM), 1, 1)
        end

        local showGraph = canSeeDebug and (TIRE.ShowTelemetryHUD ~= false) and ((TIRE.ShowPacejkaGraph == true) or (TIRE.ShowPacejkaGraph == nil and TIRE.ShowTireDebug == true))
        if showGraph and #wheelDebug.wheels > 0 then
            local activeCompound = (st and st.compound) or TIRE.DefaultCompound or "Sport"
            if specCompound ~= activeCompound then
                specCompound = activeCompound
                frontSpec = SimcadeTires.buildAxleSpec(TIRE.Front, activeCompound, nil, TIRE, "Front")
                rearSpec = SimcadeTires.buildAxleSpec(TIRE.Rear, activeCompound, nil, TIRE, "Rear")
            end
            local latScaleMult = (frontSpec.latScale + rearSpec.latScale) * 0.5
            local longScaleMult = (frontSpec.longScale + rearSpec.longScale) * 0.5

            local gx = 40 * scale
            local gy = sh - 320 * scale
            local gw = 380 * scale
            local gh = 220 * scale

            render.setColor(Color(0, 0, 0, 165))
            render.drawRect(gx, gy, gw, gh)
            render.setColor(Color(255, 255, 255, 45))
            drawOutlineSafe(gx, gy, gw, gh)

            local maxMult = math.max(latScaleMult, longScaleMult, 0.01)
            local D_Lat  = (gh * 0.75) * (latScaleMult / maxMult)
            local D_Long = (gh * 0.75) * (longScaleMult / maxMult)

            local cfgKey = string.format("%s_%d_%d_%.3f", activeCompound, sw, sh, scale)
            if not cachedPacejkaLat or not cachedPacejkaLong or lastPacejkaKey ~= cfgKey then
                cachedPacejkaLat  = {}
                cachedPacejkaLong = {}
                lastPacejkaKey    = cfgKey
                for x_slip = 0, 1.0001, 0.04 do
                    local sx = gx + (x_slip * gw)
                    local fy = (evalPacejka(frontSpec.Lat_B, frontSpec.Lat_C, D_Lat, frontSpec.Lat_E, x_slip * 0.5) + evalPacejka(rearSpec.Lat_B, rearSpec.Lat_C, D_Lat, rearSpec.Lat_E, x_slip * 0.5)) * 0.5
                    local fx = (evalPacejka(frontSpec.Long_B, frontSpec.Long_C, D_Long, frontSpec.Long_E, x_slip) + evalPacejka(rearSpec.Long_B, rearSpec.Long_C, D_Long, rearSpec.Long_E, x_slip)) * 0.5
                    table.insert(cachedPacejkaLat,  { x = sx, y = gy + gh - fy })
                    table.insert(cachedPacejkaLong, { x = sx, y = gy + gh - fx })
                end
            end

            render.setColor(Color(120, 255, 160, 145))
            for j = 1, #cachedPacejkaLat - 1 do
                local p1, p2 = cachedPacejkaLat[j], cachedPacejkaLat[j + 1]
                render.drawLine(p1.x, p1.y, p2.x, p2.y)
            end

            render.setColor(Color(80, 200, 255, 145))
            for j = 1, #cachedPacejkaLong - 1 do
                local p1, p2 = cachedPacejkaLong[j], cachedPacejkaLong[j + 1]
                render.drawLine(p1.x, p1.y, p2.x, p2.y)
            end

            setFontSafe(mathFont)
            render.setColor(Color(255, 255, 255, 225))
            drawTextAligned(gx + 8 * scale, gy - 24 * scale, string.format("Tire Simulation V2 (%s) [B=Rays]", tostring(activeCompound)), 0, 0)
            render.setColor(Color(120, 255, 160, 220))
            drawTextAligned(gx + 8 * scale, gy + 6 * scale, string.format("● Lateral (Eff: x%.2f)", latScaleMult), 0, 0)
            render.setColor(Color(80, 200, 255, 220))
            drawTextAligned(gx + 178 * scale, gy + 6 * scale, string.format("■ Longitudinal (Eff: x%.2f)", longScaleMult), 0, 0)

            for i, w in ipairs(wheelDebug.wheels) do
                local sm = smoothTelemetry[i] or {}
                local latRatio  = math.min(1.0, sm.latRatio or w.latRatio or 0)
                local longRatio = math.min(1.0, sm.longRatio or w.longRatio or 0)
                local wName = (w.name and w.name ~= "") and w.name or ("W" .. i)

                local spec = w.isFront and frontSpec or rearSpec
                local simSlipLat = sm.slipLat or math.abs(math.rad(w.slipAngle or 0))
                local x_lat = clamp(simSlipLat / 0.5, 0, 1)
                local dotLatX = gx + x_lat * gw
                local fY = (simSlipLat > 0.0001) and evalPacejka(spec.Lat_B, spec.Lat_C, D_Lat, spec.Lat_E, simSlipLat) or 0
                local dotLatY = gy + gh - fY

                local cLat = (latRatio >= 1.0 and Color(255, 40, 40)) or (latRatio > 0.85 and Color(255, 255, 0)) or Color(0, 255, 80)
                render.setColor(cLat)
                drawCircleSafe(dotLatX, dotLatY, 4 * scale)

                local simSlipLong = sm.slipLong or math.abs(w.slipRatio or 0)
                local x_long = clamp(simSlipLong / 1.0, 0, 1)
                local dotLongX = gx + x_long * gw
                local fX = (simSlipLong > 0.0001) and evalPacejka(spec.Long_B, spec.Long_C, D_Long, spec.Long_E, simSlipLong) or 0
                local dotLongY = gy + gh - fX

                local cLong = (longRatio >= 1.0 and Color(255, 40, 40)) or (longRatio > 0.85 and Color(255, 180, 0)) or Color(0, 200, 255)
                render.setColor(cLong)
                local sqSz = 7 * scale
                render.drawRect(dotLongX - sqSz * 0.5, dotLongY - sqSz * 0.5, sqSz, sqSz)

                setFontSafe(smallFont)
                render.setColor(Color(255, 255, 255, 220))
                drawTextAligned(dotLatX + 6 * scale, dotLatY, string.format("%s L:%d%%", wName, math.floor(latRatio * 100)), 0, 1)
                if simSlipLong > 0.02 then
                    render.setColor(cLong)
                    drawTextAligned(dotLongX + 6 * scale, dotLongY, string.format("%s X:%d%% (s:%.2f)", wName, math.floor(longRatio * 100), simSlipLong), 0, 1)
                end
            end
        end

        if SimcadeAddons and SimcadeAddons._clientHost then
            SimcadeAddons._clientHost.runHook("OnHudDraw", st, inThisSeat)
        end
    end)

    hook.add("postdrawtranslucentrenderables", "SimcadeHUD_DebugRays", function()
        if not TIRE.ShowSuspensionRays or not (render and render.draw3DLine) then return end
        local qMax = quotaMax and quotaMax() or 0
        if qMax > 0 then
            if quotaUsed and (quotaUsed() / qMax) > 0.80 then return end
            if quotaAverage and (quotaAverage() / qMax) > 0.80 then return end
        end
        local baseEnt = wheelDebug.base
        if not baseEnt or not baseEnt:isValid() then return end
        for _, w in ipairs(wheelDebug.wheels) do
            for _, r in ipairs(w.rays or {}) do
                local col = r.state == 2 and Color(80, 255, 110) or r.state == 1 and Color(80, 190, 255) or Color(255, 70, 70)
                local hit = baseEnt:localToWorld(r.hitLocal)
                render.setColor(col)
                render.draw3DLine(baseEnt:localToWorld(r.startLocal), hit, false)
                if r.state == 2 and render.draw3DWireframeBox then
                    render.draw3DWireframeBox(hit, Angle(0, 0, 0), Vector(-1, -1, -1), Vector(1, 1, 1), false)
                end
            end
        end
        if baseEnt and baseEnt:isValid() then
            local p = baseEnt:getPos()
            local fwd = baseEnt:localToWorldVector(wheelDebug.fwdLocal):getNormalized()
            local right = baseEnt:localToWorldVector(wheelDebug.rightLocal):getNormalized()
            local up = right:cross(fwd):getNormalized()
            render.setColor(Color(255, 60, 60))
            render.draw3DLine(p, p + fwd * 45, true)
            render.setColor(Color(60, 255, 60))
            render.draw3DLine(p, p + right * 45, true)
            render.setColor(Color(80, 140, 255))
            render.draw3DLine(p, p + up * 45, true)
        end
    end)
end
7838
-- name Simcade Wiremod & Net Sync Module
-- shared

SimcadeWire = SimcadeWire or {}
if CLIENT then return end

local solverSlotsInWireOrder = { 0, 1, 3, 2 } -- FL, FR, RL, RR
local detailedWheelOutputs = false

function SimcadeWire.initPorts(VEHICLE_CFG, WHEELS, extraInputs, extraOutputs)
    local inNames = { "Pod", "Base", "ExtraFilterStatic", "ExtraFilterSemiDynamic" }
    local inTypes = { "Entity", "Entity", "Array", "Array" }

    if VEHICLE_CFG and VEHICLE_CFG.enableExternalWireOverrides then
        local extNames = { "Active", "FrontWheels", "RearWheels", "EngineThrottle", "EngineBrake", "Handbrake", "Clutch", "GearUp", "GearDown", "Ignition", "TrailerMode" }
        local extTypes = { "Number", "Array", "Array", "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number" }
        for i = 1, #extNames do
            table.insert(inNames, extNames[i])
            table.insert(inTypes, extTypes[i])
        end
    end
    if type(extraInputs) == "table" then
        for _, inp in ipairs(extraInputs) do
            if inp.name then
                table.insert(inNames, inp.name)
                table.insert(inTypes, inp.type or "Number")
            end
        end
    end
    wire.adjustInputs(inNames, inTypes)

    local outNames = {
        "SpeedKMH", "SpeedMPH",
        "Throttle", "Brake", "Handbrake", "Ignition", "Active",
        "RPM", "FlywheelRPM", "Gear", "GearName", "Clutch",
        "Torque", "MaxTorque", "EngineLoad", "BoostPSI", "TurboPSI",
        "SlipAngle", "LongSlipRatio", "IsDrifting", "IsSlipping",
        "ActiveCompound", "AppliedForce",
        "FrontDiffLocked", "RearDiffLocked", "AllDiffLocked", "FrontActiveLockPct", "RearActiveLockPct", "ABSActive",
        "FL_Load", "FR_Load", "RL_Load", "RR_Load",
        "GroundedCount", "TotalMass",
        "SteeringAngleDeg", "FrontAxle", "RearAxle"
    }
    local outTypes = {
        "Number", "Number",
        "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number",
        "Number", "Number", "Number", "String", "Number",
        "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number",
        "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number",
        "String", "Number",
        "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number",
        "Number", "Number", "Number", "Number",
        "Number", "Number",
        "Number", "Entity", "Entity"
    }
    detailedWheelOutputs = VEHICLE_CFG and VEHICLE_CFG.detailedWheelWireOutputs == true
    if detailedWheelOutputs then
        for _, name in ipairs({ "SteeringInput", "FL_SteerDeg", "FR_SteerDeg" }) do
            table.insert(outNames, name)
            table.insert(outTypes, "Number")
        end
        for _, name in ipairs({ "WheelCamberDeg", "WheelCamberThrustKN", "WheelLateralForceKN", "WheelSlipAngleDeg", "WheelGrounded" }) do
            table.insert(outNames, name)
            table.insert(outTypes, "Array")
        end
    end
    local wheelPortNames = {}
    if type(WHEELS) == "table" and #WHEELS > 0 then
        for i, w in ipairs(WHEELS) do
            local pName = (type(w) == "table" and w.name and w.name ~= "")
                and ("Wheel_" .. tostring(w.name):gsub("%W+", "_"))
                or ("Wheel" .. i)
            wheelPortNames[i] = pName
            table.insert(outNames, pName)
            table.insert(outTypes, "Entity")
        end
    else
        for i = 1, 4 do
            local pName = "Wheel" .. i
            wheelPortNames[i] = pName
            table.insert(outNames, pName)
            table.insert(outTypes, "Entity")
        end
    end
    SimcadeWire._wheelPortNames = wheelPortNames
    if type(extraOutputs) == "table" then
        for _, outp in ipairs(extraOutputs) do
            if outp.name then
                table.insert(outNames, outp.name)
                table.insert(outTypes, outp.type or "Number")
            end
        end
    end
    wire.adjustOutputs(outNames, outTypes)
    return inNames
end

function SimcadeWire.writeOutputs(data)
    wire.ports.SpeedKMH        = data.kmh or 0
    wire.ports.SpeedMPH        = data.mph or 0
    wire.ports.Throttle        = math.round(data.throttle or 0, 3)
    wire.ports.Brake           = math.round(data.brake or 0, 3)
    wire.ports.Handbrake       = math.round(data.handbrake or 0, 3)
    wire.ports.Ignition        = data.ignitionMode or data.ignitionState or 0
    wire.ports.Active          = data.engineRunning and 1 or 0
    wire.ports.RPM             = math.round(data.rpm or 0)
    wire.ports.FlywheelRPM     = math.round(data.flywheelRPM or 0)
    wire.ports.Gear            = tonumber(data.gearNumber) or 0
    wire.ports.GearName        = tostring(data.gearName or "N")
    wire.ports.Clutch          = math.round(data.clutch or 0, 2)
    wire.ports.Torque          = math.round(data.torqueNm or 0)
    wire.ports.MaxTorque       = math.round(data.maxTorque or data.maxTorqueNm or 0)
    wire.ports.EngineLoad      = math.round(data.engineLoad or 0, 3)
    wire.ports.BoostPSI        = data.boostPSI or data.turboPSI or 0
    wire.ports.TurboPSI        = data.turboPSI or data.boostPSI or 0
    wire.ports.SlipAngle       = math.round(data.slipAngleDeg or 0, 1)
    wire.ports.LongSlipRatio   = math.round(data.longSlipPct or 0, 1)
    wire.ports.IsDrifting      = data.isDrifting and 1 or 0
    wire.ports.IsSlipping      = data.isSlipping and 1 or 0
    wire.ports.ActiveCompound  = tostring(data.activeCompound or "Sport")
    wire.ports.AppliedForce    = math.round(data.appliedForce or 0)
    wire.ports.FrontDiffLocked    = data.frontDiffLocked and 1 or 0
    wire.ports.RearDiffLocked     = data.rearDiffLocked and 1 or 0
    wire.ports.AllDiffLocked      = data.allDiffLocked and 1 or 0
    wire.ports.FrontActiveLockPct = math.round(data.frontActiveLockPct or 0, 1)
    wire.ports.RearActiveLockPct  = math.round(data.rearActiveLockPct or 0, 1)
    wire.ports.ABSActive       = data.absActive and 1 or 0
    wire.ports.FL_Load         = math.round(data.flLoad or 0)
    wire.ports.FR_Load         = math.round(data.frLoad or 0)
    wire.ports.RL_Load         = math.round(data.rlLoad or 0)
    wire.ports.RR_Load         = math.round(data.rrLoad or 0)
    wire.ports.GroundedCount   = data.groundedCount or 0
    wire.ports.TotalMass       = math.round(data.totalMass or 0)
    wire.ports.SteeringAngleDeg = math.round(data.steerDeg or 0, 2)
    if detailedWheelOutputs then
        wire.ports.SteeringInput   = data.steerInput or 0
        wire.ports.FL_SteerDeg = math.round(math.deg(data.frontLeftSteerRad or 0), 2)
        wire.ports.FR_SteerDeg = math.round(math.deg(data.frontRightSteerRad or 0), 2)
        -- Wire arrays use vehicle corner order FL, FR, RL, RR. Solver slots are FL, FR, RR, RL.
        local camber, thrust, lateral, slip, contact = {}, {}, {}, {}, {}
        for corner, slot in ipairs(solverSlotsInWireOrder) do
            local w = data.solverWheels and data.solverWheels[slot]
            local grounded = w and w.alignment and w.alignment.isGrounded
            contact[corner] = grounded and 1 or 0
            camber[corner] = grounded and math.deg(w.roadCamberRad or 0) or 0
            thrust[corner] = grounded and w.camberFy or 0
            lateral[corner] = grounded and w.Fy or 0
            slip[corner] = grounded and math.deg(w.slipAngleRad or 0) or 0
        end
        wire.ports.WheelCamberDeg = camber
        wire.ports.WheelCamberThrustKN = thrust
        wire.ports.WheelLateralForceKN = lateral
        wire.ports.WheelSlipAngleDeg = slip
        wire.ports.WheelGrounded = contact
    end
    if type(data.wheelEntities) == "table" then
        local names = SimcadeWire._wheelPortNames or {}
        for i = 1, #data.wheelEntities do
            local pName = names[i] or ("Wheel" .. i)
            wire.ports[pName] = data.wheelEntities[i]
        end
    end
end

10761
-- name Simcade Modular Addon, Custom Key & Full-Chip Override System
-- shared
-- include simcadebase/addons/lights.txt

SimcadeAddons = SimcadeAddons or {}

local function safeCall(tag, fn, ...)
    if type(fn) ~= "function" then return false, nil end
    local ok, res = pcall(fn, ...)
    if not ok then
        SimcadeAddons._loggedErrors = SimcadeAddons._loggedErrors or {}
        if not SimcadeAddons._loggedErrors[tag] then
            SimcadeAddons._loggedErrors[tag] = true
            print("[SimcadeAddons] Error in " .. tostring(tag) .. ": " .. tostring(res))
        end
        return false, nil
    end
    return true, res
end

-- Compatibility for cars using the original LIGHTS_CFG and inline ADDONS format.
function SimcadeAddons.createLightsAddon(cfg)
    return require("simcadebase/addons/lights.txt")(cfg.LIGHTS_CFG or {}, cfg)
end

function SimcadeAddons.collectAddons(cfg)
    local list = {}
    cfg = cfg or {}
    if cfg.ADDON_MODULES then
        for _, spec in ipairs(cfg.ADDON_MODULES) do
            if spec.enabled ~= false then
                local ok, addon = safeCall(tostring(spec.file), function()
                    local create = require(spec.file)
                    return create(spec.options or {}, cfg)
                end)
                if ok and type(addon) == "table" then
                    addon.name = addon.name or spec.file
                    list[#list + 1] = addon
                end
            end
        end
    else
        local lights = SimcadeAddons.createLightsAddon(cfg)
        if lights then list[#list + 1] = lights end
    end

    local userAddons = cfg and cfg.ADDONS
    if type(userAddons) == "table" then
        for k, entry in pairs(userAddons) do
            if type(entry) == "table" and entry.enabled ~= false then
                if not entry.name then entry.name = tostring(k) end
                list[#list + 1] = entry
            end
        end
    end
    return list
end

if CLIENT then
    function SimcadeAddons.initClient(cfg)
        local addons = SimcadeAddons.collectAddons(cfg)
        local keyBindings = {} -- id -> keyCode
        local controlsCfg = (cfg and cfg.CONTROLS_CFG) or {}

        local ctx = {
            config = cfg,
            syncedState = {},
            bindKey = function(id, defaultKey)
                keyBindings[id] = controlsCfg[id] or defaultKey
            end,
            addWireInput = function() end,
            addWireOutput = function() end,
            setWireOutput = function() end,
            getWireInput = function() return nil end,
            override = function() end,
            wrap = function() end,
        }

        for _, a in ipairs(addons) do
            if a.OnClientInit then
                safeCall((a.name or "addon") .. ".OnClientInit", a.OnClientInit, ctx, cfg)
            end
        end

        if next(keyBindings) ~= nil then
            local function isInSeat()
                local lp = player()
                if not (lp and lp:isValid() and lp:inVehicle()) then return false end
                local st = SimcadeHUD and SimcadeHUD.getState and SimcadeHUD.getState() or nil
                if st and st.pod and st.pod:isValid() and lp.getVehicle then
                    return lp:getVehicle() == st.pod
                end
                return true
            end
            local function sendAddonKey(id, down)
                pcall(function()
                    net.start("Simcade_AddonKey")
                    net.writeString(id)
                    net.writeBool(down and true or false)
                    net.send()
                end)
            end
            hook.add("inputPressed", "SimcadeAddons_KeyDown", function(btn)
                if not isInSeat() then return end
                for id, kCode in pairs(keyBindings) do
                    if btn == kCode then sendAddonKey(id, true) end
                end
            end)
            hook.add("inputReleased", "SimcadeAddons_KeyUp", function(btn)
                if not isInSeat() then return end
                for id, kCode in pairs(keyBindings) do
                    if btn == kCode then sendAddonKey(id, false) end
                end
            end)
        end

        net.receive("Simcade_AddonState", function()
            ctx.syncedState = net.readTable()
        end)

        local clientHost = {
            addons = addons,
            ctx = ctx,
            runHook = function(hookName, ...)
                for _, a in ipairs(addons) do
                    if a[hookName] then
                        safeCall((a.name or "addon") .. "." .. hookName, a[hookName], ctx, ...)
                    end
                end
            end,
        }
        net.receive("Simcade_AddonEvent", function()
            local name = net.readString()
            local data = net.readTable()
            clientHost.runHook("OnClientEvent", name, data)
        end)
        hook.add("removed", "Simcade_ClientAddonCleanup", function()
            clientHost.runHook("OnCleanup")
        end)
        SimcadeAddons._clientHost = clientHost
        return clientHost
    end
    return
end

-- SERVER SIDE
function SimcadeAddons.initServer(cfg, notifyDriverFn)
    local addons = SimcadeAddons.collectAddons(cfg)
    local extraInputs = {}
    local extraOutputs = {}
    local inputHandlers = {}
    local keyHandlers = {}
    local wireValues = {}
    local overrides = {}
    local wraps = {}
    local carMethodOrigs = {}
    local pendingEvents = {}

    local ctx = {
        config = cfg,
        syncedState = {},
        base = nil,
        pod = nil,
        phys = nil,
        car = nil,
        wheels = nil,
        driver = nil,
        state = {},
        pipeline = {},
        notifyDriver = notifyDriverFn,
        emitEvent = function(name, data)
            pendingEvents[name] = { data = data, time = timer.curtime() }
        end,
        bindKey = function(id, defaultKey, onPress, onRelease)
            keyHandlers[id] = { key = (cfg.CONTROLS_CFG and cfg.CONTROLS_CFG[id]) or defaultKey, onPress = onPress, onRelease = onRelease }
        end,
        addWireInput = function(name, portType, onChange)
            extraInputs[#extraInputs + 1] = { name = name, type = portType or "Number" }
            if onChange then inputHandlers[name] = onChange end
        end,
        addWireOutput = function(name, portType)
            extraOutputs[#extraOutputs + 1] = { name = name, type = portType or "Number" }
        end,
        setWireOutput = function(name, val)
            if wire and wire.ports then
                wire.ports[name] = val
            end
        end,
        getWireInput = function(name)
            return wireValues[name]
        end,
        override = function(target, fn)
            if type(fn) == "function" then overrides[target] = fn end
        end,
        wrap = function(target, fn)
            if type(fn) == "function" then
                wraps[target] = wraps[target] or {}
                wraps[target][#wraps[target] + 1] = fn
            end
        end,
    }

    for _, a in ipairs(addons) do
        if a.OnInit then
            safeCall((a.name or "addon") .. ".OnInit", a.OnInit, ctx, cfg)
        end
    end

    net.receive("Simcade_AddonKey", function(_, ply)
        if not (ctx.car and isValid(ctx.pod)) then return end
        local drv = ctx.pod:getDriver()
        if not isValid(drv) or ply ~= drv then return end
        local id = net.readString()
        local down = net.readBool()
        local h = keyHandlers[id]
        if not h then return end
        if down and h.onPress then
            safeCall("AddonKey.onPress." .. id, h.onPress, ctx, drv)
        elseif (not down) and h.onRelease then
            safeCall("AddonKey.onRelease." .. id, h.onRelease, ctx, drv)
        end
    end)

    local function buildWrapped(target, origFn)
        local fn = overrides[target] or origFn
        local wList = wraps[target]
        if wList then
            for i = 1, #wList do
                local wrapper = wList[i]
                local prev = fn
                fn = function(...)
                    return wrapper(prev, ...)
                end
            end
        end
        return fn
    end

    local CAR_METHODS = {
        "stepVPhysics", "driver", "gearbox", "auto", "clutchDrive", "torque", "drive",
        "tire", "ellipse", "aeroForces", "computeActiveDiff", "afterSolve",
        "postEngine", "postDrive", "shiftKey", "setManual", "toggleDiffLock",
    }

    local MODULE_TARGETS = {
        { tbl = SimcadeSuspension, key = "computeWheelForce" },
        { tbl = SimcadeSuspension, key = "applyCurbBlock" },
        { tbl = SimcadeEngine,     key = "synthesizeSpec" },
        { tbl = SimcadeEngine,     key = "stepIgnition" },
        { tbl = SimcadeControls,   key = "pollInputs" },
        { tbl = SimcadeGearbox,    key = "evaluateAutoShift" },
        { tbl = SimcadeWire,       key = "writeOutputs" },
    }
    local moduleOrigs = {}

    return {
        ctx = ctx,
        addons = addons,
        pendingEvents = pendingEvents,
        extraInputs = extraInputs,
        extraOutputs = extraOutputs,
        onWireInput = function(name, value)
            wireValues[name] = value
            local cb = inputHandlers[name]
            if cb then safeCall("WireInput." .. name, cb, ctx, value) end
        end,
        applyOverrides = function(car, pipeline)
            if pipeline then
                for name, origFn in pairs(pipeline) do
                    if overrides[name] or wraps[name] then
                        pipeline[name] = buildWrapped(name, origFn)
                    end
                end
            end
            if car then
                for _, mName in ipairs(CAR_METHODS) do
                    if (overrides[mName] or wraps[mName]) and type(car[mName]) == "function" then
                        carMethodOrigs[mName] = carMethodOrigs[mName] or car[mName]
                        car[mName] = buildWrapped(mName, carMethodOrigs[mName])
                    end
                end
            end
            for _, mt in ipairs(MODULE_TARGETS) do
                local tbl, k = mt.tbl, mt.key
                if tbl and type(tbl[k]) == "function" and (overrides[k] or wraps[k]) then
                    moduleOrigs[k] = moduleOrigs[k] or tbl[k]
                    tbl[k] = buildWrapped(k, moduleOrigs[k])
                end
            end
        end,
        runHook = function(hookName, ...)
            for _, a in ipairs(addons) do
                if a[hookName] then
                    safeCall((a.name or "addon") .. "." .. hookName, a[hookName], ctx, ...)
                end
            end
        end,
    }
end

7590
-- name Simcade Braking & ABS Module
-- shared

SimcadeBraking = SimcadeBraking or {}

local abs = math.abs
local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b) return v < a and a or (v > b and b or v) end
local max = math.max
local min = math.min

function SimcadeBraking.configure(engineCfg, massT, massScale)
    engineCfg = engineCfg or {}

    local baseBrakeNm = max(50, engineCfg.brakeTorque or 2800)
    local brakeMultiplier = baseBrakeNm / 2800
    local frontBias = 0.535
    if engineCfg.frontBrakeBias then
        frontBias = clamp(engineCfg.frontBrakeBias, 0.15, 0.85)
    end
    local baseBrakeKNm = baseBrakeNm * 0.001
    local fallbackRearBrake = max(0.5 * massScale * brakeMultiplier, 2.2 * massT)
    local brakeHighSpeedStartKMH = max(0, tonumber(engineCfg.BrakeHighSpeedStartKMH) or 80)

    return {
        frontBrakeKNm = engineCfg.FrontBrakeTorque and max(0.05, engineCfg.FrontBrakeTorque * 0.001) or (baseBrakeKNm * frontBias * 2.0),
        rearBrakeKNm = engineCfg.RearBrakeTorque and max(0.05, engineCfg.RearBrakeTorque * 0.001) or (baseBrakeKNm * (1.0 - frontBias) * 2.0),
        rampTime = clamp(tonumber(engineCfg.BrakeRampTime) or 0.18, 0.02, 1.0),
        highSpeedScale = clamp(tonumber(engineCfg.BrakeHighSpeedScale) or 1.0, 0.1, 2.0),
        highSpeedStartKMH = brakeHighSpeedStartKMH,
        highSpeedRefKMH = max(brakeHighSpeedStartKMH + 10, tonumber(engineCfg.BrakeHighSpeedRefKMH) or 200),
        abs = {
            enabled = (engineCfg.ABSEnabled ~= false),
            threshold = clamp(tonumber(engineCfg.ABSLockThreshold) or 0.22, 0.02, 0.85),
            pulseRate = clamp(tonumber(engineCfg.ABSPulseRate) or 14, 4, 120),
            release = clamp(tonumber(engineCfg.ABSReleaseRatio) or 0.25, 0.05, 0.90),
        },
        handbrake = {
            torqueKNm = engineCfg.HandbrakeTorque and max(0.05, engineCfg.HandbrakeTorque * 0.001) or (fallbackRearBrake * 10),
            lockWheels = (engineCfg.HandbrakeLockWheels ~= false),
        },
    }
end

function SimcadeBraking.updatePedal(car, driver, rawBrake, dt)
    local brakeCfg = car.braking
    if rawBrake > 0.05 then
        local rampTime = max(0.02, brakeCfg.rampTime or 0.18)
        driver.brakeRamp = min(1.0, (driver.brakeRamp or 0.35) + (dt or 0.015) / rampTime * 0.65)
    else
        driver.brakeRamp = 0.35
    end
    return rawBrake * (driver.brakeRamp or 1.0)
end

function SimcadeBraking.applyWheelTorque(car, wheel, peakLongKN, vFwd, absFwd)
    local brakeCfg = car.braking
    local driver = car.drv
    local isBurnout = (driver and (driver.thr or 0) > 0.5 and (driver.brk or 0) > 0.3 and absFwd < 8.0)
    local pedalBrake = (isBurnout and wheel.i >= 2) and (absFwd * 0.05) or clamp((driver and driver.brk) or wheel.brk or 0, 0, 1)
    local brakeTorque = (wheel.i < 2) and (brakeCfg.frontBrakeKNm or 2.00) or (brakeCfg.rearBrakeKNm or 1.22)
    local highSpeedScale = brakeCfg.highSpeedScale or 1.0
    if abs(highSpeedScale - 1.0) > 0.001 then
        local startMS = (brakeCfg.highSpeedStartKMH or 80) / 3.6
        if absFwd > startMS then
            local refMS = max(startMS + 2.0, (brakeCfg.highSpeedRefKMH or 200) / 3.6)
            local speedFraction = clamp((absFwd - startMS) / (refMS - startMS), 0, 1)
            brakeTorque = brakeTorque * (1.0 + (highSpeedScale - 1.0) * speedFraction)
        end
    end
    local brakeForce = brakeTorque * pedalBrake
    local absCfg = brakeCfg.abs
    local absReleasing = false
    wheel.absActive = false

    if absCfg and absCfg.enabled and wheel.hbk < 0.1 and pedalBrake > 0.05 and absFwd > 2.2 and peakLongKN > 0.001 then
        local kappaEstimate = (wheel.radius * wheel.w - vFwd) / max(2.2, absFwd + 0.5)
        local absSlip = (vFwd >= 0) and (kappaEstimate < -absCfg.threshold) or (kappaEstimate > absCfg.threshold)
        local overTorque = brakeForce > (wheel.radius * peakLongKN * 0.98)
        local allowLock = (absCfg.threshold >= 0.45) or (absCfg.release >= 0.65)
        if (wheel.locked ~= 0) or absSlip or (not allowLock and overTorque) then
            wheel.absActive = true
            if not allowLock then wheel.locked = 0 end
            local period = 1.0 / max(4, absCfg.pulseRate)
            local phase = (car.now or 0) % period
            if phase < period * 0.55 then
                brakeForce = brakeForce * absCfg.release
                absReleasing = true
                local recovery = clamp(1.0 - absCfg.release, 0.15, 0.85)
                local targetWheelSpeed = (vFwd * (1.0 - absCfg.threshold * 0.45)) / max(0.15, wheel.radius)
                wheel.w = wheel.w + (targetWheelSpeed - wheel.w) * recovery
            else
                local applyCap = allowLock and (0.95 + absCfg.threshold * 0.5) or (0.88 + absCfg.threshold * 0.35)
                brakeForce = min(brakeForce, wheel.radius * peakLongKN * applyCap)
                local floorWheelSpeed = (vFwd * (1.0 - absCfg.threshold * 0.90)) / max(0.15, wheel.radius)
                if (vFwd >= 0 and wheel.w < floorWheelSpeed) or (vFwd < 0 and wheel.w > floorWheelSpeed) then
                    wheel.w = floorWheelSpeed
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local handbrakeCfg = brakeCfg.handbrake
    local handbrakeTorque = (handbrakeCfg and handbrakeCfg.torqueKNm or 0) * wheel.hbk
    if pedalBrake > 0.05 and (driver and driver.thr or 0) < 0.05 and ((vFwd >= 0 and wheel.T > 0) or (vFwd < 0 and wheel.T < 0)) then
        wheel.T = 0
    end
    local capacity = brakeForce + handbrakeTorque
    wheel.serviceBrakeKNm = brakeForce
    if wheel.locked == 0 and capacity > 0 then
        local h = car.substepDt
        local rimSpeed = wheel.w * wheel.radius
        local stopTorque = wheel.I * wheel.w / h
        if abs(rimSpeed - vFwd) <= 0.16 * max(2.2, absFwd + 0.5) then
            -- Rolling contact couples the brake to this corner's chassis mass.
            stopTorque = car.m * 0.25 * vFwd * wheel.radius / h
        end
        -- A stationary brake can balance drive torque up to its capacity.
        -- At rest with no drive, its requested torque is exactly zero.
        local appliedTorque = clamp(stopTorque + wheel.T, -capacity, capacity)
        wheel.T = wheel.T - appliedTorque
    end

    return pedalBrake, brakeForce, handbrakeTorque, absReleasing
end

function SimcadeBraking.applyWheelLock(car, wheel, peakLongKN, peakLatKN, vFwd, absFwd, pedalBrake, brakeForce, handbrakeTorque, absReleasing)
    local brakeCfg = car.braking
    local handbrakeCfg = brakeCfg.handbrake
    if wheel.i >= 2 and (wheel.hbk or 0) > 0.1 then
        local lockWheels = (not handbrakeCfg) or handbrakeCfg.lockWheels
        if lockWheels or handbrakeTorque > wheel.radius * peakLongKN * 1.02 then
            wheel.locked = 1
            wheel.w = 0
        else
            wheel.locked = 0
        end
    elseif peakLongKN > 0.001 then
        local absCfg = brakeCfg.abs
        local allowAbsLock = absCfg and absCfg.enabled and ((absCfg.threshold >= 0.45) or (absCfg.release >= 0.65))
        local totalBrakeTorque = handbrakeTorque + abs(brakeForce)
        if absReleasing or (wheel.absActive and not allowAbsLock) or (absCfg and absCfg.enabled and not allowAbsLock and absFwd > 2.2) or totalBrakeTorque <= wheel.radius * peakLongKN * 1.02 then
            wheel.locked = 0
        elseif totalBrakeTorque > wheel.radius * peakLongKN * 1.02 then
            wheel.locked = 1
            wheel.w = 0
        else
            wheel.locked = 0
        end
    else
        wheel.locked = 0
    end
    return peakLatKN
end

return SimcadeBraking
6484
-- name Simcade Steering Module
-- shared

SimcadeSteering = SimcadeSteering or {}

local abs, min, max = math.abs, math.min, math.max
local atan2 = math.atan2
local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b) return v < a and a or (v > b and b or v) end
local ceil, floor = math.ceil, math.floor
local trunc = function(x) return x < 0 and ceil(x) or floor(x) end
local PI = math.pi
local U16_RAD = 9.587379924e-05

function SimcadeSteering.updateInput(chassis, input, dt)
    local sc = assert(chassis.steerCfg, "Steering configuration is required")
    local D = chassis.drv
    local R = chassis.R
    local stepDt = clamp(dt or 0.015, 0.001, 0.05)
    local dt66 = stepDt * 66.6666667

    local maxAng = sc.Lock or sc.maxAngle or sc.maxSteerDeg or 38
    maxAng = min(maxAng, sc.maxSteerDeg or maxAng)
    local steerSpeed = sc.SteeringSpeed or 89
    local returnSpeed = sc.ReturnSpeed or 200
    local retAtten = max(1, sc.ReturnSpeedAttenuation or 40)
    local speedEffect = max(10, sc.SpeedEffect or 950)
    local slipThresh = sc.SlipCorrectionThreshold or 1.0
    local slipCounter = sc.SlipCounterForce or 1.5
    local corrStart = sc.CorrectionStart or 0.05
    if corrStart > 1.5 then corrStart = corrStart * 0.0174532925 end
    local corrFull = sc.CorrectionFull or 0.25
    if corrFull > 1.5 then corrFull = corrFull * 0.0174532925 end
    local highSlipThreshold = sc.HighSlipThreshold or 2.5
    local stabThresh = max(1, sc.StabilityThreshold or 80)
    local minStabScale = sc.MinStabilityScale or 0.2
    local slipResistance = max(1, sc.SlipResistance or 50)
    local gyroForce = sc.GyroCounterForce or 1.5
    local gyroSpdTh = max(1, sc.GyroSpeedThreshold or 100)
    local gyroMin = sc.GyroMinScale or 0
    local gyroMax = sc.GyroMaxScale or 3
    local latAlpha = clamp(sc.LateralSmoothAlpha or 0.25, 0.05, 1.0)
    local linBlend = clamp(sc.LinearBlend or 0.3, 0.0, 1.0)

    local fwdMs = chassis.v[0] * R[0] + chassis.v[1] * R[1] + chassis.v[2] * R[2]
    local leftMs = chassis.v[0] * R[3] + chassis.v[1] * R[4] + chassis.v[2] * R[5]
    local fwdSpd = fwdMs * (39.3700787 / 14.5)
    local rawLat = -leftMs * (39.3700787 / 14.5)
    local absFwd = abs(fwdSpd)
    local spdDir = (fwdSpd >= 0) and 1 or -1

    D.smoothedLat = (D.smoothedLat or 0) + (rawLat - (D.smoothedLat or 0)) * clamp(latAlpha * dt66, 0.05, 1.0)
    local smLat = D.smoothedLat
    local wUpRad = chassis.w[0] * R[6] + chassis.w[1] * R[7] + chassis.w[2] * R[8]
    local yawVelDeg = wUpRad * 57.2957795
    local slipAng = atan2(abs(smLat), max(absFwd, 0.01))
    local span = max(0.001, corrFull - corrStart)
    local st = clamp((slipAng - corrStart) / span, 0, 1)
    local corrScale = st * st * (3 - 2 * st)
    local stabFactor = clamp(abs(yawVelDeg) / stabThresh, minStabScale, 1.0)
    local spdScale = clamp(absFwd / gyroSpdTh, 0, 1)
    local gyroScale = gyroMin + (gyroMax - gyroMin) * spdScale
    local isSlipping = (abs(smLat) > slipThresh) and (slipAng > corrStart)
    local slipCorr = isSlipping and (-smLat * slipCounter * spdDir) or 0
    local yawDamp = -yawVelDeg * stepDt * gyroForce * gyroScale * spdDir
    -- A tight rolling turn gives the chassis sideways velocity even when the
    -- rear tires track cleanly. Use rear contact slip to distinguish a slide.
    local rearSlip, rearLoad = 0, 0
    for i = 2, 3 do
        local wheel = chassis.wheels and chassis.wheels[i]
        if wheel and wheel.contact ~= 0 then
            local load = max(0, wheel.load or 0)
            rearSlip = rearSlip + abs(wheel.slipAngleRad or 0) * load
            rearLoad = rearLoad + load
        end
    end
    local rearAngle = rearLoad > 0 and rearSlip / rearLoad or 0
    local rearBlend = clamp((rearAngle - corrStart) / span, 0, 1)
    local speedKmh = math.sqrt(fwdMs * fwdMs + leftMs * leftMs) * 3.6
    local speedBlend = clamp((speedKmh - 8) / 10, 0, 1)
    local sideBlend = clamp((abs(smLat) - highSlipThreshold) / max(1, highSlipThreshold), 0, 1)
    local driftBlend = corrScale * rearBlend * speedBlend * sideBlend
    local countersteerCorrection = ((slipCorr / slipResistance) * dt66 + yawDamp) * driftBlend * stabFactor

    local rawIn = clamp(input, -1, 1)
    local curvedIn = rawIn * rawIn * rawIn * (1.0 - linBlend) + rawIn * linBlend
    local curDeg = D.u16Deg or 0
    -- Reserve directional key rates for an established slide. Rear slip alone
    -- can also be substantial in ordinary cornering.
    local manualBlend = clamp((rearAngle - math.rad(8)) / math.rad(8), 0, 1)
    manualBlend = manualBlend * manualBlend * (3 - 2 * manualBlend) * driftBlend
    -- A positive key steers left; positive smLat means travel to the right.
    local countersteering = rawIn * smLat * spdDir < 0
    local driftRate = countersteering and (sc.DriftCountersteerMultiplier or 1)
        or (sc.DriftTurnInMultiplier or 1)
    local keyRate = 1 + (max(0, driftRate) - 1) * manualBlend
    local speedFraction = min(absFwd / speedEffect, 1)
    local steerStep = curvedIn * steerSpeed * keyRate * stepDt
    local angleT = clamp(curDeg + steerStep + countersteerCorrection * (1 - abs(rawIn)), -maxAng, maxAng)
    if abs(rawIn) < 0.01 then
        local retScale = clamp(absFwd / retAtten, 0, 1)
        local retStep = returnSpeed * stepDt * retScale * (1 - driftBlend)
        if angleT > 0 then
            angleT = max(0, angleT - retStep)
        elseif angleT < 0 then
            angleT = min(0, angleT + retStep)
        end
    end

    local decayAlpha = min(0.85, speedFraction * dt66)
    -- SpeedEffect bleeds off accumulated angle every tick, including held input.
    angleT = clamp(angleT * (1.0 - decayAlpha), -maxAng, maxAng)
    D.u16Deg = angleT
    chassis.steer.u16 = trunc(trunc(angleT * 65536) / 360) % 65536
end

function SimcadeSteering.applyWheelAngles(chassis, u16)
    local v5 = u16 * U16_RAD
    if v5 > PI then v5 = v5 - 2 * PI end
    local positive = true
    if v5 < 0 then positive = false; v5 = -v5 end

    local v15
    local steerCfg = chassis.steerCfg
    if steerCfg and steerCfg.ackermann ~= nil then
        v15 = v5 / (1 + max(0, steerCfg.ackermann) * abs(math.tan(min(v5, 1.2))))
    else
        local c = chassis.c
        local v11, v14 = c[5] - c[17], c[4] - c[40]
        v15 = v14 * v5 / (v5 * v11 + v14)
    end

    if positive then
        chassis.steer.a1 = v15
        chassis.steer.a0 = v5
    else
        chassis.steer.a1 = -v5
        chassis.steer.a0 = -v15
    end
end

return SimcadeSteering
17535
-- name Simcade Vehicle Builder
-- shared

local SimcadeVehicleBuilder = {}
local abs, min, max = math.abs, math.min, math.max
local floor = math.floor
local clamp = math.clamp or function(v, a, b)
    if v < a then return a elseif v > b then return b else return v end
end
local IN_TO_M = 0.0254
local Tires = assert(SimcadeTires, "Load simcade_tires.txt before simcade_vehicle_builder.txt")
local Braking = assert(SimcadeBraking, "Load simcade_braking.txt before simcade_vehicle_builder.txt")
local Aero = assert(SimcadeAero, "Load simcade_aero.txt before simcade_vehicle_builder.txt")
local DEFAULT_VEHICLE_PARAMETERS = {
    referenceMassT = 1.22,
    frontAxleXM = 1.3,
    rearAxleXM = -1.3,
    trackHalfM = 0.75,
    wheelCenterZM = 0.08,
    wheelRadiusM = 0.3135,
    wheelWidthM = 0.205,
    groundYawInertiaMultiplier = 2.2,
    upshiftFraction = 0.8,
    clutchCoupling = 0.04,
    reverseGearRatio = -3.0,
    forwardGearRatios = { 1.0 },
    idleRPM = 800,
    redlineRPM = 6500,
    limiterRPM = 7000,
    flywheelInertia = 0.00025,
    torqueCurveKNm = { 0.14, 0.15, 0.16, 0.18, 0.20, 0.216, 0.203, 0.17, 0.15 },
    engineBrakeCurveKNm = { 0.12, 0.16, 0.12 },
    brakeAssist = 17,
}

local function makeVehicleParameters()
    local defaults = DEFAULT_VEHICLE_PARAMETERS
    local c = {
        [0] = 0, [1] = 0, [2] = 0,
        [68] = defaults.referenceMassT,
        [88] = defaults.groundYawInertiaMultiplier,
        [108] = defaults.upshiftFraction,
        [116] = defaults.reverseGearRatio,
        [117] = 0,
        [113] = defaults.clutchCoupling,
        [124] = defaults.idleRPM,
        [125] = defaults.redlineRPM,
        [126] = defaults.limiterRPM,
        [127] = defaults.flywheelInertia,
        [162] = defaults.brakeAssist,
        [163] = 0,
    }

    local wheelPositions = {
        { defaults.frontAxleXM, defaults.trackHalfM },
        { defaults.frontAxleXM, -defaults.trackHalfM },
        { defaults.rearAxleXM, -defaults.trackHalfM },
        { defaults.rearAxleXM, defaults.trackHalfM },
    }
    for i = 0, 3 do
        local offset = 4 + 12 * i
        local position = wheelPositions[i + 1]
        c[offset] = position[1]
        c[offset + 1] = position[2]
        c[offset + 2] = defaults.wheelCenterZM
        c[offset + 4] = defaults.wheelRadiusM
        c[offset + 5] = defaults.wheelWidthM
    end
    for i = 0, 8 do
        c[128 + i] = defaults.torqueCurveKNm[i + 1]
        c[137 + i] = 0
    end
    for i = 1, #defaults.forwardGearRatios do
        c[117 + i] = defaults.forwardGearRatios[i]
    end
    for i = 0, 2 do
        c[146 + i] = defaults.engineBrakeCurveKNm[i + 1]
    end
    return c
end

local function evalBezierTable(tbl, t)
    if type(tbl) ~= "table" or #tbl == 0 then return 1.0 end
    local n = #tbl - 1
    if n <= 0 then return tbl[1] or 1.0 end
    t = clamp(t, 0, 1)
    -- Piecewise smooth-step interpolation preserves exact peak/control points without Bernstein flattening
    local s = t * n
    local idx = min(n - 1, floor(s))
    local f = clamp(s - idx, 0, 1)
    local sf = f * f * (3 - 2 * f)
    local v0 = tbl[idx + 1] or 1.0
    local v1 = tbl[idx + 2] or v0
    return v0 + (v1 - v0) * sf
end

function SimcadeVehicleBuilder.buildCarData(vehicleCfg, tireCfg, suspCfg, engineCfg, steerCfg, runtimeWheels, totalMassKg, compoundName, aeroCfg, dependencies)
    dependencies = dependencies or {}
    local T = assert(dependencies.T, "Physics curves are required to build vehicle data")
    local Tune = assert(dependencies.Tune, "Shift logic is required to build vehicle data")
    vehicleCfg = vehicleCfg or {}
    tireCfg    = tireCfg    or {}
    suspCfg    = suspCfg    or {}
    engineCfg  = engineCfg  or {}
    steerCfg   = steerCfg   or {}

    local c = makeVehicleParameters()

    -- Coordinates are already CoM-centered in runtimeWheels
    c[0], c[1], c[2] = 0, 0, 0

    local targetMassT = max(0.35, (totalMassKg or 1500) / 1000)
    local massScale = targetMassT / max(0.4, c[68] or 1.1)
    c[68] = targetMassT

    local function getAxleSusp(isFront)
        if isFront and suspCfg.Front then return suspCfg.Front end
        if (not isFront) and suspCfg.Rear then return suspCfg.Rear end
        return suspCfg
    end
    local function getAxleTire(isFront)
        if isFront and tireCfg.Front then return tireCfg.Front end
        if (not isFront) and tireCfg.Rear then return tireCfg.Rear end
        return tireCfg
    end

    -- Wheel slots: front left, front right, rear right, rear left.
    local frontSumX, rearSumX, maxAbsY = 0, 0, 0.75
    local frontN, rearN = 0, 0
    local avgRadiusM, radCount = 0, 0
    if runtimeWheels and #runtimeWheels >= 4 then
        local slotByQuad = {}
        for _, rw in ipairs(runtimeWheels) do
            if rw.isFront and (rw.pos.y >= 0) and not slotByQuad[0] then slotByQuad[0] = rw
            elseif rw.isFront and (rw.pos.y < 0) and not slotByQuad[1] then slotByQuad[1] = rw
            elseif (not rw.isFront) and (rw.pos.y < 0) and not slotByQuad[2] then slotByQuad[2] = rw
            elseif (not rw.isFront) and (rw.pos.y >= 0) and not slotByQuad[3] then slotByQuad[3] = rw
            end
        end
        slotByQuad[0] = slotByQuad[0] or runtimeWheels[1]
        slotByQuad[1] = slotByQuad[1] or runtimeWheels[2]
        slotByQuad[2] = slotByQuad[2] or runtimeWheels[4] or runtimeWheels[3]
        slotByQuad[3] = slotByQuad[3] or runtimeWheels[3] or runtimeWheels[4]

        for ni = 0, 3 do
            local rw = slotByQuad[ni]
            if rw then
                local o = 4 + 12 * ni
                local isFront = (ni == 0 or ni == 1)
                local aSusp = getAxleSusp(isFront)
                local restComp = clamp(aSusp.restCompression or 0.45, 0.25, 0.70)
                local rideH_m  = max(8, aSusp.rideHeight or 24) * IN_TO_M
                local x_m = rw.pos.x * IN_TO_M
                local y_m = rw.pos.y * IN_TO_M
                local z_rest_m = SimcadeSuspension.mountHeight(rw, aSusp) * IN_TO_M - (1.0 - restComp) * rideH_m
                local rad_m = max(0.15, (rw.radius or 15) * IN_TO_M)

                c[o]     = x_m
                c[o + 1] = y_m
                c[o + 2] = z_rest_m
                c[o + 4] = rad_m
                c[o + 5] = max(0.10, (rw.width or 9) * IN_TO_M)
                avgRadiusM = avgRadiusM + rad_m
                radCount = radCount + 1
