                if isFront then frontSumX = frontSumX + x_m; frontN = frontN + 1
                else rearSumX = rearSumX + x_m; rearN = rearN + 1 end
                maxAbsY = max(maxAbsY, abs(y_m))
            end
        end
    end
    avgRadiusM = (radCount > 0) and (avgRadiusM / radCount) or (15 * IN_TO_M)

    -- Update box dimensions & inertia diagonal from wheel layout
    local wheelbase = (frontN > 0 and rearN > 0) and max(1.6, (frontSumX / frontN) - (rearSumX / rearN)) or 2.44
    local lenM   = max(3.0, wheelbase * 1.75)
    local widthM = max(1.4, maxAbsY * 2.1)
    local heightM = 1.29
    c[69], c[70], c[71] = lenM, widthM, heightM
    for i = 72, 87 do c[i] = 0 end
    c[72] = (heightM * heightM + widthM * widthM) * targetMassT * 0.0833333358168602
    c[77] = (heightM * heightM + lenM * lenM) * targetMassT * 0.0833333358168602
    c[82] = (widthM * widthM + lenM * lenM) * targetMassT * 0.0833333358168602
    c[87] = 1

    -- Wheel travel limits used to position the tire relative to its mount.
    local cornerMassT = targetMassT * 0.25
    for axle = 0, 1 do
        local row = 92 + 8 * axle
        local isFront = (axle == 0)
        local aSusp = getAxleSusp(isFront)
        local rideH_m  = max(8, aSusp.rideHeight or 24) * IN_TO_M
        local airTrav_m = max(0, (aSusp.airTravel ~= nil and aSusp.airTravel or 6) * IN_TO_M)
        c[row + 5] = clamp(rideH_m * 0.95, 0.18, 1.50)
        c[row + 6] = -airTrav_m
    end

    -- Tires: store TireSimV2 compound & per-axle specs + reversible Stage ratios
    local compName = compoundName or "Sport"
    local fTire = getAxleTire(true)
    local rTire = getAxleTire(false)

    local staticCornerKN = cornerMassT * 9.800000190734863
    local tireSimSpecs = {
        tireCfg  = tireCfg,
        frontCfg = fTire,
        rearCfg  = rTire,
        front    = Tires.buildAxleSpec(fTire, compName, staticCornerKN, tireCfg, "Front"),
        rear     = Tires.buildAxleSpec(rTire, compName, staticCornerKN, tireCfg, "Rear"),
    }

    -- Brake configuration is owned by the braking module.
    local braking = Braking.configure(engineCfg, targetMassT, massScale)
    c[152], c[153], c[154] = Aero.buildSolverCoefficients(aeroCfg, massScale)

    -- Transmission, Ratios & Automatic gearSpeeds Calibration (unlimited forward + reverse gears)
    local top = #DEFAULT_VEHICLE_PARAMETERS.forwardGearRatios
    local gearRatioMap = { [1] = 0 }
    local revList = {}
    local fwdList = {}

    local revSrc = engineCfg.ReverseGearRatios
    local fwdSrc = engineCfg.ForwardGearRatios or engineCfg.Gearbox_Ratios or engineCfg.gearRatios or engineCfg.GearRatios
    if type(fwdSrc) == "table" and #fwdSrc >= 1 then
        local zeroIdx = nil
        for idx = 1, #fwdSrc do
            if fwdSrc[idx] == 0 then zeroIdx = idx; break end
        end
        if zeroIdx and not revSrc then
            for idx = 1, zeroIdx - 1 do revList[#revList + 1] = -math.abs(fwdSrc[idx]) end
            for idx = zeroIdx + 1, #fwdSrc do fwdList[#fwdList + 1] = math.abs(fwdSrc[idx]) end
        else
            for idx = 1, #fwdSrc do
                if fwdSrc[idx] and fwdSrc[idx] ~= 0 then fwdList[#fwdList + 1] = math.abs(fwdSrc[idx]) end
            end
        end
    end
    if type(revSrc) == "table" and #revSrc >= 1 then
        revList = {}
        for idx = 1, #revSrc do
            if revSrc[idx] and revSrc[idx] ~= 0 then revList[#revList + 1] = -math.abs(revSrc[idx]) end
        end
    elseif #revList == 0 then
        revList[1] = -math.abs(engineCfg.ReverseRatio or c[116] or 3.45)
    end
    if #fwdList == 0 then
        for i = 1, top do fwdList[i] = math.abs(c[117 + i] or 1.0) end
    end

    top = #fwdList
    local minGear = 1 - #revList
    for i = 1, #revList do gearRatioMap[1 - i] = revList[i] end
    for i = 1, #fwdList do gearRatioMap[1 + i] = fwdList[i] end
    c[116] = gearRatioMap[0] or -3.45
    c[117] = 0
    for i = 1, 6 do c[117 + i] = gearRatioMap[1 + i] or 0 end

    -- Engine RPM Grid
    if engineCfg.idleRPM then c[124] = max(500, engineCfg.idleRPM) end
    if engineCfg.redlineRPM then c[125] = max(c[124] + 1500, engineCfg.redlineRPM) end
    c[126] = max(c[125] + 250, engineCfg.limiterRPM or (c[125] + 500))

    local rawFinal = engineCfg.finalDrive or engineCfg.FinalDrive or 4.10
    local fwdSpeeds = engineCfg.ForwardGearSpeeds or engineCfg.GearSpeeds or engineCfg.gearSpeeds
    local revSpeeds = engineCfg.ReverseGearSpeeds

    if engineCfg.AutoCalibrateRatiosFromSpeeds == true and rawFinal > 0.05 then
        local kNumerator = (c[125] * 2.0 * math.pi * avgRadiusM * 3.6) / (60.0 * rawFinal)
        if type(fwdSpeeds) == "table" and #fwdSpeeds >= 1 then
            fwdList = {}
            for i = 1, #fwdSpeeds do
                local spd = tonumber(fwdSpeeds[i]) or 0
                if spd > 1 then
                    fwdList[#fwdList + 1] = clamp(kNumerator / spd, 0.2, 15.0)
                end
            end
            if #fwdList >= 1 then
                top = #fwdList
                for i = 1, #fwdList do gearRatioMap[1 + i] = fwdList[i] end
                for i = 1, 6 do c[117 + i] = gearRatioMap[1 + i] or 0 end
            end
        end
        if type(revSpeeds) == "table" and #revSpeeds >= 1 then
            revList = {}
            for i = 1, #revSpeeds do
                local spd = tonumber(revSpeeds[i]) or 0
                if spd > 1 then
                    revList[#revList + 1] = -clamp(kNumerator / spd, 0.2, 15.0)
                end
            end
            if #revList >= 1 then
                minGear = 1 - #revList
                for i = 1, #revList do gearRatioMap[1 - i] = revList[i] end
                c[116] = gearRatioMap[0] or -3.45
            end
        end
    elseif engineCfg.AutoCalibrateFinalDrive == true then
        local topGearIdx = 1 + top
        local topRatio = abs(gearRatioMap[topGearIdx] or 0.763)
        local targetTopKmh = (type(fwdSpeeds) == "table") and fwdSpeeds[#fwdSpeeds] or nil
        if targetTopKmh and targetTopKmh > 60 and topRatio > 0.1 then
            local calFinal = (c[125] * 2.0 * math.pi * avgRadiusM * 3.6) / (60.0 * topRatio * targetTopKmh)
            if calFinal > rawFinal * 1.12 or calFinal < rawFinal * 0.88 then
                rawFinal = clamp(calFinal, rawFinal * 0.85, rawFinal * 1.56)
            end
        end
    end
    c[110] = rawFinal
    c[111] = rawFinal
    local shiftDelay = clamp(tonumber(engineCfg.shiftDelay) or 0.14, 0.01, 2.0)
    c[115] = 1.0 / max(0.02, shiftDelay * 0.45)
    local frontDriveShare = clamp((engineCfg.PowerDistribution or 0) / 100, 0, 1)
    local rearDriveShare  = 1.0 - frontDriveShare
    c[112] = rearDriveShare

    -- Engine Torque Curve (VirtualEngine torqueTable + Engine Specs + Turbo Boost -> kN.m)
    local baseNm = (engineCfg.baseTorque or 520) * (engineCfg.TorqueMultiplier or 1.25) * (engineCfg.ForceMult or 1.0)
    if type(engineCfg.torqueTable) == "table" and #engineCfg.torqueTable >= 2 then
        local loRpm, redRpm, limRpm = c[124], c[125], c[126]
        local spanAll = max(500, limRpm - loRpm)
        local spanRed = max(500, redRpm - loRpm)
        local cyl = engineCfg.Engine_Cylinders or 4
        local bore = engineCfg.Engine_Bore or 87
        local stroke = engineCfg.Engine_Stroke or 84
        local dispL = (math.pi * 0.25 * (bore * 0.1)^2 * (stroke * 0.1) * cyl) / 1000
        local dispBonus = clamp(0.95 + (dispL - 1.8) * 0.04, 0.90, 1.18)
        local airflow = engineCfg.Engine_Airflow or 165
        local airBonus = clamp(0.94 + (airflow - 140) / 400, 0.90, 1.15)
        local breathingMult = dispBonus * airBonus * 1.12
        for i = 0, 8 do
            local rpm = loRpm + (i / 8) * spanAll
            local u = clamp((rpm - loRpm) / spanRed, 0, 1)
            local bz = evalBezierTable(engineCfg.torqueTable, u)
            if rpm > redRpm then
                local overU = clamp((rpm - redRpm) / max(100, limRpm - redRpm), 0, 1)
                bz = bz * (1.0 - 0.06 * overU)
            end
            c[128 + i] = max(0.05, (baseNm * bz * breathingMult) / 1000)
        end
    else
        local curPeak = 0.001
        for i = 0, 8 do
            if c[128 + i] > curPeak then curPeak = c[128 + i] end
        end
        local desiredPeakKNm = baseNm / 1000
        if desiredPeakKNm > 0.05 then
            local tqScale = desiredPeakKNm / curPeak
            for i = 0, 8 do c[128 + i] = c[128 + i] * tqScale end
        end
    end

    local engineTorqueCurve, enginePeakTorqueNm
    if SimcadeEngine and engineCfg and (engineCfg.PeakTorqueNm or engineCfg.PeakHP or engineCfg.DisplacementLiters or engineCfg.TorqueCurve) then
        local spec = SimcadeEngine.synthesizeSpec(engineCfg)
        c[124] = spec.idleRPM
        c[125] = spec.redlineRPM
        c[126] = spec.limiterRPM
        c[127] = clamp(spec.flywheelInertia * 0.001, 0.00010, 0.0015)
        engineTorqueCurve = spec.torqueCurve
        enginePeakTorqueNm = spec.peakTorqueNm
        for i = 0, 8 do
            c[128 + i] = spec.torqueCurveKNm[i]
        end
        for i = 0, 2 do
            c[146 + i] = spec.brakeCurveKNm[i]
        end
    end

    -- Engine Braking
    if engineCfg.engineBrakeTorque ~= nil then
        local ebMult = max(0, tonumber(engineCfg.EngineBrakeMult) or 1.0)
        local brkKNm = clamp((engineCfg.engineBrakeTorque / 1000) * ebMult, 0.0, 5.0)
        c[146], c[147], c[148] = brkKNm * 0.45, brkKNm * 0.78, brkKNm
    else
        for i = 0, 2 do
            c[146 + i] = c[146 + i] * max(0.6, massScale)
        end
    end

    -- Zero disables the speed limiter.
    c[164] = engineCfg.SpeedLimitMPH or 0

    local up, down = Tune.shiftPoints(c, top)
    -- Ensure auto shifts happen crisply near peak power / redline without bouncing on limiter
    local redRpm = c[125]
    for g = 2, top + 1 do
        if up[g] then
            up[g] = min(up[g], redRpm * 0.935)
        end
    end
    local frontWeightBias = clamp(tonumber(vehicleCfg.frontWeightBias) or 0.50, 0.30, 0.70)
    local wheelbase_m = max(1.0, abs((c[4] or 1.32) - (c[28] or -1.32)))
    local comShiftX_m = (frontWeightBias - 0.50) * wheelbase_m

    return {
        c                 = c,
        engineTorqueCurve = engineTorqueCurve,
        enginePeakTorqueNm = enginePeakTorqueNm,
        top               = top,
        minGear           = minGear,
        gearRatioMap      = gearRatioMap,
        fwdSpeeds         = fwdSpeeds,
        reverseGearSpeeds = (engineCfg and engineCfg.ReverseGearSpeeds) or nil,
        up              = up,
        down            = down,
        T               = T,
        hull            = {},
        tireSimSpecs    = tireSimSpecs,
        steerCfg        = steerCfg,
        frontDriveShare = frontDriveShare,
        rearDriveShare  = rearDriveShare,
        turboEnabled    = (engineCfg.Turbo == true),
        shiftDelay      = shiftDelay,
        frontWeightBias = frontWeightBias,
        comShiftX_m     = comShiftX_m,
        braking         = braking,
    }
end
_G.SimcadeVehicleBuilder = SimcadeVehicleBuilder
return SimcadeVehicleBuilder

local Aero = assert(SimcadeAero, "Load simcade_aero.txt before simcade_vehicle_physics.txt")
local VehicleBuilder = assert(SimcadeVehicleBuilder, "Load simcade_vehicle_builder.txt before simcade_vehicle_physics.txt")

return {assist=1,

local function makeShiftLogic()
    local M = {}
    local function trunc(x)
        return x < 0 and math.ceil(x) or math.floor(x)
    end
    function M.shiftPoints(c, top)
        local up, down = {}, {}
        for i = 0, 11 do up[i] = 0; down[i] = 0 end
        local idle, redline, limiter = c[124], c[125], c[126]
        up[1] = (redline - idle) * c[108] + idle
        local last = top + 1
        local gear = 2
        while gear < last do
            local r0, r1 = c[108 + 9 + gear - 1], c[108 + 9 + gear]
            up[gear] = redline - 100
            down[gear + 1] = math.abs(r0) <= 1e-5 and 0 or r1 / r0 * up[gear]
            gear = gear + 1
        end
        up[gear] = limiter + 500
        return up, down
    end
    return M
end
local function makeVehicleSolver()
local M = {}
local abs, sqrt, floor, ceil, sin, cos, min, max = math.abs, math.sqrt, math.floor, math.ceil, math.sin, math.cos, math.min, math.max
local PI = math.pi
local RPM, RAD_RPM = 9.549296760559082, 0.10471975803375244
local U16_RAD = 9.587379924e-05
local SUBSTEP = 0.0044      -- flt_7FC070
local MPH = 2.2369999885559082
local function clamp(v, a, b)
    if v < a then return a elseif v > b then return b end
    return v
end
local function trunc(x)
    if x < 0 then return ceil(x) end
    return floor(x)
end
local frexp, ldexp = math.frexp, math.ldexp
local function f32(x)
    if x == 0 or x ~= x or x == math.huge or x == -math.huge then return x end
    local m, e = frexp(x)
    local bits = 24
    if e < -125 then bits = 24 - (-125 - e) end     -- a denormal keeps fewer
    if bits < 1 then return 0 * x end
    local s = ldexp(m, bits)
    local r = floor(s)
    local f = s - r
    if f > 0.5 or (f == 0.5 and r % 2 == 1) then r = r + 1 end
    return ldexp(r, e - bits)
end
M.f32 = f32
local function curve(t, x)
    if t.points then
        local points = t.points
        local first = points[1]
        if x <= first.rpm then return first.value end
        if x >= points[t.n].rpm then return points[t.n].value end
        local lower, upper = 1, t.n
        while upper - lower > 1 do
            local middle = floor((lower + upper) * 0.5)
            if x < points[middle].rpm then upper = middle else lower = middle end
        end
        local previous, current = points[lower], points[upper]
        local fraction = (x - previous.rpm) / (current.rpm - previous.rpm)
        return previous.value + (current.value - previous.value) * fraction
    end
    local v = (x - t.lo) * t.k
    local i = v < 0 and ceil(v) or floor(v)
    if i < 0 or v < 0 then return t.v[0] end
    if i >= t.n - 1 then return t.v[t.n - 1] end
    local f = v - i
    return (1 - f) * t.v[i] + f * t.v[i + 1]
end
local function points(p, x)
    local n = floor(p.len / 2)
    if x <= p[0] then return p[1] end
    if x >= p[2 * n - 2] then return p[2 * n - 1] end
    for i = 0, n - 2 do
        if x >= p[2 * i] and x < p[2 * i + 2] then
            return (p[2 * i + 3] - p[2 * i + 1]) * ((x - p[2 * i]) / (p[2 * i + 2] - p[2 * i])) + p[2 * i + 1]
        end
    end
    return p[1]
end
local function ticksOf(dt) return trunc(f32(dt) * 4000 + 0.5) end
local function vec(a, b, c) return { [0] = a, b, c } end
local function dot(a, b) return a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1] + a[2] * b[2] end
local function len(a) return sqrt(a[0] * a[0] + a[1] * a[1] + a[2] * a[2]) end
local function toWorld(R, v) return vec(v[0] * R[0] + v[1] * R[3] + v[2] * R[6], v[0] * R[1] + v[1] * R[4] + v[2] * R[7], v[0] * R[2] + v[1] * R[5] + v[2] * R[8]) end
local function toLocal(R, v) return vec(v[0] * R[0] + v[1] * R[1] + v[2] * R[2], v[0] * R[3] + v[1] * R[4] + v[2] * R[5], v[0] * R[6] + v[1] * R[7] + v[2] * R[8]) end
local function slice(c, from, to)
    local out, n = {}, 0
    for i = from, to - 1 do out[n] = c[i]; n = n + 1 end
    return out
end
local Chassis = {}
Chassis.__index = Chassis
function M.new(data)
    local self = setmetatable({}, Chassis)
    self.data = data
    self.braking = data.braking
    self.steerCfg = data.steerCfg
    self.race = nil
    self.isDrag = false
    local c = data.c
    self.c = c
    self.T = data.T
    self.top = data.top
    self.up = data.up
    self.down = data.down
    self.winScale = 1
    self.clutchK = 1
    self.ranges = { up = { [0] = -100, 0 }, down = { [0] = -100, 0 }, r3 = { [0] = -100, 0 } }
    self.now = 0
    self.yawAcc, self.yawPrev = 0, 0
    self.assist = self.T.assist     -- the car's +1124: a player's option, on by default (0x7FAA3C)
    self.manualBox = self.isDrag
    self.clutchPedal = 1
    self.m = c[68]
    self.im = 1 / self.m
    self.Iinv = vec(1 / c[72], 1 / c[77], 1 / c[82])
    self.Iinv2 = vec(1 / c[72], 1 / c[77], 1 / (c[82] * c[88]))   -- on the ground its yaw inertia counts for more
    self.cg = vec(c[0], c[1], c[2])
    self.hull = {}
    local lo, hi = vec(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge), vec(-math.huge, -math.huge, -math.huge)
    for i, p in ipairs(data.hull) do
        local h = vec(p[0] - c[0], p[1] - c[1], p[2] - c[2])
        self.hull[i] = h
        for k = 0, 2 do lo[k] = min(lo[k], h[k]); hi[k] = max(hi[k], h[k]) end
    end
    self.hullMid = vec((lo[0] + hi[0]) * 0.5, (lo[1] + hi[1]) * 0.5, (lo[2] + hi[2]) * 0.5)
    self.hullHalf = len(vec(hi[0] - self.hullMid[0], hi[1] - self.hullMid[1], hi[2] - self.hullMid[2]))
    self.pairs = {}
    self.recs = 0
    self.stepH = 0
    self.pos = vec(0, 0, 0)
    self.R = { [0] = 1, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1 }
    self.Pos = vec(0, 0, 0)
    self.P = vec(0, 0, 0)
    self.L = vec(0, 0, 0)
    self.E = 0
    self.v = vec(0, 0, 0)
    self.w = vec(0, 0, 0)
    self.a = vec(0, 0, 0)
    self.F = vec(0, 0, 0)
    self.Tq = vec(0, 0, 0)
    self.grav = 9.800000190734863
    self.damp336 = 1; self.damp340 = 1; self.mode344 = 0
    self.limit512 = 0
    self.lim516 = c[164]
    self.perf = 1
    self.restState = 0
    self.awake = true
    self.wheels = {}
    for i = 0, 3 do
        local o, tire, su = 4 + 12 * i, 52 + 8 * floor(i / 2), 92 + 8 * floor(i / 2)
        local W = { i = i, o = o, tire = tire, radius = c[o + 4], I = 0.009999999776482582, w = 0, ang = 0, T = 0, locked = 0, dir = 1, slip = 0, slip364 = 0, contact = 0, avgT = 0, Fx = 0, Fy = 0,
            world = vec(0, 0, 0), pt = vec(0, 0, 0), n = vec(0, 0, 1), load = 0, sliding = 0, brk = 0, hbk = 0, grip = 1, perf = 1, vel = vec(0, 0, 0),
            basis = { [0] = 1, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1 }, at = vec(0, 0, 0), M = { [0] = 1, 0, 0, 1 }, slipX = 0, slipY = 0, usage = 0, surface = 1, bumpZ = 0,
            ground = { p = vec(0, 0, 0), n = vec(0, 0, 1), s = 1 } }
        W.su = { i = i, row = su, x = 0, mount = vec(c[o] - c[0], c[o + 1] - c[1], c[o + 2] - c[2]), mountZ = c[o + 2], ["local"] = vec(0, 0, 0) }
        self.wheels[i] = W
    end
    local w = self.wheels
    self.steer = { a0 = 0, a1 = 0, u16 = 0 }
    self.eng = { w = 0, T = 0, tload = 0, mult = 1, lim = false, perf = 1, inductionTorqueRatio = 1.0, boostPSI = 0,
        tq = { n = 9, lo = c[124], hi = c[126], k = 8 / (c[126] - c[124]), v = slice(c, 128, 137) },
        br = { n = 3, lo = c[124], hi = c[126], k = 2 / (c[126] - c[124]), v = slice(c, 146, 149) } }
    self.dt = { clutch = 0, req = 1, gear = 1, last = 0, state = 2, shifting = 0, wt = 0, timer = 0, mem = 0, want = 1, torque = 0, split = c[112], rUp = 0, rDown = 0, r3 = 0,
        q = 0, qAt = 0 }
    self.aero = { T = 0, grounded = 0, on = 1, grip = 1 }
    self.drv = { gear = -1, want = 1, thr = 0, brk = 0, hbk = 0, gas = 0, brake = 0, handbrake = 0, reversing = false, u16Deg = 0,
        launch = 0 }
    self.ticks = 0
    self.grounded = 0
    self.heading = 0
    self.forward = 0
    self.speed = 0
    self.rpm = c[124]
    self.gearShown = 1
    self.engaged = 0
    self.throttleOut = 0
    self.clutchTq = 0
    self:place(0, 0, 0.4, 0)
    return self
end
function Chassis:place(x, y, headingRad, gz)
    local c = self.c
    local ch, sh = cos(headingRad), sin(headingRad)
    self.R = { [0] = ch, sh, 0, -sh, ch, 0, 0, 0, 1 }
    local wheelZ = c[4 + 2] - c[2]
    local rad = c[8]
    self.pos = vec(x, y, gz + rad - wheelZ)
    self.dt.q = 0; self.dt.qAt = 0
    self.Pos = vec(0, 0, 0)
    self.P = vec(0, 0, 0); self.L = vec(0, 0, 0); self.E = 0; self.v = vec(0, 0, 0); self.w = vec(0, 0, 0)
    self.yawAcc, self.yawPrev = 0, 0
    self:keepState()
    self.F = vec(0, 0, 0); self.Tq = vec(0, 0, 0)
    for i = 0, 3 do
        local W = self.wheels[i]
        W.w = 0; W.ang = 0; W.T = 0; W.locked = 0; W.avgT = 0; W.su.x = 0
    end
    local E = self.eng
    E.w = c[124] * RAD_RPM
    E.T = 0; E.tload = 0; E.lim = false
    E.inductionTorqueRatio, E.boostPSI = 1.0, 0
    local d = self.dt
    d.gear = 2; d.req = 2; d.state = 1; d.clutch = 0; d.last = 0; d.shifting = 0; d.mem = 0; d.grind = nil; d.mode = 0
    self.drv.gear = self.manualBox and 2 or -1; self.drv.reversing = false
    self.drv.reverseLaunch = false
    self.drv.reverseHold = 0
    self.drv.forwardLaunch = false
    self.drv.forwardHold = 0
    self.heldGear = self.drv.gear
    self.pairs = {}
    self:post(0)
    self.rpm = c[124]
    self.gearShown = 1
    self.forward = 0; self.speed = 0
end
-- A negative hold means this pedal has already braked the car to a near-stop.
-- A positive hold keeps the applied brake during the gap before a second press.
local function updateDirectionBrake(latched, hold, launched, pedal, otherPedal, brakeWhileMoving, speed, stopped, dt)
    hold = hold or 0
    if otherPedal >= 0.10 or abs(speed) > 0.75 then hold = 0 end
    if hold > 0 then hold = max(0, hold - dt) end
    if not launched and brakeWhileMoving and pedal > 0.25 then latched = true end
    if pedal < 0.10 then
        launched = false
        if hold < 0 then hold = 0.45 end
        if hold == 0 then latched = false end
    elseif latched and stopped and otherPedal < 0.10 and hold == 0 then
        hold = -1
    end
    return latched, hold, launched
end

function Chassis:driver(steer, gas, brake, handbrake, dt, hold, u16)
    self.ticks = self.ticks + ticksOf(dt)
    -- unoccupied keeps live tire & chassis grip
    local D = self.drv
    D.gas = gas; D.brake = brake; D.handbrake = handbrake
    local v16 = self.forward
    local v18 = D.gas
    D.hbk = D.handbrake
    -- Treat creeping downhill as motion outside this stop deadband (m/s).
    local directionChangeSpeed = 0.25
    local rollingForward = v16 > directionChangeSpeed
    local rollingBackward = v16 < -directionChangeSpeed
    local forwardBrake = D.forwardBrake or v18
    if self._vphysMode and self.classicAuto and not self.manualBox then
        local reverse = self.autoRange == 1
        local swap = reverse and self.reverseWSonReverse
        D.reversing = reverse
        D.thr = swap and (D.reverseGas or brake) or gas
        D.brk = SimcadeBraking.updatePedal(self, D, swap and forwardBrake or brake, dt)
        if self.autoRange == 0 then D.thr, D.brk = 0, 1 end
        if self.ignitionRunning == false then D.thr = 0 end
    elseif self._vphysMode and self.ignitionRunning == false then
        D.reversing = false
        D.reverseLaunch = false
        D.reverseHold = 0
        D.forwardLaunch, D.forwardHold = false, 0
        D.thr = 0
        D.brk = clamp(D.brake, 0, 1)
    elseif self._vphysMode and self.manualBox then
        D.reversing = (D.gear <= 0)
        D.thr = clamp(v18, 0, 1)
        D.brk = clamp(D.brake, 0, 1)
        if (D.reversing and rollingForward) or (D.gear > 1 and rollingBackward) then
            D.thr = 0
            D.brk = clamp(max(forwardBrake, D.brake), 0, 1)
        end
    elseif self._vphysMode and not self.manualBox then
        -- A pedal that brakes rollback stays in brake mode until a second press.
        local stopped = not rollingForward and not rollingBackward
        D.brakeLatch, D.reverseHold, D.reverseLaunch = updateDirectionBrake(
            D.brakeLatch, D.reverseHold, D.reverseLaunch, D.brake, v18,
            rollingForward, v16, stopped, dt)
        D.revBrakeLatch, D.forwardHold, D.forwardLaunch = updateDirectionBrake(
            D.revBrakeLatch, D.forwardHold, D.forwardLaunch, v18, D.brake,
            rollingBackward, v16, stopped, dt)
        -- A fresh press can select the gear that matches the current motion.
        local canStartReverse = D.reverseHold > 0
            or (not D.brakeLatch and (stopped or (not D.reversing and rollingBackward)))
        local canStartForward = D.forwardHold > 0 or (D.reversing and not rollingBackward and not D.revBrakeLatch)
        if canStartReverse and D.brake > 0.25 and v18 < 0.10 then
            -- Hold this phase until S is released so launch creep cannot reapply the brake.
            D.reversing = true
            D.brakeLatch = false
            D.reverseLaunch = true
            D.reverseHold = 0
        elseif canStartForward and v18 > 0.25 and D.brake < 0.10 then
            D.reversing = false
            D.revBrakeLatch = false
            D.forwardLaunch = true
            D.forwardHold = 0
        end
        local rawBrk
        if D.reversing then
            D.thr = clamp(D.reverseGas ~= nil and D.reverseGas or D.brake, 0, 1)
            rawBrk = clamp(forwardBrake, 0, 1)
            D.gear = 0
            local d = self.dt
            if d.gear > 0 then
                d.gear, d.req, d.shifting = 0, 0, 0
            end
        else
            D.thr = clamp(v18, 0, 1)
            rawBrk = clamp(D.brake, 0, 1)
            if D.gear <= 0 then self:gearDrive() end
        end
        if (D.reversing and not D.reverseLaunch and (rollingForward or D.brakeLatch))
            or (not D.reversing and not D.forwardLaunch and (rollingBackward or D.revBrakeLatch)) then
            D.thr = 0
            rawBrk = clamp(max(forwardBrake, D.brake), 0, 1)
        end
        if D.reverseHold > 0 or D.forwardHold > 0 then
            -- Preserve the applied brake through the release between the two presses.
            D.thr = 0
        else
            D.brk = SimcadeBraking.updatePedal(self, D, rawBrk, dt)
        end
        D.reverseGas = nil
    else
        if D.brake <= 0.6000000238418579 or D.gas >= 0.5 then
            if v16 > -2 then D.reversing = false end
        else
            D.reversing = true
        end
        local v7, v17, v21
        if v16 <= 0.5 and D.reversing then
            if v16 < -30 or v16 < -2 then
                v7 = D.brake; v17 = 0; v21 = v18
            else
                v7 = D.brake; v17 = v18; v21 = 0
            end
        else
            v7 = 0; v17 = v18; v21 = D.brake
        end
        local v19 = v7 + v7
        if v19 > 1 then v19 = 1 end
        D.brk = v21
        if v17 + 0.20000000298023224 < v19 then
            D.thr = v19
            D.gear = 0
            local d = self.dt
            if d.gear ~= 0 and (self.restState == 2 or self.speed < 0.1) then
                d.gear, d.req, d.shifting = 0, 0, 0
            end
        else
            if self.isDrag and self.gearShown == 1 then
                local v11 = D.thr
                local v23 = 1 * dt + v11
                local v12 = v11 - 1.5 * dt
                if v17 > v12 then v12 = v17 end
                if v23 < v12 then v12 = v23 end
                v17 = v12
                if D.gas > 0.6000000238418579 and self.rpm < self.c[124] + 1000 and 0.6000000238418579 > v12 then v17 = 0.6000000238418579 end
            end
            D.thr = clamp(v17, 0, 1)
            if D.gear <= 0 then self:gearDrive() end
        end
    end
    D.lamp = hold and brake or D.brk
    if hold then
        if not self.held then self.heldGear = D.gear end
        D.brk, D.gear, self.held = 1, 1, true
    elseif self.held then
        self.held = false
        if self.race then self:gearDrive() else self.drv.gear = self.manualBox and self.heldGear or -1 end
    end
    if self.clutchPedal < 1 then
        self:clutchSet(self.clutchPedal)
    elseif self.dt.mode == 3 then
        self.dt.mode = 0
    end
    if u16 ~= nil then
        local u = trunc(u16) % 65536
        self.steer.u16 = u
        local deg = u * (360 / 65536)
        if deg > 180 then deg = deg - 360 end
        self.drv.u16Deg = deg
    else
        self:steerKeyboard(steer, dt)
    end
end
function Chassis:gearDrive()
    self.drv.gear = self.manualBox and 2 or -1
end
local GRIND = 0.44999998807907104   -- flt_7A18C8
-- Selector positions follow the Red Fox order: 0 Park, 1 Reverse, 2 Neutral, 3 Drive.
function Chassis:setAutoRange(range)
    local d = self.dt
    local gear, request = 1, 1
    if range == 1 then
        gear, request = min(d.gear, 0), 0
    elseif range == 3 then
        gear, request = max(d.gear, 2), -1
    end
    self.autoRange = range
    self.drv.gear, self.drv.reversing = request, range == 1
    d.gear, d.req, d.shifting, d.grind = gear, gear, 0, nil
    if gear == 1 then d.state, d.clutch = 1, 0 end
end

function Chassis:setManual(on)
    if self.isDrag then self.userManual = on; on = true end
    self.manualBox = on
    local d = self.dt
    local D = self.drv
    D.brakeLatch = false
    D.revBrakeLatch = false
    D.reverseLaunch = false
    D.reverseHold = 0
    D.forwardLaunch, D.forwardHold = false, 0
    self.shiftHoldEnd = 0
    self.nextShiftTime = 0
    self.nextDownshiftTime = 0
    if d then
        d.shifting = 0
        d.grind = nil
        d.mode = 0
        local isStopped = abs(self.forward or 0) * 3.6 <= (self.stoppedPhaseBrakeSpeed or 5)
        if on then
            if D.reversing or d.gear <= 0 then
                local rg = (d.gear <= 0) and d.gear or 0
                d.gear = rg
                d.req = rg
                D.gear = rg
                D.reversing = true
            else
                if d.gear < 2 and not self.classicAuto then d.gear = 2 end
                d.req = d.gear
                D.gear = d.gear
                D.reversing = false
            end
            if d.gear == 1 then
                d.state = 1
                d.clutch = 0
            elseif isStopped then
                d.state = 2
                d.clutch = 0
            else
                d.state = 3
                d.clutch = 1
            end
        elseif self.classicAuto then
            self:setAutoRange(d.gear <= 0 and 1 or (d.gear == 1 and 2 or 3))
        else
            D.gear = -1
            D.reversing = false
            if d.gear <= 1 then
                d.gear = 2
            end
            d.req = d.gear
            if isStopped then
                d.state = 2
                d.clutch = 0
            else
                d.state = 3
                d.clutch = 1
            end
        end
    else
        D.gear = on and 2 or -1
        D.reversing = false
    end
    if self.held then self.heldGear = D.gear end
end
function Chassis:shiftKey(up)
    if self.parking or self.servoState then return end
    if not self.manualBox then
        if self.classicAuto then
            local range = clamp(self.autoRange + (up and 1 or -1), 0, 3)
            if range == self.autoRange then return end
            if range <= 1 and self.speed * 3.6 > self.speedToReverse then return end
            if range == 3 and -self.forward * 3.6 > self.speedToReverse then return end
            self:setAutoRange(range)
        end
        return
    end
    local D = self.drv
    if up then
        if D.gear < self.top + 1 then D.gear = D.gear + 1 end
    elseif D.gear > (self.minGear or 0) then
        D.gear = D.gear - 1
    end
end
function Chassis:resetGear()
    if not self.manualBox or self.parking or self.servoState then return end
    self.drv.gear = 1
    if self.dt then
        self.dt.req = 1
        self.dt.gear = 1
        self.dt.shifting = 0
        self.dt.grind = nil
    end
end
function Chassis:clutchSet(a)
    local d = self.dt
    d.mode = 3
    if a > 0.949999988079071 then
        d.state = 3; d.clutch = 1
    elseif a < 0.05000000074505806 then
        d.state = 1; d.clutch = 0
    elseif a == 1 then
        d.state = 3; d.clutch = a
    elseif a == 0 then
        d.state = 1; d.clutch = a
    elseif a <= d.clutch then
        d.clutch = a; d.state = 0
    else
        d.state = 2; d.clutch = a
    end
end
function Chassis:steerKeyboard(input, dt)
    Steering.updateInput(self, input, dt)
end
function Chassis:slipAngle()
    local a = (self:atan16(self.R[0], self.R[1]) - self:atan16(self.v[0], self.v[1])) % 65536
    a = a * U16_RAD
    if a > PI then return a - 2 * PI end
    return a
end
function Chassis:atan16(x, y)
    local A = self.T.atan
    local a, b = abs(x), abs(y)
    local v
    if a <= b then
        if b <= a then
            v = b == 0 and 0 or 0x2000
        else
            v = 0x4000 - 32 * A[floor(a / b * 256)]     -- (trunc of what's never below 0)
        end
    else
        v = 32 * A[floor(b / a * 256)]
    end
    if x >= 0 and y >= 0 then return v end
    if x < 0 and y >= 0 then return 0x8000 - v end
    if x < 0 and y < 0 then return 0x8000 + v end
    return 0x10000 - v
end
local PARK_SPEED = 0.3
function Chassis:updateWheelMounts()
    local c, WS = self.c, self.wheels
    for i = 0, 3 do
        local o = 4 + 12 * i
        local S = WS[i].su
        S.mount[0], S.mount[1], S.mount[2] = c[o] - c[0], c[o + 1] - c[1], c[o + 2] - c[2]
        S.mountZ = c[o + 2]
        WS[i].radius = c[o + 4]
    end
end
function Chassis:prepareFrame()
    self:updateWheelMounts()
    local c = self.c
    self.damp336 = 1; self.damp340 = 1
    self.grav = 9.800000190734863
    self.aero.on = 1
    self.aero.grip = 1
    local v11 = 1
    if self.grounded == 0 then v11 = 0 end
    if self.mode344 ~= v11 then
        if v11 == 0 then self.L[2] = self.L[2] / c[88] end
        self.mode344 = v11
    end
    if (self.drv.hbk > 0.1 and abs(self.forward) > 0.55) or self.drv.thr > 0.30000001192092896 or (self.grounded == 4 and abs(self.R[2]) > 0.15000000596046448) then self.damp336 = 0 end
    self.eng.perf = self.perf
    do local i = 0 self.wheels[i].perf = self.perf end do local i = 1 self.wheels[i].perf = self.perf end do local i = 2 self.wheels[i].perf = self.perf end do local i = 3 self.wheels[i].perf = self.perf end
    if self.assist ~= 0 then
        local v27 = c[163]
        if v27 == 0 then
            local v24 = self.forward >= 1 and self:slipAngle() or 0
            if v24 * self.L[2] < 0 and abs(v24) > 0.10000000149011612 then v27 = 20 end
        end
        self.limit512 = v27
    else
        self.limit512 = c[162]
    end
    self.lim516 = c[164]
    if c[112] > 0.800000011920929 then self.dt.split = c[112] end
end
function Chassis:updateDriverState(dt)
    local D, E, Dt = self.drv, self.eng, self.dt
    if self.gearShown ~= 1 then
        if D.thr <= D.brk or D.hbk >= D.thr then
            if D.thr < 0.5 then D.thr = 0 end
        else
            if D.brk < 0.5 then D.brk = 0 end
            if D.hbk < 0.5 then D.hbk = 0 end
        end
    end
    local v12 = D.thr
    if v12 > 0.15000000596046448 then
        if v12 > 0 and Dt.gear > 1 and E.w * RPM < 3000 and 0.5 > v12 then v12 = 0.5 end
    else
        v12 = 0
    end
    self:steerWheels(self.steer.u16)
    if self.dt.shifting ~= 0 then
        v12 = 0
    end
    E.T = v12
    if D.want > 1 and D.want <= self.top + 1 then
        if Dt.want == 1 then Dt.timer = 0 end
        Dt.want = D.want
    end
    D.gear = self:gearRequest(D.gear, self.rpm, dt)
    D.want = Dt.want
    self.aero.T = v12
    self.aero.grounded = self.grounded * 0.25
end
function Chassis:steerWheels(u16)
    Steering.applyWheelAngles(self, u16)
end
function Chassis:gearRequest(a2, a3in, dt)
    if self._vphysMode and not self.manualBox and SimcadeGearbox and SimcadeGearbox.evaluateAutoShift then
        local d = self.dt
        if a2 == -1 and d.gear > 1 and d.shifting == 0 then
            local targetG = SimcadeGearbox.evaluateAutoShift(self, dt)
            if targetG then
                d.req = targetG
            end
            return a2
        elseif a2 == 0 and self.drv and self.drv.reversing and d.gear <= 0 then
            if d.shifting == 0 then
                local targetG = SimcadeGearbox.evaluateAutoShift(self, dt)
                if targetG then
                    d.req = targetG
                end
            end
            return a2
        end
    end
    local d, c = self.dt, self.c
    local a3 = a3in
    if a3 <= -1 then a3 = self.eng.w * RPM end
    local v6 = d.gear
    local manualReverseGear = self.manualBox and a2 <= 0 and a2 >= (self.minGear or 0)
    if a2 == -1 and not manualReverseGear then
        d.want = 1
        if v6 <= 1 then
            d.req = 2
            return a2
        end
        if d.state == 3 then
            local v24 = self:shiftUp(v6, false)
            local v27 = d.rDown + self.down[v6]
            local v7 = 0
            if v6 == 2 then
                v27 = self.eng.T >= 0.20000000298023224 and 0 or c[124] + 100
            else
                local up = self:shiftUp(v6 - 1, true)
                v7 = up * (self:gearRatio(v6) / max(0.05, self:gearRatio(v6 - 1))) - 200
            end
            if self._vphysMode then
                local wRad = max(0.20, self.wheels[2].radius or 0.34)
                local roadMS = max(0, self.forward)
                local curRatio = abs(self:gearRatio(v6))
                if curRatio < 0.01 then curRatio = 1 end
                local roadSyncRPM = (roadMS / wRad) * c[111] * curRatio * RPM
                local wheelSpinMS = max(
                    abs(self.wheels[2].w or 0) * wRad,
                    abs(self.wheels[0].w or 0) * (self.wheels[0].radius or wRad)
                )
                local isWheelSpinningWildly = (wheelSpinMS - roadMS) > 7.78 -- > 28 km/h spin excess
                if a3 < v24 or v6 >= self.top + 1 then
                    if v6 > 2 and (self.now or 0) >= (self.downshiftCooldownEnd or 0) then
                        local lowerRatio = abs(self:gearRatio(v6 - 1))
                        local projectedLowerRPM = (curRatio > 0.05) and (roadSyncRPM * (lowerRatio / curRatio)) or 99999
                        local safeDownCap = (c[125] or 8000) * 0.85
                        if roadSyncRPM <= v27 and roadSyncRPM <= v7 and projectedLowerRPM < safeDownCap then
                            d.req = v6 - 1
                            self.downshiftCooldownEnd = (self.now or 0) + 0.35
                        end
                    end
                elseif not isWheelSpinningWildly then
                    local ok = (self.grounded >= 2) and (roadSyncRPM >= v24 * 0.78)
                    if ok then d.req = v6 + 1 end
                end
            else
                if a3 < v24 or v6 >= self.top + 1 then
                    if a3 <= v27 and a3 <= v7 then d.req = v6 - 1 end
                else
                    local ok = true
                    for i = 0, 3 do
                        local W = self.wheels[i]
                        ok = ok and W.contact ~= 0 and W.slip < 4
                    end
                    if ok then d.req = v6 + 1 end
                end
            end
        end
        return a2
    end
    if a2 ~= -2 or manualReverseGear then
        if d.mode == 3 then
            local g = d.gear
            if a2 ~= g then
                if self.manualBox then
                    if d.state == 1 then
                        d.gear = a2
                        d.req = a2
                        d.shifting = 0
                    else
                        d.req = a2
                        d.shifting = 0
                    end
                    d.grind = nil
                else
                    if d.state ~= 1 then d.shifting = 0; d.grind = self.now; return g end
                    if d.shifting == 0 then d.shifting = 1; d.req = a2 end
                end
            end
            if self.manualBox and d.state == 1 then return a2 end
            return g
        end
        if a2 ~= d.gear and a2 ~= d.req then
            d.req = a2
        end
        return a2
    end
    return a2
end
function Chassis:shiftUp(g, low)
    local d, c = self.dt, self.c
    local v3 = g == 3 and d.r3 or d.rUp
    if low then v3 = self.ranges.up[0] end
    return min(self.isDrag and c[126] or c[125], v3 + self.up[g])
end
function Chassis:integrate(h)
    local WS, E, d, c = self.wheels, self.eng, self.dt, self.c
    if self.lim516 ~= 0 then
        local v3 = self.perf * self.lim516 + 1.899999976158142
        local v28, v8 = self.R[2], nil
        if v28 < 0 then
            v28 = -v28
            if v28 > 0.3 then v28 = 0.3 end
            v8 = (1.1 - 1) / 0.3 * v28 + 1
        else
            if v28 > 0.3 then v28 = 0.3 end
            v8 = 1 - (1 - 0.9) / 0.3 * v28
        end
        local fwd = vec(self.R[0], self.R[1], self.R[2])
        if sqrt(self.v[0] * self.v[0] + self.v[1] * self.v[1]) * 2.2360248565673828 > v3 * v8 and dot(fwd, self.F) > 0 then
            local lo = toLocal(self.R, self.F)
            local v10 = -(5 * v28)
            if v10 > -0.5 then v10 = -0.5 end
            lo[0] = v10
            self.F = toWorld(self.R, lo)
        end
    end
    local v0, w, R = self.v, self.w, self.R
    local D = self.deriv
    if not D then D = { pos = vec(0, 0, 0), R = {}, P = vec(0, 0, 0), L = vec(0, 0, 0), w = {} }; self.deriv = D end
    local dPos, dR, dP, dL = D.pos, D.R, D.P, D.L
    dPos[0], dPos[1], dPos[2] = v0[0], v0[1], v0[2]
    dR[0], dR[1], dR[2] = R[2] * w[1] - w[2] * R[1], w[2] * R[0] - R[2] * w[0], w[0] * R[1] - R[0] * w[1]
    dR[3], dR[4], dR[5] = R[5] * w[1] - w[2] * R[4], w[2] * R[3] - R[5] * w[0], w[0] * R[4] - R[3] * w[1]
    dR[6], dR[7], dR[8] = R[8] * w[1] - w[2] * R[7], w[2] * R[6] - R[8] * w[0], w[0] * R[7] - R[6] * w[1]
    dP[0], dP[1], dP[2] = self.F[0], self.F[1], self.F[2]
    dL[0], dL[1], dL[2] = self.Tq[0], self.Tq[1], self.Tq[2]
    local wl = len(w)
    if self.damp340 ~= 0 then
        local v19 = -curve(self.T.ang_damp, wl)
        dL[0] = dL[0] + v19 * self.L[0]; dL[1] = dL[1] + v19 * self.L[1]; dL[2] = dL[2] + v19 * self.L[2]
    end
    local v29 = len(v0) / 4
    local v21 = wl / 2
    if v21 > v29 then v29 = v21 end
    local dE = curve(self.T.rest, v29) <= self.E and -10 or 0.10000000149011612
    if self.damp336 ~= 0 then
        local a, b = -(2 * self.E), -(8 * self.E)
        dL[0] = dL[0] + a * self.L[0]; dL[1] = dL[1] + a * self.L[1]; dL[2] = dL[2] + a * self.L[2]
        dP[0] = dP[0] + b * self.P[0]; dP[1] = dP[1] + b * self.P[1]; dP[2] = dP[2] + b * self.P[2]
    end
    if self.limit512 ~= 0 then
        if clamp(self.Tq[2], -self.limit512, self.limit512) ~= self.Tq[2] then
            dL[2] = self.Tq[2] > 0 and self.limit512 or -self.limit512
        end
    end
    local dw = D.w
    for i = 0, 3 do
        local W = WS[i]
        if W.contact == 0 then
            -- No contact patch to enforce rolling speed or react drive torque.
            W.avgT = 0
            if W.locked ~= 0 then
                W.w, dw[i] = 0, 0
            else
                local momentum = W.I * W.w + h * (W.T - 0.002 * W.w)
                local brakeImpulse = h * (W.airBrakeTorque or 0)
                momentum = momentum - clamp(momentum, -brakeImpulse, brakeImpulse)
                dw[i] = (momentum / W.I - W.w) / h
            end
        else
            local torque = (W.T - W.radius * W.Fx + W.avgT) * 0.5
            W.avgT = torque
            local a = (torque - 0.0020000000949949026 * W.w) / W.I
            if W.locked ~= 0 then a = 0 end
            if W.sliding == 0 and h > 0.0010000000474974513 then a = a - W.slip / (W.radius * h) end
            dw[i] = a
        end
    end
    local dEng = (self:torque() + E.tload) / c[127]
    local dCl = 0
    if d.mode ~= 3 then
        local rCl = c[115] * self.clutchK
        if d.q > 0 then rCl = rCl * (d.q + 1) elseif d.q < 0 and d.req >= 3 then rCl = rCl * 0.3499999940395355 end
        dCl = rCl * self.T.clutch_dir[d.state]
    end
    local pos, P, L = self.pos, self.P, self.L
    pos[0] = pos[0] + h * dPos[0]; P[0] = P[0] + h * dP[0]; L[0] = L[0] + h * dL[0]
    pos[1] = pos[1] + h * dPos[1]; P[1] = P[1] + h * dP[1]; L[1] = L[1] + h * dL[1]
    pos[2] = pos[2] + h * dPos[2]; P[2] = P[2] + h * dP[2]; L[2] = L[2] + h * dL[2]
    R[0] = R[0] + h * dR[0]; R[1] = R[1] + h * dR[1]; R[2] = R[2] + h * dR[2]
    R[3] = R[3] + h * dR[3]; R[4] = R[4] + h * dR[4]; R[5] = R[5] + h * dR[5]
    R[6] = R[6] + h * dR[6]; R[7] = R[7] + h * dR[7]; R[8] = R[8] + h * dR[8]
    self.E = self.E + h * dE
    local W = WS[0]; W.ang = W.ang + h * W.w; W.w = W.w + h * dw[0]
    W = WS[1]; W.ang = W.ang + h * W.w; W.w = W.w + h * dw[1]
    W = WS[2]; W.ang = W.ang + h * W.w; W.w = W.w + h * dw[2]
    W = WS[3]; W.ang = W.ang + h * W.w; W.w = W.w + h * dw[3]
    E.w = E.w + h * dEng
    d.clutch = d.clutch + h * dCl
    self:post(h)
end
function Chassis:post(h)
    self:postBody()
    do local i = 0
        local W = self.wheels[i]
        self:postWheel(W)
        self:postSusp(W)
    end
    do local i = 1
        local W = self.wheels[i]
        self:postWheel(W)
        self:postSusp(W)
    end
    do local i = 2
        local W = self.wheels[i]
        self:postWheel(W)
        self:postSusp(W)
    end
    do local i = 3
        local W = self.wheels[i]
        self:postWheel(W)
        self:postSusp(W)
    end
    self:postEngine()
    self:postDrive()
    self.stepH = h
    self:forces()
end
function Chassis:postBody()
    local im = self.im
    local P, v = self.P, self.v
    v[0], v[1], v[2] = P[0] * im, P[1] * im, P[2] * im
    local s = sqrt(v[0] * v[0] + v[1] * v[1] + v[2] * v[2])
    if s > 200 then
        local k = 200 / s
        P[0], P[1], P[2] = P[0] * k, P[1] * k, P[2] * k
        v[0], v[1], v[2] = P[0] * im, P[1] * im, P[2] * im
    end
    local k = im * 0.10204081237316132
    local F, a = self.F, self.a
    a[0], a[1], a[2] = F[0] * k, F[1] * k, F[2] * k
    local I = self.mode344 ~= 0 and self.Iinv2 or self.Iinv
    local R, L = self.R, self.L
    local l0, l1, l2 = L[0] * R[0] + L[1] * R[1] + L[2] * R[2], L[0] * R[3] + L[1] * R[4] + L[2] * R[5], L[0] * R[6] + L[1] * R[7] + L[2] * R[8]
    l0, l1, l2 = l0 * I[0], l1 * I[1], l2 * I[2]
    local w = self.w
    w[0], w[1], w[2] = l0 * R[0] + l1 * R[3] + l2 * R[6], l0 * R[1] + l1 * R[4] + l2 * R[7], l0 * R[2] + l1 * R[5] + l2 * R[8]
    local wl = sqrt(w[0] * w[0] + w[1] * w[1] + w[2] * w[2])
    if wl > 250 then
        local q = 250 / wl
        w[0], w[1], w[2] = w[0] * q, w[1] * q, w[2] * q
    end
    if self.E < 0 then self.E = 0 end
    F[0], F[1], F[2] = 0, 0, 0
    local Tq = self.Tq
    Tq[0], Tq[1], Tq[2] = 0, 0, 0
    if self.grav > 0 then self:addForce3(0, 0, -(self.m * self.grav), 0, 0, 0) end
end
function Chassis:addForce3(f0, f1, f2, r0, r1, r2)
    local l = sqrt(f0 * f0 + f1 * f1 + f2 * f2)
    if l > 1200 then
        local k = 1200 / l
        f0, f1, f2 = f0 * k, f1 * k, f2 * k
    end
    local F = self.F
    F[0] = F[0] + f0; F[1] = F[1] + f1; F[2] = F[2] + f2
    local t0, t1, t2 = r1 * f2 - r2 * f1, r2 * f0 - r0 * f2, r0 * f1 - r1 * f0
    local lt = sqrt(t0 * t0 + t1 * t1 + t2 * t2)
    if lt > 100 then
        local k = 100 / lt
        t0, t1, t2 = t0 * k, t1 * k, t2 * k
    end
    local Tq = self.Tq
    Tq[0] = Tq[0] + t0; Tq[1] = Tq[1] + t1; Tq[2] = Tq[2] + t2
end
function Chassis:postWheel(W)
    W.Fx = 0; W.Fy = 0; W.T = 0; W.slip = 0
    if W.dir == 0 then
        if W.w < 0 then W.w = 0 end
    elseif W.dir == 2 and W.w > 0 then
        W.w = 0
    end
    W.dir = W.w > 1e-5 and 0 or (W.w >= -1e-5 and 1 or 2)
    if W.ang > 6.283185 then W.ang = W.ang - 6.283185 elseif W.ang < -6.283185 then W.ang = W.ang + 6.283185 end
end
function Chassis:postSusp(W)
    local S, c, R = W.su, self.c, self.R
    local row = S.row
    local mt, pos = S.mount, self.pos
    local m0, m1, m2 = mt[0] * R[0] + mt[1] * R[3] + mt[2] * R[6], mt[0] * R[1] + mt[1] * R[4] + mt[2] * R[7], mt[0] * R[2] + mt[1] * R[5] + mt[2] * R[8]
    local G = W.ground
    local n = G.n
    W.surface = self.isDrag and 1 or G.s
    W.bumpZ = 0
    local upn = R[6] * n[0] + R[7] * n[1] + R[8] * n[2]
    local g = -100
    if upn >= 0.0010000000474974513 then
        g = ((G.p[0] - m0 - pos[0]) * n[0] + (G.p[1] - m1 - pos[1]) * n[1] + (G.p[2] - m2 - pos[2]) * n[2] + W.bumpZ * n[2]) / upn
    else
        W.surface = 0
    end
    W.n = n
    local v7 = g + W.radius
    local maxx, minx = c[row + 5], c[row + 6]
    if v7 <= maxx then
        if v7 < minx then
            S.x = minx
        else
            S.x = v7
        end
    else
        S.x = maxx
    end
    local sl = S["local"]
    sl[0], sl[1], sl[2] = mt[0], mt[1], mt[2] + S.x
    local ca, sa = 1, 0      -- cos(0), sin(0): a back wheel's
    if W.i == 0 then local a = self.steer.a0; ca, sa = cos(a), sin(a) elseif W.i == 1 then local a = self.steer.a1; ca, sa = cos(a), sin(a) end
    local M2 = W.M
    M2[0], M2[1], M2[2], M2[3] = ca, sa, -sa, ca
    local r0, r1, r2 = sl[0] * R[0] + sl[1] * R[3] + sl[2] * R[6], sl[0] * R[1] + sl[1] * R[4] + sl[2] * R[7], sl[0] * R[2] + sl[1] * R[5] + sl[2] * R[8]
    local w, v = self.w, self.v
    local wv = W.vel
    wv[0], wv[1], wv[2] = (w[1] * r2 - w[2] * r1) + v[0], (w[2] * r0 - w[0] * r2) + v[1], (w[0] * r1 - w[1] * r0) + v[2]
    local nsa = -sa
    local B = W.basis
    B[0], B[1], B[2] = ca * R[0] + sa * R[3] + 0 * R[6], ca * R[1] + sa * R[4] + 0 * R[7], ca * R[2] + sa * R[5] + 0 * R[8]
    B[3], B[4], B[5] = nsa * R[0] + ca * R[3] + 0 * R[6], nsa * R[1] + ca * R[4] + 0 * R[7], nsa * R[2] + ca * R[5] + 0 * R[8]
    B[6], B[7], B[8] = R[6], R[7], R[8]
    local at = W.at
    at[0], at[1], at[2] = r0 + pos[0], r1 + pos[1], r2 + pos[2]
end
function Chassis:postEngine()
    local E, c = self.eng, self.c
    local lo, hi = (self.ignitionRunning == false) and 0 or (c[124] * RAD_RPM), c[126] * RAD_RPM
    if self.manualBox and self.dt.gear >= 2 and (self.clutchPedal or 1) > 0.5 then
        hi = max(hi, abs(self.dt.wt or 0))
    end
    E.w = clamp(E.w, lo, hi)
    local rpm = E.w * RPM
    self.lastTload = E.tload
    E.tload = 0
    if E.lim then
        if rpm < c[126] - 0.012500000186264515 * c[126] then E.lim = false end
    elseif rpm > -25 + c[126] then
        E.lim = true
    end
end
function Chassis:gearRatio(g)
    return (self.gearRatioMap and self.gearRatioMap[g]) or self.c[116 + (g or 1)] or 0
end
function Chassis:shaftW()
    local c, W = self.c, self.wheels
    local v2 = 0
    local sp = c[112]
    if sp >= 0.0010000000474974513 then v2 = (W[3].w + W[2].w) * 0.5 * c[111] end
    if sp <= 0.9990000128746033 then
        v2 = v2 + (W[1].w + W[0].w) * 0.5 * c[110]
        if sp >= 0.0010000000474974513 then v2 = v2 * 0.5 end
    end
    return v2 * self:gearRatio(self.dt.gear)
end
function Chassis:stalled() return self.eng.T < 0.30000001192092896 and self.c[124] * RAD_RPM + 0.0001 >= self.dt.wt end
function Chassis:matched() return abs(self.eng.w - self.dt.wt) <= 50 end
function Chassis:postDrive()
    local d, E = self.dt, self.eng
    d.wt = self:shaftW()
    local st = d.state
    if d.mode == 3 then
        E.mult = 1
        if st == 1 and d.shifting ~= 0 then d.shifting = 0; d.gear = d.req end
    elseif st == 0 then
        E.mult = 0
        if d.clutch <= 0 then
            d.clutch = 0
            d.state = 1
            if d.shifting ~= 0 then
                self.shiftHoldEnd = (self.now or 0) + (self.shiftDelay or 0)
            else
                self.shiftHoldEnd = 0
            end
        end
    elseif st == 1 then
        E.mult = 0
        d.gear = d.req
        if d.shifting ~= 0 and d.gear ~= 1 and (self.clutchPedal or 1) > 0.5 and d.wt and d.wt > 0 then
            local targetW = clamp(d.wt, self.c[124] * RAD_RPM, self.c[126] * RAD_RPM)
            local syncRate = clamp((self.substepDt or 0.005) * 8, 0.015, 0.12)
            E.w = E.w + (targetW - E.w) * syncRate
        end
        if (self.now or 0) >= (self.shiftHoldEnd or 0) and (d.shifting ~= 0 or not self:stalled()) then
            d.state = 2
            d.shifting = 0
        end
    elseif st == 2 then
        E.mult = 0.699999988079071
        if d.shifting ~= 0 and d.gear ~= 1 and (self.clutchPedal or 1) > 0.5 and d.wt and d.wt > 0 then
            local targetW = clamp(d.wt, self.c[124] * RAD_RPM, self.c[126] * RAD_RPM)
            local syncRate = clamp((self.substepDt or 0.005) * 12, 0.02, 0.15)
            E.w = E.w + (targetW - E.w) * syncRate
        end
        if d.clutch >= 1 then
            d.clutch = 1
            if self:matched() or (self._vphysMode and d.gear ~= 1) then d.state = 3 end
        end
        if d.shifting == 0 and self:stalled() and d.gear ~= 1 then d.state = 0 end
        if d.gear ~= d.req and d.shifting == 0 then
            d.shifting = 1; d.last = E.w * RPM; d.state = 0
        end
    elseif st == 3 then
        if d.q > 0 and d.qAt == 0 then d.qAt = self.now end
        E.mult = 1
        local g = d.gear
        -- A manually engaged over-rev stays coupled so the wheels can force engine speed above the limiter.
        local manualOverRev = self.manualBox and g >= 2 and (self.clutchPedal or 1) > 0.5 and d.wt > self.c[126] * RAD_RPM
        if g ~= 1 and self:stalled() then
            d.state = 0
        elseif g ~= 1 and abs(E.w - (d.wt or 0)) > 150 and (self.clutchPedal or 1) > 0.5 and not manualOverRev then
            d.state = 2
        end
        if g ~= 1 and (self.clutchPedal or 1) > 0.5 and d.wt and d.wt > 0 then
            local lockRate = clamp((self.substepDt or 0.005) * 16, 0.04, 0.18)
            local maxW = self.manualBox and g >= 2 and max(self.c[126] * RAD_RPM, d.wt) or self.c[126] * RAD_RPM
            E.w = clamp(E.w + (d.wt - E.w) * lockRate, self.c[124] * RAD_RPM, maxW)
        end
        if g == d.req then
            d.shifting = 0
        else
            d.shifting = 1; d.last = E.w * RPM; d.state = 0
        end
    end
    if d.grind then
        E.mult = 0
        if self.now - d.grind >= GRIND then d.grind = nil end
    elseif d.state == 3 and d.qAt > 0 then
        local e = self.now - d.qAt
        local v = (0.5 - e) / 0.5 * d.q * 2
        if v > 0 then E.mult = v + 1 end
        if e >= 0.5 then d.qAt = 0; d.q = 0 end
    end
end
function Chassis:torque()
    if self.ignitionRunning == false then return 0 end
    local E = self.eng
    local rpm = E.w * RPM
    local T = E.lim and 0 or E.mult * E.T
    local v18 = curve(E.tq, rpm) * E.perf * T * (E.inductionTorqueRatio or 1.0)
    local v14 = -(curve(E.br, rpm) * (1 - T))
    if self.manualBox and self.dt.gear >= 2 and (self.clutchPedal or 1) > 0.5 and rpm > self.c[126] then
        local overRevRatio = rpm / max(1, self.c[126])
        v14 = v14 * clamp(overRevRatio * overRevRatio, 1, 16)
    end
    if E.lim then v14 = v14 * 2 end
    local r = v14 + v18
    if r < 0 and not E.lim then
        return r * (self.dt.gear == 1 and 0.65 or 0.90)
    end
    return r
end
-- An open differential delivers equal shaft torque. The LSD starts from that
-- same split and couples wheel speeds separately in applyDiffCoupling.
local function driveAxle(a, b, torque, locked)
    local share = 0.5
    if locked then
        if a.contact == 0 and b.contact ~= 0 then share = 0
        elseif b.contact == 0 and a.contact ~= 0 then share = 1 end
    end
    if a.locked == 0 then a.T = a.T + share * torque end
    if b.locked == 0 then b.T = b.T + (1 - share) * torque end
end

function Chassis:drive(a2, outMult)
    if self.ignitionRunning == false then return end
    local d, c, WS, E = self.dt, self.c, self.wheels, self.eng
    if d.gear == 1 then return end
    if self._vphysMode and (self.drv.brk or 0) > 0.05 and (self.drv.thr or 0) < 0.05 and a2 > 0 then return end
    local wheelMult = outMult or 1.0
    d.torque = d.torque + a2 * wheelMult
    local v3 = self.grounded * 0.25
    if 0.25 > v3 then v3 = 0.25 end
    E.tload = E.tload - v3 * a2
    local v5 = a2 * wheelMult * self:gearRatio(d.gear)
    local v18 = (1 - d.split) * c[110] * v5
    local v7 = v5 * (c[111] * d.split)
    local frontActive = (not self._vphysMode) or ((1 - d.split) >= 0.001)
    local rearActive  = (not self._vphysMode) or (d.split >= 0.001)
    if frontActive then
        local locked = (self.effFrontDiffType or self.frontDiffType) == 2 or self.frontLockActive or self.allLockActive
        driveAxle(WS[0], WS[1], v18, locked)
    end
    if rearActive then
        local locked = (self.effRearDiffType or self.rearDiffType) == 2 or self.rearLockActive or self.allLockActive
        driveAxle(WS[2], WS[3], v7, locked)
    end
end
function Chassis:clutchDrive()
    local d, E = self.dt, self.eng
    d.torque = 0
    if self.clutchType == "torque_converter" then
        local engage = ((d.state == 0 or d.state == 2) and d.clutch or 1.0) * (1.0 - (self.simClutch or 0))
        if engage <= 0.001 then return end
        local rpmNow = E.w * RPM
        local wt = d.wt or 0
        local sr = clamp(wt / max(1, E.w), 0, 1.2)
        if (self.tcLockupRPM or 0) > 0 and rpmNow >= self.tcLockupRPM and sr >= 0.90 and (self.drv and self.drv.thr or 0) > 0.05 then
            self:drive(self:torque() * engage, 1.0)
            return
        end
        local coupRatio = max(0.1, self.tcCouplingRatio or 0.85)
        local tcMult = 1.0 + max(0, (self.tcMaxMult or 2.0) - 1.0) * clamp(1.0 - sr / coupRatio, 0, 1)
        local tq = self:torque()
        if E.w >= wt then
            self:drive(tq * engage, tcMult)
        else
            self:drive(tq * engage * 0.65, 1.0)
        end
        return
    end
    if d.state == 0 or d.state == 2 then
        local engage = d.clutch * (1.0 - (self.simClutch or 0))
        local thrIn = (self.drv and self.drv.thr or 0)
        local maxSlipW = 80 + 220 * clamp(thrIn, 0, 1)
        local minSlipW = (d.gear ~= 1 and thrIn > 0.05) and 0 or -maxSlipW
        local v6 = clamp(E.w - d.wt, minSlipW, maxSlipW)
        local v7 = self.c[113]
        local v8 = (d.wt - E.w) * RPM
        if d.mem ~= 0 and v8 * d.mem < 0 and abs(v8) > 2000 and abs(d.mem) > 2000 then v7 = v7 * 0.5 end
        d.mem = v8
        local clTq = v7 * engage * v6
        if v6 > 0 then
            local baseTq = max(0, self:torque())
            clTq = min(clTq, baseTq * engage * 2.2)
        end
        self:drive(clTq)
    elseif d.state == 3 then
        local engage = 1.0 - (self.simClutch or 0)
        self:drive(self:torque() * engage)
    end
end
function Chassis:shaftAcc()
    local c, W, d = self.c, self.wheels, self.dt
    local v1 = 0
    local W0, W1, W2, W3 = W[0], W[1], W[2], W[3]
    if c[112] >= 0.0010000000474974513 then v1 = ((W3.T - W3.radius * W3.Fx) / W3.I + (W2.T - W2.radius * W2.Fx) / W2.I) * 0.5 * c[111] end
    if c[112] <= 0.9990000128746033 then
        v1 = v1 + ((W1.T - W1.radius * W1.Fx) / W1.I + (W0.T - W0.radius * W0.Fx) / W0.I) * 0.5 * c[110]
        if c[112] >= 0.0010000000474974513 then v1 = v1 * 0.5 end
    end
    return v1 * self:gearRatio(d.gear)
end
function Chassis:solve()
    local WS, R = self.wheels, self.R
    self:clutchDrive()
    local W0, W1, W2, W3 = WS[0], WS[1], WS[2], WS[3]
    self:tire(W0); self:tire(W1); self:tire(W2); self:tire(W3)
    local F = self.F
    local Fl0, Fl1 = F[0] * R[0] + F[1] * R[1] + F[2] * R[2], F[0] * R[3] + F[1] * R[4] + F[2] * R[5]
    local tq = self:torque()
    local im = self.im
    local inv = (self.mode344 ~= 0 and self.Iinv2 or self.Iinv)[2]
    local M, m = W0.M, W0.su.mount
    local a0, b0, c0, d0, p0, q0, y0, r0, I0, free0 = M[0], M[1], M[2], M[3], m[0], m[1], W0.Fy, W0.radius, W0.I, W0.contact ~= 0 and W0.sliding == 0 and W0.locked == 0
    M, m = W1.M, W1.su.mount
    local a1, b1, c1, d1, p1, q1, y1, r1, I1, free1 = M[0], M[1], M[2], M[3], m[0], m[1], W1.Fy, W1.radius, W1.I, W1.contact ~= 0 and W1.sliding == 0 and W1.locked == 0
    M, m = W2.M, W2.su.mount
    local a2, b2, c2, d2, p2, q2, y2, r2, I2, free2 = M[0], M[1], M[2], M[3], m[0], m[1], W2.Fy, W2.radius, W2.I, W2.contact ~= 0 and W2.sliding == 0 and W2.locked == 0
    M, m = W3.M, W3.su.mount
    local a3, b3, c3, d3, p3, q3, y3, r3, I3, free3 = M[0], M[1], M[2], M[3], m[0], m[1], W3.Fy, W3.radius, W3.I, W3.contact ~= 0 and W3.sliding == 0 and W3.locked == 0
    local d, c, E = self.dt, self.c, self.eng
    local lock = d.state == 3
    local c127, v7 = c[127], nil
    if lock then
        v7 = 1 / c127
        if c[112] <= 0.9990000128746033 then
            local v9 = self:gearRatio(d.gear) * c[110]
            v7 = v7 + (1 - d.split) / W0.I * v9 * v9 * 0.5
        end
        if c[112] >= 0.0010000000474974513 then
            local v10 = self:gearRatio(d.gear) * c[111]
            v7 = v7 + d.split / W2.I * v10 * v10 * 0.5
        end
    end
    do local _ = 1
        local x0, x1, x2, x3 = W0.Fx, W1.Fx, W2.Fx, W3.Fx
        local fx = c0 * y0 + a0 * x0
        local fy = d0 * y0 + b0 * x0
        local sx, sy = Fl0 + fx, Fl1 + fy
        local sT = 0 + fy * p0 - fx * q0
        fx = c1 * y1 + a1 * x1
        fy = d1 * y1 + b1 * x1
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p1 - fx * q1
        fx = c2 * y2 + a2 * x2
        fy = d2 * y2 + b2 * x2
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p2 - fx * q2
        fx = c3 * y3 + a3 * x3
        fy = d3 * y3 + b3 * x3
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p3 - fx * q3
        local v43, v44 = sx * im, im * sy
        local v42 = sT * inv
        if free0 then W0.Fx = ((W0.T - r0 * x0) / I0 * r0 - ((v42 * p0 + v44) * c0 + (v43 - v42 * q0) * a0)) / r0 * I0 + x0 end
        if free1 then W1.Fx = ((W1.T - r1 * x1) / I1 * r1 - ((v42 * p1 + v44) * c1 + (v43 - v42 * q1) * a1)) / r1 * I1 + x1 end
        if free2 then W2.Fx = ((W2.T - r2 * x2) / I2 * r2 - ((v42 * p2 + v44) * c2 + (v43 - v42 * q2) * a2)) / r2 * I2 + x2 end
        if free3 then W3.Fx = ((W3.T - r3 * x3) / I3 * r3 - ((v42 * p3 + v44) * c3 + (v43 - v42 * q3) * a3)) / r3 * I3 + x3 end
        if lock then
            local v12 = (tq + E.tload) / c127
            local v6 = v12 - self:shaftAcc()
            self:drive(v6 / v7)
        end
    end
    do local _ = 2
        local x0, x1, x2, x3 = W0.Fx, W1.Fx, W2.Fx, W3.Fx
        local fx = c0 * y0 + a0 * x0
        local fy = d0 * y0 + b0 * x0
        local sx, sy = Fl0 + fx, Fl1 + fy
        local sT = 0 + fy * p0 - fx * q0
        fx = c1 * y1 + a1 * x1
        fy = d1 * y1 + b1 * x1
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p1 - fx * q1
        fx = c2 * y2 + a2 * x2
        fy = d2 * y2 + b2 * x2
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p2 - fx * q2
        fx = c3 * y3 + a3 * x3
        fy = d3 * y3 + b3 * x3
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p3 - fx * q3
        local v43, v44 = sx * im, im * sy
        local v42 = sT * inv
        if free0 then W0.Fx = ((W0.T - r0 * x0) / I0 * r0 - ((v42 * p0 + v44) * c0 + (v43 - v42 * q0) * a0)) / r0 * I0 + x0 end
        if free1 then W1.Fx = ((W1.T - r1 * x1) / I1 * r1 - ((v42 * p1 + v44) * c1 + (v43 - v42 * q1) * a1)) / r1 * I1 + x1 end
        if free2 then W2.Fx = ((W2.T - r2 * x2) / I2 * r2 - ((v42 * p2 + v44) * c2 + (v43 - v42 * q2) * a2)) / r2 * I2 + x2 end
        if free3 then W3.Fx = ((W3.T - r3 * x3) / I3 * r3 - ((v42 * p3 + v44) * c3 + (v43 - v42 * q3) * a3)) / r3 * I3 + x3 end
        if lock then
            local v12 = (tq + E.tload) / c127
            local v6 = v12 - self:shaftAcc()
            self:drive(v6 / v7)
        end
    end
    do local _ = 3
        local x0, x1, x2, x3 = W0.Fx, W1.Fx, W2.Fx, W3.Fx
        local fx = c0 * y0 + a0 * x0
        local fy = d0 * y0 + b0 * x0
        local sx, sy = Fl0 + fx, Fl1 + fy
        local sT = 0 + fy * p0 - fx * q0
        fx = c1 * y1 + a1 * x1
        fy = d1 * y1 + b1 * x1
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p1 - fx * q1
        fx = c2 * y2 + a2 * x2
        fy = d2 * y2 + b2 * x2
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p2 - fx * q2
        fx = c3 * y3 + a3 * x3
        fy = d3 * y3 + b3 * x3
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p3 - fx * q3
        local v43, v44 = sx * im, im * sy
        local v42 = sT * inv
        if free0 then W0.Fx = ((W0.T - r0 * x0) / I0 * r0 - ((v42 * p0 + v44) * c0 + (v43 - v42 * q0) * a0)) / r0 * I0 + x0 end
        if free1 then W1.Fx = ((W1.T - r1 * x1) / I1 * r1 - ((v42 * p1 + v44) * c1 + (v43 - v42 * q1) * a1)) / r1 * I1 + x1 end
        if free2 then W2.Fx = ((W2.T - r2 * x2) / I2 * r2 - ((v42 * p2 + v44) * c2 + (v43 - v42 * q2) * a2)) / r2 * I2 + x2 end
        if free3 then W3.Fx = ((W3.T - r3 * x3) / I3 * r3 - ((v42 * p3 + v44) * c3 + (v43 - v42 * q3) * a3)) / r3 * I3 + x3 end
        if lock then
            local v12 = (tq + E.tload) / c127
            local v6 = v12 - self:shaftAcc()
            self:drive(v6 / v7)
        end
    end
    do local _ = 4
        local x0, x1, x2, x3 = W0.Fx, W1.Fx, W2.Fx, W3.Fx
        local fx = c0 * y0 + a0 * x0
        local fy = d0 * y0 + b0 * x0
        local sx, sy = Fl0 + fx, Fl1 + fy
        local sT = 0 + fy * p0 - fx * q0
        fx = c1 * y1 + a1 * x1
        fy = d1 * y1 + b1 * x1
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p1 - fx * q1
        fx = c2 * y2 + a2 * x2
        fy = d2 * y2 + b2 * x2
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p2 - fx * q2
        fx = c3 * y3 + a3 * x3
        fy = d3 * y3 + b3 * x3
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p3 - fx * q3
        local v43, v44 = sx * im, im * sy
        local v42 = sT * inv
        if free0 then W0.Fx = ((W0.T - r0 * x0) / I0 * r0 - ((v42 * p0 + v44) * c0 + (v43 - v42 * q0) * a0)) / r0 * I0 + x0 end
        if free1 then W1.Fx = ((W1.T - r1 * x1) / I1 * r1 - ((v42 * p1 + v44) * c1 + (v43 - v42 * q1) * a1)) / r1 * I1 + x1 end
        if free2 then W2.Fx = ((W2.T - r2 * x2) / I2 * r2 - ((v42 * p2 + v44) * c2 + (v43 - v42 * q2) * a2)) / r2 * I2 + x2 end
        if free3 then W3.Fx = ((W3.T - r3 * x3) / I3 * r3 - ((v42 * p3 + v44) * c3 + (v43 - v42 * q3) * a3)) / r3 * I3 + x3 end
        if lock then
            local v12 = (tq + E.tload) / c127
            local v6 = v12 - self:shaftAcc()
            self:drive(v6 / v7)
        end
    end
    do local _ = 5
        local x0, x1, x2, x3 = W0.Fx, W1.Fx, W2.Fx, W3.Fx
        local fx = c0 * y0 + a0 * x0
        local fy = d0 * y0 + b0 * x0
        local sx, sy = Fl0 + fx, Fl1 + fy
        local sT = 0 + fy * p0 - fx * q0
        fx = c1 * y1 + a1 * x1
        fy = d1 * y1 + b1 * x1
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p1 - fx * q1
        fx = c2 * y2 + a2 * x2
        fy = d2 * y2 + b2 * x2
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p2 - fx * q2
        fx = c3 * y3 + a3 * x3
        fy = d3 * y3 + b3 * x3
        sx = sx + fx; sy = sy + fy
        sT = sT + fy * p3 - fx * q3
        local v43, v44 = sx * im, im * sy
        local v42 = sT * inv
        if free0 then W0.Fx = ((W0.T - r0 * x0) / I0 * r0 - ((v42 * p0 + v44) * c0 + (v43 - v42 * q0) * a0)) / r0 * I0 + x0 end
        if free1 then W1.Fx = ((W1.T - r1 * x1) / I1 * r1 - ((v42 * p1 + v44) * c1 + (v43 - v42 * q1) * a1)) / r1 * I1 + x1 end
        if free2 then W2.Fx = ((W2.T - r2 * x2) / I2 * r2 - ((v42 * p2 + v44) * c2 + (v43 - v42 * q2) * a2)) / r2 * I2 + x2 end
        if free3 then W3.Fx = ((W3.T - r3 * x3) / I3 * r3 - ((v42 * p3 + v44) * c3 + (v43 - v42 * q3) * a3)) / r3 * I3 + x3 end
        if lock then
            local v12 = (tq + E.tload) / c127
            local v6 = v12 - self:shaftAcc()
            self:drive(v6 / v7)
        end
    end
    if self.tireSimSpecs then
        local coasting = (self.drv.thr or 0) < 0.05 and (self.drv.brk or 0) < 0.05 and (self.drv.hbk or 0) < 0.05
        for i = 0, 3 do
            local W = WS[i]
            if W.contact ~= 0 and coasting and W.T < 0 then
                local pLon = max(0, W.peakLongKN or W.load or 0)
                local maxCoastT = pLon * 0.85 * max(0.15, W.radius)
                if W.T < -maxCoastT then W.T = -maxCoastT end
            end
            if W.contact ~= 0 and W.sliding == 0 and W.locked == 0 then
                local pLon = max(0, W.peakLongKN or W.load or 0)
                W.Fx = clamp(W.T / max(0.15, W.radius), -pLon, pLon)
            end
        end
    end
    self:ellipse(W0); self:ellipse(W1); self:ellipse(W2); self:ellipse(W3)
    self:afterSolve()
end
function Chassis:applyDiffCoupling()
    local c, W = self.c, self.wheels
    self.frontDiffLocked = false
    self.rearDiffLocked = false
    local function couple(a, b, diffType, stiffness, lockRatio, forceLock)
        if a.locked ~= 0 and b.locked ~= 0 then return false end
        local wa, wb = a.w, b.w
        local isLocked = false
        if forceLock or diffType == 2 then
            local avg = (wa + wb) * 0.5
            a.w = avg
            b.w = avg
            isLocked = true
        elseif (diffType == nil or diffType == 1) then
            local dw = abs(wa - wb)
            if dw > 0.2 then
                local stiffScale = clamp((stiffness or 3.0) * 0.08, 0.05, 1.0)
                local maxRatio = clamp(lockRatio or 0.90, 0.05, 1.0)
                local lockFrac = clamp((dw - 0.2) * stiffScale, 0, maxRatio)
                local avg = (wa + wb) * 0.5
                a.w = wa + (avg - wa) * lockFrac
                b.w = wb + (avg - wb) * lockFrac
                isLocked = lockFrac > 0.05
            end
        end
        return isLocked
    end
    local rType = self.effRearDiffType ~= nil and self.effRearDiffType or self.rearDiffType
    local fType = self.effFrontDiffType ~= nil and self.effFrontDiffType or self.frontDiffType
    if c[112] >= 0.001 then
        self.rearDiffLocked = couple(W[2], W[3], rType, self.effRearStiffness or self.rearLSDStiffness, self.effRearLockRatio or self.rearLSDLockRatio, self.rearLockActive or self.allLockActive)
    end
    if c[112] <= 0.999 then
        self.frontDiffLocked = couple(W[0], W[1], fType, self.effFrontStiffness or self.frontLSDStiffness, self.effFrontLockRatio or self.frontLSDLockRatio, self.frontLockActive or self.allLockActive)
    end
    local cType = self.allLockActive and 2 or (self.centerDiffType or 0)
    if cType > 0 and c[112] >= 0.001 and c[112] <= 0.999 then
        local wF = (W[0].w + W[1].w) * 0.5
        local wR = (W[2].w + W[3].w) * 0.5
        local cLock = (cType == 2) and 1.0 or clamp(abs(wF - wR) * 0.15, 0, 0.85)
        if cLock > 0.01 then
            local wAvg = (wF + wR) * 0.5
            local dF = (wAvg - wF) * cLock
            local dR = (wAvg - wR) * cLock
            W[0].w = W[0].w + dF; W[1].w = W[1].w + dF
            W[2].w = W[2].w + dR; W[3].w = W[3].w + dR
        end
    end
end
function Chassis:afterSolve()
    if self._vphysMode then
        self:applyDiffCoupling()
    end
    if self.ignitionRunning == false then
        self.eng.tload = 0
        return
    end
    local d, E = self.dt, self.eng
    if self._vphysMode and d.gear ~= 1 and self.grounded > 0 then
        local dw = self:shaftW() - E.w
        local lim = abs(self:torque())
        if d.state == 3 then
            E.tload = E.tload + clamp(dw * self.c[113] * 0.65, -lim, lim)
        end
        if E.T > 0.1 then
            E.tload = clamp(E.tload, -lim * 0.95, lim)
        end
    elseif d.gear ~= 1 and d.state == 3 and self.grounded > 0 then
        local dw = self:shaftW() - E.w
        local avgSlip = self:avgUsage()
        if E.T <= 0.20000000298023224 or avgSlip <= 0.10000000149011612 then
            local lim = abs(self:torque())
            E.tload = E.tload + clamp(dw * self.c[113], -lim, lim)
        else
            d.wt = E.w
            self:syncWheels(E.w)
        end
    end
    if d.gear == 2 then
        local v6, v7 = self:torque(), E.tload
        local v12 = v7 + v6
        if v7 < v6 and v12 < 0 then E.tload = E.tload + points(self.T.first_gear, v12) * self.T.firstGearLoadScale * v12 end
    end
end
function Chassis:avgUsage()
    local c, W = self.c, self.wheels
    local v2, v4 = 0, 0
    if c[112] >= 0.0010000000474974513 then v2 = W[3].slip364 + W[2].slip364; v4 = 2 end
    if c[112] <= 0.9990000128746033 then v4 = v4 + 2; v2 = v2 + W[1].slip364 + W[0].slip364 end
    return v2 / v4
end
function Chassis:syncWheels(a2)
    local c, W, d = self.c, self.wheels, self.dt
    local v3 = a2 - self:shaftW()
    local fast = (not self._vphysMode) and (self.speed * MPH < 40)
    self.frontDiffLocked = false
    self.rearDiffLocked = false
    local function one(a, b, final, diffType, stiffness, lockRatio, forceLock)
        local k = v3 / (self:gearRatio(d.gear) * final)
        local l = fast or (a.contact == 0 and b.contact == 0)
        local wa, wb = a.w, b.w
        local isLocked = false
        if l or forceLock or diffType == 2 then
            wb = (wb + wa) * 0.5; wa = wb
            isLocked = true
        elseif (diffType == nil or diffType == 1) then
            local dw = abs(wa - wb)
            if dw > 0.4 then
                local stiffScale = clamp((stiffness or 3.0) * 0.06, 0.05, 1.0)
                local maxRatio = clamp(lockRatio or 0.90, 0.05, 1.0)
                local lockFrac = clamp((dw - 0.4) * stiffScale, 0, maxRatio)
                local avg = (wa + wb) * 0.5
                wa = wa + (avg - wa) * lockFrac
                wb = wb + (avg - wb) * lockFrac
                isLocked = lockFrac > 0.05
            end
        end
        a.w = wa + k; b.w = wb + k
        return isLocked
    end
    local rType = self.effRearDiffType ~= nil and self.effRearDiffType or self.rearDiffType
    local fType = self.effFrontDiffType ~= nil and self.effFrontDiffType or self.frontDiffType
    if c[112] >= 0.0010000000474974513 then
        self.rearDiffLocked = one(W[2], W[3], c[111], rType, self.effRearStiffness or self.rearLSDStiffness, self.effRearLockRatio or self.rearLSDLockRatio, self.rearLockActive or self.allLockActive)
    end
    if c[112] <= 0.9990000128746033 then
        self.frontDiffLocked = one(W[0], W[1], c[110], fType, self.effFrontStiffness or self.frontLSDStiffness, self.effFrontLockRatio or self.frontLSDLockRatio, self.frontLockActive or self.allLockActive)
    end
    local cType = self.allLockActive and 2 or (self.centerDiffType or 0)
    if cType > 0 and c[112] >= 0.001 and c[112] <= 0.999 then
        local wF = (W[0].w + W[1].w) * 0.5
        local wR = (W[2].w + W[3].w) * 0.5
        local cLock = (cType == 2) and 1.0 or clamp(abs(wF - wR) * 0.15, 0, 0.85)
        if cLock > 0.01 then
            local wAvg = (wF + wR) * 0.5
            local dF = (wAvg - wF) * cLock
            local dR = (wAvg - wR) * cLock
            W[0].w = W[0].w + dF; W[1].w = W[1].w + dF
            W[2].w = W[2].w + dR; W[3].w = W[3].w + dR
        end
    end
end
function Chassis:aeroForces()
    Aero.applyForces(self)
end
function Chassis:forces()
    self:aeroForces()
    for i = 0, 3 do
        local W = self.wheels[i]
        local extLoad = self.vphysLoadsKN and self.vphysLoadsKN[i] or 0
        local gndS = (W.ground and W.ground.s) or 0
        if gndS ~= 0 and extLoad > 0.001 then
            W.contact = 1
            W.load = extLoad
            local pt, at, n = W.pt, W.at, W.n
            pt[0], pt[1], pt[2] = at[0] + n[0] * -W.radius, at[1] + n[1] * -W.radius, at[2] + n[2] * -W.radius
        else
            W.contact = 0
            W.load = 0
        end
    end
    self:solve()
    local pos = self.pos
    local shiftX = self.comShiftX_m or 0
    local ox = pos[0] + self.R[0] * shiftX
    local oy = pos[1] + self.R[1] * shiftX
    local oz = pos[2] + self.R[2] * shiftX
    do local i = 0
        local W = self.wheels[i]
        if W.contact ~= 0 then
            local f, pt = W.world, W.pt
            self:addForce3(f[0], f[1], f[2], pt[0] - ox, pt[1] - oy, pt[2] - oz)
        end
    end
    do local i = 1
        local W = self.wheels[i]
        if W.contact ~= 0 then
            local f, pt = W.world, W.pt
            self:addForce3(f[0], f[1], f[2], pt[0] - ox, pt[1] - oy, pt[2] - oz)
        end
    end
    do local i = 2
        local W = self.wheels[i]
        if W.contact ~= 0 then
            local f, pt = W.world, W.pt
            self:addForce3(f[0], f[1], f[2], pt[0] - ox, pt[1] - oy, pt[2] - oz)
        end
    end
    do local i = 3
        local W = self.wheels[i]
        if W.contact ~= 0 then
            local f, pt = W.world, W.pt
            self:addForce3(f[0], f[1], f[2], pt[0] - ox, pt[1] - oy, pt[2] - oz)
        end
    end
    local wl = len(self.w)
    if self.grounded < 2 and wl < 0.5 then
        local k = -(points(self.T.yaw_damp, wl) * self.m)
        self.Tq[0] = self.Tq[0] + k * self.w[0]; self.Tq[1] = self.Tq[1] + k * self.w[1]; self.Tq[2] = self.Tq[2] + k * self.w[2]
    end
end
local SAME_N, SAME_D = 0.995, 0.02     -- the same plane again: its normal within about 6 degrees, a point's depth within 2 cm. The chip's
local NEAR, IN = 0.0010000000474974513, 9.999999747378752e-06    -- 0x5B3D20: touching within 0.001, into it within 0.00001
local px, py, pz, ps = {}, {}, {}, {}
function Chassis:orthonormalize()
    local R = self.R
    local a0, a1, a2 = R[0], R[1], R[2]
    local l = sqrt(a0 * a0 + a1 * a1 + a2 * a2)
    if l == 0 then l = 1 end
    a0, a1, a2 = a0 / l, a1 / l, a2 / l
    local r3, r4, r5 = R[3], R[4], R[5]
    local c0, c1, c2 = a1 * r5 - a2 * r4, a2 * r3 - a0 * r5, a0 * r4 - a1 * r3
    l = sqrt(c0 * c0 + c1 * c1 + c2 * c2)
    if l == 0 then l = 1 end
    c0, c1, c2 = c0 / l, c1 / l, c2 / l
    local b0, b1, b2 = c1 * a2 - c2 * a1, c2 * a0 - c0 * a2, c0 * a1 - c1 * a0
    R[0], R[1], R[2], R[3], R[4], R[5], R[6], R[7], R[8] = a0, a1, a2, b0, b1, b2, c0, c1, c2
end
function Chassis:state(dt)
    local R, c = self.R, self.c
    local v = self.cg
    local g0, g1, g2 = v[0] * R[0] + v[1] * R[3] + v[2] * R[6], v[0] * R[1] + v[1] * R[4] + v[2] * R[7], v[0] * R[2] + v[1] * R[5] + v[2] * R[8]
    local Ps, pos = self.Pos, self.pos
    Ps[0], Ps[1], Ps[2] = pos[0] - g0, pos[1] - g1, pos[2] - g2
    self.speed = len(self.v)
    self.forward = self.v[2] * R[2] + self.v[1] * R[1] + self.v[0] * R[0]
    local g = 0
    do local i = 0
        if self.wheels[i].contact ~= 0 then g = g + 1 end
    end
    do local i = 1
        if self.wheels[i].contact ~= 0 then g = g + 1 end
    end
    do local i = 2
        if self.wheels[i].contact ~= 0 then g = g + 1 end
    end
    do local i = 3
        if self.wheels[i].contact ~= 0 then g = g + 1 end
    end
    self.grounded = g
    local d, E = self.dt, self.eng
    self.throttleOut = E.lim and 0 or E.mult * E.T
    self.gearShown = d.gear
    self.engaged = d.state == 3 and 1 or 0
    self.clutchTq = d.torque
    local v50 = E.w * RPM
    local shifting = d.shifting ~= 0 and d.req > 2
    local v65 = -(shifting and 15 or 2.5) / c[127]
    if dt > 0 and (v50 - self.rpm) / dt < v65 then
        local v53 = self.rpm + v65 * dt
        if v53 >= v50 then v50 = v53 end
    end
    self.rpm = self.rpm * 0.44999998807907104 + v50 * 0.550000011920929
    self:keepState()
end
function Chassis:keepState()
    local R, v, w = self.R, self.v, self.w
    local S = self.ps
    if not S then S = { up = vec(0, 0, 0) }; self.ps = S end
    S.h, S.v0, S.v1, S.w2 = self:atan16(R[0], R[1]), v[0], v[1], w[2]
    S.up[0], S.up[1], S.up[2] = R[6], R[7], R[8]
end
function Chassis:carryFrom(old)
    do local i = 0
        local W, O = self.wheels[i], old.wheels[i]
        W.ground, W.n, W.surface, W.contact = O.ground, O.n, O.surface, O.contact
        W.w, W.ang = O.w, O.ang
        W.su.x = O.su.x
    end
    do local i = 1
        local W, O = self.wheels[i], old.wheels[i]
        W.ground, W.n, W.surface, W.contact = O.ground, O.n, O.surface, O.contact
        W.w, W.ang = O.w, O.ang
        W.su.x = O.su.x
    end
    do local i = 2
        local W, O = self.wheels[i], old.wheels[i]
        W.ground, W.n, W.surface, W.contact = O.ground, O.n, O.surface, O.contact
        W.w, W.ang = O.w, O.ang
        W.su.x = O.su.x
    end
    do local i = 3
        local W, O = self.wheels[i], old.wheels[i]
        W.ground, W.n, W.surface, W.contact = O.ground, O.n, O.surface, O.contact
        W.w, W.ang = O.w, O.ang
        W.su.x = O.su.x
    end
    self.eng.w = old.eng.w
    local d, od = self.dt, old.dt
    d.gear, d.req, d.state, d.clutch, d.shifting, d.timer, d.want, d.last = od.gear, od.req, od.state, od.clutch, od.shifting, od.timer, od.want, od.last
    self.drv.gear, self.drv.want, self.drv.u16Deg = old.drv.gear, old.drv.want, old.drv.u16Deg
