    self.steer.a0, self.steer.a1, self.steer.u16 = old.steer.a0, old.steer.a1, old.steer.u16
    self.held, self.parked = old.held, old.parked
    self.stepH = old.stepH
    self.yawAcc, self.yawPrev = old.yawAcc, old.yawPrev
    self.manualBox, self.clutchPedal, self.heldGear = old.manualBox, old.clutchPedal, old.heldGear
    self.autoRange = old.autoRange
    d.mode, d.grind = od.mode, od.grind and od.grind - old.now + self.now
end
M.Chassis = Chassis
return M
end

SimcadeVehiclePhysics.T    = makePhysicsCurves()
SimcadeVehiclePhysics.Tune = makeShiftLogic()
SimcadeVehiclePhysics.Phys = makeVehicleSolver()


local function initializeVehiclePhysicsBridge(PhysicsApi)
    local T    = PhysicsApi.T
    local Tune = PhysicsApi.Tune
    local Phys = PhysicsApi.Phys
    local Tires = SimcadeTires
    local Braking = assert(SimcadeBraking, "Load simcade_braking.txt before simcade_vehicle_physics.txt")
    local abs, min, max, clamp = math.abs, math.min, math.max, math.clamp or function(v, a, b)
        if v < a then return a elseif v > b then return b else return v end
    end
    local floor, ceil, sqrt = math.floor, math.ceil, math.sqrt
    local trunc = function(x) return x < 0 and ceil(x) or floor(x) end

    local IN_TO_M    = 0.0254
    local M_TO_IN    = 1.0 / IN_TO_M
    local SUBSTEP    = 0.0044
    local RPM_TO_RAD = 0.10471975803375244
    local RAD_TO_RPM = 9.549296760559082
    local MPH_CONV   = 2.2369999885559082

    local evaluateMagicFormula = Tires.evaluateMagicFormula

    function PhysicsApi.mapSurface(matType, surfaceProps)
        return Tires.mapSurface(matType, surfaceProps)
    end

    local function cloneArr(src, a, b)
        local dst = {}
        for i = a, b do dst[i] = src[i] end
        return dst
    end

    function PhysicsApi.buildCarData(vehicleCfg, tireCfg, suspCfg, engineCfg, steerCfg, runtimeWheels, totalMassKg, compoundName, aeroCfg)
        return VehicleBuilder.buildCarData(vehicleCfg, tireCfg, suspCfg, engineCfg, steerCfg, runtimeWheels, totalMassKg, compoundName, aeroCfg, { T = T, Tune = Tune })
    end
    function PhysicsApi.newFromConfig(vehicleCfg, tireCfg, suspCfg, engineCfg, steerCfg, runtimeWheels, totalMassKg, compoundName, aeroCfg)
        local data = PhysicsApi.buildCarData(vehicleCfg, tireCfg, suspCfg, engineCfg, steerCfg, runtimeWheels, totalMassKg, compoundName, aeroCfg)
        local car = Phys.new(data)
        for _, wheel in ipairs(runtimeWheels or {}) do
            if wheel.physicsIndex ~= nil then car.wheels[wheel.physicsIndex].alignment = wheel end
        end
        if data.engineTorqueCurve then car.eng.tq = data.engineTorqueCurve end
        car.maxTorqueNm = data.enginePeakTorqueNm or (engineCfg and engineCfg.PeakTorqueNm) or 260
        car.baseParams = cloneArr(data.c, 0, 164)
        car.tireSimSpecs = data.tireSimSpecs
        car.compoundName = compoundName or "Sport"
        car.turboEnabled = data.turboEnabled and true or false
        car.shiftDelay = data.shiftDelay or 0.14
        car.frontWeightBias = data.frontWeightBias or 0.50
        car.comShiftX_m = data.comShiftX_m or 0
        car.clutchType             = string.lower(tostring((engineCfg and engineCfg.ClutchType) or "friction"))
        car.tcStallRPM             = (engineCfg and engineCfg.TC_StallRPM) or 2200
        car.tcMaxMult              = (engineCfg and engineCfg.TC_MaxMult) or 2.0
        car.tcCouplingRatio        = (engineCfg and engineCfg.TC_CouplingRatio) or 0.85
        car.tcLockupRPM            = (engineCfg and engineCfg.TC_LockupRPM) or 3200
        car.tcCreepTorque          = (engineCfg and engineCfg.TC_CreepTorque) or 0.12
        car.centrifugalEngageRPM   = (engineCfg and engineCfg.CentrifugalEngageRPM) or 1800
        car.centrifugalFullRPM     = (engineCfg and engineCfg.CentrifugalFullRPM) or 3200
        car.useClutchLerpInManual  = (engineCfg and engineCfg.UseClutchLerpInManual ~= nil) and engineCfg.UseClutchLerpInManual or true
        car.clutchLerp             = (engineCfg and engineCfg.ClutchLerp) or 0.4
        car.clutchLerpReverse      = (engineCfg and engineCfg.ClutchLerpReverse) or 0.5
        car.stoppedPhaseBrakeSpeed = (engineCfg and engineCfg.StoppedPhaseBrakeSpeed) or 5
        car.downshiftCooldown      = (engineCfg and engineCfg.DownshiftCooldown) or 0.45
        car.alwaysSportMode        = (engineCfg and engineCfg.AlwaysSportMode == true)
        car.comfortUpshiftRPM      = (engineCfg and engineCfg.ComfortUpshiftRPM) or 62
        car.sportUpshiftRPM        = (engineCfg and engineCfg.SportUpshiftRPM) or 90
        car.comfortDownshiftRPM    = (engineCfg and engineCfg.ComfortDownshiftRPM) or 34
        car.sportDownshiftRPM      = (engineCfg and engineCfg.SportDownshiftRPM) or 55
        car.gearRatioMap           = data.gearRatioMap
        car.minGear                = data.minGear or 0
        car.fwdGearSpeeds          = (engineCfg and (engineCfg.ForwardGearSpeeds or engineCfg.GearSpeeds)) or nil
        car.gearSpeeds             = (engineCfg and engineCfg.gearSpeeds) or nil
        car.reverseGearSpeeds      = data.reverseGearSpeeds
        car.frontDiffType     = (engineCfg and engineCfg.FrontDiffType ~= nil) and engineCfg.FrontDiffType or 1
        car.frontLSDStiffness = (engineCfg and engineCfg.FrontLSDStiffness) or 3.0
        car.frontLSDLockRatio = ((engineCfg and engineCfg.FrontLSDLockRatio) or 90) / 100
        car.frontLSDPreload   = (engineCfg and engineCfg.FrontLSDPreload) or 15.0
        car.rearDiffType      = (engineCfg and engineCfg.RearDiffType ~= nil) and engineCfg.RearDiffType or 1
        car.rearLSDStiffness  = (engineCfg and engineCfg.RearLSDStiffness) or 3.0
        car.rearLSDLockRatio  = ((engineCfg and engineCfg.RearLSDLockRatio) or 90) / 100
        car.rearLSDPreload    = (engineCfg and engineCfg.RearLSDPreload) or 15.0
        car.centerDiffType    = (engineCfg and engineCfg.CenterDiffType ~= nil) and engineCfg.CenterDiffType or 0
        car.diffLockCfg       = {
            toggleable     = (not engineCfg) or (engineCfg.DiffLockToggleable ~= false),
            frontType      = (engineCfg and engineCfg.FrontLockType) or 2,
            frontStiffness = (engineCfg and engineCfg.FrontLockStiffness) or 30.0,
            frontRatio     = ((engineCfg and engineCfg.FrontLockRatio) or 95) / 100,
            rearType       = (engineCfg and engineCfg.RearLockType) or 2,
            rearStiffness  = (engineCfg and engineCfg.RearLockStiffness) or 30.0,
            rearRatio      = ((engineCfg and engineCfg.RearLockRatio) or 95) / 100,
            centerType     = (engineCfg and engineCfg.CenterLockType) or 2,
        }
        car.frontLockActive    = false
        car.rearLockActive     = false
        car.allLockActive      = false
        car.frontActiveLockPct = 0
        car.rearActiveLockPct  = 0
        car.frontDriveShare = data.frontDriveShare or 0
        car.rearDriveShare = data.rearDriveShare or (1.0 - car.frontDriveShare)
        car.c[112] = car.rearDriveShare
        car.baseParams[112] = car.rearDriveShare
        if car.dt then car.dt.split = car.rearDriveShare end
        if car.eng then car.eng.inductionTorqueRatio, car.eng.boostPSI = 1.0, 0 end
        car.classicAuto = engineCfg and engineCfg.ClassicAuto == true
        car.speedToReverse = (engineCfg and engineCfg.SpeedToReverse) or 5
        car.reverseWSonReverse = engineCfg and engineCfg.ReverseWSonReverse ~= false
        if engineCfg and engineCfg.isAutomatic == false then
            car:setManual(true)
        elseif car.classicAuto and not car.manualBox then
            car:setAutoRange(0)
        end
        car.aeroCfg = aeroCfg
        return car
    end

    local Chassis = Phys.Chassis

    function Chassis:toggleDiffLock(which)
        if self.diffLockCfg and self.diffLockCfg.toggleable == false then return nil end
        if which == "front" then
            self.frontLockActive = not self.frontLockActive
            return self.frontLockActive
        elseif which == "rear" then
            self.rearLockActive = not self.rearLockActive
            return self.rearLockActive
        elseif which == "all" then
            self.allLockActive = not self.allLockActive
            return self.allLockActive
        end
        return nil
    end

    function Chassis:computeActiveDiff(controls)
        local cfg = self.activeDiffCfg
        local dlc = self.diffLockCfg or {}
        local thr = clamp((self.drv and self.drv.thr) or (controls and controls.gas) or 0, 0, 1)
        local spdKmh = abs(self.forward or 0) * 3.6
        local steerAmt = clamp(abs((controls and controls.steer) or 0), 0, 1)

        local function evalAxle(isFront, wA, wB)
            local manualOn = self.allLockActive or (isFront and self.frontLockActive) or ((not isFront) and self.rearLockActive)
            if manualOn then
                local t = isFront and (dlc.frontType or 2) or (dlc.rearType or 2)
                local s = isFront and (dlc.frontStiffness or 30.0) or (dlc.rearStiffness or 30.0)
                local r = isFront and (dlc.frontRatio or 0.95) or (dlc.rearRatio or 0.95)
                return t, s, r, r * 100
            end
            local targeted = cfg and cfg.enabled and ((isFront and cfg.TargetFrontAxle) or ((not isFront) and cfg.TargetRearAxle))
            if not targeted then
                local baseT = isFront and self.frontDiffType or self.rearDiffType
                local baseS = isFront and self.frontLSDStiffness or self.rearLSDStiffness
                local baseR = isFront and self.frontLSDLockRatio or self.rearLSDLockRatio
                return baseT, baseS, baseR, 0
            end
            local minR = isFront and (cfg.FrontMinLockRatio or 10) or (cfg.RearMinLockRatio or 15)
            local maxR = isFront and (cfg.FrontMaxLockRatio or 95) or (cfg.RearMaxLockRatio or 40)
            local minS = isFront and (cfg.FrontMinStiffness or 1.0) or (cfg.RearMinStiffness or 5.0)
            local maxS = isFront and (cfg.FrontMaxStiffness or 30.0) or (cfg.RearMaxStiffness or 20.0)
            local slipRPM = abs((wA and wA.w or 0) - (wB and wB.w or 0)) * RAD_TO_RPM
            local declSpd = max(5, cfg.SteerDeclineSpeed or 40)
            local spdFactor = clamp((declSpd - spdKmh) / declSpd, 0, 1)
            local lockReduction = 1.0 - (steerAmt * spdFactor * 0.85)
            local lockRange = max(1, maxR - minR)
            local lockPct = (minR + thr * lockRange + slipRPM * (lockRange * 0.1) * thr * (cfg.SlipScale or 2.0) * 0.5) * lockReduction
            local dynThresh = (cfg.SlipThreshold or 12.0) * (1 + steerAmt * 2.0)
            if not (cfg.BypassCapsOnSlip and slipRPM >= dynThresh) then
                local cap = 0
                if thr > 0 then
                    if thr < 0.2 then cap = (thr / 0.2) * 25
                    elseif thr < 0.6 then cap = 25 + ((thr - 0.2) / 0.4) * 15
                    else cap = 40 + ((thr - 0.6) / 0.4) * max(0, maxR - 40) end
                end
                lockPct = min(lockPct, cap)
            end
            lockPct = clamp(lockPct, minR, maxR)
            local stiffness = minS + ((lockPct - minR) / lockRange) * (maxS - minS)
            local activeT = isFront and (cfg.FrontActiveType or 1) or (cfg.RearActiveType or 1)
            return activeT, stiffness, lockPct / 100, lockPct
        end

        local W = self.wheels
        self.effFrontDiffType, self.effFrontStiffness, self.effFrontLockRatio, self.frontActiveLockPct = evalAxle(true,  W[0], W[1])
        self.effRearDiffType,  self.effRearStiffness,  self.effRearLockRatio,  self.rearActiveLockPct  = evalAxle(false, W[2], W[3])
    end

    local function getWheelPeakGrip(self, W, spec)
        local wsMult = W._wsMult or 1.0
        local tireLoadKN = max(0, (W.load or 0) * wsMult)
        local Fz = Tires.addAeroLoadKN(tireLoadKN, self.aeroCfg, self.m or 1450, self.forward or 0, W.i < 2)
        W.effectiveLoadKN = Fz
        if Fz <= 0.001 or (W.contact or 0) == 0 then
            return 0, 0
        end
        local matType = W.ground and W.ground.matType
        local surfLat, surfLon = Tires.computeSurfaceMult(spec, W.surface or 1, matType)
        local loadRatio = clamp(Fz / max(0.5, spec.nominalLoadKN or 3.68), 0.15, 2.8)
        local loadSens  = clamp(1.0 - (spec.LoadSens or 0.12) * (loadRatio - 1.0), 0.65, 1.20)
        local wGrip = (W.grip or 1) * (W.perf or 1)
        local pLat = Fz * spec.CoF * loadSens * surfLat * spec.latScale  * wGrip
        local pLon = Fz * spec.CoF * loadSens * surfLon * spec.longScale * wGrip
        return pLat, pLon
    end

    -- The axle points left on both sides. Rotate static camber about forward, then
    -- rotate the axle about the rearward-tilted steering pivot (Rodrigues' formula).
    function Chassis:updateAlignment(W, steer)
        local wheel = W.alignment or {}
        local right = W.i == 1 or W.i == 2
        local camber = ((wheel.camberDeg or 0) + (W.travelCamberDeg or 0)) * (right and 1 or -1)
        if (wheel.legacySteeringCamber or 0) ~= 0 then
            local limit = max(0.01, self.steerCfg.Lock or self.steerCfg.maxAngle or 38)
            camber = camber - wheel.legacySteeringCamber * clamp(math.deg(steer) / limit, -1, 1)
        end
        W.hubCamberDeg = camber
        local c, k = math.rad(camber), math.rad(wheel.casterDeg or 0)
        local cy, cz = math.cos(c), math.sin(c)
        local px, pz = -math.sin(k), math.cos(k)
        local cs, sn = math.cos(steer), math.sin(steer)
        local d = pz * cz * (1 - cs)
        W.axleX = -pz * cy * sn + px * d
        W.axleY = cy * cs - px * cz * sn
        W.axleZ = cz * cs + px * cy * sn + pz * d
        if (W.beamRollDeg or 0) ~= 0 then
            local roll = math.rad(W.beamRollDeg)
            local cr, sr = math.cos(roll), math.sin(roll)
            W.axleY, W.axleZ = cr * W.axleY - sr * W.axleZ, sr * W.axleY + cr * W.axleZ
        end
    end

    function Chassis:tire(W)
        W.roadCamberRad, W.camberFy = 0, 0
        if not self.tireSimSpecs then
            W.Fx, W.Fy, W.slip, W.slipX, W.slipY = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
            return
        end
        if W.contact == 0 then
            W.Fx, W.Fy, W.peakLatKN, W.peakLongKN = 0, 0, 0, 0
            W.slip, W.slip364, W.slipX, W.slipY = 0, 0, 0, 0
            W.slipAngleRad, W.slipRatio, W.sliding, W.absActive = 0, 0, 0, false
            local brakes = self.braking
            local service = W.i < 2 and brakes.frontBrakeKNm or brakes.rearBrakeKNm
            W.airBrakeTorque = service * self.drv.brk + brakes.handbrake.torqueKNm * W.hbk
            W.locked = (W.hbk > 0.1 and brakes.handbrake.lockWheels) and 1 or 0
            if W.locked ~= 0 then W.w = 0 end
            return
        end
        local b, vel = W.basis, W.vel
        local vFwd = vel[0] * b[0] + vel[1] * b[1] + vel[2] * b[2]
        local vLat = vel[0] * b[3] + vel[1] * b[4] + vel[2] * b[5]
        local absFwd = abs(vFwd)

        local spec = (W.i < 2) and self.tireSimSpecs.front or self.tireSimSpecs.rear
        local peakLatKN, peakLongKN = getWheelPeakGrip(self, W, spec)

        local pedalBrk, vBrk, vHbk, absReleasing = Braking.applyWheelTorque(self, W, peakLongKN, vFwd, absFwd)

        local isAxleLocked = (W.i < 2 and ((self.effFrontDiffType == 2) or (self.frontDiffType == 2) or self.frontLockActive or self.allLockActive))
            or (W.i >= 2 and ((self.effRearDiffType == 2) or (self.rearDiffType == 2) or self.rearLockActive or self.allLockActive))
        local isCoastingInput = (self.drv and self.drv.thr or 0) < 0.05 and pedalBrk < 0.05 and W.hbk < 0.05
        local slipSpeedScale = max(2.2, absFwd + 0.5)
        local spinThreshold = 0.16 * slipSpeedScale
        if (not isAxleLocked) and W.locked == 0 and (W.contact or 0) ~= 0 and abs(W.T) < 0.001 and pedalBrk < 0.01 and W.hbk < 0.01 and abs(W.w * W.radius) <= absFwd + spinThreshold then
            W.w = vFwd / max(0.15, W.radius)
        elseif W.locked == 0 and (W.contact or 0) ~= 0 and W.T > 0 and pedalBrk < 0.05 and W.hbk < 0.05 and vFwd >= 0 and (W.w * W.radius) < vFwd then
            -- Never let a driven wheel lag behind forward speed and falsely apply negative braking Fx
            W.w = vFwd / max(0.15, W.radius)
        elseif W.locked == 0 and (W.contact or 0) ~= 0 and W.T < 0 and pedalBrk < 0.05 and W.hbk < 0.05 and vFwd < 0 and (W.w * W.radius) > vFwd then
            -- Never let a reverse-driven wheel lag behind reverse speed and falsely apply positive braking Fx
            W.w = vFwd / max(0.15, W.radius)
        elseif W.locked == 0 and (W.contact or 0) ~= 0 and isCoastingInput and W.T <= 0 and vFwd >= 0 and (W.w * W.radius) < vFwd then
            -- Never let mild coasting engine-braking drag lock the tire into a sliding stall at low update rates
            if abs(W.T / max(0.15, W.radius)) < max(0.25, peakLongKN * 0.55) then
                W.w = vFwd / max(0.15, W.radius)
            end
        elseif W.locked == 0 and (W.contact or 0) ~= 0 and isCoastingInput and W.T >= 0 and vFwd < 0 and (W.w * W.radius) > vFwd then
            -- Same mild coasting guard in reverse (only when off-throttle)
            if abs(W.T / max(0.15, W.radius)) < max(0.25, peakLongKN * 0.55) then
                W.w = vFwd / max(0.15, W.radius)
            end
        end
        peakLatKN = Braking.applyWheelLock(self, W, peakLongKN, peakLatKN, vFwd, absFwd, pedalBrk, vBrk, vHbk, absReleasing)
        local slipVel = W.radius * W.w - vFwd
        W.slip = slipVel
        W.slip364 = slipVel

        local V0 = (spec and spec.V0) or 1.016
        local denomSpd = max(absFwd, V0)
        local alphaRaw = math.atan2(vLat, denomSpd)
        local dz = (spec and spec.lowSpeedDeadzone) or 0.08
        if absFwd < 3.5 and abs(alphaRaw) < dz then
            alphaRaw = alphaRaw * ((spec and spec.lowSpeedHoldScale) or 0.35)
        end
        local alphaRad = alphaRaw
        local kappa = clamp(slipVel / slipSpeedScale, -1.0, 1.0)
        W.slipAngleRad = alphaRad
        W.slipRatio = kappa

        W.peakLatKN  = peakLatKN
        W.peakLongKN = peakLongKN

        if peakLatKN <= 0.001 and peakLongKN <= 0.001 then
            W.Fx, W.Fy = 0, 0
            W.sliding = 0
            W.slipX, W.slipY = 0, vLat
            return
        end

        local totalSpd = sqrt(vFwd * vFwd + vLat * vLat)
        local latVelRamp = min(1.0, totalSpd / 0.55)
        local latSign = (vLat > 0) and -1 or ((vLat < 0) and 1 or 0)
        local latShape = evaluateMagicFormula(spec.Lat_B, spec.Lat_C, spec.Lat_E, abs(alphaRad))
        W.Fy = peakLatKN * latShape * latSign * latVelRamp
        if W.axleX then
            local R, n = self.R, W.n
            local nx = R[0]*n[0] + R[1]*n[1] + R[2]*n[2]
            local ny = R[3]*n[0] + R[4]*n[1] + R[5]*n[2]
            local nz = R[6]*n[0] + R[7]*n[1] + R[8]*n[2]
            local inclination = math.asin(clamp(W.axleX*nx + W.axleY*ny + W.axleZ*nz, -1, 1))
            W.roadCamberRad = (W.i == 1 or W.i == 2) and inclination or -inclination
            if W.locked == 0 then
                local thrust = clamp(-inclination * spec.camberStiffness, -1, 1)
                W.camberFy = peakLatKN * thrust * (1 - abs(latShape)) * min(1, absFwd / 0.55)
                W.Fy = W.Fy + W.camberFy
            end
        end

        -- The lateral curve already accounts for sideslip. Reduce its available
        -- force by longitudinal spin without restoring grip as sideslip grows.
        local excessSpin = abs(W.radius * W.w) - absFwd - spinThreshold
        if excessSpin > 0 and W.locked == 0 then
            local normalizedSpin = excessSpin / spinThreshold
            local retention = 1 / sqrt(1 + normalizedSpin * normalizedSpin)
            W.Fy, W.camberFy = W.Fy * retention, W.camberFy * retention
        end

        local reqFx = W.T / max(0.15, W.radius)
        local overGripSpin = ((self.drv and self.drv.thr or 0) > 0.1) and (abs(reqFx) > peakLongKN * 1.02)
        local isSliding = (W.locked ~= 0) or (abs(kappa) > 0.16) or overGripSpin
        if not isSliding then
            W.sliding = 0
            W.Fx = clamp(reqFx, -peakLongKN, peakLongKN)
            W.slipX = 0
            W.slipY = vLat
        else
            W.sliding = 1
            if W.locked ~= 0 then
                -- A stopped tread scrubs against the road in the direction of travel.
                local slide = evaluateMagicFormula(spec.Long_B, spec.Long_C, spec.Long_E, 1)
                local scale = slide / max(totalSpd, 0.8)
                W.Fx = -peakLongKN * vFwd * scale
                W.Fy = -peakLatKN * vLat * scale
            else
                local lonShape = evaluateMagicFormula(spec.Long_B, spec.Long_C, spec.Long_E, abs(kappa))
                W.Fx = peakLongKN * lonShape * ((kappa >= 0) and 1 or -1)
                if excessSpin > 0 then
                    local scrubSpeed = sqrt(slipVel * slipVel + vLat * vLat)
                    local spinBlend = min(1, excessSpin / spinThreshold)
                    W.Fx = W.Fx * (1 - spinBlend * (1 - abs(slipVel) / scrubSpeed))
                end
            end
            W.slipX = slipVel
            W.slipY = vLat
        end

        if (W.contact or 0) ~= 0 and absFwd > 0.15 and (W.effectiveLoadKN or 0) > 0.001 then
            local rrMult = Tires.computeRollingResistanceMult(spec, W.surface or 1, W.ground and W.ground.matType)
            W.rrForce = W.effectiveLoadKN * (spec.rollingRes or 0.015) * rrMult * min(1.0, absFwd / 1.5)
        else
            W.rrForce = 0
        end
    end

    function Chassis:ellipse(W)
        if not self.tireSimSpecs then
            W.Fx, W.Fy = 0, 0
            return
        end
        local b, vel = W.basis, W.vel
        if (W.contact or 0) == 0 or (W.load or 0) <= 0.001 then
            W.Fx, W.Fy = 0, 0
            W.usage = 0
            W.skidIntensity = 0
            local wf = W.world
            wf[0], wf[1], wf[2] = 0, 0, 0
            return
        end

        local pLon = max(0.05, W.peakLongKN or W.load)
        local pLat = max(0.05, W.peakLatKN or W.load)
        local rx = (W.Fx / pLon)
        local ry = (W.Fy / pLat)
        local r  = sqrt(rx * rx + ry * ry)

        if r > 1.0 then
            W.Fx = W.Fx / r
            W.Fy = W.Fy / r
            W.camberFy = W.camberFy / r
        end

        W.usage = clamp(r, 0, 1.35)
        local vFwd = vel[0] * b[0] + vel[1] * b[1] + vel[2] * b[2]
        local vLat = vel[0] * b[3] + vel[1] * b[4] + vel[2] * b[5]
        local hdt = max(0.002, self.substepDt or 0.004)
        local cornerMass = max(0.08, self.m * 0.25)
        -- Rolling camber generates thrust even at zero lateral velocity.
        local jyMax = (0.85 * abs(vLat) * cornerMass) / hdt + abs(W.camberFy)
        if abs(W.Fy) > jyMax then
            W.camberFy = W.camberFy * (jyMax / abs(W.Fy))
            W.Fy = (W.Fy >= 0 and 1 or -1) * jyMax
        end

        local activeSpd = max(sqrt(vFwd * vFwd + vLat * vLat), abs(W.radius * W.w))
        local slipVelLong = (W.radius * W.w) - vFwd
        local totalSlipSpeedIn = sqrt(slipVelLong * slipVelLong + vLat * vLat) * 39.3700787
        local overRatio = sqrt(((W.slipRatio or 0) / 0.12) ^ 2 + ((W.slipAngleRad or 0) / 0.16) ^ 2)
        local speedGate = clamp((totalSlipSpeedIn - 24) / 95, 0, 1)
        local skid = clamp((overRatio - 0.58) / 1.25, 0, 1) * speedGate
        if (W.locked ~= 0 or W.sliding ~= 0 or (W.usage > 0.92 and not W.absActive)) and activeSpd > 0.8 then
            skid = max(skid, clamp(W.usage * 0.85, 0.35, 1.0) * max(0.35, speedGate))
        end
        if W.absActive and W.locked == 0 and W.sliding == 0 and (self.braking and self.braking.abs and self.braking.abs.threshold or 0.32) <= 0.14 then
            skid = 0
        end
        W.skidIntensity = skid

        if (W.rrForce or 0) > 0.0001 and abs(vFwd) > 0.15 then
            W.Fx = W.Fx - ((vFwd >= 0) and W.rrForce or -W.rrForce)
        end

        local wf = W.world
        wf[0] = W.Fx * b[0] + W.Fy * b[3] + W.load * b[6]
        wf[1] = W.Fx * b[1] + W.Fy * b[4] + W.load * b[7]
        wf[2] = W.Fx * b[2] + W.Fy * b[5] + W.load * b[8]
    end

    function Chassis:updateMass(newMassKg)
        local newMassT = max(0.35, (newMassKg or 1500) / 1000)
        local oldMassT = self.baseParams[68] or self.m or 1.22
        local ratio = newMassT / max(0.1, oldMassT)
        if abs(ratio - 1) < 0.005 then return end

        local function scaleCarArray(arr)
            arr[68] = newMassT
            arr[72] = arr[72] * ratio
            arr[77] = arr[77] * ratio
            arr[82] = arr[82] * ratio
            arr[153] = arr[153] * ratio
            arr[154] = arr[154] * ratio
        end

        scaleCarArray(self.baseParams)
        self.c = self.baseParams
        if self.rearDriveShare ~= nil then self.c[112] = self.rearDriveShare end
        if self.rearDriveShare ~= nil and self.dt then self.dt.split = self.rearDriveShare end

        self.m = self.c[68]
        self.im = 1 / self.m
        self.Iinv = { [0] = 1 / self.c[72], 1 / self.c[77], 1 / self.c[82] }
        self.Iinv2 = { [0] = self.Iinv[0], self.Iinv[1], self.Iinv[2] / self.c[88] }
        if self.tireSimSpecs then
            local cornerKN = (self.m * 0.25) * 9.800000190734863
            self.tireSimSpecs.front.nominalLoadKN = cornerKN
            self.tireSimSpecs.rear.nominalLoadKN  = cornerKN
        end
        self:updateWheelMounts()
    end

    -- Synchronize the solver state from GMod VPhysics and advance across 0.0044s substeps
    function Chassis:stepVPhysics(dt, pos_m, R, vel_m, angVel_rad, wheelGrounds, controls)
        dt = clamp(dt or 0.015, 0.001, 0.05)
        self._vphysMode = true

        -- 1. Update wheel ground planes from Base raycasts
        if wheelGrounds then
            for i = 0, 3 do
                local g = wheelGrounds[i]
                if g then
                    local wg = self.wheels[i].ground
                    wg.p[0], wg.p[1], wg.p[2] = g.p[0], g.p[1], g.p[2]
                    wg.n[0], wg.n[1], wg.n[2] = g.n[0], g.n[1], g.n[2]
                    wg.s = g.s or 1
                    wg.matType = g.matType
                end
            end
        end

        -- 2. Copy GMod VPhysics rigid-body pose and velocity into the solver body
        self.pos[0], self.pos[1], self.pos[2] = pos_m[0], pos_m[1], pos_m[2]
        for k = 0, 8 do self.R[k] = R[k] end
        self.v[0], self.v[1], self.v[2] = vel_m[0], vel_m[1], vel_m[2]
        self.w[0], self.w[1], self.w[2] = angVel_rad[0], angVel_rad[1], angVel_rad[2]
        self.speed = sqrt(self.v[0] * self.v[0] + self.v[1] * self.v[1] + self.v[2] * self.v[2])
        self.forward = self.v[0] * R[0] + self.v[1] * R[1] + self.v[2] * R[2]

        local groundedHint = 0
        for i = 0, 3 do
            if (self.wheels[i].ground.s or 0) ~= 0 then groundedHint = groundedHint + 1 end
        end
        self.mode344 = (groundedHint > 0) and 1 or 0

        self.P[0], self.P[1], self.P[2] = self.v[0] * self.m, self.v[1] * self.m, self.v[2] * self.m
        local I = (self.mode344 ~= 0) and self.Iinv2 or self.Iinv
        local wx = angVel_rad[0] * R[0] + angVel_rad[1] * R[1] + angVel_rad[2] * R[2]
        local wy = angVel_rad[0] * R[3] + angVel_rad[1] * R[4] + angVel_rad[2] * R[5]
        local wz = angVel_rad[0] * R[6] + angVel_rad[1] * R[7] + angVel_rad[2] * R[8]
        local lx, ly, lz = wx / I[0], wy / I[1], wz / I[2]
        self.L[0] = lx * R[0] + ly * R[3] + lz * R[6]
        self.L[1] = lx * R[1] + ly * R[4] + lz * R[7]
        self.L[2] = lx * R[2] + ly * R[5] + lz * R[8]

        -- Initialize wheel travel from the current ground height.
        if not self._vphysInit then
            self._vphysInit = true
            self:updateWheelMounts()
            for i = 0, 3 do
                self:postWheel(self.wheels[i])
                self:postSusp(self.wheels[i])
            end
            self:keepState()
        end

        -- 3. Apply driver controls & Clutch Pedal
        controls = controls or {}
        if controls.aeroCfg ~= nil then self.aeroCfg = controls.aeroCfg end
        if controls.weightShiftCfg ~= nil then self.weightShiftCfg = controls.weightShiftCfg end

        -- Simulated Dynamic Weight Shift (adjusts per-wheel normal load Fz from accel Gs)
        local wsCfg = self.weightShiftCfg
        local rawFwdG = controls.fwdAccelG
        if rawFwdG == nil then
            local prevFwd = self._prevFwdSpeed or (self.forward or 0)
            rawFwdG = ((self.forward or 0) - prevFwd) / (max(0.002, dt) * 9.81)
        end
        self._prevFwdSpeed = self.forward or 0
        local rawLatG = controls.latAccelG or 0
        local rawVertG = controls.vertAccelG or 0
        local wsSmooth = clamp(((wsCfg and wsCfg.Smoothing) or 10.0) * dt, 0.05, 1.0)
        self._wsFwdG  = (self._wsFwdG  or 0) + (rawFwdG  - (self._wsFwdG  or 0)) * wsSmooth
        self._wsLatG  = (self._wsLatG  or 0) + (rawLatG  - (self._wsLatG  or 0)) * wsSmooth
        self._wsVertG = (self._wsVertG or 0) + (rawVertG - (self._wsVertG or 0)) * wsSmooth

        local wsEnabled = (wsCfg == nil) or (wsCfg.Enabled ~= false)
        local dLong, dLat, vertMult = 0, 0, 1.0
        if wsEnabled then
            local cgH = (wsCfg and wsCfg.CGHeight) or 0.55
            local wb  = max(0.5, (wsCfg and wsCfg.Wheelbase) or 2.55)
            local tw  = max(0.5, (wsCfg and wsCfg.TrackWidth) or 1.55)
            local maxFrac = (wsCfg and wsCfg.MaxTransferFrac) or 0.45
            local longMul = (wsCfg and wsCfg.LongitudinalTransfer) or 1.0
            local latMul  = (wsCfg and wsCfg.LateralTransfer) or 1.0
            local vertMul = (wsCfg and wsCfg.VerticalTransfer) or 1.0
            local minVert = (wsCfg and wsCfg.MinVertLoadMult) or 0.20
            local maxVert = (wsCfg and wsCfg.MaxVertLoadMult) or 2.00
            dLong    = clamp(-self._wsFwdG * (cgH / wb) * longMul, -maxFrac, maxFrac)
            dLat     = clamp(-self._wsLatG * (cgH / tw) * latMul,  -maxFrac, maxFrac)
            vertMult = clamp(1.0 + self._wsVertG * vertMul, minVert, maxVert)
        end
        local minLoadFrac = (wsCfg and wsCfg.MinWheelLoadFrac) or 0.15
        local gripEffect  = (wsCfg and wsCfg.GripEffectMult ~= nil) and wsCfg.GripEffectMult or 0.65
        local gripLong    = (wsCfg and wsCfg.GripEffectLongMult ~= nil) and wsCfg.GripEffectLongMult or 1.0
        local gripLat     = (wsCfg and wsCfg.GripEffectLatMult  ~= nil) and wsCfg.GripEffectLatMult  or 1.0
        local fLongM      = (wsCfg and wsCfg.FrontLongMult    ~= nil) and wsCfg.FrontLongMult    or 1.0
        local rLongM      = (wsCfg and wsCfg.RearLongMult     ~= nil) and wsCfg.RearLongMult     or 1.0
        local fLatM       = (wsCfg and wsCfg.FrontLateralMult ~= nil) and wsCfg.FrontLateralMult or 1.0
        local rLatM       = (wsCfg and wsCfg.RearLateralMult  ~= nil) and wsCfg.RearLateralMult  or 1.0
        local frontSum, rearSum, leftSum, rightSum, totalW = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
        local rawMultSum, activeCount = 0, 0
        for i = 0, 3 do
            local wObj = self.wheels[i]
            if wObj then
                local isFront   = (i < 2)
                local frontSign = isFront and 1 or -1
                local rightSign = (i == 1 or i == 2) and 1 or -1 -- 0=FL, 1=FR, 2=RR, 3=RL
                local axLongM   = isFront and fLongM or rLongM
                local axLatM    = isFront and fLatM  or rLatM
                local dispMult  = wsEnabled and max(minLoadFrac, 1.0 + frontSign * dLong + rightSign * dLat) or 1.0
                local physDelta = (frontSign * dLong * gripLong * axLongM) + (rightSign * dLat * gripLat * axLatM)
                local physMult  = wsEnabled and max(minLoadFrac, 1.0 + physDelta * gripEffect) or 1.0
                wObj._wsMult = physMult
                if (wObj.load or 0) > 0.001 then
                    rawMultSum = rawMultSum + physMult
                    activeCount = activeCount + 1
                end
                local wLoad = max(0, (wObj.load or 0) * dispMult)
                totalW = totalW + wLoad
                if i < 2 then frontSum = frontSum + wLoad else rearSum = rearSum + wLoad end
                if i == 0 or i == 3 then leftSum = leftSum + wLoad else rightSum = rightSum + wLoad end
            end
        end
        if activeCount > 0 and rawMultSum > 0.001 then
            local normScale = (activeCount / rawMultSum) * vertMult
            for i = 0, 3 do
                local wObj = self.wheels[i]
                if wObj and (wObj.load or 0) > 0.001 then
                    wObj._wsMult = wObj._wsMult * normScale
                end
            end
        end
        local fb = self.frontWeightBias or 0.50
        local fPct = (totalW > 0.001) and (frontSum / totalW * 100) or clamp((fb + 0.5 * dLong) * 100, 5, 95)
        local lPct = (totalW > 0.001) and (leftSum / totalW * 100) or clamp((0.5 - 0.5 * dLat) * 100, 5, 95)
        self.weightShiftDebug = {
            enabled  = wsEnabled,
            fwdG     = self._wsFwdG,
            latG     = self._wsLatG,
            vertG    = self._wsVertG,
            vertMult = vertMult,
            dLong    = dLong,
            dLat     = dLat,
            frontPct = fPct,
            rearPct  = 100 - fPct,
            leftPct  = lPct,
            rightPct = 100 - lPct,
        }
        self.ignitionRunning = (controls.ignitionRunning ~= false)
        if not self.ignitionRunning and self.eng then
            self.eng.w = (controls.crankRPM or 0) * 0.10471975512
            self.rpm = controls.crankRPM or 0
        end
        self.vphysLoadsKN = controls.vphysLoadsKN
        if controls.clutchPedal ~= nil then self.clutchPedal = controls.clutchPedal else self.clutchPedal = 1 end
        if self.drv then self.drv.reverseGas = controls.reverseGas; self.drv.forwardBrake = controls.forwardBrake end
        self:driver(
            controls.steer or 0,
            controls.gas or 0,
            controls.brake or 0,
            controls.handbrake or 0,
            dt,
            controls.hold and true or false,
            controls.u16
        )

        -- 4. Frame pre-substep setup & velocity-based suspension sync
        local hbkIn = (self.drv and self.drv.hbk) or 0
        for i = 0, 3 do
            self.wheels[i].hbk = (i >= 2) and hbkIn or 0
        end
        self:prepareFrame()
        self:updateDriverState(dt)

        for i = 0, 3 do
            self:updateAlignment(self.wheels[i], i == 0 and self.steer.a0 or (i == 1 and self.steer.a1 or 0))
        end
        self:postBody()
        for i = 0, 3 do
            self:postWheel(self.wheels[i])
            self:postSusp(self.wheels[i])
        end
        self:postEngine()
        self:postDrive()
        local maxSub = (controls and controls.maxSubsteps) or 4
        local n = min(maxSub, max(1, math.ceil(dt / 0.010)))
        local h = dt / n
        self.substepDt = h
        self:forces()

        -- 5. Record start-of-substeps rigid-body velocities & run bounded substeps (2 max by default)
        local vx0, vy0, vz0 = self.v[0], self.v[1], self.v[2]
        local wx0, wy0, wz0 = self.w[0], self.w[1], self.w[2]
        local ux, uy, uz    = R[6], R[7], R[8]
        local fx, fy, fz    = R[0], R[1], R[2]
        local lfx, lfy, lfz = R[3], R[4], R[5]



        for i = 0, 3 do
            self.wheels[i].absActiveTick = false
        end

        -- Red Fox Engine Master Clutch Control (0 = engaged/driving, 1 = disengaged)
        local d = self.dt
        local speedKmh = abs(self.forward) * 3.6
        local stopSpd = self.stoppedPhaseBrakeSpeed or 5
        local thrIn = (self.drv and self.drv.thr) or 0
        local brkIn = (self.drv and self.drv.brk) or 0
        local hasThrInput = thrIn > 0.05
        local simClutch = 0
        if d.gear == 1 or d.state == 0 or d.state == 1 or d.shifting ~= 0 or (self.clutchPedal == 0) or ((controls and controls.handbrake or 0) > 0.5) then
            simClutch = 1
        elseif self.clutchType == "torque_converter" then
            local rpmNow = self.eng.w * RAD_TO_RPM
            local idleRPM = self.c[124] or 800
            local stallRPM = max(idleRPM + 200, self.tcStallRPM or 2200)
            local cap = clamp((rpmNow - idleRPM) / (stallRPM - idleRPM), 0, 1)
            local creep = clamp(self.tcCreepTorque or 0.12, 0, 0.5)
            local engage = clamp(creep + (1.0 - creep) * cap, 0, 1)
            if brkIn > 0.05 and not hasThrInput and speedKmh <= stopSpd then
                engage = creep * 0.25
            end
            simClutch = 1.0 - engage
        elseif self.clutchType == "centrifugal" then
            local rpmNow = self.eng.w * RAD_TO_RPM
            local engRPM = self.centrifugalEngageRPM or 1800
            local fullRPM = max(engRPM + 100, self.centrifugalFullRPM or 3200)
            local engage = clamp((rpmNow - engRPM) / (fullRPM - engRPM), 0, 1)
            simClutch = 1.0 - engage
        else
            if brkIn > 0.05 and not hasThrInput and speedKmh <= stopSpd then
                simClutch = 1
            elseif brkIn > 0.05 and not hasThrInput and speedKmh <= (stopSpd + 15) then
                simClutch = 1.0 - clamp((speedKmh - stopSpd) / 15.0, 0, 1)
            elseif not hasThrInput and speedKmh <= stopSpd then
                simClutch = 1
            end
            if simClutch < 1 and (not self.manualBox or self.useClutchLerpInManual) then
                local rpmNow = self.eng.w * RAD_TO_RPM
                local redline = max(1000, self.c[126] or 8000)
                local lerpLim = (d.gear <= 0 or (self.drv and self.drv.reversing)) and (self.clutchLerpReverse or 0.5) or (self.clutchLerp or 0.4)
                local rpmClutch = max(0, lerpLim - (rpmNow / redline))
                simClutch = max(simClutch, rpmClutch)
            end
        end
        self.simClutch = clamp(simClutch, 0, 1)

        self:computeActiveDiff(controls)
        for _ = 1, n do
            self:integrate(h)
            self.now = self.now + h
            self:orthonormalize()
            for i = 0, 3 do
                local W = self.wheels[i]
                if W.absActive then W.absActiveTick = true end
            end
        end

        self:state(dt)
        if dt > 0 then
            self.yawAcc  = (self.w[2] - self.yawPrev) / dt
            self.yawPrev = self.w[2]
        end

        -- 6. Remove internal gravity from the velocity delta before converting to car-frame axes
        local dvx = self.v[0] - vx0
        local dvy = self.v[1] - vy0
        local dvz = (self.v[2] - vz0) + self.grav * dt

        local dv_fwd_m  = dvx * fx  + dvy * fy  + dvz * fz
        local dv_left_m = dvx * lfx + dvy * lfy + dvz * lfz
        local dv_up_m   = dvx * ux  + dvy * uy  + dvz * uz

        local dwx = self.w[0] - wx0
        local dwy = self.w[1] - wy0
        local dwz = self.w[2] - wz0
        local dw_roll_rad  = dwx * fx  + dwy * fy  + dwz * fz
        local dw_pitch_rad = dwx * lfx + dwy * lfy + dwz * lfz
        local dw_yaw_rad   = dwx * ux  + dwy * uy  + dwz * uz

        local boostPSI = self.eng and self.eng.boostPSI or 0

        local g = self.gearShown or 2
        local gName
        if self.classicAuto and not self.manualBox and self.autoRange == 0 then
            gName = "P"
        elseif g == 1 then
            gName = "N"
        elseif g >= 2 then
            gName = (self.classicAuto and not self.manualBox and "D" or "") .. tostring(g - 1)
        else
            gName = ((self.minGear or 0) == 0) and "R" or ("R" .. tostring(1 - g))
        end
        local peakTqKNm = 0
        for i = 128, 136 do
            local v = self.c[i] or 0
            if v > peakTqKNm then peakTqKNm = v end
        end
        local engineLoad = clamp(math.abs(self.lastTload or (self.eng and self.eng.tload) or 0) / math.max(0.05, peakTqKNm * (self.eng and self.eng.perf or 1)), 0, 1.5)
        return {
            dv_fwd_m      = dv_fwd_m,
            dv_left_m     = dv_left_m,
            dv_up_m       = dv_up_m,
            dw_roll_rad   = dw_roll_rad,
            dw_pitch_rad  = dw_pitch_rad,
            dw_yaw_rad    = dw_yaw_rad,
            grounded      = self.grounded,
            restState     = self.restState,
            speed_ms      = self.speed,
            forward_ms    = self.forward,
            rpm           = self.rpm,
            flywheel_rpm  = self.eng.w * RAD_TO_RPM,
            gearIndex     = self.dt.gear,
            gearShown     = g,
            gearName      = gName,
            clutch        = self.simClutch ~= nil and self.simClutch or (1.0 - (self.dt.clutch or 0)),
            shifting      = self.dt.shifting,
            throttle      = (self.dt.shifting ~= 0) and 0 or (self.eng and self.eng.T or (self.drv and self.drv.thr or self.throttleOut)),
            brake         = self.drv and self.drv.brk or 0,
            frontDiffLocked = (self.frontDiffType == 2 and self.c[112] <= 0.999) or (self.frontDiffLocked == true) or (self.frontLockActive == true) or (self.allLockActive == true),
            rearDiffLocked  = (self.rearDiffType == 2 and self.c[112] >= 0.001) or (self.rearDiffLocked == true) or (self.rearLockActive == true) or (self.allLockActive == true),
            allDiffLocked   = (self.allLockActive == true),
            frontActiveLockPct = self.frontActiveLockPct or 0,
            rearActiveLockPct  = self.rearActiveLockPct or 0,
            torqueNm      = self:torque() * 1000,
            engineLoad    = engineLoad,
            boostPSI      = boostPSI,
            slipDeg       = self:slipAngle() * 57.2957795,
            manual        = self.manualBox,
            a0            = self.steer.a0,
            a1            = self.steer.a1,
        }
    end
end


initializeVehiclePhysicsBridge(SimcadeVehiclePhysics)

_G.SimcadeVehiclePhysics = SimcadeVehiclePhysics
return SimcadeVehiclePhysics
104965
-- name Simcade Core Orchestrator
-- author SimcadeBase
-- shared

SimcadeCore = SimcadeCore or {}

function SimcadeCore.networkInterval(vehicleCfg)
    return 1 / math.max(1, tonumber(vehicleCfg and vehicleCfg.NetworkRate) or 20)
end

local function writeDirection(v)
    net.writeInt(math.clamp(math.round(v.x * 127), -127, 127), 8)
    net.writeInt(math.clamp(math.round(v.y * 127), -127, 127), 8)
    net.writeInt(math.clamp(math.round(v.z * 127), -127, 127), 8)
end

local IN_TO_M = 0.0254
local M_TO_IN = 1.0 / IN_TO_M
local G_SOURCE = 600.0
-- Starfall subtracts the buffered packet from the remaining byte budget.
function SimcadeCore.sendPacket(target, unreliable)
    if net.getBytesLeft() < 0 then
        net.abort()
        return false
    end
    local ok, reason = pcall(net.send, target, unreliable)
    if not ok then
        net.abort()
        error(reason)
    end
    return true
end

function SimcadeCore.init(CONFIG)
    CONFIG = CONFIG or {}
    SimcadeTires.applySurfaceProfiles(CONFIG)
    local VEHICLE_CFG    = CONFIG.VEHICLE_CFG or {}
    local ENGINE_CFG     = CONFIG.ENGINE_CFG or {}
    local TIRE_CFG       = CONFIG.TIRE_CFG or {}
    local SUSPENSION_CFG = CONFIG.SUSPENSION_CFG or {}
    local STEERING_CFG   = CONFIG.STEERING_CFG or {}
    local AERO_CFG       = CONFIG.AERO_CFG or {}
    local WHEELS         = CONFIG.WHEELS or {}
    local WHEEL_DEFAULTS = CONFIG.WHEEL_DEFAULTS or {}
    local REVERSE_CFG    = CONFIG.REVERSE_CFG or {}
    local CONTROLS_CFG   = CONFIG.CONTROLS_CFG or {}
    local CAMERA_CFG     = CONFIG.CAMERA_CFG or {}
    local ACTIVE_DIFF_CFG = CONFIG.ACTIVE_DIFF_CFG or {}

    local function keyLabel(k, fallback)
        if input and input.getKeyName and k then
            local ok, n = pcall(input.getKeyName, k)
            if ok and n and n ~= "" then return string.upper(n) end
        end
        if type(KEY) == "table" and k then
            for name, val in pairs(KEY) do
                if val == k then return name end
            end
        end
        return fallback or "?"
    end

    local kManual   = CONTROLS_CFG.ManualToggle  or (KEY and KEY.M)
    local kIgn      = CONTROLS_CFG.Ignition      or (KEY and KEY.F)
    local kCam      = CONTROLS_CFG.CameraToggle  or CAMERA_CFG.toggleKey or (KEY and KEY.V)
    local kGearRst  = CONTROLS_CFG.GearReset     or (KEY and KEY.R)
    local kFrontLk  = CONTROLS_CFG.FrontLock     or (KEY and KEY.K)
    local kRearLk   = CONTROLS_CFG.RearLock      or (KEY and KEY.L)
    local kAllLk    = CONTROLS_CFG.AllLock       or (KEY and KEY.J)

    -- ========================================================================
    -- CLIENT SIDE
    -- ========================================================================
    if CLIENT then
        if VEHICLE_CFG.DriverPrediction and SimcadePrediction then SimcadePrediction.initClient(CONFIG) end
        if SimcadeHUD and SimcadeHUD.initClient then SimcadeHUD.initClient(CONFIG) end
        if SimcadeAddons and SimcadeAddons.initClient then SimcadeAddons.initClient(CONFIG) end
        if SimcadeSkidmarks and SimcadeSkidmarks.initClient then SimcadeSkidmarks.initClient(CONFIG) end
        if SimcadeEffects and SimcadeEffects.initClient then SimcadeEffects.initClient(CONFIG) end
        if SimcadeSounds and SimcadeSounds.initClient then SimcadeSounds.initClient(CONFIG) end
        if SimcadeCamera and SimcadeCamera.initClient then SimcadeCamera.initClient(CONFIG) end

        net.receive("Simcade_TireEffects", function()
            local function direction()
                return Vector(net.readInt(8), net.readInt(8), net.readInt(8)) / 127
            end
            local fwd, up = direction(), direction()
            local first, count = net.readUInt(32), net.readUInt(4)
            local now = timer.curtime()
            for i = first, first + count - 1 do
                local pos, normal = net.readVector(), direction()
                local skid, material = net.readUInt(8) / 170, net.readUInt(8)
                local rimSpeed, grounded = net.readUInt(16), net.readBool()
                local width, rear = net.readUInt(8), net.readBool()
                if SimcadeSkidmarks and SimcadeSkidmarks.sampleWheel then
                    SimcadeSkidmarks.sampleWheel(i, pos, normal, skid, material, width, rear)
                end
                if SimcadeEffects and SimcadeEffects.sampleWheel then
                    SimcadeEffects.sampleWheel(i, pos, skid, material, rimSpeed, grounded, now, fwd, up)
                end
            end
        end)

        hook.add("inputPressed", "Simcade_ClientModeKeys", function(btn)
            local lp = player()
            if not (lp and lp:isValid() and lp:inVehicle()) then return end
            if lp.isTyping and lp:isTyping() then return end
            if input and input.isGameUIVisible and input.isGameUIVisible() then return end
            if btn == kManual then
                net.start("Simcade_ClientKeyEvent")
                net.writeUInt(2, 4)
                net.writeBool(true)
                SimcadeCore.sendPacket()
            elseif btn == kIgn then
                net.start("Simcade_ClientKeyEvent")
                net.writeUInt(4, 4)
                net.writeBool(true)
                SimcadeCore.sendPacket()
            elseif btn == kCam then
                net.start("Simcade_ClientKeyEvent")
                net.writeUInt(5, 4)
                net.writeBool(true)
                SimcadeCore.sendPacket()
            elseif btn == kGearRst then
                net.start("Simcade_ClientKeyEvent")
                net.writeUInt(7, 4)
                net.writeBool(true)
                SimcadeCore.sendPacket()
            elseif btn == kFrontLk then
                net.start("Simcade_ClientKeyEvent")
                net.writeUInt(8, 4)
                net.writeBool(true)
                SimcadeCore.sendPacket()
            elseif btn == kRearLk then
                net.start("Simcade_ClientKeyEvent")
                net.writeUInt(9, 4)
                net.writeBool(true)
                SimcadeCore.sendPacket()
            elseif btn == kAllLk then
                net.start("Simcade_ClientKeyEvent")
                net.writeUInt(10, 4)
                net.writeBool(true)
                SimcadeCore.sendPacket()
            end
        end)

        hook.add("inputReleased", "Simcade_ClientModeKeysRel", function(btn)
            if btn == kIgn then
                net.start("Simcade_ClientKeyEvent")
                net.writeUInt(4, 4)
                net.writeBool(false)
                SimcadeCore.sendPacket()
            end
        end)
        return
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- SERVER SIDE
    -- ========================================================================
    local function sendDriverPrint(ply, msg)
        if not (isValid(ply) and msg and msg ~= "") then return end
        local clean = string.gsub(msg, "^%[Simcade%]%s*", "")
        local prefix = "[Simcade] "
        local blue, white = Color(100, 200, 255), Color(255, 255, 255)
        if ply == owner() then
            pcall(print, blue, prefix, white, clean)
            return
        end
        -- Starfall includes its own "[SF] " prefix in printHud's 52-byte limit.
        local limit = 52 - #"[SF] " - #prefix
        while #clean > 0 do
            local length = math.min(#clean, limit)
            if #clean > limit then
                length = clean:sub(1, limit + 1):match("^.*()%s") or limit + 1
                length = math.max(1, length - 1)
            end
            if not pcall(printHud, ply, blue, prefix, white, clean:sub(1, length)) then return end
            clean = clean:sub(length + 1):gsub("^%s+", "")
        end
    end

    local addonHost = (SimcadeAddons and SimcadeAddons.initServer)
        and SimcadeAddons.initServer(CONFIG, sendDriverPrint) or nil
    if addonHost and addonHost.applyOverrides then
        addonHost.applyOverrides(nil, nil)
    end

    local inputNames
    if SimcadeWire and SimcadeWire.initPorts then
        inputNames = SimcadeWire.initPorts(
            VEHICLE_CFG, WHEELS,
            addonHost and addonHost.extraInputs or nil,
            addonHost and addonHost.extraOutputs or nil
        )
    end
    local camServerState = {}
    if SimcadeCamera and SimcadeCamera.initServer then
        SimcadeCamera.initServer(camServerState, CONFIG)
    end

    local Base = nil
    local inertiaPhys, originalInertia, appliedInertia
    local function restoreBaseInertia()
        if isValid(inertiaPhys) and inertiaPhys:getInertia() == appliedInertia then
            local ok, err = pcall(function() inertiaPhys:setInertia(originalInertia) end)
            if not ok then print("[Simcade] Could not restore Base inertia: " .. tostring(err)) end
        end
        inertiaPhys, originalInertia, appliedInertia = nil, nil, nil
    end
    local Pod = nil
    local FrontWheelEnts = {}
    local RearWheelEnts = {}
    local ExtraFilterStatic = {}
    local ExtraFilterSemiDynamic = {}
    local wireInputs = {}

    local runtimeWheels = {}
    local axleAnchors = {}
    local traceFilter = {}
    local filterSeen = {}
    local physicsCar = nil
    local activeCompound = TIRE_CFG.DefaultCompound or "Sport"
    if not SimcadeTires.COMPOUNDS[activeCompound]
        and (not TIRE_CFG.Compounds or TIRE_CFG.Compounds[activeCompound] == nil) then
        activeCompound = "Sport"
    end

    local totalVehicleMass = VEHICLE_CFG.DefaultMassKG or 1450
    local lastKnownBaseMass = nil
    local resolvedLocalFwd   = Vector(1, 0, 0)
    local resolvedLocalRight = Vector(0, -1, 0)
    local resolvedLocalUp    = Vector(0, 0, 1)

    local ignState = SimcadeEngine and SimcadeEngine.newIgnitionState(ENGINE_CFG)
        or { mode = (ENGINE_CFG.StartRunning ~= false) and 3 or 0, timer = 0, running = (ENGINE_CFG.StartRunning ~= false), crankRPM = 0 }
    local turboState = (SimcadeEngine and SimcadeEngine.newTurboState) and SimcadeEngine.newTurboState() or { rpm = 0, psi = 0, boost = 1.0 }
    local superchargerState = (SimcadeEngine and SimcadeEngine.newSuperchargerState) and SimcadeEngine.newSuperchargerState() or { psi = 0, boost = 1.0 }

    local exitHbkDefault = tonumber(ENGINE_CFG.AutoHandbrakeOnExitSpeed)
    if exitHbkDefault == nil and ENGINE_CFG.AutoHandbrakeOnExitSpeed ~= false then exitHbkDefault = 15 end
    local ctrlState = {
        autoParkHandbrake = (exitHbkDefault ~= nil and exitHbkDefault > 0) and true or false,
    }
    ctrlState._addonHost = addonHost
    ctrlState._weightShiftCfg = CONFIG.WEIGHT_SHIFT_CFG
    local prevGearUpIn, prevGearDownIn = false, false
    local prevGearName, prevThrottle, prevBoostPSI = "1", 0, 0
    local smoothedFwdAccel, smoothedLatAccel = 0, 0
    local lastVelWorld = nil
    local lastHudDriver = nil
    pcall(function() if enableHud then enableHud(owner(), true) end end)
    local cachedLiftForcePerSec = nil
    local skippedLiftApplied = Vector(0, 0, 0)
    local lastPhysTickTime = 0
    local nextAudioNetTime, nextFxNetTime, nextHudNetTime, nextWheelDebugNetTime = 0, 0, 0, 0
    ctrlState.wheelPage, ctrlState.fxPage = 1, 1
    ctrlState.wheelBaselines, ctrlState.wheelKeyframes = {}, {}
    local prevHadTireFx = false
    local debugRaysEnabled = (TIRE_CFG.ShowSuspensionRays == true)

    local function getSimMass(physMass)
        local vm = VEHICLE_CFG.VirtualMassKG
        if vm and vm > 0 then
            return math.clamp(vm, 100, 50000)
        end
        return math.clamp(totalVehicleMass or physMass or 1450, 100, 50000)
    end

    local function updateChassisLocalAxes()
        if not isValid(Base) then return end
        local f = VEHICLE_CFG.chassisForward or Vector(1, 0, 0)
        resolvedLocalFwd = f:getNormalized()
        local u = Vector(0, 0, 1)
        local r = resolvedLocalFwd:cross(u)
        if r:getLengthSqr() < 0.01 then r = Vector(0, -1, 0) end
        resolvedLocalRight = r:getNormalized()
        resolvedLocalUp = resolvedLocalRight:cross(resolvedLocalFwd):getNormalized()
    end

    local function getVehicleAxes()
        if not isValid(Base) then
            return Vector(1, 0, 0), Vector(0, -1, 0), Vector(0, 0, 1), Angle(0, 0, 0)
        end
        local fwd   = Base:localToWorldVector(resolvedLocalFwd):getNormalized()
        local right = Base:localToWorldVector(resolvedLocalRight):getNormalized()
        local up    = Base:localToWorldVector(resolvedLocalUp):getNormalized()
        return fwd, right, up, Base:getAngles()
    end

    local FilterScan = { Active = false, Queue = {}, Seen = {}, Result = {} }

    local function expandFilterEntity(cur, seenMap, queue, outList)
        if not isValid(cur) then return end
        local okP, parent = pcall(function() return cur:getParent() end)
        if okP and isValid(parent) and not seenMap[parent] then
            seenMap[parent] = true
            outList[#outList + 1] = parent
            queue[#queue + 1] = parent
        end
        if cur.getChildren then
            local okC, children = pcall(function() return cur:getChildren() end)
            if okC and type(children) == "table" then
                for _, child in ipairs(children) do
                    if isValid(child) and not seenMap[child] then
                        seenMap[child] = true
                        outList[#outList + 1] = child
                        queue[#queue + 1] = child
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if constraint and constraint.getTable then
            local okCt, cons = pcall(constraint.getTable, cur)
            if okCt and type(cons) == "table" then
                for i = 1, #cons do
                    local c = cons[i]
                    local other = c and ((c.Ent1 == cur) and c.Ent2 or c.Ent1)
                    if isValid(other) and not seenMap[other] then
                        seenMap[other] = true
                        outList[#outList + 1] = other
                        queue[#queue + 1] = other
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if cur.getAllConstrainedEntities then
            local okCon, cons = pcall(function() return cur:getAllConstrainedEntities() end)
            if okCon and type(cons) == "table" then
                for _, ce in pairs(cons) do
                    if isValid(ce) and not seenMap[ce] then
                        seenMap[ce] = true
                        outList[#outList + 1] = ce
                        queue[#queue + 1] = ce
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function addFilterTree(rootEnt, seenMap, outList)
        outList = outList or traceFilter
        if not isValid(rootEnt) then return end
        local queue = { rootEnt }
        if not seenMap[rootEnt] then
            seenMap[rootEnt] = true
            outList[#outList + 1] = rootEnt
        end
        while #queue > 0 do
            local cur = table.remove(queue, 1)
            expandFilterEntity(cur, seenMap, queue, outList)
        end
    end

    local function updateSemiDynamicFilter()
        if type(ExtraFilterSemiDynamic) ~= "table" or #ExtraFilterSemiDynamic == 0 then return end
        for _, ent in ipairs(ExtraFilterSemiDynamic) do
            addFilterTree(ent, filterSeen, traceFilter)
        end
    end

    local function seedFilterRoots(seenMap, outList, driverEnt)
        local function seed(e)
            if isValid(e) and not seenMap[e] then
                seenMap[e] = true
                outList[#outList + 1] = e
            end
        end
        seed(Base)
        seed(Pod)
        seed(driverEnt)
        for _, e in ipairs(FrontWheelEnts) do seed(e) end
        for _, e in ipairs(RearWheelEnts)  do seed(e) end
        for _, e in ipairs(ExtraFilterStatic) do seed(e) end
        if type(ExtraFilterSemiDynamic) == "table" then
            for _, e in ipairs(ExtraFilterSemiDynamic) do seed(e) end
        end
        return seed
    end

    local function rebuildStaticFilter(driverEnt)
        traceFilter = {}
        filterSeen = {}
        FilterScan.Active = false
        local seed = seedFilterRoots(filterSeen, traceFilter, driverEnt)
        local queue = {}
        for i = 1, #traceFilter do queue[i] = traceFilter[i] end
        while #queue > 0 do
            local cur = table.remove(queue, 1)
            expandFilterEntity(cur, filterSeen, queue, traceFilter)
        end
        if find and find.allPlayers then
            local ok, plys = pcall(find.allPlayers)
            if ok and type(plys) == "table" then
                for _, p in ipairs(plys) do seed(p) end
            end
        end
    end

    local function startConstraintRefresh()
        if FilterScan.Active or not isValid(Base) then return end
        local drv = isValid(Pod) and Pod:getDriver() or nil
        FilterScan.Active = true
        FilterScan.Queue = {}
        FilterScan.Seen = {}
        FilterScan.Result = {}
        seedFilterRoots(FilterScan.Seen, FilterScan.Result, drv)
        for i = 1, #FilterScan.Result do
            FilterScan.Queue[i] = FilterScan.Result[i]
        end
    end

    local function tickFilterScanner()
        if not FilterScan.Active then return end
        local processed = 0
        local queue = FilterScan.Queue
        while #queue > 0 and processed < 6 do
            local cur = table.remove(queue, 1)
            expandFilterEntity(cur, FilterScan.Seen, queue, FilterScan.Result)
            processed = processed + 1
        end
        if #queue == 0 then
            FilterScan.Active = false
            if find and find.allPlayers then
                local ok, plys = pcall(find.allPlayers)
                if ok and type(plys) == "table" then
                    for _, p in ipairs(plys) do
                        if isValid(p) and not FilterScan.Seen[p] then
                            FilterScan.Seen[p] = true
                            FilterScan.Result[#FilterScan.Result + 1] = p
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            traceFilter = FilterScan.Result
            filterSeen = FilterScan.Seen
        end
    end

    if timer and timer.create then
        timer.create("Simcade_ConstraintRefresh", math.max(0.25, TIRE_CFG.ConstraintRefreshInterval or 2.0), 0, startConstraintRefresh)
        timer.create("Simcade_SemiDynamicFilterUpdate", 10, 0, updateSemiDynamicFilter)
    end

    local function cleanupWheelHolos()
        for _, w in ipairs(runtimeWheels) do
            if isValid(w.holo) then
                pcall(function() w.holo:remove() end)
            end
            if isValid(w.radiusHolo) then
                pcall(function() w.radiusHolo:remove() end)
            end
        end
        runtimeWheels = {}
    end

    local function clearAxleAnchor(port)
        local anchor = axleAnchors[port]
        if isValid(anchor) then anchor:remove() end
        axleAnchors[port] = nil
        wire.ports[port] = entity(-1)
    end

    local function setupAxleAnchor(port, leftSlot, rightSlot)
        local cfg = SimcadeSuspension.getAxleCfg(SUSPENSION_CFG, leftSlot == 0)
        local wheels = physicsCar and physicsCar.wheels
        local left = wheels and wheels[leftSlot].alignment
        local right = wheels and wheels[rightSlot].alignment
        if not (cfg.solidAxle and left and right) then
            if axleAnchors[port] then clearAxleAnchor(port) end
            return
        end
        local anchor = axleAnchors[port]
        if isValid(anchor) and anchor:getParent() == Base then return end
        if anchor then clearAxleAnchor(port) end
        local center = (left.localMountPos + right.localMountPos) * 0.5
            - resolvedLocalUp * ((cfg.rideHeight or 24) * (1 - (cfg.restCompression or 0.45)))
        local ok, holo = pcall(hologram.create, Base:localToWorld(center),
            Base:localToWorldAngles(resolvedLocalFwd:getAngle()), "models/holograms/cube.mdl")
        if ok and isValid(holo) then
            holo:setColor(Color(255, 255, 255, 0))
            holo:setParent(Base)
            axleAnchors[port] = holo
            wire.ports[port] = holo
        end
    end

    local function rebuildPhysicsCar()
        if not isValid(Base) or #runtimeWheels == 0 then
            physicsCar = nil
            return
        end
        local phys = Base:getPhysicsObject()
        if isValid(phys) then
            pcall(function() phys:enableDrag(false) end)
            pcall(function() phys:setDamping(0, 0) end)
        end
        local physMass = isValid(phys) and phys:getMass() or 1450
        local simMass = getSimMass(physMass)
        local oldCar = physicsCar
        local newCar = SimcadeVehiclePhysics.newFromConfig(
            VEHICLE_CFG, TIRE_CFG, SUSPENSION_CFG, ENGINE_CFG, STEERING_CFG,
            runtimeWheels, simMass, activeCompound, AERO_CFG
        )
        if oldCar then
            newCar:carryFrom(oldCar)
        end
        newCar.activeDiffCfg = ACTIVE_DIFF_CFG
        physicsCar = newCar
        if addonHost and addonHost.applyOverrides then
            addonHost.ctx.car = physicsCar
            addonHost.ctx.base = Base
            addonHost.ctx.pod = Pod
            addonHost.ctx.wheels = runtimeWheels
            addonHost.applyOverrides(physicsCar, addonHost.ctx.pipeline)
        end
    end

    local function orientWheelModel(angle, offset)
        if not offset then return angle end
        if offset.y ~= 0 then angle = angle:rotateAroundAxis(angle:getUp(), offset.y) end
        if offset.p ~= 0 then angle = angle:rotateAroundAxis(angle:getRight(), offset.p) end
        if offset.r ~= 0 then angle = angle:rotateAroundAxis(angle:getForward(), offset.r) end
        return angle
    end

    local function setupWheels()
        ctrlState.rollLoadG = 0
        ctrlState.corneringG = 0
        cleanupWheelHolos()
        if not isValid(Base) then return end
        updateChassisLocalAxes()

        local phys = Base:getPhysicsObject()
        local massCenterLocal = isValid(phys) and phys:getMassCenter() or Vector(0, 0, 0)
        local comLocal = massCenterLocal
            + resolvedLocalFwd   * ((VEHICLE_CFG.comOffset and VEHICLE_CFG.comOffset.x) or 0)
            + resolvedLocalRight * -((VEHICLE_CFG.comOffset and VEHICLE_CFG.comOffset.y) or 0)
            + resolvedLocalUp    * ((VEHICLE_CFG.comOffset and VEHICLE_CFG.comOffset.z) or 0)

        for idx, cfg in ipairs(WHEELS) do
            local isFront     = (cfg.isFront ~= nil) and cfg.isFront or (cfg.pos.x > 0)
            local axleSusp    = SimcadeSuspension and SimcadeSuspension.getAxleCfg(SUSPENSION_CFG, isFront) or SUSPENSION_CFG
            local camberDeg   = axleSusp.camberDeg or 0
            if cfg.camberDeg ~= nil then camberDeg = cfg.camberDeg end
            local alignment = (cfg.casterDeg ~= nil or cfg.steeringCamberDeg ~= nil) and cfg or axleSusp
            local axleVis     = (isFront and WHEEL_DEFAULTS.Front or WHEEL_DEFAULTS.Rear) or {}
            local function pickVis(k, def)
                if cfg[k] ~= nil then return cfg[k] end
                if axleVis[k] ~= nil then return axleVis[k] end
                if WHEEL_DEFAULTS[k] ~= nil then return WHEEL_DEFAULTS[k] end
                return def
            end

            local isRightSide = (cfg.pos.y < 0)
            local rightOffset = -cfg.pos.y
            local localMountPos = comLocal
                + resolvedLocalFwd   * cfg.pos.x
                + resolvedLocalRight * rightOffset
                + resolvedLocalUp    * SimcadeSuspension.mountHeight(cfg, axleSusp)

            local holo = nil
            local visFlag = pickVis("visible", true)
            local dbgHolo = pickVis("debugHolo", true)
            local useWheelHolo = (visFlag ~= false) and (dbgHolo ~= false)
            local leftAng  = pickVis("leftModelAng", Angle(0, 0, 0))
            local rightAng = pickVis("rightModelAng", Angle(0, 180, 0))
            local extraAng = pickVis("angOffset", nil)
            local visOff   = pickVis("visualOffset", nil)

            if useWheelHolo then
                local initHubLocal = localMountPos - resolvedLocalUp * ((1.0 - (axleSusp.restCompression or 0.45)) * (axleSusp.rideHeight or 24))
                local wModel = pickVis("model", "models/sprops/trans/wheel_c/t_wheel25.mdl")
                local wScale = pickVis("scale", Vector(1, 1, 1))
                if type(wScale) == "number" then wScale = Vector(wScale, wScale, wScale) end
                local ok, h = pcall(function()
                    local angle = orientWheelModel(Base:getAngles(), isRightSide and rightAng or leftAng)
                    return hologram.create(Base:localToWorld(initHubLocal), orientWheelModel(angle, extraAng), wModel, wScale)
                end)
                if ok and isValid(h) then
                    pcall(function()
                        local wCol = pickVis("color", nil) or Color(255, 255, 255, 255)
                        local wMat = pickVis("material", nil)
                        -- Starfall sets the render mode from the color's alpha.
                        -- Initialize it for every wheel, including pasted cars.
                        h:setColor(wCol)
                        h:setParent(Base)
                        local wSkin = pickVis("skin", nil)
                        if wSkin ~= nil and h.setSkin then h:setSkin(wSkin) end
                        local wBg = pickVis("bodygroups", nil)
                        if type(wBg) == "table" and h.setBodygroup then
                            for bgId, bgVal in pairs(wBg) do
                                h:setBodygroup(bgId, bgVal)
                            end
                        end
                        local mirrored = wScale.x * wScale.y * wScale.z < 0
                        local reverseFaces = pickVis("reverseFaces", false)
                        h:setCullMode(mirrored ~= reverseFaces and 1 or 0)
                        if wMat and wMat ~= "" and h.setMaterial then h:setMaterial(wMat) end
                    end)
                    holo = h
                end
            end

            local radiusHolo = nil
            local useRadiusHolo = pickVis("debugRadiusHolo", false)
            if useRadiusHolo then
                local rad = cfg.radius or 15
                local wid = cfg.width or 9
                local initHubLocal = localMountPos - resolvedLocalUp * ((1.0 - (axleSusp.restCompression or 0.45)) * (axleSusp.rideHeight or 24))
                local okR, rh = pcall(function()
                    return hologram.create(Base:localToWorld(initHubLocal), Base:getAngles(), "models/holograms/hq_cylinder.mdl", Vector(rad / 6, rad / 6, wid / 12))
                end)
                if okR and isValid(rh) then
                    pcall(function()
                        if rh.setSize then rh:setSize(Vector(rad * 2, rad * 2, wid)) end
                        rh:setColor(Color(0, 220, 255, 110))
                        rh:setParent(Base)
                    end)
                    radiusHolo = rh
                end
            end

            local physicsIdx = (isFront and (not isRightSide) and 0)
                or (isFront and isRightSide and 1)
                or ((not isFront) and isRightSide and 2)
                or 3

            runtimeWheels[idx] = {
                name          = cfg.name,
                physicsIndex    = physicsIdx,
                pos           = cfg.pos,
                mountZ        = SimcadeSuspension.mountHeight(cfg, axleSusp),
                rightOffset   = rightOffset,
                localMountPos = localMountPos,
                isRightSide   = isRightSide,
                isFront       = isFront,
                camberDeg     = camberDeg,
                casterDeg     = alignment.casterDeg or 0,
                legacySteeringCamber = alignment.casterDeg == nil and alignment.steeringCamberDeg or 0,
                steer         = cfg.steer or (isFront and 1 or 0),
                drive         = (cfg.drive ~= false),
                radius        = cfg.radius or 15,
                radiusHolo    = radiusHolo,
                width         = cfg.width or 9,
                leftModelAng  = leftAng,
                rightModelAng = rightAng,
                angOffset     = extraAng,
                visualOffset  = visOff,
                holo          = holo,
                compFraction  = axleSusp.restCompression or 0.45,
                normalLoad    = 0,
                isGrounded    = false,
                contactPos    = Vector(),
                hitNormal     = Vector(0, 0, 1),
                matType       = 67,
                speed         = 0,
                spinDeg       = 0,
                lastTireRes   = nil,
            }
        end

        rebuildStaticFilter()
        rebuildPhysicsCar()
        setupAxleAnchor("FrontAxle", 0, 1)
        setupAxleAnchor("RearAxle", 3, 2)
    end

    net.receive("Simcade_ClientKeyEvent", function(_, ply)
        if not (physicsCar and isValid(Pod)) then return end
        local drv = Pod:getDriver()
        if not isValid(drv) or ply ~= drv then return end

        local code = net.readUInt(4)
        local down = net.readBool()

        if code == 4 then
            ctrlState.ignKeyHeld = down
            return
        end
        if not down then return end

        if code == 2 then
            local man = not physicsCar.manualBox
            physicsCar:setManual(man)
            nextHudNetTime = 0
            sendDriverPrint(drv, man and "Gearbox: MANUAL" or "Gearbox: AUTOMATIC")
        elseif code == 5 then
            if SimcadeCamera and SimcadeCamera.toggleMode then
                local newCam = SimcadeCamera.toggleMode(camServerState)
                pcall(function()
                    net.start("Simcade_CameraMode")
                    net.writeUInt(newCam % 3, 2)
                    SimcadeCore.sendPacket(drv)
                end)
                sendDriverPrint(drv, "Camera Mode: " .. ((newCam == 0 and "Off") or (newCam == 1 and "Chase") or "FPV"))
            end
        elseif code == 7 then
            if physicsCar.manualBox and physicsCar.resetGear then
                physicsCar:resetGear()
            end
        elseif code == 8 or code == 9 or code == 10 then
            if ENGINE_CFG.DiffLockToggleable ~= false and physicsCar.toggleDiffLock then
                local which = (code == 8 and "front") or (code == 9 and "rear") or "all"
                local state = physicsCar:toggleDiffLock(which)
                if state ~= nil then
                    local label = (which == "front" and "Front Diff Lock") or (which == "rear" and "Rear Diff Lock") or "All/Center Diff Lock"
                    sendDriverPrint(drv, string.format("%s: %s", label, state and "LOCKED" or "AUTO/UNLOCKED"))
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local startupInputs = inputNames and wire.isConnected and {}
    local function onWireInput(name, value)
        if startupInputs then startupInputs[name] = true end
        if name == "Pod" then
            Pod = isValid(value) and value or nil
            if isValid(Base) then setupWheels() end
            rebuildStaticFilter()
        elseif name == "Base" then
            if Base ~= value then restoreBaseInertia() end
            Base = isValid(value) and value or nil
            if isValid(Base) then
                local phys = Base:getPhysicsObject()
                if isValid(phys) then
                    lastKnownBaseMass = phys:getMass()
                    totalVehicleMass = math.clamp(lastKnownBaseMass, 15, 50000)
                    if VEHICLE_CFG.BaseInertia and phys ~= inertiaPhys then
                        local previous = phys:getInertia()
                        local ok, err = pcall(function() phys:setInertia(VEHICLE_CFG.BaseInertia) end)
                        if ok then
                            inertiaPhys, originalInertia, appliedInertia = phys, previous, phys:getInertia()
                        else
                            print("[Simcade] Could not set BaseInertia: " .. tostring(err))
                        end
                    end
                end
                setupWheels()
            else
                cleanupWheelHolos()
                for port in pairs(axleAnchors) do clearAxleAnchor(port) end
                physicsCar = nil
            end
        elseif name == "FrontWheels" then
            FrontWheelEnts = type(value) == "table" and value or {}
            if isValid(Base) then setupWheels() end
        elseif name == "RearWheels" then
            RearWheelEnts = type(value) == "table" and value or {}
            if isValid(Base) then setupWheels() end
        elseif name == "ExtraFilterStatic" then
            ExtraFilterStatic = type(value) == "table" and value or {}
            rebuildStaticFilter()
        elseif name == "ExtraFilterSemiDynamic" then
            ExtraFilterSemiDynamic = type(value) == "table" and value or {}
            updateSemiDynamicFilter()
        else
            wireInputs[name] = value
            if addonHost and addonHost.onWireInput then
                addonHost.onWireInput(name, value)
            end
        end
    end
    hook.add("input", "Simcade_WireInputs", onWireInput)

    if startupInputs then
        -- Update checks can finish after Wiremod restores the chip's input values.
        timer.simple(0, function()
            local self = chip()
            local function readConnected(name)
                if startupInputs[name] then return end
                local ok, connected = pcall(wire.isConnected, self, name)
                if ok and connected then onWireInput(name, wire.ports[name]) end
            end
            for _, name in ipairs(inputNames) do
                if name ~= "Base" then readConnected(name) end
            end
            readConnected("Base")
            startupInputs = nil
        end)
    end

    hook.add("think", "Simcade_PhysicsTick", function()
        if not isValid(Base) then return end
        local Phys = Base:getPhysicsObject()
        if not isValid(Phys) then return end

        local nowTick = timer.curtime()
        local targetInterval = 1.0 / math.clamp(VEHICLE_CFG.UpdateRate or 60, 15, 120)
        if lastPhysTickTime > 0 and (nowTick - lastPhysTickTime) < (targetInterval * 0.85) then
            if cachedLiftForcePerSec and not (Base.isPlayerHolding and Base:isPlayerHolding()) then
                local fdt = math.clamp(timer.frametime() or 0.015, 0.002, 0.05)
                local imp = cachedLiftForcePerSec * fdt
                Phys:applyForceCenter(imp)
                skippedLiftApplied = skippedLiftApplied + imp
            end
            return
        end
        local dt = (lastPhysTickTime > 0) and (nowTick - lastPhysTickTime) or timer.frametime()
        lastPhysTickTime = nowTick
        if not dt or dt <= 0 then dt = 0.015 end
        dt = math.clamp(dt, 0.005, 0.05)

        local cpuLoadPct = 0
        if quotaUsed and quotaMax and quotaMax() > 0 then
            cpuLoadPct = (quotaUsed() / quotaMax()) * 100
        end
        local maxCPU = VEHICLE_CFG.MaxCPUPercent or 75
        if cpuLoadPct > 97 then return end
        local physicsMaxSubsteps = (cpuLoadPct > 88) and 1 or ((targetInterval < 0.025) and 2 or ((cpuLoadPct > maxCPU) and 2 or 3))
        tickFilterScanner()

        if Phys:isAsleep() then Phys:wake() end
        local isPhysgunHeld = (Base.isPlayerHolding and Base:isPlayerHolding()) and true or false
        if isPhysgunHeld then
            for _, w in ipairs(runtimeWheels) do w.restAnchor = nil end
            smoothedFwdAccel, smoothedLatAccel = 0, 0
            lastVelWorld = Phys:getVelocity()
            cachedLiftForcePerSec = nil
        end

        if not physicsCar then
            rebuildPhysicsCar()
            if not physicsCar then return end
        end

        local Driver = isValid(Pod) and Pod:getDriver() or nil
        local hasDriver = isValid(Driver)
        if hasDriver and Driver ~= lastHudDriver then
            lastHudDriver = Driver
            ctrlState.autoParkHandbrake = false
            ctrlState._netTokens = 3.0
            rebuildStaticFilter(Driver)
            local hudEnabled, hudError = pcall(enableHud, Driver, true)
            if not hudEnabled then
                pcall(printConsole, "[Simcade] Could not connect the driver's HUD: " .. tostring(hudError))
            end
            if (not net.canSend or net.canSend()) and net.getBytesLeft() > 64 then
                pcall(function()
                    net.start("Simcade_CameraMode")
                    net.writeUInt((camServerState.camMode or 1) % 3, 2)
                    SimcadeCore.sendPacket(Driver)
                end)
            end
            local sIgn = keyLabel(kIgn, "F")
            local sMan = keyLabel(kManual, "M")
            local sRst = keyLabel(kGearRst, "R")
            local sCam = keyLabel(kCam, "V")
            sendDriverPrint(Driver, "Controls:")
            sendDriverPrint(Driver, "WASD=Drive | Shift=Full Thr | Alt=Clutch | Space=Handbrake")
            sendDriverPrint(Driver, string.format("%s=Ignition | %s=Manual (M1/M2=Shift, %s=Reset) | %s=Camera", sIgn, sMan, sRst, sCam))
            if ENGINE_CFG.ClassicAuto then
                sendDriverPrint(Driver, "Automatic: GearUp / GearDown (default M1 / M2) select P, R, N, D")
            end
            if ENGINE_CFG.DiffLockToggleable ~= false then
                sendDriverPrint(Driver, string.format("Diff Locks: %s=Front | %s=Rear | %s=All", keyLabel(kFrontLk, "K"), keyLabel(kRearLk, "L"), keyLabel(kAllLk, "J")))
            end
        elseif not hasDriver and lastHudDriver ~= nil then
            if isValid(lastHudDriver) and lastHudDriver ~= owner() then
