            return effectsSample.wheels and effectsSample or nil
        end

        -- Trace from the displayed car. The predicted chassis can be ahead of
        -- the rendered wheels and would put smoke and marks in the wrong place.
        local up = base:localToWorldVector(localUp):getNormalized()
        local sample = {base = base, time = now, up = up,
            fwd = base:localToWorldVector(localFwd):getNormalized(), wheels = {}}
        for i, mount in ipairs(mounts) do
            local tire = view.tires[mount.index]
            local wheel = {grounded = false, skid = 0, time = now}
            if tire then
                local offset = mount.offset
                if mount.halfTrack then
                    local roll = math.rad(car.wheels[mount.index].beamRollDeg or 0)
                    offset = offset + localLeft * (mount.halfTrack * (math.cos(roll) - 1))
                end
                local origin = base:localToWorld(view.comLocal + offset)
                local hit = trace.line(origin, origin - up * mount.length, {base, pod}, MASK.SOLID)
                -- Props and other dynamic contacts need the server's filters.
                if hit.Hit and not hit.HitWorld then
                    sample.wheels = nil
                    effectsSample = sample
                    return
                end
                if hit.HitWorld and not hit.StartSolid then
                    wheel.grounded = true
                    wheel.pos, wheel.norm, wheel.matType = hit.HitPos, hit.HitNormal, SimcadeTires.resolveMaterial(hit, cfg.TIRE_CFG)
                    wheel.skid = tire.skid
                    wheel.rimSpeed = math.abs(tire.spin * mount.radius)
                    wheel.width, wheel.isRear = mount.width, mount.isRear
                end
            end
            sample.wheels[i] = wheel
        end
        effectsSample = sample
        return sample
    end
end
4755
-- name Simcade Lights Addon
-- shared

return function(L, cfg)
    if L.enabled == false then return nil end

    local state = {
        headlightMode = 0, -- 0 = Off, 1 = Low Beam, 2 = High Beam
        holdStart = nil,
        holdOffHandled = false,
    }

    local function bindHeadlights(ctx)
        local defaultKey = (cfg.CONTROLS_CFG and cfg.CONTROLS_CFG.Headlights) or (KEY and KEY.H) or 18
        ctx.bindKey("Headlights", defaultKey,
            function(_c, _drv)
                state.holdStart = timer.curtime and timer.curtime() or 0
                state.holdOffHandled = false
            end,
            function(c, drv)
                if state.holdStart and not state.holdOffHandled then
                    if state.headlightMode == 0 then
                        state.headlightMode = 1
                    else
                        state.headlightMode = (state.headlightMode == 1) and 2 or 1
                    end
                    local msg = (state.headlightMode == 1) and "Headlights: Low Beam" or "Headlights: High Beam"
                    if c.notifyDriver and isValid(drv) then c.notifyDriver(drv, msg) end
                end
                state.holdStart = nil
                state.holdOffHandled = false
            end
        )
    end

    return {
        name = "SimcadeLights",
        OnClientInit = bindHeadlights,
        OnInit = function(ctx)
            bindHeadlights(ctx)
            ctx.addWireOutput("HeadlightsOn", "Number")
            ctx.addWireOutput("LowBeam", "Number")
            ctx.addWireOutput("LowBeamFOV", "Number")
            ctx.addWireOutput("LowBeamBrightness", "Number")
            ctx.addWireOutput("HighBeam", "Number")
            ctx.addWireOutput("HighBeamFOV", "Number")
            ctx.addWireOutput("HighBeamBrightness", "Number")
            ctx.addWireOutput("HeadlightFOV", "Number")
            ctx.addWireOutput("HeadlightBrightness", "Number")
            ctx.addWireOutput("HeadlightColor", "Vector")
            ctx.addWireOutput("ReverseLight", "Number")
            ctx.addWireOutput("ReverseLightColor", "Vector")
            ctx.addWireOutput("BrakeLight", "Number")
            ctx.addWireOutput("BrakeLightColor", "Vector")
        end,
        OnPostPhysics = function(ctx, _dt, out)
            if state.holdStart and not state.holdOffHandled then
                local now = timer.curtime and timer.curtime() or 0
                if (now - state.holdStart) >= (L.HoldOffTime or 0.40) then
                    state.headlightMode = 0
                    state.holdOffHandled = true
                    if ctx.notifyDriver and isValid(ctx.driver) then
                        ctx.notifyDriver(ctx.driver, "Headlights: OFF")
                    end
                end
            end

            local mode = state.headlightMode
            local on = mode > 0
            local sep = (L.SeparateBulbs ~= false)
            local excl = (L.ExclusiveLowHigh == true)

            local lowOn = (mode == 1) or (mode == 2 and (not sep or not excl))
            local highOn = (mode == 2)

            local lowFov  = L.LowBeamFov or 65
            local lowBri  = L.LowBeamBrightness or 1
            local highFov = L.HighBeamFov or 80
            local highBri = L.HighBeamBrightness or 2

            local fov = highOn and highFov or (lowOn and lowFov or 0)
            local bri = highOn and highBri or (lowOn and lowBri or 0)
            local col = on and (L.WireLColor or Vector(255, 255, 255)) or Vector(0, 0, 0)

            ctx.setWireOutput("HeadlightsOn", on and 1 or 0)
            ctx.setWireOutput("LowBeam", lowOn and 1 or 0)
            ctx.setWireOutput("LowBeamFOV", lowOn and lowFov or 0)
            ctx.setWireOutput("LowBeamBrightness", lowOn and lowBri or 0)
            ctx.setWireOutput("HighBeam", highOn and 1 or 0)
            ctx.setWireOutput("HighBeamFOV", highOn and highFov or 0)
            ctx.setWireOutput("HighBeamBrightness", highOn and highBri or 0)
            ctx.setWireOutput("HeadlightFOV", fov)
            ctx.setWireOutput("HeadlightBrightness", bri)
            ctx.setWireOutput("HeadlightColor", col)

            local st = ctx.state or {}
            local isRev = (out and out.gearShown == 0) or st.reversing or false
            local isBrk = (not isRev) and ((st.brake or 0) > 0.05)

            ctx.setWireOutput("ReverseLight", isRev and 1 or 0)
            ctx.setWireOutput("ReverseLightColor", isRev and (L.ReverseWireLightColor or Vector(255, 255, 255)) or Vector(0, 0, 0))
            ctx.setWireOutput("BrakeLight", isBrk and 1 or 0)
            ctx.setWireOutput("BrakeLightColor", isBrk and (L.BrakeWireLightColor or Vector(255, 0, 0)) or Vector(0, 0, 0))
        end,
    }
end
5236
-- name Simcade Brake Temperature Addon
-- shared

return function(options)
    local ambient = options.AmbientTemp or options.ambientC or 20
    local ceiling = math.max(ambient, options.MaxTemp or options.maxC or 800)
    local heatRate = options.HeatRate or (0.35 * 4 / math.max(0.01, options.heatCapacityKJPerC or 4))
    local heatScale = math.max(0, heatRate * (options.SpeedHeatScale or 1))
    local coolRate = math.max(0, options.CoolRate or 0.08)
    local airflow = math.max(0, options.SpeedCoolScale or 0.15)
    local stoppedMult = math.max(0, options.StoppedCoolMult or 0.5)
    -- Solver slots run FL, FR, RR, RL.
    local fields = { [0] = "TempFL", "TempFR", "TempRR", "TempRL" }
    local temperatures = {}
    local extraWheels = {}
    local extraTemperatures = {}
    local rpmToKmh = math.max(0, options.WheelRPMToSpeed or 0.15)
    local values = { TempMax = ambient, WheelTemps = {}, ExtraWheelTemps = {} }
    for slot = 0, 3 do
        values[fields[slot]] = ambient
    end

    return {
        name = "BrakeTemperature",
        OnClientInit = function(ctx)
            ctx.syncedState.BrakeTemperature = values
        end,
        OnInit = function(ctx)
            ctx.syncedState.BrakeTemperature = values
            for slot = 0, 3 do
                ctx.addWireOutput("Brake" .. fields[slot], "Number")
            end
            ctx.addWireOutput("BrakeTempMax", "Number")
            ctx.addWireOutput("BrakeWheelTemps", "Array")
            ctx.addWireOutput("ExtraBrakeWheelTemps", "Array")
            ctx.addWireOutput("ExtraBrakeWheels", "Array")
            ctx.addWireInput("ExtraBrakeWheels", "Array", function(c, wheels)
                extraWheels = type(wheels) == "table" and wheels or {}
                -- Keep the temperature with the entity when a trailer is rewired.
                local retained = {}
                for _, ent in ipairs(extraWheels) do
                    if isValid(ent) then retained[ent] = extraTemperatures[ent] or ambient end
                end
                extraTemperatures = retained
                c.setWireOutput("ExtraBrakeWheels", extraWheels)
            end)
        end,
        OnPrePhysics = function(ctx)
            -- Wheels can skip their brake solve while sleeping or airborne.
            for slot = 0, 3 do
                ctx.car.wheels[slot].serviceBrakeKNm = 0
            end
        end,
        OnPostPhysics = function(ctx, dt)
            local speed = math.abs(ctx.state.kmh)
            local passive = speed < 2 and coolRate * stoppedMult or coolRate
            -- Convert the engine sim's 66 Hz cooling step to Simcade's tick length.
            local loss = (passive + speed * airflow / 66) / math.max(1, ceiling)
            local retention = math.max(0, 1 - loss) ^ (66 * dt)
            local brakes = ctx.car.braking
            local fullBrake = math.max(0.001, brakes.frontBrakeKNm, brakes.rearBrakeKNm)
            local hottest = ambient
            local function integrate(previous, demand, kmh)
                local heat = demand * kmh * heatScale * dt
                return math.clamp(ambient + (previous + heat - ambient) * retention, ambient, ceiling)
            end
            local corners = {}
            for slot = 0, 3 do corners[slot] = ambient end
            local wheelTemps = {}
            for i, runtime in ipairs(ctx.wheels) do
                local slot = runtime.physicsIndex
                local wheel = ctx.car.wheels[slot]
                local wheelKmh = math.abs(wheel.w) * runtime.radius * 0.0254 * 3.6
                temperatures[i] = integrate(temperatures[i] or ambient, wheel.serviceBrakeKNm / fullBrake, wheelKmh)
                wheelTemps[i] = math.round(temperatures[i], 1)
                corners[slot] = math.max(corners[slot], wheelTemps[i])
                hottest = math.max(hottest, wheelTemps[i])
            end
            local extraTemps = {}
            local updated = {}
            for i, ent in ipairs(extraWheels) do
                local temp = ambient
                if isValid(ent) and ent.getAngleVelocity then
                    -- Spin magnitude is independent of how the wheel prop was mounted.
                    local rpm = ent:getAngleVelocity():getLength() / 6
                    temp = updated[ent] or integrate(extraTemperatures[ent] or ambient, ctx.state.brake, rpm * rpmToKmh)
                    updated[ent] = temp
                    extraTemps[i] = math.round(temp, 1)
                else
                    extraTemps[i] = ambient
                end
                hottest = math.max(hottest, extraTemps[i])
            end
            extraTemperatures = updated
            for slot = 0, 3 do
                local field = fields[slot]
                values[field] = corners[slot]
                ctx.setWireOutput("Brake" .. field, corners[slot])
            end
            values.TempMax = math.round(hottest, 1)
            values.WheelTemps = wheelTemps
            values.ExtraWheelTemps = extraTemps
            ctx.setWireOutput("BrakeTempMax", values.TempMax)
            ctx.setWireOutput("BrakeWheelTemps", wheelTemps)
            ctx.setWireOutput("ExtraBrakeWheelTemps", extraTemps)
        end,
    }
end
10349
-- name Simcade Backfire and Anti-Lag Addon
-- shared

local defaults = {
    TriggerMode = 3, -- 1: Overrun only, 2: RevLimit only, 3: Both
    MinRPMThreshold = 4000, -- Minimum RPM to trigger overrun backfire
    OverrunThrottleDrop = 0.4, -- Throttle must drop below this to trigger Overrun backfire
    OverrunPopChance = 0.35, -- Chance when throttle is released after load
    OverrunSustainChance = 0.12, -- Chance per second for extra pops while coasting at high RPM
    OverrunMinLoadTime = 0.25, -- Must be on throttle this long before lift-off can pop
    OverrunWindow = 0.55, -- Seconds after lift-off where overrun pops are allowed
    OverrunCooldownMin = 0.06,
    OverrunCooldownMax = 0.25,
    OverrunBurstChance = 0.35,
    OverrunBurstMin = 2,
    OverrunBurstMax = 4,
    BurstIntervalMin = 0.04,
    BurstIntervalMax = 0.12,
    RPMChancePower = 1.35, -- Higher values make low-RPM pops rarer
    EnableAntiLag = true,
    AntiLagBoostThreshold = 1.05,
    AntiLagThrottleMax = 0.35,
    AntiLagMinRPM = 3500,
    AntiLagPopChance = 0.45, -- Chance per second while off-throttle with boost
    AntiLagCooldownMin = 0.10,
    AntiLagCooldownMax = 0.24,
    EnableDownshiftPops = true,
    DownshiftPopChance = 0.65, -- Chance when gear drops under RPM/load
    DownshiftSustainChance = 0.18, -- Chance per second for follow-up burbles after downshift
    DownshiftMinRPM = 3200,
    DownshiftThrottleMax = 0.55,
    DownshiftWindow = 0.45,
    DownshiftCooldownMin = 0.05,
    DownshiftCooldownMax = 0.18,
    DownshiftBurstChance = 0.45,

    RevLimitPopChance = 10, -- Chance per second while on rev limiter
    RevLimitRPMThreshold = 0.95, -- % of RevLimiter RPM required to start popping (0.95 = 95%)
    RevLimitThrottle = 0.4, -- Minimum Throttle needed to trigger 2-Step / RevLimit popping
    RevLimitCooldownMin = 0.01,
    RevLimitCooldownMax = 0.05,
    RevLimitBurstChance = 1,

    -- Sounds
    SoundVolume = 1,
    SoundPitchMin = 85,
    SoundPitchMax = 115,

    -- Support for both Web URLs and standard paths
    UseURLSounds = true,
    URLSounds = {
        -- Add URLs to raw .ogg or .mp3 files here
        "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/nokillnando/forzaapexsounds/main/misc/burble/burble_oneshot_test1.ogg",
        "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/nokillnando/forzaapexsounds/main/misc/burble/burble_oneshot_test2.ogg",
        "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/nokillnando/forzaapexsounds/main/misc/burble/burble_oneshot_test3.ogg"
    },
    InGameSounds = {
        "acf_thecrew/useful/backfire/backfire_drain_10.wav",
        "acf_thecrew/useful/backfire/backfire_gargle_15.wav",
        "acf_thecrew/useful/backfire/backfire_splutter_20.wav"
    },

    -- Effects: Multiple effects can play simultaneously
    Effects = {
        { effecttype = "effect", effectname = "MuzzleEffect", scale = 0.4 },
        { effecttype = "effect", effectname = "AirboatMuzzleFlash", scale = 0.7 },
    },
}

return function(options)
    local cfg = {}
    for key, value in pairs(defaults) do cfg[key] = value end
    for key, value in pairs(options) do cfg[key] = value end

    local name = "BackfireAntiLag"
    if CLIENT then
        local playSound, soundEntry, emitter, flashMat
        local function flash(ent, spec)
            local pos = ent:getPos()
            local scale = spec.scale or 1
            if spec.effecttype == "custom" then
                flashMat = flashMat or material.load("effects/muzzleflash1")
                emitter = emitter or particle.create(pos, false)
                if emitter.setPos then emitter:setPos(pos) end
                local puff = emitter:add(flashMat, pos, 20 * scale, 35 * scale, 0, 0, 255, 0, 0.08)
                if puff then puff:setVelocity(ent:getForward() * -100) end
            elseif spec.effecttype == "particle" then
                particleEffect.attach(ent, spec.effectname, 1, {origin = pos, angles = ent:getAngles()})
            elseif spec.effecttype == "effect" then
                local fx = effect.create()
                fx:setEntity(ent)
                fx:setOrigin(pos)
                fx:setAngles(ent:getAngles())
                fx:setScale(scale)
                if fx.play then fx:play(spec.effectname) else effect.play(spec.effectname, fx) end
            end
        end

        local function play(ent)
            for _, spec in ipairs(cfg.Effects) do pcall(flash, ent, spec) end
            if playSound then playSound(ent, ent:getPos(), soundEntry) end
        end

        return {
            name = name,
            OnSoundReady = function(_, preload, oneShot)
                local paths = cfg.UseURLSounds and cfg.URLSounds or cfg.InGameSounds
                if #paths == 0 or cfg.SoundVolume <= 0 then return end
                soundEntry = {
                    variants = paths, volume = cfg.SoundVolume, loadMode = "stream",
                    pitchMin = cfg.SoundPitchMin / 100, pitchMax = cfg.SoundPitchMax / 100,
                }
                preload(soundEntry)
                playSound = oneShot
            end,
            OnClientEvent = function(_, eventName, exhausts)
                if eventName ~= name then return end
                if #exhausts == 0 then play(chip()) end
                for _, id in ipairs(exhausts) do
                    local ent = entity(id)
                    if isValid(ent) then play(ent) end
                end
            end,
            OnCleanup = function()
                if emitter and emitter.destroy then emitter:destroy() end
            end,
        }
    end

    local function range(low, high)
        return low + math.random() * (high - low)
    end
    local function chance(p)
        return math.random() < p
    end

    local prevThrottle, prevRPM, prevGear, loaded = 0, 0, 0, 0
    local overrunUntil, downshiftUntil, nextPop = 0, 0, 0
    local burstSource, burstLeft, nextBurst = nil, 0, 0
    return {
        name = name,
        OnInit = function(ctx)
            ctx.addWireInput("Exhausts", "Array")
        end,
        OnPostPhysics = function(ctx, dt)
            local s = ctx.state
            if not s.running then
                prevThrottle, prevRPM, prevGear, loaded = 0, 0, 0, 0
                overrunUntil, downshiftUntil, nextPop, burstLeft = 0, 0, 0, 0
                return
            end
            local now = timer.curtime()
            local rpm, throttle, gear = s.rpm, s.throttle, s.gear
            local limiter = s.limiterRPM
            local factor = math.clamp((rpm - cfg.MinRPMThreshold) / math.max(1, limiter - cfg.MinRPMThreshold), 0, 1) ^ cfg.RPMChancePower
            local lowThrottle = throttle <= cfg.OverrunThrottleDrop
            local lift = lowThrottle and prevThrottle > cfg.OverrunThrottleDrop and loaded >= cfg.OverrunMinLoadTime
            local drop = math.clamp((prevThrottle - throttle) / math.max(0.01, cfg.OverrunThrottleDrop), 0.25, 1)
            local down = gear > 0 and rpm > cfg.DownshiftMinRPM and throttle <= cfg.DownshiftThrottleMax and cfg.EnableDownshiftPops
            local shifted = down and prevGear > gear
            local kick = math.clamp((rpm - prevRPM) / math.max(1, limiter * 0.18), 0.55, 1)
            prevThrottle, prevRPM, prevGear = throttle, rpm, gear
            loaded = lowThrottle and math.max(0, loaded - dt * 0.5) or loaded + dt
            if lift and rpm > cfg.MinRPMThreshold then overrunUntil = now + cfg.OverrunWindow end
            if shifted then downshiftUntil = now + cfg.DownshiftWindow end

            local overrun = cfg.TriggerMode ~= 2 and lowThrottle and rpm > cfg.MinRPMThreshold and now < overrunUntil
            down = cfg.TriggerMode ~= 2 and down and now < downshiftUntil
            local limiting = cfg.TriggerMode ~= 1 and rpm > limiter * cfg.RevLimitRPMThreshold and throttle > cfg.RevLimitThrottle
            local boosted = cfg.TriggerMode ~= 2 and cfg.EnableAntiLag and throttle <= cfg.AntiLagThrottleMax
                and rpm > cfg.AntiLagMinRPM and s.turboBoostRatio >= cfg.AntiLagBoostThreshold

            if (burstSource == "Overrun" and not overrun) or (burstSource == "Downshift" and not down)
                or (burstSource == "RevLimit" and not limiting) then burstLeft = 0 end
            if now < nextPop then return end

            local source, startBurst
            if burstLeft > 0 and now >= nextBurst then
                source = burstSource
                burstLeft = burstLeft - 1
            elseif limiting and chance(cfg.RevLimitPopChance * math.max(0.5, factor) * dt) then
                source, startBurst = "RevLimit", true
            elseif down and shifted and chance(cfg.DownshiftPopChance * math.max(0.35, factor) * kick
                * math.max(0.35, 1 - throttle / math.max(0.01, cfg.DownshiftThrottleMax))) then
                source, startBurst = "Downshift", true
            elseif overrun and lift and chance(cfg.OverrunPopChance * factor * drop) then
                source, startBurst = "Overrun", true
            elseif down and chance(cfg.DownshiftSustainChance * math.max(0.35, factor) * dt) then
                source = "Downshift"
            elseif overrun and chance(cfg.OverrunSustainChance * factor * dt) then
                source = "Overrun"
            elseif boosted and chance(cfg.AntiLagPopChance * math.max(0.35, factor)
                * math.clamp((s.turboBoostRatio - cfg.AntiLagBoostThreshold) / 0.5, 0.25, 1) * dt) then
                source = "AntiLag"
            end
            if not source then return end
            if startBurst and chance(cfg[source .. "BurstChance"]) then
                local low = source == "Overrun" and cfg.OverrunBurstMin or 2
                local high = source == "Overrun" and cfg.OverrunBurstMax or 3
                burstLeft = math.random(low, math.max(low, high)) - 1
                burstSource = source
            end
            nextBurst = now + range(cfg.BurstIntervalMin, cfg.BurstIntervalMax)
            nextPop = now + math.max(0.10, range(cfg[source .. "CooldownMin"], cfg[source .. "CooldownMax"]))
            local exhausts = {}
            for _, ent in ipairs(ctx.getWireInput("Exhausts") or {}) do
                if isValid(ent) then exhausts[#exhausts + 1] = ent:entIndex() end
            end
            ctx.emitEvent(name, exhausts)
        end,
    }
end
